




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saddanītippakaraṇaṃ (padamālā)

ganthārambhakathā

dhīraihi  magganāyaina, yaina buddhaina daisitaṃ|

sitaṃ dhammamidhañ‍ñāya, ñāyatai amataṃ padaṃ||1||

taṃ namitvā mahāvīraṃ, sabbañ‍ñuṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

mahākāruṇikaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, visuddhaṃ suddhidāyakaṃ||2||

saddhammañ‍cassa pūjaitvā, suddhaṃ santamasaṅkhataṃ|

atak‍kāvacaraṃ suṭṭhu, vibhattaṃ madhuraṃ sivaṃ||3||

saṅghassa ca’ñ‍jaliṃ katvā, puñ‍ñakkhaittassa tādinau|

sīlasamādhipañ‍ñādi-visuddhaguṇajautinau||4||

namassanādipuñ‍ñassa, katassa ratanattayai|

taijasāhaṃ pahantvāna, antarāyai asaisatau||5||

laukanītiviyattassa, satthu saddhammanītinau|

sāsanatthaṃ pavakkhāmi, saddanītimanākulaṃ||6||

āsavakkhayalābhaina, hauti sāsanasampadā|

āsavakkhayalābhau ca, sac‍cādhigamahaitukau||7||

sac‍cādhigamanaṃ  tañ‍ca, paṭipattissitaṃ mataṃ|

paṭipatti ca sā kāmaṃ, pariyattiparāyaṇā||8||

pariyattābhiyuttānaṃ, viditvā saddalakkhaṇaṃ|

yasmā na hauti sammauhau, akkharaisu padaisu ca||9||

yasmā cāmauhabhāvaina, akkharaisu padaisu ca|

pāḷiyatthaṃ vijānanti, viñ‍ñū sugatasāsanai||10||

pāḷiyatthāvabaudhaina, yaunisau satthusāsanai|

sappañ‍ñā paṭipaj‍janti, paṭipattimatandikā||11||

yaunisau paṭipaj‍jitvā, dhammaṃ laukuttaraṃ varaṃ|

pāpuṇanti visuddhāya, sīlādipaṭipattiyā||12||

tasmā tadatthikā suddhaṃ, nayaṃ nissāya viñ‍ñunaṃ|

bhañ‍ñamānaṃ mayā sadda-nītiṃ gaṇhantu sādhukaṃ||13||

dhātu dhātūhi nipphan‍na-rūpāni ca salakkhaṇau|

sandhināmādibhaidau ca, padānaṃ tu vibhatti ca||14||

pāḷinayādayauc‍caiva-maittha nānappakāratau|

sāsanassaupakārāya, bhavissati vibhāvanā||14||
1. savikaraṇākhyātavibhāga

tattha dhātūti kainaṭṭhaina dhātu? sakatthampi dhāraitīti dhātu, atthātisayayaugatau paratthampi dhāraitīti dhātu, vīsatiyā upasaggaisu yaina kainaci upasaggaina atthavisaisakāraṇaina paṭibaddhā atthavisaisampi dhāraitīti dhātu, ‘‘ayaṃ imissā atthau, ayamitau pac‍cayau parau’’tiādinā anaikappakāraina paṇḍitaihi dhāriyati aisātipi dhātu, vidahanti vidunau aitāya saddanipphattiṃ ayalauhādimayaṃ  ayalauhādidhātūhi viyātipi dhātu| aivaṃ tāva dhātusaddassatthau vaiditabbau|

dhātusaddau jinamatai, itthiliṅgattanai matau|

satthai pul‍liṅgabhāvasmiṃ, kac‍cāyanamatai dvisu||

atha vā jinamatai ‘‘tatau gautamidhātūnī’’ti aittha dhātusaddau liṅgavipal‍lāsai vattati ‘‘pabbatāni vanāni cā’’ti aittha pabbatasaddau viya, na panaittha vattabbaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhivācakattā napuṃsakaniddaisau’’ti aṭṭhivācakattaipi ‘‘dhātuyau’’ti itthiliṅgadassanatau| bhūvādayau saddā dhātavau| saiyyathidaṃ? bhū i ku kai tak‍ka taka taki sukaic‍cādayau| gaṇatau tai aṭṭhavidhā bhūvādigaṇau rudhādigaṇau divādigaṇau svādigaṇau kiyādigaṇau gahādigaṇau tanādigaṇau curādigaṇau cāti| idāni taisaṃ vikaraṇasañ‍ñitai pac‍cayai dassaissāma| anaikavidhā hi pac‍cayā nānappakāraisu nāmanāma kitanāma samāsanāma taddhitanāmākhyātaisu pavattanatau| saṅkhaipatau pana duvidhāva nāmapac‍cayau ākhyātapac‍cayau cāti| tatrāpi ākhyātapac‍cayā duvidhā vikaraṇapac‍cayanauvikaraṇapac‍cayavasaina| tattha vikaraṇapac‍cayau akārādisattarasavidhau aggahitaggahaṇaina pan‍narasavidhau ca| nauvikaraṇapac‍cayau pana kha cha sādinaikavidhau| yai rūpanipphattiyā upakārakā atthavisaisassa jautakā vā ajautakā vā laupanīyā vā alaupanīyā vā, tai saddā pac‍cayā|

paṭic‍ca kāraṇaṃ taṃ taṃ, aintīti pac‍cayātha vā|

paṭic‍ca saddanipphatti, itau aitīti pac‍cayā||

nāmikappac‍cayānaṃ  yau, vibhāgau āvi haissati|

nāmakappai yatau tasmā, na taṃ vitthārayāmasai||

yau nauvikaraṇānaṃ tu, pac‍cayānaṃ vibhāgatau|

sau panākhyātakappamhi, vitthārainā’gamissatīti||

ic‍cānaikavidhaisu pac‍cayaisu ‘‘vikaraṇapac‍cayā nāma imai’’ti sal‍lakkhaitabbā| kathaṃ? bhūvādigaṇatau apac‍cayau hauti kattari, rudhādigaṇatau akārivaṇṇaikāraukārapac‍cayā haunti kattari, pubbamajjhaṭṭhānai niggahītāgamau ca, divādigaṇatau yapac‍cayau hauti kattari, svādigaṇatau ṇu ṇā uṇāpac‍cayā haunti kattari, kiyādigaṇatau nāpac‍cayau hauti kattari, gahādigaṇatau ppa ṇhāpac‍cayā haunti kattari, tanādigaṇatau au yirapac‍cayā haunti kattari, curādigaṇatau ṇai ṇayapac‍cayā haunti kattari|

akārau ca ivaṇṇau ca, ai aukārā ca yau tathā|

ṇu ṇā uṇā ca nā ppa ṇhā-yirā ṇai ṇayapac‍cayā||

aggahitaggahaṇaina, aivaṃ pan‍narasai’ritā|

vikaraṇavhayā aitai, pac‍cayāti vibhāvayai||

yai aivaṃ niddiṭṭhaihi vikaraṇapac‍cayaihi tadañ‍ñaihi ca sappac‍cayā aṭṭhavidhā dhātugaṇā suttantaisu bahūpakārā, taisvāyaṃ bhūvādigaṇau| bhū sattāyaṃ, bhūdhātu vij‍jamānatāyaṃ vattati| sakammikākammikāsu dhātūsu ayaṃ akammikā dhātu, na pana ‘‘dhammabhūtau’’tiādīsu pattiatthavācikā aparā bhūdhātu viya sakammikā| aisā hi pariabhiādīhi upasaggaihi yuttāyaiva sakammikā bhavati, na upa parā pātuādīhi upasagganipātaihi yuttāpi| atau imissā siddhāni rūpāni dvidhā ñaiyyāni akammakapadāni sakammakapadāni cāti|

suddhakattukriyāpadaniddaisa

tatra  bhavati ubbhavati samubbhavati pabhavati parābhavati sambhavati vibhavati, bhauti sambhauti vibhauti pātubhavati pātubbhavati pātubhauti, imāni akammakapadāni| aittha pātuiti nipātau, sau ‘‘āvibhavati tiraubhavatī’’tiādīsu āvi tiraunipātā viya bhūdhātutau nipphan‍nākhyātasaddassa naiva visaisakarau, na ca sakammakattasādhakau| uic‍cādayau upasaggā, tai pana visaisakarā, na sakammakattasādhakā| yaisamatthau kammaina sambandhanīyau na hauti, tāni padāni akammakāni| akammakapadānaṃ yathārahaṃ sakammakākammakavasaina atthau kathaitabbau| paribhauti paribhavati, abhibhauti abhibhavati, adhibhauti adhibhavati, atibhauti atibhavati, anubhauti anubhavati, samanubhauti samanubhavati, abhisambhauti abhisambhavati, imāni sakammakapadāni| aittha pariic‍cādayau upasaggā, tai bhūdhātutau nipphan‍nākhyātasaddassa visaisakarā caiva sakammakattasādhakā ca| yaisamatthau kammaina sambandhanīyau, tāni padāni sakammakāni| sakammakapadānaṃ sakammakavasaina atthau kathaitabbau, k‍vaci akammakavasainapi| aivaṃ suddhakattukriyāpadāni bhavanti| uddaisauyaṃ|

tatra bhavatīti hauti vij‍jati pañ‍ñāyati sarūpaṃ labhati| ubbhavatīti uppaj‍jati sarūpaṃ labhati| samubbhavatīti samuppaj‍jati sarūpaṃ labhati| pabhavatīti hauti sambhavati| atha vā pabhavatīti yatau kutauci sandati, na vicchij‍jati, avicchin‍naṃ hauti, taṃ taṃ ṭhānaṃ visarati| parābhavatīti parābhavau hauti byasanaṃ āpaj‍jati avuddhiṃ pāpuṇāti| sambhavatīti suṭṭhu bhavati vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jati| vibhavatīti ucchij‍jati vinassati vipaj‍jati, visaisatau vā bhavati sampaj‍jati| bhauti sambhauti vibhautīti imāni ‘‘bhavati  sambhavati vibhavatī’’ti imaihi yathāk‍kamaṃ samānaniddaisāni| pātubhavatīti pakāsati dissati pañ‍ñāyati pākaṭaṃ hauti| pātubbhavati pātubhautīti imāni ‘‘pātubhavatī’’ti iminā samānaniddaisāni| aivaṃ akammakapadānaṃ yathārahaṃ sakammakākammakavasaina atthakathanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamuttaratrāpi añ‍ñaisampi akammakapadānaṃ|

paribhautidukādīsu pana sattasu dukaisu yathāk‍kamaṃ dvai dvai padāni samānatthāni, tasmā dvai dvai padāniyaiva gahaitvā niddisissāma| tatra paribhauti paribhavatīti paraṃ hiṃsati pīḷaiti, atha vā hīḷaiti avajānāti| abhibhauti abhibhavatīti paraṃ ajjhauttharati maddati| adhibhauti adhibhavatīti paraṃ abhimadditvā bhavati attanau vasaṃ vattāpaiti| atibhauti atibhavatīti paraṃ atik‍kamitvā bhavati| anubhauti anubhavatīti sukhadukkhaṃ vaidaiti paribhuñ‍jati sukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidī hauti| samanubhauti samanubhavatīti sukhadukkhaṃ suṭṭhu vaidaiti suṭṭhu paribhuñ‍jati suṭṭhu sukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidī hauti| abhisambhauti abhisambhavatīti paraṃ ajjhauttharati maddati| aivaṃ sakammakapadānaṃ sakammakavasaina atthakathanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| katthaci pana gacchatīti pavattatīti aivaṃ akammakavasainapi| aivamuttaratrāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ sakammakapadānaṃ|

apac‍cayau parau hauti, bhūvādigaṇatau sati|

suddhakattukriyākhyānai, sabbadhātukanissitai||

ayaṃ suddhakattukriyāpadānaṃ niddaisau|

haitukattukriyāpadaniddaisa

bhāvaiti vibhāvaitisambhāvaiti paribhāvaiti, aivaṃ haitukattukriyāpadāni bhavanti| aikakammakavasainaisamatthau gahaitabbau| pacchimassa pana dvikammakavasainapi| paribhāvāpaiti abhibhāvāpaiti anubhāvāpaiti, aivampi haitukattukriyāpadāni bhavanti| dvikammakavasainaisamatthau gahaitabbau| ic‍caivaṃ dvidhā haitukattukriyāpadāni ñaiyyāni, añ‍ñānipi gahaitabbāni|

tatra bhāvaitīti puggalau bhāvaitabbaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci bhāvaiti āsaivati bahulīkarauti, atha vā bhāvaitīti vaḍḍhaiti| vibhāvaitīti bhāvaitabbaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci vibhāvaiti visaisaina bhāvaiti, vividhaina vā ākāraina bhāvaiti bhāvayati vaḍḍhaiti, atha vā vibhāvaitīti abhāvaiti antaradhāpaiti| sambhāvaitīti yassa kassaci guṇaṃ sambhāvaiti sambhāvayati suṭṭhu pakāsaiti uk‍kaṃsaiti| paribhāvaitīti paribhāvaitabbaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci paribhāvaiti paribhāvayati samantatau vaḍḍhaiti| aivaṃ aikakammakavasainatthau gahaitabbau| atha vā paribhāvaitīti vāsaitabbaṃ vatthuṃ paribhāvaiti paribhāvayati vāsaiti gandhaṃ gāhāpaiti| aivaṃ dvikammakavasaināpi atthau gahaitabbau| paribhāvāpaitīti puggalau puggalaina sapattaṃ paribhāvāpaiti hiṃsāpaiti, atha vā paribhāvāpaitīti hīḷāpaiti avajānāpaiti| abhibhāvāpaitīti puggalau puggalaina sapattaṃ abhibhāvāpaiti ajjhauttharāpaiti| anubhāvāpaitīti puggalau puggalaina sampattiṃ anubhāvāpaiti paribhaujaiti|

payuttau kattunā yaugai, ṭhitauyaivāppadhāniyai|

kriyaṃ sādhaiti aitassa, dīpakaṃ sāsanai padaṃ||

karaṇavacanaṃyaiva, yaibhuyyaina padissati|

ākhyātai kāritaṭṭhānaṃ, sandhāya kathitaṃ idaṃ||

na  nāmai kāritaṭṭhānaṃ, ‘‘baudhaitā’’ itiādikaṃ|

‘‘sunakhaihipi khādāpainti’’, ic‍cādīni padāni ca|

āharitvāna dīpaiyya, payaugakusalau budhau||

tatridaṃ karaṇavacanaṃ kammatthadīpakaṃ, upayaugasāmivacanānipi taddīpakāni yaujaitabbāni| kathaṃ? paribhāvāpaitīti puggalau puggalaṃ sapattaṃ paribhāvāpaitīti, tathā paribhāvāpaitīti puggalau puggalassa sapattaṃ paribhāvāpaitīti| saisāni nayānusāraina niddisitabbāni| aivaṃ sabbānaitāni karaṇaupayaugasāmivacanāni kammatthadīpakāniyaiva haunti, tasmā dvikammakavasainatthau gahaitabbau|

ayaṃ haitukattukriyāpadānaṃ niddaisau|

kammakriyāpadaniddaisa

bhaviyatai vibhaviyatai paribhaviyatai abhibhaviyatai anubhaviyatai paribhūyatai abhibhūyatai anubhūyatai, aivaṃ kammunau kriyāpadāni bhavanti| añ‍ñathā ca bhaviyyatai vibhaviyyatai paribhaviyyatai abhibhaviyyatai anubhaviyyatai paribhuyyatai abhibhuyyatai anubhuyyataiti| aittha kammunau kriyāpadāniyaiva kammakattunau kriyāpadāni katvā yaujaitabbāni| visuñhi kammakattunau kriyāpadāni na labbhanti|

tatra bhaviyataiti bhāvaitabbaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci puggalaina bhāviyatai āsaiviyatai bahulīkariyatai, atha vā bhaviyataiti vaḍḍhiyatai| vibhaviyataiti vibhāvaitabbaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci puggalaina vibhaviyatai visaisaina bhaviyatai, vividhaina  vā ākāraina bhaviyatai vaḍḍhiyatai, atha vā vibhaviyataiti abhaviyatai antaradhāpiyatai| paribhaviyataiti sapattau puggalaina paribhaviyatai hiṃsiyatai, atha vā paribhaviyataiti hīḷiyatai avajāniyatai| abhibhaviyataiti sapattau puggalaina abhibhaviyatai ajjhautthariyatai abhimaddiyatai| anubhaviyataiti sampatti puggalaina anubhaviyatai paribhuñ‍jiyatai| paribhūyataitiādīni tīṇi ‘‘paribhaviyatai’’tiādīhi tīhi samānaniddaisāni| saisāni pana yathāvuttaihi yaṃ kammamaiva padhānatau gahaitvā niddisiyati padaṃ, taṃ kammatthadīpakaṃ| tasmā kattari aikavacanaina niddiṭṭhaipi yadi kammaṃ bahuvacanavasaina vattabbaṃ, bahuvacanantañ‍ñaiva kammunau kriyāpadaṃ dissati| yadi panaikavacanavasaina vattabbaṃ, aikavacanantañ‍ñaiva| tathā kattari bahuvacanaina niddiṭṭhaipi yadi kammaṃ aikavacanavasaina vattabbaṃ, aikavacanantañ‍ñaiva kammunau kriyāpadaṃ dissati| yadi pana bahuvacanavasaina vattabbaṃ, bahuvacanantañ‍ñaiva| kathaṃ? bhikkhunā dhammau bhaviyatai, bhikkhunā dhammā bhaviyantai, bhikkhūhi dhammau bhaviyatai, bhikkhūhi dhammā bhaviyantaiti| iminā nayaina sabbattha kammunau kriyāpadaisu vauhārau kātabbau| yasmiṃ pana kammunau kriyāpadai kammatthadīpakai kammabhūtassaivatthassa kattubhāvaparikappau hauti, taṃ kammakattutthadīpakaṃ, taṃ kammunau kriyāpadatau visuṃ na labbhati| ayaṃ panaittha atthaviñ‍ñāpanai payaugaracanā| sayamaiva paribhaviyatai dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ bālau tappac‍cayā añ‍ñaihi paribhūtaupi, sayamaiva abhibhaviyatai pāpakārī nirayai nirayapālaihi abhibhūtaupi tathārūpassa kammassa sayaṃ katattāti| aittha hi sayamaiva pīyatai pānīyaṃ, sayamaiva kaṭau kariyataitiādīsu viya  sukhābhisaṅkharaṇīyatā labbhataiva, tatau kammakattutā ca|

ayaṃ kammunau kriyāpadānaṃ niddaisau|

bhāvakriyāpadaniddaisa

bhūyatai bhaviyatai ubbhaviyatai, aivaṃ bhāvassa kriyāpadāni bhavanti| añ‍ñathā ca bhuyyatai bhaviyyatai ubbhaviyyataiti| tatra yathā ṭhīyataipadassa ṭhānanti bhāvavasaina atthakathanamicchanti, aivaṃ bhūyataitiādīnampi bhavanantiādinā bhāvavasaina atthakathanamicchitabbaṃ| yathā ca ṭhānaṃ ṭhiti bhavanantiādīhi bhāvavācakakitantanāmapadaihi saddhiṃ sambandhai chaṭṭhiyaujanamicchanti, na tathā ṭhīyatai bhūyataitiādīhi bhāvavācakākhyātapadaihi saddhiṃ sambandhai chaṭṭhiyaujanā icchitabbā sambandhai pavattachaṭṭhiyantasaddaihi asambandhanīyattā ākhyātikapadānaṃ| yasmiṃ payaugai yaṃ kammunau kriyāpadaina samānagatikaṃ katvā vinā kammaina niddisiyati kriyāpadaṃ, kattuvācakapadaṃ pana pac‍cattavacanaina vā karaṇavacanaina vā niddisiyati, taṃ tattha bhāvatthadīpakaṃ| na hi sabbathā kattāramanissāya bhāvau pavattati| aivaṃ santaipi bhāvau nāma kaivalau bhavanalavanapacanādikau dhātuatthauyaiva| akkharacintakā pana ‘‘ṭhīyatai bhūyatai’’tiādīsu bhāvavisayaisu karaṇavacanamaiva payuñ‍janti ‘‘nanu nāma pabbajitaina sunivatthaina bhavitabbaṃ supārutaina ākappasampan‍nainā’’tiādīsu viya, tasmā taisaṃ matai ‘‘taina ubbhaviyatai’’ti karaṇavacanaina yaujaitabbaṃ| jinamataina pana ‘‘sau bhūyatai’’tiādinā pac‍cattavacanainaiva| sac‍casaṅkhaipappakaraṇai hi dhammapālācariyaina, niddaisapāḷiyaṃ pana dhammasaināpatinā, dhajaggasuttantai bhagavatā ca bhāvapadaṃ pac‍cattavacanāpaikkhavasainu’c‍cāritaṃ|

kathitau  sac‍casaṅkhaipai, pac‍cattavacanaina vai,

‘‘bhūyatai’’ iti saddassa, sambandhau bhāvadīpanau||

niddaisapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘rūpaṃ, vibhauti vibhaviyyati’’|

iti dassanatau vāpi, pac‍cattavacanaṃ thiraṃ||

tathā dhajaggasuttantai, munināhac‍cabhāsitai|

‘‘sau pahīyissati’’ iti, pāḷidassanataupi ca||

pāramitānubhāvaina, mahaisīnaṃva daihatau|

santi nipphādanā, naiva, sak‍katādivacau viya||

pac‍cattadassanainaiva, purisattayayaujanaṃ|

aikavacanikañ‍cāpi, bahuvacanikampi ca|

kātabbamiti nau khanti, parassapadaādikai||

tasmā rūpaṃ vibhaviyyati, rūpāni vibhaviyyanti, tvaṃ vibhaviyyasi, tumhai vibhaviyyatha, ahaṃ vibhaviyyāmi, mayaṃ vibhaviyyāma, rūpaṃ vibhaviyyatai, rūpāni vibhaviyyantai ic‍caivamādi jinavacanānurūpatau yaujaitabbaṃ| atrāyaṃ padasaudhanā –

vibhaviyyatīti idaṃ, kammapadasamānakaṃ|

na ca kammapadaṃ nāpi, kammakattupadādikaṃ||

yadi kammapadaṃ aitaṃ, pac‍cattavacanaṃ pana|

kammaṃ dīpaiyya karaṇa-vacanaṃ kattudīpakaṃ||

yadi kammakattupadaṃ, ‘‘pīyatai’’ti padaṃ viya|

siyā sakammakaṃ, naitaṃ, tathā hautīti dīpayai||

yadi kattupadaṃ aitaṃ, vibhavatipadaṃ viya|

vinā yapac‍cayaṃ tiṭṭhai, na tathā tiṭṭhatai idaṃ||

na kattari bhuvādīnaṃ, gaṇai yapac‍cayau rutau|

divādīnaṃ gaṇaiyaiva, kattari samudīritau||

na  bhūdhātu divādīnaṃ, dhātūnaṃ dissatai gaṇai|

bhūvādikacurādīnaṃ, gaṇaisuyaiva dissati||

‘‘vibhaviyyati’’ ic‍cādau, tasmā yapac‍cayau pana|

bhāvaiyaivāti viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, viñ‍ñunā samayañ‍ñunā||

aittha hi pākaṭaṃ katvā, bhāvakārakalakkhaṇaṃ|

dassayissāmahaṃ dāni, sak‍kac‍caṃ mai nibaudhatha||

‘‘tissau gacchati’’ic‍catra, kattāraṃ kattunau padaṃ|

‘‘dhammau daisiyati’’c‍catra, kammaṃ tu kammunau padaṃ||

sarūpatau pakāsaiti, tasmā tai pākaṭā ubhau|

tathā vibhaviyyatīti-ādibhāvapadaṃ pana||

sarūpatau na dīpaiti, kārakaṃ bhāvanāmakaṃ|

dabbabhūtaṃ tu kattāraṃ, pakāsaiti sarūpatau||

kattāraṃ pana dīpaintaṃ, kattusan‍nissitampi taṃ|

bhāvaṃ dīpaiti svākārau, pac‍cayaina vibhāvitau||

yasmā ca kattubhāvaina, bhāvau nāma na tiṭṭhati|

kattāva kattubhāvaina, bhāvaṭṭhānai ṭhitau tatau||

yaj‍jaivaṃ kattuvauhārau, bhāvassa tu kathaṃ siyā|

‘‘sāvakānaṃ san‍nipātau, ahausi’’itiādisu||

iti cai nissayānaṃ tu, vasā nissitasambhavā|

kattuṭṭhānaipi bhāvassa, kattupañ‍ñatti sijjhati||

kārakai kattukammavhai, kriyāsan‍nissayai yathā|

dhāraintī āsanathālī, kriyādhārauti kappitā||

tathā bhāvapadaṃ dhīrā, kattāraṃ bhāvanissayaṃ|

dīpayantampi kappainti, bhāvassa vācakaṃ iti||

kaici  adabbabhūtassa, bhāvassaikattatau bravuṃ|

bhāvaidaikavacauvādi-purisassaiva hautiti||

pāḷiṃ patvāna taisaṃ tu, vacanaṃ appamāṇakaṃ|

‘‘tai saṃkilaisikā dhammā, pahīyissanti’’ iti hi||

pāṭhau pāvacanai diṭṭhau, tasmā aivaṃ vadaimasai|

pac‍cattadassanainaiva, purisattayayaujanaṃ||

vacanaihi yutaṃ dvīhi, icchitabbanti nau ruci|

bhāvai kriyāpadaṃ nāma, pāḷiyaṃ atiduddasaṃ|

tasmā taggahaṇūpāyau, vuttau aittāvatā mayāti||

ayaṃ bhāvassa kriyāpadānaṃ niddaisau|

aivaṃ suddhakattukriyāpadāni haitukattukriyāpadāni kammunau kriyāpadāni, bhāvassa kriyāpadāni cāti catudhā, kammakattukriyāpadaihi vā pañ‍cadhā bhūdhātutau nipphan‍nāni kriyāpadāni nānappakāraina niddiṭṭhāni, aitāni laukiyānaṃ bhāvabhaidavasaina vauhārabhaidau hautīti dassanatthaṃ visuṃ visuṃ vuttāni| atthatau pana kammakattubhāvakārakattayavasaina tividhānaiva| haitukattā hi suddhakattusaṅkhātai kārakai tassaṅgabhāvatau saṅgahamupagacchati, tathā kammakattā kammakārakai, bhāvau pana kaivalau| sau hi gamanapacanalavanādivasainānaikavidhaupi kriyāsabhāvattā bhaidarahitau kārakantarau| aivaṃ santaipi dabbasan‍nissitattā dabbabhaidaina bhij‍jati| taina pāvacanai bhāvavācakaṃ padaṃ bahuvacanantampi dissati| ākhyātikapadai bhāvakārakavauhārau niruttinayaṃ nissāya gatau, atthatau pana bhāvassa kārakatā nupapaj‍jati| sau hi na  kiñ‍ci janaiti, na ca kriyāya nimittaṃ| kriyānimittabhāvauyaiva hi kārakalakkhaṇaṃ| iti mukhyatau vā haitutau vā bhāvassa kārakatā na labbhati| aivaṃ santaipi sau karaṇamattattā kārakaṃ| tathā hi karaṇaṃ kārau, kriyā, tadaiva kārakanti bhāvassa kārakatā daṭṭhabbā| yasmā pana kriyānimittabhāvauyaiva kārakalakkhaṇaṃ, tasmā nāmikapadai kārakalakkhaṇai bhāvakārakanti vauhāraṃ pahāya kattukammakaraṇasampadānāpādānādhikaraṇānaṃ chan‍naṃ vatthūnaṃ kattukārakakammakārakantiādi vauhārau kariyati vaiyyākaraṇaihi| aivaṃ niruttinayaṃ nissāya vuttaṃ bhāvakārakañ‍ca dvai ca kammakattukārakānīti kārakattayaṃ bhavati| taddīpakañ‍cākhyātikapadaṃ tikārakaṃ|

imamatthañhi sandhāya, vuttamācariyaihipi|

mahāvaiyyākaraṇaihi, niruttinayadassibhi||

‘‘yaṃ tikālaṃ tipurisaṃ, kriyāvāci tikārakaṃ|

atiliṅgaṃ dvivacanaṃ, tadākhyātanti vuc‍catī’’ti||

idha bhāvakammaisu attanaupaduppattiṃ kaici akkharacintakā avassamicchantīti taisaṃ mativibhāvanatthamamhaihi bhāvakammānaṃ kriyāpadāni attanaupadavasainuddiṭṭhāni caiva niddiṭṭhāni ca| sabbānipi panaitāni tikārakāni kriyāpadāni kriyāpadamālamicchatā parassapadattanaupadavasaina yaujaitabbāni| pāḷiādīsu hi tikārakāni kriyāpadāni parassapadattanaupadavasaina dvidhā ṭhitāni| saiyyathidaṃ? bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| samādhijjhānakusalā, vandanti laukanāyakaṃ| maunaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ| atthābhisamayā dhīrau, paṇḍitauti pavuc‍cati| kathaṃ paṭipan‍nassa puggalassa rūpaṃ vibhauti vibhaviyyati| sau  pahīyissati| paṇḍukambalai nikkhittaṃ bhāsatai tapatai| pūjakau labhatai pūjaṃ| puttakāmā thiyau yācaṃ, labhantai tādisaṃ sutaṃ| asitau tādi vuc‍catai sabrahmā| aggijādi pubbaiva bhūyatai| sau pahīyaithāpi nau pahīyaithāti aivaṃ dvidhā ṭhitāni| atridaṃ pāḷivavatthānaṃ –

tikārakāni sabbāni, kriyāpadāni pāyatau|

parassapadayaugaina, dissanti piṭakattayai||

attanaupadayuttāni, cuṇṇiyaisu padaisu hi|

atīvappāni gāthāsu, padānīti bahūni tu||

gāthāsu caivitarāni, cuṇṇiyaisu padaisu ca|

subahūnaiva hutvāna, dissantīti pakāsayai||

padānaṃ niddaisau panati antiādīnaṃ taisaṃ taisaṃ vacanānamanurūpaina yaujaitabbau| aivaṃ tikārakakriyāpadāni sarūpatau vavatthānatau niddaisatau ca vaiditabbāni|

idāni naupasaggākammikādivasaina bhavatissa dhātussa vinicchayaṃ vadāma –

naupasaggā akammā ca, saupasaggā akammikā|

saupasaggā sakammā ca, iti bhūti vibhāvitā||

idaṃ tu vacanaṃ ‘‘dhamma-bhūtau bhutvā’’tiādisu|

pattānubhavanatthaṃ mai, vivaj‍jaitvā udīritaṃ||

aitaina pana atthaina, naupasaggasakammikaṃ|

gahaitvā catudhā hauti, iti ñaiyyaṃ visaisatau||

naupasaggā akammā ca, saupasaggā akammikā|

bhūdhātu kāritai santai, aikakammā bhavanti hi||

‘‘bhāvaiti  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, vibhāvaitī’’timānidha|

dassaitabbāni viñ‍ñūhi, sāsanañ‍ñūhi sāsanai||

saupasaggā sakammā tu, kāritappac‍cayai sati|

dvikammāyaiva hautīti, ñātabbaṃ viñ‍ñunā kathaṃ||

abhibhāvainti purisā, purisai pāṇajātikaṃ|

anubhāvaiti purisau, sampattiṃ purisaṃ iti||

idaṃ sakammakaṃ nāma, akammakamidaṃ iti|

kathamamhaihi ñātabbaṃ, vitthāraina vadaitha nau||

vitthārainaiva kiṃ vattuṃ, sak‍kaumi aikadaisatau|

kathayissāmi sak‍kac‍caṃ, vadatau mai nibaudhatha||

ākhyātikapadaṃ nāma, duvidhaṃ samudīritaṃ|

sakammakamakammañ‍ca, iti viñ‍ñū vibhāvayai||

tatra yassa payaugamhi, padassa kattunā kriyā|

nipphāditā vinā kammaṃ, na hauti taṃ sakammakaṃ||

‘‘pacatī’’ti hi vuttai tu, yaina kainaci jantunā|

audanaṃ vā panañ‍ñaṃ vā, kiñ‍ci vatthunti ñāyati||

yassa pana payaugamhi, kammaina rahitā kriyā|

padassa ñāyatai aitaṃ, akammakanti tīrayai||

‘‘tiṭṭhati daivadattau’’ti, vuttai kainaci jantunā|

ṭhānaṃva buddhivisayau, kammabhūtaṃ na kiñ‍cipi||

sakammakapadaṃ tattha, kattāraṃ kammamaiva ca|

pakāsaiti yathāyauga-miti viñ‍ñū vibhāvayai||

‘‘audanaṃ pacati pausau, audanau pac‍catai sayaṃ’’|

ic‍cudāharaṇā ñaiyyā, avuttaipi ayaṃ nayau||

akammakapadaṃ  nāma, kattāraṃ bhāvamaiva ca|

yathārahaṃ pakāsaiti, iti dhīraupalakkhayai||

kattāraṃ ‘‘tiṭṭhati’’c‍catra, sūcaiti bhāvanāmakaṃ|

‘‘upaṭṭhīyati’’ ic‍catra, avuttaipi ayaṃ nayau||

aivaṃ sakammakākammaṃ, ñatvā yaujaiyya buddhimā|

tikammakañ‍ca jānaiyya, karādau kāritai sati||

‘‘suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ pausau, kāraiti purisa’’nti ca|

‘‘purisau purisai gāmaṃ, rathaṃ vāhaiti’’ic‍capi||

aittha bhavatidhātumhi, nayau aisau na labbhati|

tasmā dvikammakañ‍ñaiva, padamaittha vibhāvitaṃ||

aidisau ca nayau nāma, pāḷiyaṃ tu na dissati|

aikac‍cānaṃ matainaiva, mayā aivaṃ pakāsitau||

aittha ca ‘‘tamainaṃ rājā, vividhā kammakāraṇā|

kārāpaitī’’ti yau pāṭhau, niddaisai taṃ suniddisai||

‘‘manussaihī’’ti āharitvā, pāṭhasaisaṃ sumaidhasau|

‘‘sunakhaihipi khādāpainti’’, iti pāṭhassa dassanā||

aitaṃ nayaṃ vidū ñatvā, yaujai pāṭhānurūpatau|

‘‘suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ pausau, kāraiti purisaini’’ti||

vikaraṇappac‍cayāva, vuttā aittha sarūpatau|

sagaṇai sagaṇai taisaṃ, vuttiṃ dīpaitumaiva ca||

‘‘asmiṃ gaṇai ayaṃ dhātu, hautī’’ti taihi viñ‍ñunau|

viñ‍ñāpaituñ‍ca añ‍ñaihi, ñāpanā pac‍cayaihi na||

tathā hi bhāvakammaisu, vihitau pac‍cayau tu yau|

aṭṭhavidhaipi dhātūnaṃ, gaṇasmiṃ sampavattati||

bhūdhātujaisu  rūpaisu, asammauhāya sautunaṃ|

nānāvidhau nayau aivaṃ, mayā aittha pakāsitau||

yai laukai appayuttā vividhavikaraṇākhyātasaddaisvachaikā,

tai patvākhyātasaddai avigatavimatī haunti ñāṇīpi tasmā|

ac‍cantañ‍ñaiva dhīrau saparahitaratau sāsanai daḷhapaimau,

yaugaṃ taisaṃ payaugai paṭutaramatitaṃ patthayānau karaiyya||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu

viñ‍ñūnaṃ kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

savikaraṇākhyātavibhāgau nāma

paṭhamau paricchaidau|

2. bhavatikriyāpadamālāvibhāga

itau paraṃ pavakkhāmi, sautūnaṃ mativaḍḍhanaṃ|

kriyāpadak‍kamaṃ nāma, vibhattādīni dīpayaṃ||

tatra ākhyātikassa kriyālakkhaṇattasūcikā tyādayau vibhattiyau, tā aṭṭhavidhā vattamānāpañ‍camīsattamīparaukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanībhavissantī kālātipattivasaina|

tatthati anti, si tha, mi ma, tai antai, sai vhai, ai mhai ic‍caitā vattamānāvibhattiyau nāma|

tu antu, hi tha, mi ma, taṃ antaṃ, su vhau, ai āmasai ic‍caitā pañ‍camīvibhattiyau nāma|

aiyya  aiyyuṃ, aiyyāsi aiyyātha, aiyyāmi aiyyāma, aitha airaṃ, aithau aiyyāvhau, aiyyaṃ aiyyāmhai ic‍caitā sattamīvibhattiyau nāma|

a u, ai ttha, aṃ mha, ttha rai, tthau vhau, iṃ mhai ic‍caitā paraukkhāvibhattiyau nāma|

ā ū, au ttha, aṃ mhā, ttha tthuṃ, sai vhaṃ, iṃ mhasai ic‍caitā hiyyattanīvibhattiyau nāma|

ī uṃ, au ttha, iṃ mhā, ā ū, sai vhaṃ, aṃ mhai ic‍caitā aj‍jatanīvibhattiyau nāma|

ssati ssanti, ssasi ssatha, ssāmi ssāma, ssatai ssantai, ssasai ssavhai, ssaṃ ssāmhai ic‍caitā bhavissantīvibhattiyau nāma|

ssā ssaṃsu, ssai ssatha, ssaṃ ssāmhā, ssatha ssisu, ssasai ssavhai, ssiṃ ssāmhasai ic‍caitā kālātipattivibhattiyau nāma|

sabbāsamaitāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ yāni yāni pubbakāni cha padāni, tāni tāni parassapadāni nāma| yāni yāni pana parāni cha padāni, tāni tāni attanaupadāni nāma| tattha parassapadāni vattamānā cha, pañ‍camiyau cha, sattamiyau cha, paraukkhā cha, hiyyattaniyau cha, aj‍jataniyau cha, bhavissantiyau cha, kālātipattiyau chāti aṭṭhacattālīsavidhāni haunti, tathā itarāni, sabbāni tāni piṇḍitāni chan‍navutividhāni|

parassapadānamattanaupadānañ‍ca dvai dvai padāni paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisā nāma| tai vattamānādīsu cattārau cattārau, aṭṭhan‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ vasaina dvattiṃsa, piṇḍitāni parimāṇānaiva|

dvīsu  dvīsu padaisu paṭhamaṃ paṭhamaṃ aikavacanaṃ, dutiyaṃ dutiyaṃ bahuvacanaṃ|

tatra vattamānavibhattīnanti anti, si tha, mi ma ic‍caitāni parassapadāni| tai antai, sai vhai, ai mhai ic‍caitāni attanaupadāni| parassapadattanaupadaisupiti anti iti paṭhamapurisā, sitha iti majjhimapurisā, mi ma iti uttamapurisā, tai antai iti paṭhamapurisā, sai vhai iti majjhimapurisā, ai mhai iti uttamapurisā|

paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisaisupi ti-iti aikavacanaṃ, anti-iti bahuvacananti aivaṃ aikavacanabahuvacanāni kamatau ñaiyyāni| aivaṃ saisāsu vibhattīsu parassapadattanaupadapaṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisaikavacanabahuvacanāni ñaiyyāni|

tattha vibhattīti kainaṭṭhaina vibhatti? kālādivasaina dhātvatthaṃ vibhajatīti vibhatti, syādīhi nāmikavibhattīhi saha sabbasaṅgāhakavasaina pana sakatthaparatthādibhaidai atthai vibhajatīti vibhatti, kammādayau vā kārakai aikavacanabahuvacanavasaina vibhajatīti vibhatti, vibhajitabbā ñāṇainātipi vibhatti, vibhajanti atthai aitāyātipi vibhatti, atha vā satipi jinasāsanai avibhattikaniddaisai sabbaina sabbaṃ vibhattīhi vinā atthassā’niddisitabbatau visaisaina vividhaina vā ākāraina bhajanti saivanti naṃ paṇḍitātipi vibhatti| tattha avibhattikaniddaisalakkhaṇaṃ vadāma saha payauganidassanādīhi|

avibhattikaniddaisau, nāmikaisupalabbhati|

nākhyātaisūti viñ‍ñaiyya-midamaittha nidassanaṃ||

nigraudhauva mahārukkhau, thaira vādānamuttamau|

anūnaṃ anadhikañ‍ca, kaivalaṃ jinasāsanaṃ||

tatra  thaira-iti avibhattikau niddaisau, thairānaṃ ayanti thairau| kau sau? vādau| thairavādau añ‍ñaisaṃ vādānaṃ uttamauti ayamatthau vaiditabbau|

‘‘kāyau tai sabba sauvaṇṇau’’, ic‍cādimhipi nāmikai|

avibhattikaniddaisau, gahaitabbau nayañ‍ñunā||

avibhattikaniddaisau, nanvākhyātaipi dissati|

‘‘bhau khāda piva’’ic‍catra, vadai yau kauci caudakau||

yadi aivaṃ matainassa, bhavaiyya avibhattikaṃ|

‘‘bhikkhu, bhau purisi’’c‍cādi, padampi, na hidaṃ tathā||

‘‘bhikkhu, bhau purisi’’c‍cādi, si ga laupaina vuc‍cati|

tathā ‘‘khādā’’tiādīni, hi laupaina pavuc‍carai||

aivaṃ avibhattikaniddaisau ākhyātaisu na labbhati, nāmaisuyaiva labbhati| tatrāpi ‘‘aṭṭha ca puggala dhammadasā tai’’ti aittha chandavasaina puggala iti rassakaraṇaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, na ‘‘kakusandha kauṇāgamanau ca kassapau’’ti aittha kakusandha iti avibhattikaniddaisau viya avibhattikaniddaisau daṭṭhabbau| ‘‘bhikkhu nisin‍nai mātugāmau upanisin‍nau vā hauti upanipan‍nau vā’’ti aittha pana bhikkhūti idaṃ bhikkhumhīti vattabbatthattā bhummai pac‍cattantipi adiṭṭhavibhattikaniddaisautipi vattuṃ yuj‍jati| tattha pana chandavasaina katarassattā tāni padāni avibhattikaniddaisapakkhampi bhajantīti vattuṃ na yuj‍jati|

tattha parassapadānīti parassa atthabhūtāni padāni parassapadāni| aitthuttamapurisaisu attanau atthaisupi attanaupadavauhārau na kariyati|

kiñ‍cāpi  attanau atthā, purisā uttamavhayā|

tathāpi itaraisāna-mussan‍nattāva tabbasā|

tabbauhārau imaisānaṃ, paurāṇaihi niraupitau||

attanaupadānīti attanau atthabhūtāni padāni attanaupadāni| aittha pana paṭhamamajjhimapurisaisu parassatthaisupi parassapadavauhārau na kariyati|

paṭhamamajjhimā caitai, parassatthā tathāpi ca|

itaraisaṃ nirūḷhattā, tabbauhārassa sac‍catau||

imassa panimaisānaṃ, pubbavauhāratāya ca|

tathā saṅkaradausassa, haraṇatthāya sau ayaṃ|

attanaupadavauhārau, aisamāraupitau dhuvaṃ||

parassapadasañ‍ñādi-sañ‍ñāyau bahukā idha|

paurāṇaihi katattātā, sañ‍ñā paurāṇikā matā||

tasmā idha paṭhamapurisādīnaṃ tiṇṇaṃ purisānaṃ vacanatthaṃ na pariyaisāma| rūḷhiyā hi paurāṇaihi tyādīnaṃ purisasañ‍ñā vihitā|

aikavacanabahuvacanaisu pana aikassatthassa vacanaṃ aikavacanaṃ| bahūnamatthānaṃ vacanaṃ bahuvacanaṃ| atha vā bahuttaipi sati samudāyavasaina jātivasaina vā cittaina sampiṇḍaitvā aikīkatassatthassa aikassa viya vacanampi aikavacanaṃ, bahuttai nissitassa nissayavauhāraina vuttassa nissayavasaina aikassa viya vacanampi aikavacanaṃ, aikattalakkhaṇaina bavhatthānaṃ aikavacanaṃ viya vacanampi aikavacanaṃ| abahuttaipi sati attagarukārāparicchaidamātikānusandhinayapucchāsabhāgaputhucitta- samāyaugaputhuārammaṇavasaina aikatthassa bahūnaṃ viya vacanaṃ bahuvacanaṃ, tathā yai yai bahavau tan‍nivāsataṃputtasaṅkhātassaikassatthassa rūḷhīvasaina bahūnaṃ viya vacanampi bahuvacanaṃ, aikassatthassa añ‍ñainatthaina aikābhidhānavasaina bahūnaṃ  viya vacanampi bahuvacanaṃ, aikassatthassa nissitavasaina bahūnaṃ viya vacanampi bahuvacanaṃ, aikassatthassa ārammaṇabhaidakic‍cabhaidavasaina bahūnaṃ viya vacanampi bahuvacanaṃ| aivamimaihi ākāraihi aikamhi vattabbai, aikamhi viya ca vattabbai aikavacanaṃ, bahumhi vattabbai, bahumhi viya ca vattabbai bahuvacanaṃ hautīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| puthuvacanaṃ, anaikavacananti ca imassaiva nāmaṃ|

vacanaisu ayaṃ atthau, nāmākhyātavibhattinaṃ|

vasaina adhigantabbau, sāsanatthagavaisinā||

tasmā tadatthaviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ idha nāmikapayaugaihi sahaivākhyātapayaugai pavakkhāma – ‘‘rājā āgacchati, sahāyau mai āgacchati, aikaṃ citta’’ mic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa aikavacanapayaugā| ‘‘rājānau āgacchanti, sahāyā mai āgacchanti, na mai daissā ubhau puttā, dvai tīṇi’’ ic‍caivamādayau bavhatthānaṃ bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘sā sainā mahatī āsi, bahuj‍janau pasan‍nausi, sabbau taṃ janau aujināyatu, itthigumbassa pavarā, buddhassāhaṃ vatthayugaṃ adāsiṃ, dvayaṃ vau bhikkhavai daisaissāmi, paimaṃ mahantaṃ ratanattayassa, karai pasādañ‍ca narau avassaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghau, balakāyau, daivanikāyau, ariyagaṇau’’- ic‍caivamādayau, ‘‘dvikaṃ tika’’mic‍cādayau ca samudāyavasaina bavhatthānaṃ aikavacanapayaugā|

katthaci pana īdisaisu ṭhānaisu bahuvacanapayaugāpi dissanti| tathā hi ‘‘pūjitā ñātisaṅghaihi, daivakāyā samāgatā, sabbaitai daivanikāyā, dvai daivasaṅghā, tīṇi  dukāni, cattāri navakāni’’ ic‍caivamādayau payaugāpi dissanti| imai aikavacanavasaina vattabbassa samudāyassa bahusamudāyavasaina bahuvacanapayaugāti gahaitabbā, saṅgayhamānā ca bavhatthabahuvacanai saṅgahaṃ gacchanti visuṃyaiva vā, tasmā bahusamudāyāpaikkhabahuvacananti aitaisaṃ nāmaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

‘‘pāṇaṃ na hanai, sassau sampaj‍jati’’ ic‍caivamādayau jātivasaina bavhatthānaṃ aikavacanapayaugā, tabbhāvasāmañ‍ñaina bavhatthānaṃ aikavacanapayaugātipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati|

‘‘nāgaṃ raṭṭhassa pūjitaṃ, sāvatthī saddhā ahausi pasan‍nā’’ ic‍caivamādayau nissayavasaina pavattānaṃ nissayavauhāraina vuttānamaikavacanapayaugā|

‘‘tilakkhaṇaṃ, kusalākusalaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, dhammavinayau, cittasainau ca gandhabbau, natiyā asati āgatigati na hauti, āgatigatiyā asati cutūpapātau na hauti’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikattalakkhaṇai bavhatthānaṃ aikavacanapayaugā|

‘‘aivaṃ mayaṃ gaṇhāma, amhākaṃ pakati, padhānanti khau maighiya vadamānaṃ kinti vadaiyyāma’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa attavasaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘tai manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ ‘bhuñ‍jatha bhantai’ti, ahaṃ manussaisu manussabhūtā, abbhāgatānā’sanakaṃ adāsiṃ’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa garukāravasaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘appac‍cayā  dhammā, asaṅkhatā dhammā’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa aparicchaidavasaina bahuvacanapayaugā, aniyamitasaṅkhāvasaina bahuvacanapayaugā vā|

kaici pana ‘‘daisanāsautapātavasaina bahuvacanapayaugā’’tipi vadanti, taṃ na gahaitabbaṃ| na hi tathāgatau satisampajañ‍ñarahitau dhammaṃ daisaiti, yutti ca na dissati ‘‘mātikāyaṃ pucchāyaṃ vissaj‍janai cāti tīsupi ṭhānaisu appac‍cayādidhammai daisaintau satthā punappunaṃ bahuvacanavasaina daisanāsautai patitvā dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti|

‘‘katamai dhammā appac‍cayā’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa mātikānusandhinayaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘imai dhammā appac‍cayā’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa pucchānusandhinayaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘katamai dhammā nau parāmāsā, tai dhammai ṭhapaitvā avasaisā kusalākusalābyākatā dhammā’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa pucchāsabhāgaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘atthi bhikkhavai añ‍ñaiva dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaitī’’ti ayamaikassatthassa puthucittasamāyaugaputhuārammaṇavasaina bahuvacanapayaugau|

‘‘aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ mahāvanai,

santi  puttā vidaihānaṃ, dīghāvu raṭṭhavaḍḍhanau|

tai raj‍jaṃ kārayissanti, mithilāyaṃ pajāpati’’ –

ic‍caivamādayau saddā yai yai bahavau, tan‍nivāsataṃputtasaṅkhātassaikatthassa rūḷhīvasaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘sāriputtamauggal‍lānai āmantaisi ‘gacchatha tumhai sāriputtā kīṭāgiriṃ gantvā assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ karautha, tumhākaṃ aitai saddhivihārinau’ti’’, ‘‘kac‍ci vau kulaputtā, aitha byagghā nivattavhau’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa añ‍ñainatthaina aikābhidhānavasaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘mañ‍cā uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ karaunti’’ ic‍caivamādayau aikassatthassa nissitavasaina bahuvacanapayaugā|

‘‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti ayamārammaṇabhaidaina aikassatthassa bahuvacanapayaugau|

‘‘cattārau sammappadhānā’’ti ayaṃ pana kic‍cabhaidaina aikassatthassa bahuvacanapayaugau|

tattha aikatthaikavacanaṃ, samudāyāpaikkhaikavacanaṃ, jātyāpaikkhaikavacanaṃ, tan‍nissayāpaikkhaikavacanaṃ, aikattalakkhaṇaikavacananti pañ‍cavidhaṃ aikavacanaṃ bhavati| aittha pana jātyāpaikkhaikavacanaṃ atthatau sāmañ‍ñāpaikkhaikavacanamaivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

bavhatthabahuvacanaṃ, bahusamudāyāpaikkhabahuvacanaṃ, attabahuvacanaṃ, garukārabahuvacanaṃ, aparicchaidabahuvacanaṃ, mātikānusandhinayabahuvacanaṃ, pucchānusandhinayabahuvacanaṃ, pucchāsabhāgabahuvacanaṃ, puthucittasamāyaugaputhuārammaṇabahuvacanaṃ, tan‍nivāsabahuvacanaṃ, taṃputtabahuvacanaṃ, aikābhidhānabahuvacanaṃ, tan‍nissitāpaikkhabahuvacanaṃ, ārammaṇabhaidabahuvacanaṃ, kic‍cabhaidabahuvacananti pan‍narasavidhaṃ bahuvacanaṃ bhavati| ic‍caivaṃ vīsadhā sabbāni aikavacanabahuvacanāni saṅgahitāni| atridaṃ pāḷivavatthānaṃ –

aikatthai daikavacana-ñ‍citarasmitarampi ca|

samudāyajātiaikatta-lakkhaṇaikavacaupi ca|

sāṭṭhakathai piṭakamhi, pāṭhai pāyaina dissarai||

garumhi cattanaikasmiṃ, bahuvacanakaṃ pana|

pāḷiyaṃ appakaṃ aṭṭha-kathāṭīkāsu taṃ bahuṃ||

tathā hi bahukaṃ daika-vacanaṃyaiva pāḷiyaṃ|

garumhi cattanaikasmiṃ, idamaittha nidassanaṃ||

‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

tava sāsanamāgamma, pattaumhi amataṃ padaṃ’’||

ic‍caivamādayau pāṭhā, bahudhā jinasāsanai|

dissantīti vijānaiyya, vidvā akkharacintakau||

sātisayaṃ garukārā-rahassāpi mahaisinau|

aikavacanayaugaina, niddaisau dissatai yatau||

tatau vauhārakusalau, karaiyyatthānurūpatau|

aikavacanayaugaṃ vā, itaraṃ vā sumaidhasau||

pāyaina tan‍nivāsamhi, bahuvacanakaṃ ṭhitaṃ|

taṃputtai appakaṃ tan‍ni-ssayaikavacanampi ca||

puthucittāparicchaida-mātikāsandhiādisu|

bahuvacanakañ‍cāpi, appakanti pakāsayai||

aikābhidhānatau kic‍cā, tathā gaucarataupi ca|

bahuvacanakaṃ tan‍ni-ssitāpaikkhañ‍ca appakaṃ||

ic‍caivaṃ  sappayaugaṃ tu, ñatvāna vacanadvayaṃ|

kātabbau pana vauhārau, yathāpāḷi vibhāvinā||

idāni kālādivasaina ākhyātappavattiṃ dīpayissāma – kālakārakapurisaparidīpakaṃ kriyālakkhaṇaṃ ākhyātikaṃ| tatra kālanti atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍navasaina tayau kālā, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nāṇattiparikappakālātipattivasaina pana cha, tai aikaikā tipurisakā|

vuttappakārakālaisu, yadidaṃ vattatai yatau|

ākhyātikaṃ tatau tassa, kāladīpanatā matā||

kārakanti kammakattubhāvā| tai hi upacāramukhyasabhāvavasaina karaunti karaṇanti ca ‘‘kārakā’’ti vuc‍canti| taiva yathāk‍kamaṃ kriyānimitta taṃsādhaka taṃsabhāvāti vaiditabbā|

kammaṃ kattā ca bhāvau ca, ic‍caivaṃ kārakā tidhā|

vibhattipac‍cayā aittha, vuttā nāñ‍ñatra sac‍catau||

‘‘paribhaviyyati’’c‍cādī, kammai sijjhanti kārakai|

‘‘sambhavatī’’tiādīni, sijjharai kattukārakai||

‘‘vibhaviyyati’’ic‍cādī, bhāvai sijjhanti kārakai|

tividhaisvaivamaitaisu, vibhattipac‍cayā matā||

kārakattayamuttaṃ yaṃ, ākhyātaṃ natthi sabbasau|

tasmā taddīpanattampi, tassākhyātassa bhāsitaṃ||

kārakattaṃ tu bhāvassa, sacaipi na samīritaṃ|

kārakalakkhaṇai taina, bhāvaina ca avatthunā||

kriyānipphatti  natthīti, yuttitaupi ca natthi taṃ|

tathāpākhyātikai tassa, tabbauhārau niruttiyaṃ|

patiṭṭhitanayauvāti, mantvā amhaihi bhāsitau||

purisauti aikavacanabahuvacanakā paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisā| tattha paṭhamapurisau ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇai sādhakavācakai vā kammavācakai vā tumhā’mhasaddavaj‍jitai pac‍cattavacanabhūtai nāmamhi ‘‘abhinīhārau samijjhati, baudhi vuc‍cati catūsu maggaisuñāṇa’’ntiādīsu viya payuj‍jamānaipi, taṭṭhāniyattai sati ‘‘bhāsati vā karauti vā, pīḷiyakkhauti maṃ vidū, vuc‍catīti vacana’’ntiādīsu viya appayuj‍jamānaipi sabbadhātūhi parau hauti| katthaci pana pāḷippadaisai nāmassa appayuttattā paṭhamapurisapayaugatthau duranubaudhau bhavati, yathā ‘‘dukkhaṃ tai vaidayissāmi, tattha assāsayantu ma’’nti| tathā hi aittha ‘‘pādā’’ti pāṭhasaisau, tasmiṃ dukkhasāsanāraucanai vattuṃ avisahanavasaina kilamantaṃ maṃ daivassa ubhau pādā assāsaintu, vissaṭṭhau kathaihīti maṃ vadathāti adhippāyau ca bhavati|

adhippāyau sudubbaudhau, yasmā vij‍jati pāḷiyaṃ|

tasmā upaṭṭhahaṃ gaṇhai, garuṃ garumataṃ vidū||

tatrimāni bhūdhātādhikārattā bhūdhātuvasaina nidassanapadāni| sau paribhavati, tai paribhavanti, paribhavati, paribhavanti| sapattau abhibhaviyatai, sabbā vityā’nubhūyatai, abhibhaviyatai, anubhūyataiti| yattha satipi nāmassa sādhakavācakattai apac‍cattavacanattā ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇatā na labbhati, tattha kammavācakaṃ pac‍cattavacanabhūtaṃ tulyādhikaraṇapadaṃ paṭic‍ca paṭhamapurisādayau tayau labbhanti| taṃ  yathā? paribhaviyyatai purisau daivadattaina, paribhaviyyasai tvaṃ daivadattaina, paribhaviyyamhai mayaṃ akusalaihi dhammaihi| aittha panidaṃ vacanaṃ na vattabbaṃ ‘‘nindanti tuṇhimāsina’ntiādīsu satipi nāmassa kammavācakattai apac‍cattavacanattā ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇatā na labbhatīti paṭhamapurisuppatti na siyā’’ti| kasmāti cai? ‘‘nindanti tuṇhimāsina’’ntiādīsu ‘‘janā’’ti ajjhāharitabbassa sādhakavācakassa nāmassa saddhimākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇabhāvassa icchitattā| aivamuttaratrāpi nayau|

majjhimapurisau ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇai sādhakavācakai vā kammavācakai vā pac‍cattavacanabhūtai tumhasaddai payuj‍jamānaipi, taṭṭhāniyattai sati appayuj‍jamānaipi sabbadhātūhi parau hauti| tvaṃ atibhavasi, tumhai atibhavatha, atibhavasi, atibhavatha| tvaṃ paribhaviyasai daivadattaina, tumhai paribhaviyavhai| yattha satipi tumhasaddassa sādhakavācakattai apac‍cattavacanattā ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇatā na labbhati, na tattha majjhimapurisau hauti| itarai pana dvai haunti kammavācakaṃ pac‍cattavacanabhūtaṃ tulyādhikaraṇapadaṃ paṭic‍ca| taṃ yathā? tayā abhibhaviyatai sapattau, tayā abhibhaviyai ahaṃ|

uttamapurisau ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇai sādhakavācakai vā kammavācakai vā pac‍cattavacanabhūtai amhasaddai payuj‍jamānaipi, taṭṭhāniyattai sati appayuj‍jamānaipi sabbadhātūhi parau hauti| ahaṃ paribhavāmi, mayaṃ paribhavāma, paribhavāmi, paribhavāma| ahaṃ paribhaviyyāmi akusalaihi dhammaihi, mayaṃ paribhaviyyāma, paribhaviyyāmi, paribhaviyyāma| yattha satipi amhasaddassa sādhakavācakattai apac‍cattavacanattā ākhyātapadaina tulyādhikaraṇatā na labbhati, na tattha  uttamapurisau hauti| itarai pana dvai haunti kammavācakaṃ pac‍cattavacanabhūtaṃ tulyādhikaraṇapadaṃ paṭic‍ca| taṃ yathā? mayā anubhaviyatai sampatti, mayā abhibhaviyasai tvaṃ| aivaṃ yattha yattha sādhakavācakānaṃ vā kammavācakānaṃ vā nāmādīnaṃ pac‍cattavacanabhūtānaṃ ākhyātapadaihi tulyādhikaraṇattai laddhai tattha tattha paṭhamapurisādayau labbhanti, tasmā nāmādīnaṃ pac‍cattavacanabhūtānaṃ tulyādhikaraṇabhāvauyaiva paṭhamapurisādīnamuppattiyā kāraṇaṃ|

dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ vā purisānamaikābhidhānai parau purisau gahaitabbau| aittha aikābhidhānaṃ nāma aikatau abhidhānaṃ aikakālābhidhānañ‍ca| tañ‍ca khau casaddapayaugaiyaiva, acasaddapayaugai bhin‍nakālābhidhānai taggahaṇābhāvatau| ‘‘tumhai atthakusalā bhavatha, mayamatthakusalā bhavāma’’ ic‍caivamādayau tappayaugā| tattha tumhai atthakusalā bhavatha – ic‍caitasmiṃ vauhārai ‘‘sau ca atthakusalau bhavati, tvañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavasi, tumhai atthakusalā bhavathā’’ti aivaṃ dvin‍namaikābhidhānai parau purisau gahaitabbau| ‘‘mayamatthakusalā bhavāma’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ pana ‘‘sau ca atthakusalau bhavati, ahañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavāmi, mayamatthakusalā bhavāmā’’ti vā ‘‘tvañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavasi, ahañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavāmi, mayamatthakusalā bhavāmā’’ti vā aivampi dvin‍namaikābhidhānai parau purisau gahaitabbau| ‘‘sau ca atthakusalau bhavati, tvañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavasi, ahañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavāmi, mayamatthakusalā bhavāmā’’ti vā ‘‘sau ca atthakusalau bhavati, tai ca atthakusalā bhavanti, tvañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavasi, tumhai ca atthakusalā bhavatha, ahañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavāmi, mayamatthakusalā bhavāmā’’ti vā aivaṃ tiṇṇamaikābhidhānai parau purisau gahaitabbau|

aparaupi  atthanayau vuc‍cati – ‘‘tvañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavasi, sau ca atthakusalau bhavati, tumhai atthakusalā bhavathā’’ti vā ‘‘ahañ‍ca atthakusalau bhavāmi, sau ca atthakusalau bhavati, mayamatthakusalā bhavāmā’’ti vā iminā nayaina anaikappabhaidau atthanayau| aivaṃ saisāsu vibhattīsu pañ‍camīsattamiyādīsu paraupurisau gahaitabbau| sabbaisu ca kriyāpadaisu bavhatthavācakaisu bahuvacanantaisu, na pana bahuvacanantaisupi aikassattanau vācakaisu garukātabbassaikassatthassa vācakaisu ca kriyāpadaisu| aittha caudanāsandīpaniyau imā gāthā –

‘‘tvañ‍ca bhavasi sau cāpi, bhavati’’c‍cādibhāsanai|

‘‘tumhai bhavatha’’ ic‍cādi, paraupausau kathaṃ siyā?|

‘‘ahaṃ bhavāmi sau cāpi, bhavati’’c‍cādibhāsanai|

‘‘mayaṃ bhavāma’’ic‍cādi, uttamau ca kathaṃ siyā?|

aittha ca vuc‍catai –

pacchā vuttau parau nāma, sañ‍ñāya paṭipāṭiyā|

aivaṃ pana gahaitabbau, paraupurisanāmakau||

paṭhamamhā parau nāma, majjhimau uttamaupi ca|

majjhimamhā parau nāma, uttamau purisau rutau||

aivaṃ tu gahaṇañhaittha, vauhārassānulaumakaṃ|

dausau tadanulaumamhi, gahaṇasmiṃ na vij‍jati||

‘‘tvañ‍ca bhaddai sukhī hauhi, aisau cāpi mahāmigau’’|

iti pāṭhau yatau diṭṭhau, tasmā aivaṃ vadaimasai||

‘‘tumhai dvai sukhitā hautha’’, ic‍catthau tattha dissati|

aivaṃpyayaṃ nayau vuttau, attanaumatiyā mama||

attanaumati  kiñ‍cāpi, kathitā sabbadubbalā|

tathāpi nayamādāya, kathitattā akaupiyā||

‘‘dhammaina raj‍jaṃ kāraintaṃ, raṭṭhā pabbājayittha maṃ|

tvañ‍ca jānapadā caiva, naigamā ca samāgatā’’||

‘‘ahañ‍ca maddidaivī ca, jālīkaṇhājinā cubhau|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saukanudā, vasāma assamai tadā’’||

aitā gāthāpi aitassa, atthassa pana sādhikā|

tāsu vuttanayainaiva, atthau supākaṭau siyā|

aivaṃ viñ‍ñūhi viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, bahunā bhāsitaina kiṃ||

ākāraina manāpaina, kathanai yaina kainaci|

na virujjhati cai atthau, taṃ pamāṇaṃ sudhīmataṃ||

purisattayatau aisau, paraupurisanāmakau|

nupalabbhati pac‍caikaṃ, tadantaugadhatauva yaṃ||

pāṭavatthāya sautūnaṃ, vauhāratthaisu sabbasau|

visuṃ alabbhamānaupi, labbhamānauva uddhaṭau||

saṅkhaipataupaittha purisappavatti aivaṃ upalakkhitabbā ‘‘amhavacanatthai uttamau, tumhavacanatthai majjhimau, añ‍ñaisaṃ vacanatthai paṭhamau’’ti|

tyādīnaṃ purisasañ‍ñā, yasmā vuttā tatau idaṃ|

tabbantākhyātikaṃ ñaiyyaṃ, purisaparidīpakaṃ||

aivaṃ sabbathāpi ākhyātikassa kālakārakapurisaparidīpanatā vuttā|

kriyālakkhaṇanti aittha kathaṃ ākhyātikassa kriyālakkhaṇatā vaiditabbā?

lakkhiyati  kriyāyaitaṃ, kriyā vā assa lakkhaṇaṃ|

kriyālakkhaṇatā aivaṃ, vaiditabbā tathā hi ca||

‘‘gacchati’’c‍cādikaṃ sutvā, kriyāsandīpanaṃ padaṃ|

‘‘ākhyātika’’nti dhīraihi, ākhyātañ‍ñūhi sañ‍ñitaṃ||

lakkhaṇaṃ hauti nāmassa, yathā satvābhidhānatā|

kriyābhidhānatā aivaṃ, ākhyātassaiva lakkhaṇaṃ||

atthatau pana aitassa, kriyāvācakatā idha|

lakkhaṇaṃ iti viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, lakkhaṇañ‍ñūhi lakkhitaṃ||

‘‘kiṃ karausī’’ti puṭṭhassa, ‘‘pacāmi’’c‍cādinā ‘‘ahaṃ’’|

paṭivācāya dānaina, kriyāvācakatā matā||

aivamākhyātikassa kriyālakkhaṇatā vaiditabbā|

idāni kālaisu vibhattippavatti aivaṃ vaiditabbā –

pac‍cuppan‍namhi kālasmiṃ, vattamānā pavattati|

āsiṭṭhāṇāpanatthaisu, pac‍cuppan‍namhi pañ‍camī||

pac‍cuppan‍nai parikappā-numatyatthaisu sattamī|

appac‍cakkhai atītamhi, paraukkhā sampavattati||

hiyyau pabhuti kālasmiṃ, atītamhi pavattati|

pac‍cakkhai vā apac‍cakkhai, hiyyattanī niruttitā||

aj‍jappabhuti kālasmiṃ, atītamhi pavattati|

pac‍cakkhai vā apac‍cakkhai, samīpaij‍jatanavhayā||

anāgatai bhavissantī, kālasmiṃ sampavattati|

kriyātipan‍namattamhi, tītai kālātipattikā|

anāgataipi hautīti, niruttañ‍ñūhi bhāsitā||

aivaṃ kālaisu vibhattippavattiṃ ñatvā yai tai suttantaisu vicittā suvisadavipulatikhiṇabuddhivisayabhūtā payaugā dissanti, taisu pāṭavamicchantaihi tyādik‍kamaina vuc‍camānā kriyāpadamālā  sal‍lakkhitabbā – bhavati, bhavanti| bhavasi, bhavatha| bhavāmi, bhavāma| bhavatai, bhavantai| bhavasai, bhavavhai| bhavai, bhavāmhai| ayaṃ añ‍ñayaugādirahitā kriyāpadamālā|

dissanti ca suttantaisu atthasambhavaipi añ‍ñayaugādirahitāni kriyāpadāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati| yaṃ maṃ bhaṇasi sārathi| añ‍ñaṃ saipaṇṇi gacchāmi’’ ic‍caivamādīni aitassatthassa paridīpaniyā kriyāpadamālā|

aittha tividhau kriyāpadaisu yaugau tayaugau, mayaugau, añ‍ñayaugau ca| tattha majjhimapurisā tayaugavasaina gahaitabbā, uttamapurisā mayaugavasaina| paṭhamapurisā añ‍ñayaugavasaina| tyādīnamaittha paṭipāṭiyā ayaṃ anugīti –

añ‍ñayaugaina paṭhamā, tayaugaina tu majjhimā|

mayaugainuttamā haunti, gahaitabbā vibhāvinā||

sautūnaṃ payaugaisu kausal‍latthaṃ añ‍ñayaugādisahitamaparampi kriyāpadamālaṃ vadāma – sau bhavati, tai bhavanti| tvaṃ bhavasi, tumhai bhavatha| ahaṃ bhavāmi, mayaṃ bhavāma| sau bhavatai, tai bhavantai| tvaṃ bhavasai, tumhai bhavavhai| ahaṃ bhavai, mayaṃ bhavāmhai| ayaṃ añ‍ñayaugādisahitā kriyāpadamālā|

dissanti ca suttantaisu añ‍ñayaugādisahitānipi kriyāpadāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘yaṃpāyaṃ daiva kumārau suppatiṭṭhitapādau, idampimassa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti| yau dandhakālai tarati, taraṇīyai ca dandhati, tvaṃsi ācariyau mama, ahampi daṭṭhukāmausmi, pitaraṃ mai idhāgataṃ’’ ic‍caivamādīni aitassatthassa paridīpaniyā kriyāpadamālā|

yau tumhasaddaina vattabbai atthai nipatati, na pana hauti tumhatthavācakau, naisau saddau kriyāpadassa tayaugasahitattaṃ sādhaiti, añ‍ñadatthu añ‍ñayaugasahitattañ‍ñaiva sādhaiti| yau ca amhasaddaina vattabbai atthai nipatati, na pana hauti amhatthavācakau, na saupi saddau kriyāpadassa mayaugasahitattaṃ sādhaiti, añ‍ñadatthu añ‍ñayaugasahitattañ‍ñaiva sādhaiti|

tatra tumhasaddaina tāva vattabbatthai – ‘‘na bhavaṃ aiti puñ‍ñatthaṃ, sivirājassa dassanaṃ| māyasmā samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami| idha bhantai bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ dhauvatū’’ti ic‍caivamādayau payaugā| amhasaddaina pana vattabbatthai ‘‘upāli taṃ mahāvīra, pādai vandati satthunau| sāvakau tai mahāvīra, saraṇau vandati satthunau’’ti ca ic‍caivamādayau payaugā| idamaitthupalakkhitabbaṃ ‘‘tvaṃ tumhai ahaṃ maya’’nti atthadīpaka tayauga mayaugatau añ‍ñau añ‍ñatthadīpanau payaugauyaiva añ‍ñayaugau nāma, tattha paṭhamapurisau bhavatīti|

yaj‍jaivaṃ ‘‘sabbāyasaṃ kūṭamatippamāṇaṃ, paggayha sau tiṭṭhasi antalikkhai| aisa sutvā pasīdāmi, vacau tai isisattamā’’tiādīsu kathaṃ| aittha hi majjhimuttamapurisasambhavauyaiva dissati, na tu paṭhamapurisasambhavauti? vuc‍catai – ‘‘sabbāyasaṃ kūṭamatippamāṇaṃ, paggayha sau tiṭṭhasi antalikkhai’’tiādīsu ‘‘sau’’tiādikassa nāmasaddassa tumha’mhasaddassatthavācakasaddaihi‘‘tiṭṭhasī’’tiādīnaṃ syādyantānaṃ padānaṃ dassanatau ac‍cantamajjhāharitabbaihi  samānādhikaraṇattā tagguṇabhūtattā ca majjhimuttamapurisasambhavau samadhigantabbau| īdisaisu payaugaisu syādyantānaṃ dassanavasaina avij‍jamānānipi ajjhāharitabbāni ‘‘tvamaha’’mic‍cādīni padāni bhavanti| katthaci pana paripuṇṇāni dissanti ‘‘sātvaṃ vaṅkamanuppattā, kathaṃ maddi karissasi| sau ahaṃ vicarissāmi, gāmā gāmaṃ purā pura’’nti ic‍caivamādīsu|

ākhyātikassa kriyālakkhaṇattā aliṅgabhaidattā ca tiṇṇaṃ liṅgānaṃ sādhāraṇabhāvaparidīpanatthaṃ aparampi kriyāpadamālaṃ vadāma –

purisau bhavati, kañ‍ñā bhavati, cittaṃ bhavati, purisā bhavanti, kañ‍ñāyau bhavanti, cittāni bhavanti| bhau purisa tvaṃ bhavasi, bhauti kañ‍ñai tvaṃ bhavasi, bhau citta tvaṃ bhavasi, bhavantau purisā tumhai bhavatha, bhautiyau kañ‍ñāyau tumhai bhavatha, bhavantau cittāni tumhai bhavatha| ahaṃ purisau bhavāmi, ahaṃ kañ‍ñā bhavāmi, ahaṃ cittaṃ bhavāmi, mayaṃ purisā bhavāma, mayaṃ kañ‍ñāyau bhavāma, mayaṃ cittāni bhavāma|

aisa nayau attanaupadaisu, saisavibhattīnaṃ sabbapadaisupi| ayamākhyātikassa tiṇṇaṃ liṅgānaṃ sādhāraṇabhāvaparidīpanī kriyāpadamālāva|

vuttañhaitaṃ niruttipiṭakai ‘‘kriyālakkhaṇamākhyātikamaliṅgabhaida’’miti| tatra aliṅgabhaidamiti kau atthau? itthipumanapuṃsakānaṃ avisaisatthau vuc‍catai ‘‘aliṅgabhaida’’miti| yathā ‘‘purisau gacchati, kañ‍ñā gacchati, cittaṃ gacchatī’’ti| catudhā uddiṭṭhakriyāpadaisu yathā ‘‘bhavatī’’ti akārānantaratyantapadaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘bhavati bhavanti bhavasī’’tiādinā kriyāpadamālā sabbathā katā, aivaṃ ‘‘ubbhavati’’c‍cādīnipi akārānantaratyantapadāni gahaitvā ‘‘ubbhavati ubbhavanti ubbhavasī’’tiādinā  kriyāpadamālā sabbathā kātabbā| ‘‘bhauti sambhautī’’tiādīni pana aukārānantaratyantapadāni, ‘‘bhāvaiti vibhāvaitī’’tiādīni ca aikārānantaratyantapadāni gahaitvā pāḷinayānusārainaiva padamālā kātabbā, nayidha vuttanayānusāraina| īdisaisu hi ṭhānaisu duranubaudhā kriyāpadagati| atau labbhamānavasaina kriyāpadamālā kātabbā| na hi laukai laukiyā sabbai dhātusaddai pac‍caikaṃ sabbaihipi chan‍navutiyā vacanaihi yaujaitvā vadanti, aivaṃ avadantānampi naisaṃ kathā aparipuṇṇā nāma na hauti, tasmā vaj‍jaitabbaṭṭhānaṃ vaj‍jaitvā yathāsambhavaṃ padamālā kātabbā| aivaṃ pañ‍camiyādīsupi vibhattīsu| ayaṃ vattamānavibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

itau paṭṭhāya pana yathuddiṭṭhapadānaiva pariṇāmaitvā pariṇāmaitvā pañ‍camiyādīnaṃ mātikābhāvaina gahaitabbāni| idāni pana tayaugādisahitāsahitavasaina dvidhā kriyāpadamālāyau dassaissāma k‍vacādaisavasaina sambhūtāni ca rūpantarāni sautūnaṃ sukhadhāraṇatthañ‍caiva purisappayaugai asammauhatthañ‍ca|

bhavatu, bhavantu| bhavāhi, bhava, bhavatha| bhavāmi, bhavāma| bhavataṃ, bhavantaṃ| bhavassu, bhavavhau| bhavai, bhavāmasai| sau bhavatu, tai bhavantu| tvaṃ bhavāhi, bhava, tumhai bhavatha| ahaṃ bhavāmi, mayaṃ bhavāma| sau bhavataṃ, tai bhavantaṃ| tvaṃ bhavassu, tumhai bhavavhau| ahaṃ bhavai, mayaṃ bhavāmasai| ayaṃ pañ‍camīvibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

bhavaiyya, bhavai, bhavaiyyuṃ| bhavaiyyāsi, bhavaiyyātha| bhavaiyyāmi, bhavaiyyāma, bhavaimu| bhavaitha, bhavairaṃ| bhavaithau, bhavaiyyāvhau| bhavaiyyaṃ, bhavaiyyāmhai iti vā, sau bhavaiyya, bhavai, tai bhavaiyyuṃ| tvaṃ bhavaiyyāsi, tumhai bhavaiyyātha| ahaṃ bhavaiyyāmi, mayaṃ bhavaiyyāma, bhavaimu| sau bhavaitha, tai bhavairaṃ| tvaṃ bhavaithau, tumhai bhavaiyyāvhau| ahaṃ bhavaiyyaṃ, mayaṃ bhavaiyyāmhai iti vā| ayaṃ sattamīvibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

babhūva, babhūvu| babhūvai, babhūvittha| babhūvaṃ, babhūvimha| babhūvittha, babhūvirai| babhūvitthau, babhūvivhau| babhūviṃ, babhūvimhai iti vā, sau babhūva, tai babhūvu| tvaṃ babhūvai, tumhai babhūvittha| ahaṃ babhūvaṃ, mayaṃ babhūvimha| sau babhūvittha, tai babhūvirai| tvaṃ babhūvitthau, tumhai babhūvivhau| ahaṃ babhūviṃ, mayaṃ babhūvimhai iti vā| ayaṃ paraukkhāvibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

abhavā, abhavū| abhavau, abhavattha| abhavaṃ, abhavamhā| abhavattha, abhavatthuṃ| abhavasai, abhavavhaṃ| abhaviṃ, abhavamhasai iti vā, sau abhavā, tai abhavū| tvaṃ abhavau, tumhai abhavattha| ahaṃ abhavaṃ, mayaṃ abhavamhā| sau abhavattha, tai abhavatthuṃ| tvaṃ abhavasai, tumhai abhavavhaṃ| ahaṃ abhaviṃ, mayaṃ abhavamhasai iti vā| ayaṃ hiyyattanīvibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

abhavi, abhavuṃ| abhavau, abhavittha| abhaviṃ, abhavimhā| abhavā, abhavū| abhavasai, abhavivhaṃ| abhavhaṃ, abhavimhai iti vā, sau abhavi, tai abhavuṃ| tvaṃ abhavau, tumhai abhavittha| ahaṃ abhaviṃ, mayaṃ abhavimhā| sau abhavā, tai abhavū| tvaṃ abhavasai, tumhai abhavivhaṃ| ahaṃ abhavaṃ, mayaṃ abhavimhai iti vā| ayaṃ aj‍jatanīvibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

aittha panaj‍jataniyā uṃvacanassa iṃsumādaisavasaina bhavatinau rūpantarānipi vaiditabbāni| saiyyathidaṃ? tai bhaviṃsu, samubbhaviṃsu, pabhaviṃsu, parābhaviṃsu, sambhaviṃsu, pātubhaviṃsu, pātubbhaviṃsu, imāni akammakapadāni| paribhaviṃsu, abhibhaviṃsu, adhibhaviṃsu, atibhaviṃsu, anubhaviṃsu, samanubhaviṃsu, abhisambhaviṃsu|

‘‘adhibhausu’’nti rūpampi, yasmā dissati pāḷiyaṃ|

tasmā hi nayatau ñai yyaṃ, ‘‘paribhausu’’ntiādikaṃ||

tatrāyaṃ pāḷi – ‘‘aivaṃvihāriñ‍cāvusau bhikkhuṃ rūpā adhibhausuṃ, na bhikkhu rūpai adhibhausī’’ti| imāni sakammakapadāni, aivamaj‍jataniyā uṃvacanassa iṃsumādaisavasaina bhavatinau rūpantarāni bhavanti| apica

‘‘anvabhi’’ itirūpampi, aj‍jatanyā padissati|

tasmā hi nayatau ñaiyyaṃ, ‘‘ajjhabhi’’c‍cādikampi ca||

tatrāyaṃ pāḷi – sau taina kammaina divaṃ samak‍kami, sukhañ‍ca khiḍḍāratiyau ca anvabhīti| tattha anvabhīti anu abhīti chaidau| anūti upasaggau| abhīti ākhyātikapadanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

bhavissati, bhavissanti| bhavissasi, bhavissatha| bhavissāmi, bhavissāma| bhavissatai, bhavissantai| bhavissasai, bhavissavhai| bhavissaṃ, bhavissāmhai iti vā, sau bhavissati, tai bhavissanti| tvaṃ bhavissasi, tumhai bhavissatha| ahaṃ bhavissāmi, mayaṃ bhavissāma| sau bhavissatai, tai bhavissantai| tvaṃ bhavissasai, tumhai bhavissavhai| ahaṃ bhavissaṃ, mayaṃ bhavissāmhai iti vā| ayaṃ bhavissantīvibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

abhavissā, abhavissaṃsu| abhavissai, abhavissatha| abhavissaṃ, abhavissāmhā| abhavissatha, abhavissisu| abhavissasai, abhavissavhai| abhavissiṃ, abhavissāmhasai iti vā, sau abhavissā, tai abhavissaṃsu| tvaṃ abhavissai, tumhai  abhavissatha| ahaṃ abhavissaṃ, mayaṃ abhavissāmhā| sau abhavissatha, tai abhavissisu| tvaṃ abhavissasai, tumhai abhavissavhai| ahaṃ abhavissiṃ, mayaṃ abhavissāmhasai iti vā| ayaṃ kālātipattivibhattivasaina kriyāpadamālāniddaisau|

vauhārabhaidakusalaina subuddhinā yau,

kac‍cāyanaina kathitau jinasāsanatthaṃ|

tyādik‍kamau tadanugaṃ kiriyāpadānaṃ,

katvā kamau bhavatidhātuvasaina vuttau||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

bhavatinau kriyāpadamālāvibhāgau nāma

dutiyau paricchaidau|

3. pakiṇṇakavinicchaya

itau paraṃ pavakkhāmi, pakiṇṇakavinicchayaṃ|

sappayaugaisu atthaisu, viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāṭavatthayā||

tattha atthuddhārau, atthasaddacintā, atthātisayayaugau, samānāsamānavasainavacanasaṅgahau, āgamalakkhaṇavasaina vibhattivacanasaṅgahau, kālavasaina vibhattivacanasaṅgahau, kālasaṅgahau, pakaraṇasaṃsandanā, vattamānādīnaṃ vacanatthavibhāvanā cāti navadhā vinicchayau vaiditabbau|

atthuddhārai tāva samānasutikapadānamatthuddhāraṇaṃ karissāma| aitthākhyātapadasañ‍ñitānaṃ bhautisadda bhavaisaddānamatthau uddharitabbau| tathā haitai nāmikapadasañ‍ñitaihi aparaihi bhautisadda bhavaisaddaihi samānasutikāpi asamānatthā caiva haunti asamānavibhattikā ca| sāsanasmiñhi  kaici saddā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ samānasutikā samānāpi asamānatthā asamānapavattinimittā asamānaliṅgā asamānavibhattikā asamānavacanakā asamānantā asamānakālikā asamānapadajātikā ca bhavanti|

taisamasamānatthattai ‘‘sabbañhi taṃ jīrati daihanissitaṃ| appassutāyaṃ purisau, balibaddauva jīrati| santau tasitau| pahu santau na bharati| santau ācikkhatai muni| santau sappurisā laukai| santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti aivamādayau payaugā| aittha jīratisaddadvayaṃ yathāsambhavaṃ navabhāvāpagamavaḍḍhanavācakaṃ| santausaddapañ‍cakaṃ yathāsambhavaṃ parissamappattasamānaupasantaupalabbhamānavācakanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

asamānapavattinimittattai pana ‘‘akatañ‍ñū mittadubbhī, assaddhau akatañ‍ñūcā’’tiaivamādayau| aittha ca akatañ‍ñūsaddadvayaṃ katākatājānanajānanapavattinimittaṃ paṭic‍ca sambhūtattā asamānapavattinimittakanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

asamānaliṅgattai ‘‘sukhī hautu pañ‍casikha sak‍kau daivānamindau| tvañ‍ca bhaddai sukhī hauhi| yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti| mātā mai atthi, sā mayā pausaitabbā’’ti aivamādayau| aittha sukhīsaddadvayaṃ sāsaddadvayañ‍ca pumitthiliṅgavasaina asamānaliṅganti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

asamānavibhattikattai ‘‘āhārai udarai yatau| yatau pajānāti sahaitudhamma’’nti aivamādayau| aittha yatausaddadvayaṃ  paṭhamāpañ‍camīvibhattisahitattā asamānavibhattikanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

asamānavacanakattai imai payaugā –

‘‘yāya mātu bhatau pausau, imaṃ laukaṃ avaikkhati|

tampi pāṇadadiṃ santiṃ, hanti kuddhau puthuj‍janau’’ti

ādīsu hantisaddau aikavacanau|

‘‘imai nūna arañ‍ñasmiṃ, migasaṅghāni luddakā|

vākurāhi parikkhippa, saubbhaṃ pātaitvā tāvadai|

vik‍kausamānā tibbāhi, hanti naisaṃ varaṃ vara’’nti||

ādīsu pana bahuvacanau| ‘‘sīlavā vattasampan‍nau| aitha tumhai āyasmantau sīlavā hautha| santau dantau niyatau brahmacārī| santau havai sabbhi pavaidayanti| mahārājā yasassī sau| cattārau mahārājā’’ti aivamādīsu sīlavāsaddādayau aikavacanabahuvacanakā|

asamānantattai pana yattha samānasutikānaṃ asamānavibhattikattaṃ vā asamānavacanattaṃ vā upalabbhati| taiyaiva payaugā| taṃ yathā? ‘‘sataṃ sampajānaṃ, sataṃ dhammau, santau dantau, santau sappurisā’’ ic‍caivamādayau|

asamānakālattai ‘‘nanu tai sutaṃ brāhmaṇa bhañ‍ñamānai, daivā na issanti purisaparak‍kamassa| tai janā pāramissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttara’’nti aivamādayau| aittha issantisaddadvayaṃ vattamānābhavissantīkālavasaina asamānakālanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| vattamānābhavissantīvibhattivasaina pana asamānavibhattikantipi|

asamānapadajātikattai  ‘‘sayaṃ samāhitau nāgau, sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kamuddisaiyyaṃ| pathai dhāvantiyā pati, aikaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā, pādaisu sirasā pati| giriṃ caṇḍauraṇaṃ patī’’ti aivamādayau| aittha sayaṃsaddadvayaṃ nāmanipātavasaina patisaddattayaṃ nāmākhyātaupasaggavasaina asamānapadajātikanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

iminā nayaina sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ| aivaṃ sāsanasmiṃ kaici saddā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ samānasutikā samānāpi asamānatthā asamānapavattinimittā asamānaliṅgā asamānavibhattikā asamānavacanakā asamānantā asamānakālikā asamānapadajātikā ca bhavanti| aitādisaisu saddaisu yau kriyāpadattaṃ pakāsaiti, na sau nāmikapadattaṃ| yau ca nāmikapadattaṃ pakāsaiti, na sau kriyāpadattaṃ| aivaṃ santaipi sutisāmañ‍ñatau aikattaina gahaitvā atthuddhārau karaṇīyauti yathāvuttakriyāpadānaṃ nāmapadaihi samānasutikānaṃ bhautisadda bhavai saddānamatthuddhāraṃ vadāma|

kathaṃ? bhautisaddau kattuyaugai kriyāpadaṃ, kriyāyaugai nāmikapadaṃ, tasmā sau dvīsu atthaisu vattati kriyāpadatthai nāmikapadatthai ca| tattha kriyāpadatthai vattamānavasaina, nāmikapadatthai panālapanavasaina| kriyāpadatthai tāva ‘‘aikau bhauti’’, nāmikapadatthai ‘‘mā bhauti paridaivaisi’’| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

bhāvai nāmapadatthai ca, ālapanavisaisitai|

imaisu dvīsu atthaisu, bhautisaddau pavattati||

bhavaisaddau  pana ‘‘bhavāmī’’timassa vattamānāvibhattiyuttassa saddassatthaipi vattati| ‘‘bhavāmī’’timassa pañ‍camīvibhattiyuttassa saddassa āṇatyāsīsanatthaisupi vattati| ‘‘bhavaiyyāmī’’timassa sattamīvibhattisahitassa saddassa anumatiparikappatthaisupi vattati| tatridaṃ paṭhamatthassa sādhakaṃ āhac‍cavacanaṃ –

‘‘daivānaṃ adhikau haumi, bhavāmi manujādhipau|

rūpalakkhaṇasampan‍nau, pañ‍ñāya asamau bhavai’’ti||

ayaṃ pana sabbaisaṃ taisamatthānaṃ sādhikā amhākaṃ gāthāracanā –

‘‘sukhī bhavati aisau ca, ahañ‍cāpi sukhī bhavai|

sukhī bhavatu aisau ca, ahañ‍cāpi sukhī bhavai||

imāya buddhapūjāya, bhavantu sukhitā pajā|

bhavai’hañ‍ca sukhappattau, sāmac‍cau saha ñātibhi||

sukhī bhavaiyya aisau ca, ahañ‍cāpi sukhī bhavai|

sukhī bhavaiyya cai aisau, ahañ‍cāpi sukhī bhavai’’ti||

ic‍caivaṃ –

vattamānāya pañ‍camyaṃ, sattamyañ‍ca vibhattiyaṃ|

aitaisu tīsu ṭhānaisu, bhavaisaddau pavattati||

aikadhā vattamānāyaṃ, pañ‍camīsattamīsu ca|

dvaidhā dvaidhātimassatthaṃ, pañ‍cadhā paridīpayai||

dvaidhā vā vattamānāya-mādipurisavācakau|

atthau ‘‘bhavai’’ti aitassa, ‘‘bhavatī’’tipiyuj‍jati||

idāni pana aitassa, vuttassatthassa sādhakaṃ|

aittha pāḷippadaisaṃ tu, āharissaṃ suṇātha mai||

kau’yaṃ  majjhaisamuddasmiṃ, apassaṃ tīramāyuhai|

kaṃ tvaṃ atthavasaṃ ñatvā, aivaṃ vāyamasai bhusaṃ||

nisamma vattaṃ laukassa, vāyāmassa ca daivatai|

tasmā majjhaisamuddasmiṃ, apassaṃ tīramāyuhai||

assaṃ purimagāthāyaṃ, ‘‘āyuhai’’tipadassa hi|

‘‘āyūhatī’’ti atthauti, viñ‍ñātabbau vibhāvinā||

vibhattiyā vipal‍lāsa-vasaināyaṃ samīritau|

vattamānai sattamīti, tissaikāravasaina vā||

pacchimāya ca gāthāyaṃ, ‘‘āyuhai’’tipadassa tu|

‘‘āyūhāmī’’ti atthauti, saddatthañ‍ñū vibhāvayai||

tathā ‘‘bhavai’’tiaitassa, vattamānāvibhattiyaṃ|

‘‘bhavatī’’ti, ‘‘bhavāmī’’ti, catthaṃ dvaidhā vibhāvayai||

aivaṃvidhaisu añ‍ñaisu, pāṭhaisupi ayaṃ nayau|

naitabbau nayadakkhaina, nayasāgarasāsanai||

aivamayaṃ bhavaisaddau pañ‍casu chasu vā kriyāpadatthaisu pavattati| tathā sattamīvibhatyantanāmikapadassa vuddhisaṃsārakammabhavūpapattibhavasaṅkhātaisu atthaisupi| tathā hi ‘‘abhavai nandati tassa, bhavai tassa na nandatī’’tiādīsu vuddhimhi| ‘‘bhavai vicarantau’’tiādīsu saṃsārai| ‘‘bhavai khau sati jāti hauti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’ntiādīsu kammabhavai| ‘‘aivaṃ bhavaivij‍jamānai’’tiādīsu upapattibhavaiti daṭṭhabbaṃ| iminā nayaina bhūdhātutau nipphan‍nānaṃ añ‍ñataupi añ‍ñaisaṃ kriyāpadānaṃ yathāsambhavamatthau uddharitabbau|

ākhyātatthamhimai  atthā, na lātabbā kudācanaṃ|

atthuddhāravasainaitai, uddhaṭā nāmatau yatau||

idamaittha saṅkhaipatau atthuddhāranayanidassanaṃ|

atthasaddacintāyaṃ pana aivamupalakkhaitabbaṃ – ‘‘bhavantai, parābhavantai, parābhavai’’ic‍cādayau gacchati gacchaṃ gacchatausaddādayau viya visaisasaddā, na yācanaupatāpanatthādivācakau nāthatisaddau viya, na ca rājadaivatādivācakau daivasaddau viya sāmañ‍ñasaddā| yai caittha visaisasaddā, tai sabbakālaṃ visaisasaddāva| yai ca sāmañ‍ñasaddā, taipi sabbakālaṃ sāmañ‍ñasaddāva|

tatra gacchatītiādīnaṃ visaisasaddatā aivaṃ daṭṭhabbā – gacchatīti aikaṃ nāmapadaṃ, aikamākhyātaṃ| tathā gacchanti aikaṃ nāmapadaṃ, aikamākhyātaṃ| gacchatauti aikau kitantau, aparau rūḷhīsaddau| satipi visaisasaddattai sadisattā sutisāmañ‍ñatau tabbisayaṃ buddhiṃ nuppādaiti vināva’tthappakaraṇasaddantarābhisambandhaina| tathā hi saddantarābhisambandhaina ‘‘gacchati patiṭṭhita’’nti vuttai sattamyantaṃ nāmapadanti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘gacchati tissau’’ti vuttai panākhyātanti| tathā ‘‘sa gacchaṃ na nivattatī’’ti vuttai paṭhamantaṃ nāmapadanti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘gacchaṃ puttanivaidakau’’ti vuttai ākhyātanti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘gacchatau hayatau patitau’’ti vuttai kitantauti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘gacchatau paṇṇapupphāni patantī’’ti vuttai rukkhavācakau rūḷhīsaddauti| iti visaisasaddānaṃ ākhyātanāmānaṃ nāmākhyātaihi samānasutikānaṃ atthābhisambandhādīsu yau kauci atthavisaisañāpakau sambandhau avassamicchitabbau| aivaṃ ‘‘gacchatī’’tiādīnaṃ ākhyātanāmattādivasaina pac‍caikaṃ ṭhitānaṃ aikaikatthavācakānaṃ visaisasaddatā daṭṭhabbā|

‘‘nāthati  daivau’’tiādīnaṃ pana ākhyātanāmānaṃ nāmākhyātaihi asamānasutikānaṃ anaikatthavācakānaṃ sāmañ‍ñasaddatā aiva daṭṭhabbā| atthasambandhādīsu hi vinā yaina kainaci sambandhaina ‘‘nāthatī’’ti vuttai ‘‘yācatī’’ti vā ‘‘upatāpaitī’’ti vā ‘‘issariyaṃ karautī’’ti vā ‘‘āsīsatī’’ti vā atthau paṭibhāti, tathā ‘‘daivau’’ti vuttai ‘‘maighau’’ti vā ‘‘ākāsau’’ti vā ‘‘rājā’’ti vā ‘‘daivatā’’ti vā ‘‘visuddhidaivau’’ti vā atthau paṭibhāti| yadā pana saddantarābhisambandhaina ‘‘nāthati suppaṭipatti’’nti vuttai tadā ‘‘nāthatī’’ti kriyāpadassa ‘‘yācatī’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati, ‘‘nāthati sabbakilaisai’’ti vuttai ‘‘upatāpaitī’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘nāthati sakacittai’’ti vuttai ‘‘issariyaṃ karautī’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘nāthati laukassa hita’’nti vuttai ‘‘āsīsatī’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| tathā ‘‘daivau gaj‍jatī’’ti vuttai ‘‘daivau’’ti nāmapadassa ‘‘maighau’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘viddhau vigatavalāhakau daivau’’ti vuttai ‘‘ākāsau’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘pivatu daivau pānīya’’nti vuttai ‘‘rājā’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘daivau daivakāyā cavati āyusaṅkhayā’’ti vuttai ‘‘daivatā’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘daivātidaivau satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇau’’ti vuttai ‘‘visuddhidaivau’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| iminā nayaina añ‍ñaipi sāmañ‍ñasaddā ñātabbā|

sabbamaitaṃ ñatvā yathā atthau saddaina, saddau catthaina na virujjhati, tathātthasaddā cintanīyā| tatridaṃ upalakkhaṇamattaṃ cintākāranidassanaṃ – ‘‘atthakusalā bhavantai’’ti vā ‘‘kic‍cāni bhavantai’’ti vā vuttai ‘‘bhavantai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘bhavantī’’timinā samānatthamākhyātapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘bhavantai passāmī’’ti vā ‘‘icchāmī’’ti vā vuttai upayaugatthavaṃ  nāmapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘bhavantai janai pasaṃsatī’’ti vā ‘‘kāmaitī’’ti vā vuttai pac‍cattaupayaugatthavantāni dvai nāmapadānīti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘caurā parābhavantai’’ti vuttai ‘‘parābhavantai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘parābhavantī’’timinā samānatthamākhyātikapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘parābhavantai janā icchanti amittāna’’nti vuttai ‘‘parābhavantai’’ti imāni upayaugapac‍cattatthavantāni dvai nāmapadānīti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘aisau parābhavai’’ti vuttai ‘‘parābhavai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘parābhavaiyyā’’timinā samānatthamākhyātapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘aitai parābhavai laukai, paṇḍitau samavaikkhiyā’’ti vuttai ‘‘parābhavai’’ti idaṃ upayaugatthavaṃ bahuvacanaṃ nāmapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘parābhavai satī’’ti vuttai bhāvalakkhaṇabhummatthaikavacanakaṃ nāmapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘tumhai mai pasādā sambhavai’’ti vuttai ‘‘sambhavai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘sambhavathā’’timinā samānatthamākhyātapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘aihi tvaṃ sambhavavhai’’ti vuttai ‘‘sambhavavhai’’ti idaṃ sambhavāya nāma itthiyā vācakaṃ itthiliṅgaṃ sālapanaṃ nāmikapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘aihi tvaṃ sambhavavhaipatiṭṭhita’’nti vuttai sambhavanāmakassa purisassa vācakaṃ pul‍liṅgaṃ bhummavacananti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau|

‘‘varuṇau brahmadaivau ca, ahaisuṃ aggasāvakā|

sambhavau nāmupaṭṭhākau, raivatassa mahaisinau’’ti –

hi pāḷi| ‘‘dhammā pātubhavantai’’ti vuttai ‘‘pātubhavantai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘pātubhavantī’’timinā samānatthaṃ sanipātamākhyātapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘pātu bhavantai janai’’ti vuttai ‘‘tai janai bhavaṃ rakkhatū’’ti atthavācakāni ākhyātakitantasabbanāmikapadānīti  aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘pātubhavasai tvaṃ guṇaihī’’ti vuttai ‘‘pātubhavasai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘pātubhavasī’’timinā samānatthamākhyātapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘pātubhavasai guṇai yau tva’’nti vuttai ‘‘pātubhavāhi attanau guṇahaitu tva’’nti atthavācakāninipātayuttākhyātanāmapadānīti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘ahamattanau guṇaihi pātubhavai’’ti vuttai ‘‘pātubhavai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘pātubhavāmī’’timinā samānatthaṃ sanipātamākhyātapadanti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| ‘‘maṃ pātu bhavai idaṃ puñ‍ñakamma’’nti vuttai ‘‘maṃ rakkhatu saṃsārai idaṃ puñ‍ñakamma’’nti atthavācakāni ākhyātanāmapadānīti aivamatthau ca saddau ca cintanīyau| iminā nayaina sabbattha yathārahamatthasaddā cintanīyā| tattha samānasutikānaṃ kaisañ‍ci saddānaṃ ‘‘na taisaṃ kauṭṭhai aupainti| na taisaṃ antarā gacchai| satta vau licchavī aparihānīyai dhammai daisaissāmi, imai tai daiva sattavau, tvañ‍ca uttamasattavau’’tiādīsu samānasutikānaṃ viya uc‍cāraṇavisaisau icchanīyau| uc‍cāraṇavisaisai hi sati padāni paribyattāni, padaisu paribyattaisu atthau paribyattau hauti, atthapariggāhakānaṃ atthādhigamau akicchau hauti, suparisuddhādāsatalai paṭibimbadassanaṃ viya, sau ca gahitapubbasaṅkaitassa atthasambandhādīsu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ ñātaiyaiva hauti, na itarathā| vuttañhaitaṃ paurāṇaihi –

‘‘visayattamanāpan‍nā, saddā naivatthabaudhakā|

na padamattatau atthai, tai añ‍ñātā pakāsakā’’ti||

yadidamaittha vuttamamhaihi ‘‘uc‍cāraṇavisaisau icchanīyau’’ti| atrāyamuc‍cāraṇavisaisadīpanī gāthā sahatthappakāsananayadānagāthāya|

‘‘na  tai saṃ kauṭṭhai aupainti’’, iti pāṭhai sumaidhasau|

padaṃ ‘‘na tai’’ti chinditvā, ‘‘saṃ kauṭṭhai’’ti paṭhaiyya vai||

‘‘saṃ na aupainti kauṭṭhai tai, bhikkhū’’ti atthamīrayai|

aivamimaisu añ‍ñaisu, pāṭhaisupi ayaṃ nayau||

atha yaṃ panidampi vuttaṃ ‘‘kaisañ‍cī’’ti, taṃ kimatthaṃ? ‘‘gacchati patiṭṭhitaṃ, gacchati tissau, bhavantai passāmi, atthakusalā bhavantai, vadantaṃ aikapaukkharā, vadantaṃ paṭivadatī’’tiādīsu samānasutikānamuc‍cāraṇavisaisau na labbhatīti dassanatthaṃ| tasmā idamaittha sal‍lakkhaitabbaṃ – yattha samānasutikānamuc‍cāraṇavisaisau labbhati atthavisaisau ca padānaṃ vibhāgavasaina vā avibhāgavasaina vā, tattha payaugai samānasutikamaikac‍caṃ padaṃ vicchinditvā uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘haitu haitusampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sau taina saddhiṃ bhāsati, sautaina vuyhati| bhavantai janai pasaṃsati, bhavantai passāmī’’ti aivamādayau payaugā| aittha ‘‘haitū’’ti īsakaṃ vicchinditvā ‘‘haitusampayuttāna’’nti uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| tathā ‘‘sau’’ti vicchinditvā ‘‘taina saddhi’’nti uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| ‘‘bhava’’nti vicchinditvā ‘‘tai janai’’ti uc‍cāraitabbaṃ|

saisaṃ pana samānasutikaṃ vicchinditvā na uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| avicchindanīyasmiñhi ṭhānai vicchinditvā paṭhitassa atthau duṭṭhau hauti| aivaṃ padavibhāgāvibhāgavasaina samānasutikānamatthuc‍cāraṇavisaisau vaiditabbau| aittha hi ‘‘sau tainā’’tiādīsu dvipadatthaggahaṇaṃ vibhāgau, aikapadatthaggahaṇamavibhāgauti adhippaitau| aittha ca visuṃ vavatthitānaṃ asamānasutikānaṃ aikatau katvā samānasutikabhāvaparikappanaṃ atthantaraviñ‍ñāpanatthañ‍caiva uc‍cāraṇavisaisadassanatthañ‍ca| na hi aitāni  ‘‘sappau sappau’’tiādīsu viya aikasmiṃyaivatthai samānasutikāni| aivaṃ santaipi aikajjhakaraṇaina laddhaṃ samānasutilaisaṃ gahaitvā atthantaraviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ uc‍cāraṇavisaisadassanatthañ‍ca ‘‘samānasutikānī’’ti vuttāni| aisa nayau añ‍ñatrāpi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu| idamaittha sal‍lakkhaitabbaṃ –

yattha samānasutikānaṃ aṭṭhārasākāraisu yaina kainaci ākāraina atthavisaisau labbhati, vicchinditvā pana uc‍cāraṇai saddavilāsau na hauti, atthau vā duṭṭhau hauti, na tādisaisu payaugaisu samānasutikāni padāni vicchinditvā uc‍cāraitabbāni| tatra katamaina cākāraina atthavisaisalābhau bhavati? padānaṃ vibhāgavasaina vā avibhāgavasaina vā akkharasan‍nidhānavasaina vā padasan‍nidhānavasaina vā padakkharasan‍nidhānavasaina vā vicchāvasaina vā kammappavacanīyavasaina vā bhayakaudhādīsu uppan‍naisu kathitāmaiḍitavacanavasaina vā guṇavācakasaddassa dviruttavasaina vā kriyāpadassa dviruttavasaina vā saṃhitāpadacchaidavasaina vā agāravatthaparidīpanavasaina vā nirantaratthaparidīpanavasaina vā nanirantaratthaparidīpanavasaina vā ‘‘punappuna’’mic‍catthaparidīpanavasaina vā upamānai iva saddavasaina vā itisaddaṃ paṭic‍ca saddapadatthavācakatthaparidīpanavasaina vā tathāpavattacittaparidīpanavasaina vāti imaisuṭṭhārasākāraisu, vitthāratau pana chabbīsāya ākāraisu tatau vādhikaisu yaina kainaci ākāraina atthavisaisalābhau bhavati|

aittha padānaṃ tāva vibhāgavasaina vā avibhāgavasaina vā samānasutikānamatthavisaisalābhai ‘‘sā naṃ saṅgati pālaiti, abhik‍kamau sānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| mā nau daiva avadhi, mānau mayhaṃ na vij‍jatī’’ti aivamādayau payaugā|

akkharasan‍nidhānavasaina  pana atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘santaihi mahitau hitau| saṅgā saṅgāmajiṃ muttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ| dāṭhī dāṭhīsu pakkhandi, mañ‍ñamānau yathā purai| sabbābhibhuṃva sirasāsirasā namāmi| bhūmitau uṭṭhitā yāva, brahmalaukā vidhāvati| ac‍ci ac‍cimatau laukai, ḍayhamānamhi taijasā’’ti aivamādayau payaugā|

padasan‍nidhānavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘āpau āpaugataṃ| rājarājamahāmattādayau, sukhau’laukassa laukassa, kārakau ñāṇacakkhudau, nirāpadai padai nin‍nau, anantañāṇaṃ karuṇālayaṃ layaṃ, malassa buddhaṃ susamāhitaṃ hitaṃ| namāmi dhammaṃ bhavasaṃvaraṃ varaṃ, guṇākarañ‍caiva niraṅgaṇaṃ gaṇa’’nti aivamādayau payaugā|

padakkharasan‍nidhānavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘pamāṇarahitaṃ hitaṃ, siddhatthau sabbasiddhatthau, tilaukamahitau hitau| upagantvāna sambuddhau, idaṃ vacanamabravī’’ti aivamādayau payaugā| tatrimā akkharasan‍nidhānādīsu adhippāyaviñ‍ñāpaniyau gāthā –

mahitauiti saddamhā, makārau cai vivaicitau|

saddau niratthakau aittha, ‘‘akkhara’’nti vadai budhau||

ñaiyyā akkharayaugaina,

‘‘santaihi mahitau hitau’’|

ic‍cādīsu sarūpānaṃ,

hauti atthavisaisatā||

upasaggā nipātā ca, yañ‍cañ‍ñaṃ atthajautakaṃ|

aikakkharampi viñ‍ñūhi, taṃ ‘‘pada’’nti samīritaṃ||

padānaṃ  san‍nidhānañ‍ca, padakkharānamaiva ca|

samāsai labbhamānattaṃ, sandhāya lapitaṃ mayā||

vicchāvasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘gāmai gāmai sataṃ kumbhā, gāmau gāmau ramaṇīyau’’ti aivamādayau payaugā| aittha hi vicchāvasaina sabbaipi gāmā pariggahitā|

nānādhikaraṇānaṃ tu, vattumaikakkhaṇamhi yā|

icchatau byāpituṃ icchā, sā vicchāti pakittitā||

kammappavacanīyavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai candau, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pari vij‍jautatai candau’’ti payaugā, rukkhānaṃ upari upari vij‍jautataiti atthau|

bhayakaudhādīsu uppan‍naisu kathitāmaiḍitavacanavasaina pana atthavisaisalābhai imai payaugā – bhayai tāva ‘‘caurau caurau, sappau sappau’’ic‍cādayau| kaudhai ‘‘vasala vasala, caṇḍāla caṇḍāla, vijjha vijjha, pahara pahara’’ic‍cādayau| pasaṃsāyaṃ ‘‘sādhu sādhu sāriputta, abhik‍kantaṃ bhantai abhik‍kantaṃ bhantai’’ic‍cādayau| turitai ‘‘abhik‍kama vāsaiṭṭha abhik‍kama vāsaiṭṭha, gaccha gaccha, lunāhi lunāhi’’ic‍cādayau| kautūhalai ‘‘āgaccha āgaccha’’ic‍cādayau| acchariyai ‘‘ahau buddhau ahau buddhau’’ic‍cādayau| hāsai ‘‘ahau sukhaṃ ahau sukhaṃ, ahau manāpaṃ ahau manāpaṃ’’ic‍cādayau| saukai ‘‘kahaṃ aikaputtaka kahaṃ aikaputtaka’’ic‍cādayau| pasādai ‘‘bhavissanti vaj‍jī bhavissanti vaj‍jī’’ic‍cādayau| aivaṃ bhayakaudhādīsu uppan‍naisu kathitāmaiḍitavacanavasaina atthavisaisalābhau bhavati| aittha pana atthantarābhāvaipi daḷhīkammavasaina padānamatthajautakabhāvauyaiva atthavisaisalābhau|

bhayai  kaudhai pasaṃsāyaṃ,

turitai kautūhala’ccharai|

hāsai saukai pasādai ca,

karai āmaiḍitaṃ budhau||

casaddau avuttasamuc‍cayatthau, taina garahāasammānādīnaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| ‘‘pāpau pāpau’’tiādīsu hi garahāyaṃ| ‘‘abhirūpaka abhirūpakā’’tiādīsu asammānai| ‘‘k‍vāyaṃ abalabalau viyā’’tiādīsu atisayatthai āmaiḍitaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| guṇavācakassa dviruttavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘kaṇhau kaṇhau ca ghaurau cā’’ti aivamādayau| ‘‘kaṇhau kaṇhau’’ti hi atīva kaṇhauti atthau| kriyāpadassa dviruttavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘dhamai dhamai nātidhamai’’ti aivamādayau| tattha dhamai dhamaiti dhamaiyya nau na dhamaiyya| nātidhamaiti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ pana na dhamaiyya| saṃhitāpadacchaidavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘narānarā, surāsurā, katākatakusalākusalavisayaṃ vippaṭisārākāraina pavattaṃ anusaucanaṃ kuk‍kuc‍ca’’nti aivamādayau| aittha pana viñ‍ñūnaṃ paramakausal‍lajananatthaṃ silaukaṃ racayāma –

hitāhitā hitaṃhitaṃ, ānubhāvaina tai jina|

pavarāpavarāhac‍ca, bhavāmā’nāmayā mayanti||

agāravatthaparidīpanavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘tuvaṃtuvaṃ paisuñ‍ñakalahaviggahavivādā’’ti aivamādayau| nirantaratthaparidīpanavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘divasai divasai paribhuñ‍jatī’’ti aivamādayau| nanirantaratthaparidīpanavasaina atthavisaisalābhai  ‘‘khaṇai khaṇai pīti uppaj‍jatī’’ti aivamādayau| ‘‘punappuna’’mic‍catthaparidīpanavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘muhuṃ muhuṃ bhāyayatai kumārai’’ti aivamādayau| upamānai ivasaddavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘rājā rakkhatu dhammaina, attanauva pajaṃ paja’’nti aivamādayau| itisaddaṃ paṭic‍ca saddapadatthavācakattaparidīpanavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘buddhau buddhauti kathayantau, saumanassaṃ pavaidayi’’nti aivamādayau| tathāpavattacittaparidīpanavasaina atthavisaisalābhai ‘‘buddhau buddhauti cintaintau, maggaṃ saudhaimahaṃ tadā’’ti aivamādayau| aivaṃ īdisaisu payaugaisu samānasutikapadaṃ vicchinditvā na uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| vicchinditvā hi uc‍cāraṇai sati saddavilāsau na bhavati, katthaci pana ‘‘katākatākusalākusalavisaya’’nti aivamādīsu vicchinditvā uc‍cāritassa atthau duṭṭhau hauti, tasmā vicchinditvā na uc‍cāraitabbaṃ, aikābaddhaṃyaiva katvā uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| iti samānasutikaisu vinicchayau chabbīsāya ākāraihi adhikaihi ca maṇḍaitvā dassitau|

yasmā pana samānasutikaisu vinicchayai dassitai asamānasutikaisupi vinicchayau dassaitabbau hauti, tasmā tampi dassaissāma – yattha niggahītamhā parākāralaupaupi pāṭhau pañ‍ñāyati, saṃyaugabyañ‍janassa visaṃyaugattampi| taisu payaugaisu niggahītapadaṃ anantarapadaina saddhiṃ aikābaddhaṃyaiva katvā uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| katamāni tāni? ‘‘sacai bhuttau bhavaiyyāhaṃ-sā’jīvau garahitau mama| pupphaṃ’sā uppaj‍ji| khayamattaṃ na nibbānaṃ’sa gambhīrādivācatau’’ti aivamādayau| aittha hi ‘‘sacai bhuttau bhavaiyyāha’’ntiādinā vicchaidamakatvā  anantarai dvīsu gāthāpadaisu antarībhūtānaṃ dvin‍naṃ samānasutikapadānaṃ aikatau uc‍cāraṇamiva anantarapadaihi saddhiṃ aikābaddhuc‍cāraṇavasaina ‘‘sacai bhuttau bhavaiyyāhaṃ-sā’jīvau garahitau mamā’’tiādinā uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| aivarūpauyaiva hi uc‍cāraṇavisaisau sakalaihipi paurāṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi anumatau uc‍cāritau ca ‘‘assa ājīvau garahitau mama, assā uppaj‍ji, assa gambhīrādivācatau’’tiaivamādiatthappaṭipādanassānurūpattā|

yattha pana yādisai uc‍cāraṇai kariyamānai atthau paribyattau hauti, taisu payaugaisu k‍vaci casadda panasaddādiyaugaṭṭhānai īsakaṃ vicchinditvā padamuc‍cāraitabbaṃ| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘vāḷā ca lapasakkharā| ac‍cantasantā pana yā, ayaṃ nibbānasampadā, ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti vācaṃ bhāsatau ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti? āmantā| iti ca danti ca duti ca khanti ca ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti? na haivaṃ vattabbai’’ti aivamādayau payaugā|

aitaisu hi paṭhamapayaugai ‘‘vāḷā cā’’ti īsakaṃ vicchinditvā ‘‘lapasakkharā’’ti uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| tattha lapasakkharāti sakkharasadisamadhuravacanā| jātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘niratthakavacanaihi sakkharā viya madhurā’’ti vuttaṃ, tasmātra bahubbīhitappurisavasaina dvidhā samāsau daṭṭhabbau ‘‘lapā sakkharā viya yāsaṃ tā lapasakkharā, lapaihi vā sakkharā viyāti lapasakkharā’’ti|

dutiyapayaugai ‘‘ac‍cantasantā pana’’iti īsakaṃ vicchinditvā ‘‘yā’’ti uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| yā pana ayaṃ nibbānasampadā ac‍cantasantāti hi atthau|

tatiyapayaugai  iti ca danti ca duti ca khanti cāti aitaisu catūsu ṭhānaisu ikārañ‍ca daṃkārañ‍ca dukārañ‍ca khaṃkārañ‍ca īsakaṃ vicchinditvā tadanantaraṃ ti ca saddā uc‍cāraitabbā|

aittha hi avicchinditvā uc‍cāraṇai sati añ‍ñathā gahaitabbattā atthau duṭṭhau bhavati| kathaṃ? īdisaisu ṭhānaisu avicchinditvā uc‍cāraṇai sati itisaddau aivanti atthavācakau nipātau siyā, sandhivasaina pana ikāratthavācakau rūḷhīsaddau na siyā| dantisaddau damanatthau siyā, daṃkāravācakau na siyā| dutisaddau niratthakau siyā, dukāravācakau na siyā| khantisaddau khamanatthau siyā, khaṃkāravācakau na siyā| tasmā ikāra daṃkāra dukāra khaṃkārāni īsakaṃ vicchinditabbāni|

aittha hi iiti daṃiti duiti khaṃitītiādinā saṃhitāpadacchaidau vaiditabbau, parabhūtassa ca ikārassa laupau| na panaittha idaṃ vattabbaṃ ‘‘sarūpasarānaṃ visayai parabhūtassa sarūpasarassa laupau na hauti, pubbasarassaiva laupau hauti tatrāyanti aittha viyā’’ti ‘‘akilāsunau vaṇṇapathai khaṇantā, udaṅgaṇai tattha papaṃ avindu’’nti pāḷiyaṃ sarūpaparasarassa laupadassanatau| tathā hi aṭṭhakathācariyaihi ‘‘pavaddhaṃ āpaṃ papa’’nti atthau saṃvaṇṇitau| tasmā ‘‘iti cā’’ti aitthāpi iiti cāti chaidaṃ katvā dvīsu ikāraisu parassa ikārassa laupau kātabbau, na pubbassa|

pubbasmiñhi ikāravācakai ikārai naṭṭhai sati nipātabhūtaina itisaddaina ikārasaṅkhātau atthau na viñ‍ñāyaiyya, nipātabhūtassa pana itisaddassa ikārai naṭṭhaipi sau atthau viñ‍ñāyataiva ‘‘daivadattauti mai suta’’nti aittha  daivadattapadatthau viya| tasmā itisaddassa parabhūtassa ikārassaiva laupau kātabbau, na pubbassa ikāravācakassa ikārassa| kac‍cāyanai pana yaibhuyyappavattiṃ sandhāya asarūpasaratau parassaiva asarūpasarassa laupau vuttau, na sarūpasaratau parassa sarūpasarassa| mahāpadaisasuttaihi vā sarūpassa parasarassa laupau vuttauti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷanda’’ntiādīsu pana casaddādiyaugaṭṭhānaipi sati vicchinditvā padaṃ na uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| yattha ca āgamakkharādīni dissanti, taisu payaugaisu pubbapadāni vicchinditvā na uc‍cāraitabbāni, āgamakkharavantaihi parapadaihi saddhiṃyaiva uc‍cāraitabbāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘nakkhattarājāriva tārakānaṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca’’ic‍caivamādayau payaugā| yattha yaisaṃ visuṃ visuṃ sambandhau dissati, atthau ca yuj‍jati, tattha tāni atthānurūpaṃ vicchinditvā uc‍cāraitabbāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, ussuk‍kampi akāsi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ’’ ic‍caivamādayau payaugā| aittha hi ‘‘nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ akāsī’’ti vicchinditvā ‘‘ussuk‍kampi akāsi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmi’’nti uc‍cāraitabbaṃ| aivañhi sati na kaivalaṃ sau bhikkhu nahānaiyaiva ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, atha khau yāguyāpi khādanīyaipi bhattasmimpi ussuk‍kaṃ akāsīti atthappakāsanai samatthau bhavati, aṭṭhānappayuttau samuc‍cayatthavācakau apisaddau|
