yattha pana yaisamitaraina vā itaraina vā aikaikapadaina ubhayapadaihi vā sambandhau dissati sahaivatthayuttiyā, tattha tāni yathārahaṃ vicchinditvā uc‍cāraitabbāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘sau dhammaṃ daisaitiādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ  pakāsaiti| paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ vau bhikkhavai daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarautha| ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāva’’nti aivamādayau payaugā| tatrimā adhippāyaviñ‍ñāpikā gāthā –

dhammasaddaina vā brahma-cariyasaddaina vā padaṃ|

yaujaitvā īrayai viñ‍ñū, ‘‘sātthaṃ sabyañ‍jana’’ntidaṃ||

‘‘sādhuka’’nti padaṃ viñ‍ñū, ‘‘suṇāthā’’ti padaina vā|

tathā ‘‘manasikarautha’’, iti vuttapadaina vā|

īrayai yaujayitvāna, ubhayaihi padaihi vā||

aikamaikaina sambandhau, sambandhau ubhayaihi vā|

dissatīti vijānaiyya, saddhimaivatthayuttiyā||

nattanaumatiyā aisau, atthau aittha mayā rutau|

pubbācariyasīhānaṃ, nayaṃ nissāya mai rutau||

aivaṃvidhaisu añ‍ñaisu, pāṭhaisupi ayaṃ nayau|

naitabbau nayadakkhaina, sāsanatthagavaisinā||

atthānurūpatau saddaṃ, atthaṃ saddānurūpatau|

cintayitvāna maidhāvī, vauharai na yathā tathāti||

ayamaittha atthasaddacintā|

atthātisayayaugai aivaṃ upalakkhaitabbaṃ – bhūdhātuatthātisayayaugatau vaḍḍhanai diṭṭhā ‘‘aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau licchavī udānaṃ udānaisi ‘bhavissanti vaj‍jī bhavissanti vaj‍jī’ti’’ iti vā ‘‘ahamaiva dūsiyā bhūnahatā, rañ‍ñau mahāpatāpassā’’ti vā ‘‘vaidā na tāṇāya bhavantidassa, mittaddunau bhūnahunau narassā’’ti vā ‘‘bhūnahac‍caṃ kataṃ mayā’’ti vā aivaṃ vaḍḍhanai diṭṭhā|

vacanasaṅgahai  aivaṃ upalakkhaitabbaṃ – vattamānāya vibhattiyā parassapadaṃ majjhimapurisabahuvacanaṃ pañ‍camiyā parassapadaina majjhimapurisabahuvacanaina sadisaṃ| tumhai bhavatha|

vattamānapañ‍camīnaṃ parassapadai uttamapurisacatuk‍kai aikavacanaṃ aikavacanaina, bahuvacanampi bahuvacanaina sadisaṃ| ahaṃ bhavāmi, mayaṃ bhavāma|

vattamānāya attanaupadaṃ majjhimapurisaikavacanaṃ hiyyattanaj‍jatanīnaṃ attanaupadaihi dvīhi majjhimapurisaikavacanaihi sadisaṃ katthaci vaṇṇasamudāyavasaina kiñ‍ci visaisaṃ vaj‍jaitvā, aisa nayau uttaratrāpi yaujaitabbau| tvaṃ bhavasai, idaṃ vattamānāya rūpaṃ| tvaṃ abhavasai, idaṃ hiyyattanaj‍jatanīnaṃ rūpaṃ|

vattamānāya attanaupadaṃ uttamapurisaikavacanaṃ pañ‍camiyā attanaupadainuttamapurisaikavacanaina ca paraukkhāya parassapadaina majjhimapurisaikavacanaina cāti dvīhi vacanaihi sadisaṃ| ahaṃ bhavai, idaṃ vattamānapañ‍camīnaṃ rūpaṃ| tvaṃ babhūvai, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ|

vattamānāya attanaupadaṃ uttamapurisabahuvacanaṃ paraukkhaj‍jatanīnaṃ attanaupadaihi dvīhi uttamapurisabahuvacanaihi sadisaṃ| mayaṃ bhavāmhai, idaṃ vattamānāya rūpaṃ| mayaṃ babhūvimhai, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ| mayaṃ abhavimhai, idamaj‍jataniyā rūpaṃ|

pañ‍camiyā attanaupadaṃ majjhimapurisabahuvacanaṃ paraukkhāya attanaupadaina majjhimapurisabahuvacanaina sadisaṃ| tumhai bhavavhau, idaṃ pañ‍camiyā rūpaṃ| tumhai babhūvivhau, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ|

paraukkhāya parassapadaṃ paṭhamapurisabahuvacanaṃ hiyyattaniyā parassapadaina paṭhamapurisabahuvacanaina ca aj‍jataniyā attanaupadaina paṭhamapurisabahuvacanaina cāti dvīhi vacanaihi sadisaṃ| tai babhūvu, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ| tai abhavu, idaṃ hiyyattanaj‍jatanīnaṃ rūpaṃ|

paraukkhāya  parassapadaṃ majjhimapurisabahuvacanaṃ attanaupadaina paṭhamapurisaikavacanaina ca hiyyattaniyā parassapadaina majjhimapurisabahuvacanaina ca attanaupadaina paṭhamapurisaikavacanaina ca aj‍jataniyā parassapadaina majjhimapurisabahuvacanaina cāti catūhi vacanaihi sadisaṃ| tumhai babhūvittha, sau babhūvittha, imāni paraukkhāya rūpāni| tumhai abhavattha, sau abhavattha, imāni hiyyattaniyā rūpāni| tumhai abhavittha, idamaj‍jataniyā rūpaṃ|

paraukkhāya parassapadaṃ uttamapurisaikavacanaṃ hiyyattaniyā parassapadainuttamapurisaikavacanaina ca aj‍jataniyā attanaupadainuttamapurisaikavacanaina cāti dvīhi vacanaihi sadisaṃ| ahaṃ babhūvaṃ, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ| ahaṃ abhavaṃ, idaṃ hiyyattanaj‍jatanīnaṃ rūpaṃ|

paraukkhāya parassapadaṃ uttamapurisabahuvacanaṃ hiyyattaniyā parassapadainuttamapurisabahuvacanaina sadisaṃ| mayaṃ babhūvimha, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ| mayaṃ abhavamha, idaṃ hiyyattaniyā rūpaṃ|

paraukkhāya attanaupadauttamapurisaikavacanaṃ hiyyattaniyā attanaupadainuttamapurisaikavacanaina ca aj‍jataniyā parassapadainuttamapurisaikavacanaina cāti dvīhi vacanaihi sadisaṃ| ahaṃ babhūviṃ, idaṃ paraukkhāya rūpaṃ| ahaṃ abhaviṃ, idaṃ hiyyattanaj‍jatanīnaṃ rūpaṃ|

hiyyattaniyā parassapadaṃ paṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ aj‍jataniyā attanaupadaina paṭhamapurisaikavacanaina sadisaṃ| sau abhavā|

hiyyattaniyā parassapadaṃ majjhimapurisaikavacanaṃ aj‍jataniyā parassapadaina majjhimapurisaikavacanaina sadisaṃ| tvaṃ abhavau|

bhavissantiyā parassapadaṃ majjhimapurisabahuvacanaṃ kālātipattiyā parassapadaina majjhimapurisabahuvacanaina attanaupadaina paṭhamapurisaikavacanaina cāti dvīhi vacanaihi sadisaṃ| tumhai  bhavissatha, idaṃ bhavissantiyā rūpaṃ| tumhai abhavissatha, sau abhavissatha, imāni kālātipattiyā rūpāni|

bhavissantiyā attanaupadaṃ majjhimapurisaikavacanaṃ kālātipattiyā attanaupadaina majjhimapurisaikavacanaina sadisaṃ| tvaṃ bhavissasai, idaṃ bhavissantiyā rūpaṃ| tvaṃ abhavissasai, idaṃ kālātipattiyā rūpaṃ|

bhavissantiyā attanaupadaṃ majjhimapurisabahuvacanaṃ kālātipattiyā attanaupadaina majjhimapurisabahuvacanaina sadisaṃ| tumhai bhavissavhai, idaṃ bhavissantiyā rūpaṃ| tumhai abhavissavhai, idaṃ kālātipattiyā rūpaṃ|

bhavissantiyā attanaupadaṃ uttamapurisaikavacanaṃ kālātipattiyā parassapadainuttamapurisaikavacanaina sadisaṃ| ahaṃ bhavissaṃ, idaṃ bhavissantiyā rūpaṃ| ahaṃ abhavissaṃ, idaṃ kālātipattiyā rūpaṃ| saisāni sabbāsamaṭṭhan‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ vacanāni añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ visadisānīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| bhavanti catra –

vattamānāpañ‍camīsu, thadvayaṃ samudīritaṃ|

‘‘tumhai bhavatha’’ic‍catra, udāharaṇakaṃ dvidhā||

midvayaṃ madvayañ‍caiva, tāsu vuttaṃ dvidhā dvidhā|

‘‘bhavāmī’’ti ‘‘bhavāmā’’ti, caittha rūpāni niddisai||

vattamānakahiyyatta-naj‍jatanīvibhattisu|

saittayaṃ ‘‘bhavasai tva’’nti, vattamānāvibhattitau|

‘‘abhavasai’’ti hiyyatta-naj‍jatanīvibhattitau||

vattamānāpañ‍camikā-paraukkhāsu vibhattisu|

aittayaṃ lapitaṃ tattha, ādau dvin‍naṃ vasaina tu||

jañ‍ñā ‘‘ahaṃ bhavai’’ti ‘‘tvaṃ, babhūvai’’ti paraukkhatau|

vattamānāparaukkhaj‍ja-tanīsu tīsu sadditaṃ||

mhaittayaṃ  kamatau rūpaṃ, mayaṃsaddavisaisiyaṃ|

‘‘sambhavāmhai babhūvimhai, abhavimhai’’ti niddisai||

pañ‍camikāparaukkhāsu, vhaudvayaṃ rūpamaittha hi|

‘‘bhavavhau babhūvivhau’’ti, tumhaisaddavisaisiyaṃ||

paraukkhamhi vā hiyyatta-naj‍jatanīvibhattisu|

uttayaṃ ‘‘tai babhūvū’’ti, rūpaṃ jañ‍ñā paraukkhatau|

hiyyattanaj‍jatanitau, jañ‍ñā ‘‘tai abhavū’’iti||

paraukkhamhi vā hiyyatta-naj‍jatanīvibhattisu|

sadditaṃ tathasaṃyauga- pañ‍cakaṃ iti niddisai||

babhūvitthadvayaṃ tattha, rūpaṃ jañ‍ñā paraukkhajaṃ|

bavhattaikattatau vuttaṃ, majjhimapaṭhamavhayaṃ||

abhavatthadvayaṃ ñaiyyaṃ, hiyyattanīvibhattijaṃ|

bavhattaikattatau vuttaṃ, majjhimau paṭhamau ca sau|

‘‘abhavitthā’’tidaṃ rūpaṃ, aj‍jatanīvibhattijaṃ||

tañ‍ca khau bahukattamhi, tumhaisaddaina yaujayai|

paraukkhāvhayahiyyatta-naj‍jatanīsu kittitaṃ||

aṃtayaṃ tattha ādiyaṃ, ‘‘babhūvaṃ’’rūpamīritaṃ|

duvin‍naṃ abhavaṃrūpaṃ, ahaṃsaddaina yaujayai||

paraukkhakāhiyyattanī-vasaina mhadukaṃ ‘‘mayaṃ|

babhūvimha abhavimha’’, iti rūpadvayaṃ kamā||

paraukkhāvhayahiyyatta-naj‍jatanīvibhattisu|

iṃtayaṃ tu tahiṃ rūpaṃ, ‘‘babhūvi’’nti paraukkhajaṃ|

‘‘abhavi’’ntītarāsaṃ tu, ahaṃsaddayutākhilaṃ||

hiyyattanaj‍jatanīsu, ādvayaṃ matamaittha hi|

‘‘abhavā’’ iti aikattai, rūpaṃ paṭhamapaurisaṃ||

hiyyattanaj‍jatanīsu, audvayaṃ vuttamaittha tu|

‘‘abhavau’’iti aikattai, rūpaṃ majjhimapaurisaṃ||

bhavissantiyakālāti-pattīsu dvīsu bhāsitaṃ|

bavhattai bahuaikattai, sasaṃyaugaṃ ssathattayaṃ||

‘‘tumhai bhavissathi’’c‍caitaṃ, bhavissantiyatau mataṃ|

‘‘abhavissatha tumhai’’ti, ‘‘abhavissatha sau’’ti ca|

kālātipattitau vuttaṃ, aitañhi vacanadvayaṃ||

bhavissantiyakālāti-pattīsu samudīritaṃ|

majjhimapurisaṭṭhānai, sasaṃyaugaṃ ssasaiyugaṃ||

‘‘bhavissasai tva’’mic‍caitaṃ, ‘‘tvaṃ abhavissasai’’ti ca|

imāni tu payaugāni, tattha viñ‍ñū pakāsayai||

ssavhaidvayaṃ saina yutaṃ, ssaṃdvayañ‍ca catuk‍kakaṃ|

idampi kathitaṃ dvīsu, yathārutavibhattisu||

‘‘bhavissavhai’’ti bavhattai, bhavissantikamajjhimau|

bavhattai ‘‘abhavissavhai’’, kālātipattimajjhimau||

‘‘bhavissaṃ’’ iti aikattai, bhavissantikamuttamau|

‘‘abhavissa’’nti aikattai, kālātipattikuttamau||

iti vuttāni vuttaihi, vacanaihi samānataṃ|

yantai’kac‍caihi taṃ sabbaṃ, aikatālīsadhā ṭhitaṃ||

saisāni pañ‍capañ‍ñāsa, asamānāni sabbathā|

aitaṃ nayaṃ gahaitvāna, vadai sabbattha sambhavāti||

ayamaittha samānāsamānavasaina vacanasaṅgahau|

āgamalakkhaṇavasaina  vibhattivacanasaṅgahai aivaṃ upalakkhaitabbaṃ –

bhavissantīparaukkhaj‍ja-tanīkālātipattisu|

nic‍caṃ k‍vaci k‍vacā’nic‍caṃ, ikārāgamanaṃ bhavai||

ikārāgamanaṃ tañhi, paraukkhāyaṃ vibhattiyaṃ|

bavhattai majjhimaṭṭhānai, bavhattai cuttamai siyā|

parassapadaṃ sandhāya, idaṃ vacanamīritaṃ||

uttamaikavacau cāpi, naitassa attanaupadai|

hautīti avagantabbaṃ, bhavissantimhi sabbasau||

hiyyattanaj‍jatanika-kālātipattīsu pana|

akārāgamanaṃ hauti, sabbasau iti lakkhayai||

aj‍jatanimhi bavhattai, majjhimai uttamai tathā|

bavhattamhi akāraina, ikārāgamanaṃ bhavai||

ikārāgamanaṃ nic‍caṃ, kālātipattiyaṃ bhavai|

akārāgamanaṃ tattha, anaikantikamīritaṃ||

akārāgamanaṃyaiva, hiyyattanyaṃ pakāsati|

paraukkhāyaṃ bhavissantya-ñ‍cikārauyaiva dissati||

akārāgamanañ‍caiva, ikārāgamanampi ca|

aj‍jatanikakālāti-pattīsu pana dissati||

tīsu saisavibhattīsu, nā’kārattayamīritaṃ|

vattamānāya pañ‍camyaṃ, sattamiyanti sabbasau||

ikārainaiva sahitā, dvai bhavanti vibhattiyau|

satta dvādasa hauntaittha, vacanānīti lakkhayai||

akārainaiva sahitā, aikāyaiva vibhatti tu|

dvādasa vacanānaittha, bhavantīti ca lakkhayai||

akārikārasahitā, duvaiyaiva vibhattiyau|

cattāri dvādasañ‍caiva, vacanāni bhavantidha||

ākārattayamuttā tu, tissauyaiva vibhattiyau|

vacanānaittha chattiṃsa, hauntīti paridīpayai||

paraukkhāaj‍jatanīsu, pañ‍caṭṭha ca yathāk‍kamaṃ|

ikāratau vimuttāni, vacanāni bhavantiti||

aivamaittha vibhattīnaṃ, chan‍navutividhāna ca|

saṅgahau vacanānanti, viñ‍ñātabbau vibhāvināti||

ayamaittha āgamalakkhaṇavasaina vibhattivacanasaṅgahau|

kālavasaina pana vibhattivacanasaṅgahai duvidhau saṅgahau kālattayavasaina saṅgahau, kālachak‍kavasaina saṅgahau cāti| tattha vattamānāpañ‍camīsattamīvibhattiyau pac‍cuppan‍nakālikā, vattamānāpañ‍camīsattamīvibhatyantāni padāni pac‍cuppan‍navacanāni| paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanīvibhattiyau atītakālikā, paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanīvibhatyantāni padāni atītavacanāni| bhavissantīvibhatti anāgatakālikā, bhavissantīvibhatyantāni padāni anāgatavacanāni| kālātipattivibhatti pana katthaci atītakālikā katthaci anāgatakālikā, tasmā tadantāni padāni atītavacanānipi anāgatavacanānipi haunti| ayaṃ kālattayavasaina vibhattivacanasaṅgahau|

ayaṃ pana kālachak‍kavasaina vibhattivacanasaṅgahau – paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanīvibhattiyau atītakālikā, paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanīvibhatyantāni padāni atītavacanāni| bhavissantīvibhatti anāgatakālikā, bhavissantīvibhatyantāni padāni anāgatavacanāni| vattamānāvibhatti pac‍cuppan‍nakālikā, vattamānāvibhatyantāni padāni pac‍cuppan‍navacanāni| pañ‍camīvibhatti āṇattikālikā, pañ‍camīvibhatyantāni padāni āṇattivacanāni| sattamīvibhatti parikappakālikā, sattamīvibhatyantāni padāni parikappavacanāni| aittha pana ‘‘āṇattivacanānī’’ti ca ‘‘parikappavacanānī’’ti ca idaṃ tathāsīsamattaṃ āsiṭṭhānumatyādīsu pañ‍camyādīnaṃ dissanatau| kālātipattivibhatti kālātipattikālikā, kālātipattivibhatyantāni padāni kālātipattivacanāni| aivaṃ kālachak‍kavasaina vibhattivacanasaṅgahau vaiditabbau|

kālasaṅgahai tividhau kālasaṅgahau kālattayasaṅgahau kālacatuk‍kasaṅgahau kālachak‍kasaṅgahau cāti|

pac‍cuppan‍nai vattamānā, pañ‍camī sattamī cimā|

hauntātītai paraukkhādī, saha kālātipattiyā||

anāgatai bhavissantī, kālātipattikāpi vā|

aivaṃ kālattayaṃ ñaiyyaṃ, ākhyātaṃ tappakāsakaṃ||

nanu kac‍cāyanai ganthai, kālau vuttau catubbidhau|

pac‍cuppan‍nainuttakālai, atītaināgatai iti||

sac‍caṃ vuttau nuttakālau, pac‍cuppan‍nauti icchitau|

samīpai vuttakālauti, atthasambhavatau pana||

tathā hi ‘‘yaṃ tikāla’’nti, vuttamācariyaihipi|

na kālatau vinimuttaṃ, ākhyātaṃ kiñ‍ci dissati||

nanu cāvuttakālaiti, atthau tatra tu yuj‍jati|

tathā hi chabbidhau kālau, niruttimhi pakāsitau||

atītānāgatau pac‍cu-ppan‍nau āṇattimaiva ca|

parikappau ca kālassa, atipattīti chabbidhau||

duvai vibhattiyau tattha, āṇattiparikappikā|

kālamanāmasitvāpi, niruttañ‍ñūhi bhāsitā||

‘‘gacchatu  gacchaiyyi’’c‍cādi-vacanai kathitai na hi|

kriyā nipphaj‍jati niṭṭhaṃ, nāgatā nātipan‍nikā||

kālātipattikā saddā, atītaināgataipi ca|

bhavantīti yathāvuttā, niruttimhi vidūhi vai||

pañ‍camīsattamīvhitā, āṇattiparikappikā|

pac‍cuppan‍nai bhavantīti, na tathā tattha bhāsitā||

tasmā kac‍cāyanai ganthai, ‘‘nuttakālai’’ti yaṃ padaṃ|

atthau ‘‘avuttakālai’’ti, tassa ñāyatimaividaṃ||

sac‍camaivaṃ tu santaipi, āṇattiparikappikā|

pac‍cuppan‍naipi daṭṭhabbā, paṇḍitaina nayañ‍ñunā||

kasmāti cai āṇāpanaṃ, parikappau ca sac‍catau|

pac‍cuppan‍nai yatau atthā, nipphan‍nā dissarai imai||

‘‘anuttakālai’’ti padaṃ, aitassatthassa jautakaṃ|

‘‘samīpai vuttakālai’’ti, atthadīpanatautha vā||

atthānaṃ gamanādīnaṃ, nipphatti na tu dissati|

‘‘gacchatu gacchaiyyi’’c‍cādi, vuttakālai yatau tatau|

avuttakālai niddiṭṭhā, taddīpakavibhattiyau||

kālau vā vuttakālauti, ic‍caivaṃ gahitau idha|

dakkhiṇāsuddhipāṭhamhi, katāva tatiyā ayaṃ|

kāladīpanatā tāsaṃ, iti yuj‍jati nāñ‍ñathā||

atthadvayaṃ pakāsaituṃ, ganthai kac‍cāyanavhayai|

thairau kac‍cāyanau ‘‘nutta-kālai’’ti padamabravi||

aivaṃ tidhā catudhāpi, vuttau kālāna saṅgahau|

chadhā idāni kālānaṃ, saṅgahau nāma niyyatai||

vibhattiyau  paraukkhā ca, hiyyattanīvibhattiyau|

atha aj‍jatanī cāti, tissau’tītai pakāsitā||

anāgatai bhavissantī, bhavatīti pakittitā|

pac‍cuppan‍nai vattamānā, tikālai pañ‍cadhā katā||

pañ‍camīsattamīvhitā, āṇattiparikappikā|

saṅgayhamānā tā yanti, pac‍cuppan‍namhi saṅgahaṃ||

yasmā pañ‍camibhūtāya, vattamānāya ṭhānatau|

samānā pañ‍camī hauti, tasmā sā pañ‍camī matā||

sattamī pana kiñ‍cāpi, samānā tāhi sattamā|

hauti yasmā tatau vuttā, sattamītvaiva nau mati||

kālātipattiyādīhi, yaj‍jaivaṃ vattamānikā|

chaṭṭhī bhavaiyya kālāti-pattikātītavācikā||

pañ‍camī tāya chaṭṭhassa, tulyattā ṭhānatau nanu|

tāhi sattavibhattīhi, sattamī aṭṭhamī siyā||

iti cai kauci bhāsaiyya, ‘‘tan‍nā’’ti paṭisaidhayai|

atītaināgatai cāpi, kālātipattisambhavā||

tathā hi bhāsitā cūḷa-niruttimhi visuṃ ayaṃ|

kālātipatyatītamhā-nāgatai cāti dīpayai||

kriyātipan‍naitītaiti, kasmā kac‍cāyanai rutaṃ|

athāpi cai vadaiyyatra, ‘‘pāyainā’’ti pakāsayai||

yaibhuyyaina hi laukasmiṃ, atītamhi pavattati|

kālātipattisaṃyuttau, vauhārau iti lakkhayai||

atridaṃ kālātipattiyā atītavacanaṃ – ‘‘sacāyaṃ bhikkhavai rājā pitaraṃ dhammikaṃ dhammarājānaṃ jīvitā na vauraupaissatha, imasmiṃyaivassa āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhu  uppaj‍jissathā’’ti| ‘‘passānanda imaṃ mahādhanasaiṭṭhiputtaṃ imasmiṃyaiva nagarai dvaiasītikauṭidhanaṃ khaipaitvā bhariyaṃ ādāya bhikkhāya carantaṃ| sacai hi ayaṃ paṭhamavayai bhaugai akhaipaitvā kammantai payaujayissā, imasmiṃyaiva nagarai aggasaiṭṭhi abhavissā| sacai pana nikkhamitvā pabbajissā, arahattaṃ pāpuṇissā, bhariyāpissa anāgāmiphalai patiṭṭhahissā| sacai majjhimavayai bhaugai akhaipaitvā kammantai payaujayissā, dutiyasaiṭṭhi abhavissā| nikkhamitvā pabbajantau anāgāmī abhavissā, bhariyāpissa sakadāgāmiphalai patiṭṭhahissā| sacai pacchimavayai bhaugai akhaipaitvā kammantai payaujayissā, tatiyasaiṭṭhi abhavissā| nikkhamitvā pabbajantau sakadāgāmī abhavissā, bhariyāpissa sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhahissā’’ iti vā, ‘‘sacai satthā agāraṃ ajjhāvasissā, cak‍kavattirājā abhavissā| rāhulasāmaṇairau pariṇāyakaratanaṃ, thairī itthiratanaṃ, sakalacak‍kavāḷaraj‍jaṃ aitaisañ‍ñaiva abhavissā’’ iti vā aivaṃ kālātipattiyā atītavacanaṃ bhavati|

kathaṃ kālātipattiyā anāgatavacanaṃ bhavati? ‘‘cirampi bhakkhau abhavissā, sacai na vivadāmasai| asīsakaṃ anaṅguṭṭhaṃ, siṅgālau harati rauhitaṃ’’itivā, ‘‘sacai ānanda nālabhissā mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ānanda brahmacariyaṃ abhavissā’’ti iti vā, ‘‘ayaṃ aṅgulimālassa mātā ‘aṅgulimālaṃ ānaissāmī’ti gacchati, sacai samāgamissati, aṅgulimālau ‘aṅgulisahassaṃ pūraissāmī’ti mātaraṃ māraissati| sacāhaṃ na gamissāmi, mahājānikau abhavissā’’ iti vā aivaṃ kālātipattiyā anāgatavacanaṃ bhavati| kac‍cāyanai pana yaibhuyyaina atītappavattiṃ sandhāya kālātipattivibhattiyā atītakālikatā vuttāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kac‍cāyanaipi vā aisā, kālātipattikā pana|

anāgataipi hautīti, ayamatthaupi dissati||

appac‍cakkhai paraukkhāya-tītai iti hi lakkhaṇai|

santaipyatītaggahaṇai, anapaikkhiya taṃ idaṃ||

anāgatai bhavissantī, iti suttassanantaraṃ|

kālātipattivacanā, anāgatānukaḍḍhanaṃ|

tasmā aniyataṃ kālaṃ, kālātipattikaṃ vinā||

atītānāgatapac‍cu-ppan‍nikāhi vibhattihi|

sattamī sattamīyaiva, bhavatai na tu aṭṭhamī||

pañ‍camīsattamīnaṃ tu, pac‍cuppan‍navibhattiyaṃ|

saṅgaṇhanatthamaitāsaṃ, majjhai chaṭṭhī na vuc‍cati||

tathā pañ‍ca upādāya, bhavitabbañ‍ca chaṭṭhiyā|

pañ‍camiyā tu sā aisā, ‘‘chaṭṭhī’’ti na samīritā||

chaṭṭhībhāvamhi santaipi, ‘‘pañ‍camī’’ti vacau pana|

pañ‍camiyā vibhattiyā, pac‍cuppan‍navibhattiyaṃ||

saṅgaṇhanatthaṃ vuttanti, viñ‍ñātabbā vibhāvinā|

pañ‍camiṃ tu upādāya, sattamiyā vibhattiyā||

chaṭṭhiyā ca bhavitabbaṃ, na sā ‘‘chaṭṭhī’’ti īritā|

chaṭṭhiṃ pana upādāya, ‘‘sattamī’’tvaiva īritā||

majjhai chaṭṭhiṃ adassaitvā, aivaṃ tu kathanampi ca|

sattamiyā vibhattiyā, pac‍cuppan‍navibhattiyaṃ|

saṅgaṇhanatthaṃ vuttanti, adhippāyaṃ vibhāvayai||

sabhāvau haisa vatthūnaṃ, gambhīratthaisu attanau|

yaina kainacākāraina, adhippāyassa ñāpanaṃ||

yaj‍jaivaṃ  paṭhamaṃtītai-nāgatai ca vibhattiyau|

vatvā tatau pac‍cuppan‍nai, kathaitabbā vibhattiyau||

kac‍cāyanavhayai ganthai, kasmā aivaṃ na bhāsitā|

pac‍cuppan‍navibhatyauva, kasmā ādimhi bhāsitā?

yasmā vadanti vauhāra-pathai aitāva pāyatau|

tasmā bahuppayaugattaṃ, hautaitāsaṃ vibhattinaṃ||

ādaubahuppayaugauva, kathaitabbauti ñāyatau|

pac‍cuppan‍namhi sambhūtā, vibhatyauvāditau matā||

atītānāgataṃ vatvā, pac‍cuppan‍nai tatau paraṃ|

yasmā vuttamhi laukasmiṃ, hauti vācāsiliṭṭhatā||

tasmā siliṭṭhakathanai, atītādimapaikkhiya|

pañ‍camī sattamī caitā, vattamānāyanantaraṃ|

saṅgaṇhanatthamakkhātā, pac‍cuppan‍navibhattisu||

aittha hi yathā ‘‘mātāpitarau’’ti vuttai siliṭṭhakathanaṃ hauti, tasmiṃyaiva vacanai vipariyayaṃ katvā samāsavasaina ‘‘pitāmātarau’’ti vuttai siliṭṭhakathanaṃ na hauti, tasmā tādisī saddaracanā apūjanīyā, ‘‘pitā mātā ca mai daj‍ju’’nti pāṭhau pana byāsavasaina yathicchitappayaugattā pūjanīyau, aivamaiva ‘‘atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍na’’nti vuttai siliṭṭhakathanaṃ hauti, ‘‘atītapac‍cuppan‍nānāgata’’nti aivamādinā vuttai siliṭṭhakathanaṃ na hauti, tasmā tādisī saddaracanā apūjanīyā siyā| ‘‘atītārammaṇā pac‍cu-ppan‍nānāgatagaucarā’’ti vacanaṃ pana gāthābandhasukhatthaṃ yathicchitappayaugattā pūjanīyamaiva| ayamaittha pāḷi vaiditabbā –

‘‘yaṃkiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍na’’nti ca,

‘‘aikāyanaṃ  jātikhayantadassī,

maggaṃ pajānāti hitānukampī|

aitaina maggaina ataṃsu pubbai,

tarissanti yai ca taranti augha’’nti ca,

‘‘yai cabbhatītā sambuddhā, yai ca buddhā anāgatā|

yai caitarahi sambuddhā, bahūnaṃ saukanāsakā||

sabbai saddhammagarunau, vihaṃsu viharanti ca|

athaupi viharissanti, aisā buddhāna dhammatā’’ti ca

aivamanaikaisu saddappayaugaisu| idha yathicchitappayaugavasaina atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nakālikāsu aṭṭhasu vibhattīsu tissau pac‍cuppan‍nakālikā vibhattiyau ādimhi kathitā, tañ‍ca kathanaṃ tāsañ‍ñaiva vauhārapathai yaibhuyyaina pavattitau bahuppayaugatāñāpanatthaṃ| tāsu pana dvin‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ ‘‘pañ‍camīsattamī’’tisañ‍ñā siliṭṭhakathanicchāyaṃ kamaina vattabbā, atītānāgatakālikā vibhattiyau apaikkhitvā katā| ic‍caivaṃ

yathicchitappayaugaina, pac‍cuppan‍navibhattiyau|

tidhā katvāna ādimhi, kac‍cānaina udīritā||

ādimhi kathanaṃ tañ‍ca, tāsaṃ pāyaina vuttitau|

bahuppayaugabhāvassa, ñāpanatthanti niddisai||

atītādimapaikkhitvā, siliṭṭhakathanai dhuvaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍camī sattami’’c‍caiva, dvin‍naṃ nāmaṃ katanti ca|

kālātipattiṃ vaj‍jaitvā, idaṃ vacanamīritaṃ||

yadi aivaṃ ayaṃ dausau, āpaj‍jati na saṃsayau|

iti cai kauci bhāsaiyya, atthai akusalaunarau||

taikālikākhyātapadai, kālātipattiyā pana|

asaṅgahauva hautīti, ‘‘tan‍nā’’ti paṭisaidhayai||

taikālikākhyātapadai, na nau kālātipattiyā|

iṭṭhau asaṅgahau tattha, saṅgahauyaiva icchitau||

pañ‍camīsattamīsañ‍ñā, kālātipattikaṃ pana|

vibhattimanapaikkhitvā, katā ic‍caiva nau mati||

nānānayaṃ gahaitvāna,

pac‍caitabbaṃ tu sāratau|

yāya aisau rutau atthau,

tasmā aisā na dubbalā||

atthau labbhati pāsaṃsau,

yattha yattha yathā yathā|

tathā tathā gahaitabbau,

tattha tattha vibhāvinā||

vuttañhaitaṃ abhidhammaṭīkāyaṃ ‘‘yattha yattha yathā yathā atthau labbhati, tattha tattha tathā tathā gahaitabbau’’ti|

pañ‍camīsattamīsañ‍ñā, rūḷhīsañ‍ñāti kaicana|

na panaivaṃ gahaitabbaṃ, ajānitvā vadanti tai||

naisā purisasañ‍ñādi, jhala sañ‍ñādayau viya|

rūḷhiyā bhāsitā sañ‍ñā, bhūtainatthaina bhāsitā||

upanidhāya pañ‍ñatti, aisā sañ‍ñā yatau tatau|

anvatthasañ‍ñā ṭhapitā, paurāṇaihīti lakkhayai||

ic‍caivaṃ kālachak‍kaṃ tu, saṅkhaipaina tidhā mataṃ|

aitamatthañhi sandhāya, ‘‘yaṃ tikāla’’nti bhāsitaṃ||

ayamaittha kālachak‍kasaṅgahau|

aivaṃ  tidhā catudhā vā, chadhā vāpi sumaidhasau|

kālabhaidaṃ vibhāvaiyya, kālañ‍ñūhi vibhāvitaṃ||

atītānāgataṃ kālaṃ, visuṃ kālātipattikaṃ|

gahaitvā pañ‍cadhā hauti, aivañ‍cāpi vibhāvayai||

aittha nayauva ‘‘ajjhatta-bahiddhā vā’’ti pāḷiyaṃ|

atītānāgatakālī, vibhatti samudīritā||

ic‍caivaṃ sabbathāpi kālasaṅgahau samattau|

idāni viñ‍ñūnaṃ atthaggahaṇai kausal‍lajananatthaṃ pakaraṇantaravasainapi imasmiṃ pakaraṇai vattamānānantaraṃ vuttānaṃ āṇattiparikappakālikānaṃ ‘‘pañ‍camīsattamī’’tisaṅkhātānaṃ dvin‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ paṭipāṭiṭṭhapanai pakaraṇasaṃsandanaṃ kathayāma – kātantappakaraṇasmiñhi sak‍katabhāsānurūpaina dasadhā ākhyātavibhattiyau ṭhapitā, kac‍cāyanappakaraṇai pana māgadhabhāsānurūpaina aṭṭhadhā ṭhapitā, niruttiyañ‍ca pana māgadhabhāsānurūpainaiva atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nāṇattiparikappakālātipattivasaina chadhā ṭhapitā| taisu hi kātantai vattamānā, sattamī, pañ‍camī, hiyyattanī, aj‍jatanī, paraukkhā, svātanī, āsī, bhavissantī, kriyātipatti cāti dasadhā vibhattā| kac‍cāyanai pana vattamānā, pañ‍camī, sattamī, paraukkhā, hiyyattanī, aj‍jatanī, bhavissantī, kālātipatti cāti aṭṭhadhā vibhattā| iti aitaisu dvīsu kātantakac‍cāyanaisu vibhattiyau visadisāya paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapitā| kiñ‍cāpaittha visadisā paṭipāṭi, tathāpaitā niruttiyaṃ vuttātītādikālavibhāgavasaina aikatau saṃsandanti samainti kiñ‍ci visaisaṃ ṭhapaitvā|

kathaṃ? kātantai tāva hiyyattanī aj‍jatanī paraukkhā cāti imā tissau aikantaina atītakālikā, svātanī āsī bhavissanti cāti imā tissau aikantaina anāgatakālikā, vattamānā aikāyaiva pac‍cuppan‍nakālikā, sattamī pana pañ‍camī ca pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatakālavasaina dvikālikā ‘‘aj‍ja puñ‍ñaṃ karaiyya, svaipi karaiyya| aj‍ja gacchatu, svai vā gacchatū’’ti payaugārahattā| kriyātipatti aniyatakālikā ‘‘sau cai hiyyau yānaṃ alabhissā, agacchissā| sau cai aj‍ja anatthaṅgatai sūriyai yānaṃ alabhissā, agacchissā| sau cai svai yānaṃ alabhissā, agacchissā’’ti payaugārahattā| aivaṃ asaṅkaratau vavatthapaitabbaṃ|

aivaṃ vavatthapaitvā ayamamhaihi vuc‍camānau nayau sādhukaṃ sal‍lakkhaitabbau| kathaṃ? hiyyattanaj‍jatanīparaukkhāsvātanyāsībhavissantivasaina aikantātītānāgatakālikā vibhattiyau cha, vattamānavasaina aikantapac‍cuppan‍nakālikā vibhatti aikāyaiva, sā paṭipāṭiyā gaṇiyamānā sattamaṃ ṭhānaṃ bhajati| aivaṃ aitasmiṃ vattamānāsaṅkhātai sattamaṭṭhānai pakkhipituṃ niruttinayaina ‘‘parikappakālikā’’ti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ satthanayaina ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatakālikā’’tivattabbaṃ aikaṃ vibhattiṃ sattamībhūtāya vattamānāya samānaṭṭhānattā sattamīsañ‍ñaṃ katvā ṭhapaisi| tatau punadaiva svātanyāsībhavissantivasaina aikantānāgatakālikā tissau vibhattiyau gaṇaitvā taṃ pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatakālikaṃ ‘‘sattamī’’ti laddhasañ‍ñaṃ vibhattiṃ anāgatakālikabhāvaina tāhi tīhi saddhiṃ samānaṭṭhānattā catutthaṃ katvā niruttinayaina ‘‘āṇattikālikā’’ti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ satthanayaina ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatakālikā’’ti vattabbaṃ aikaṃ vibhattiṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ pūraṇaina pañ‍camīsañ‍ñaṃ katvā ṭhapaisi|

kriyātipattiyā  pana aniyatakālikattā taṃ vaj‍jaitvā ayaṃ vinicchayau katau, sau ca khau niruttinayaṃyaiva nissāya| ayaṃ tāva kātantai vattamānānantaraṃ vuttānaṃ sattamīpañ‍camīnaṃ anvatthasañ‍ñaṃ icchantānaṃ amhākaṃ ruci, aisā saddhammavidūhi garūhi appaṭik‍kausitā anumatā sampaṭicchitā ‘‘aivamaivaṃ āvusau, aivamaivaṃ āvusau’’ti| vaiyyākaraṇaihipi appaṭik‍kausitā anumatā sampaṭicchitā ‘‘aivamaivaṃ bhantai, aivamaivaṃ bhantai’’ti| aivaṃ sabbaihipi taihi pubbācariyaihi abbhanumauditā appaṭik‍kausitā|

kac‍cāyanappakaraṇai pana buddhavacanānurūpaina aṭṭhadhā vibhattīnaṃ vuttattā vattamānāvibhatti pañ‍camaṭṭhānai ṭhitā| kathaṃ? paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanībhavissantivasaina aikantātītānāgatakālikā catassau vibhattiyau, vattamānavasaina aikantapac‍cuppan‍nakālikā vibhatti aikāyaiva, sā paṭipāṭiyā gaṇiyamānā pañ‍camaṃ ṭhānaṃ bhajati| aivaṃ aitasmiṃ vattamānāsaṅkhātai pañ‍camaṭṭhānai pakkhipituṃ niruttinayaina ‘‘āṇattikālikā’’ti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ ‘‘anuttakālikā’’ti vuttaṃ vibhattiṃ pañ‍camībhūtāya vattamānāya samānaṭṭhānattā pañ‍camīsañ‍ñaṃ katvā ṭhapaisi| tatau paraṃ taṃ pañ‍camaṃ chaṭṭhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā paraukkhā hiyyattanī aj‍jatanī bhavissantī vattamānā pañ‍camīti aivaṃ gaṇanāvasaina cha vibhattiyau upādāya niruttinayaina ‘‘parikappakālikā’’ti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ ‘‘anuttakālikā’’ti vuttaṃ vibhattiṃ sattan‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ pūraṇaina sattamīsañ‍ñaṃ katvā ṭhapaisi|

kālātipattiyā pana atītānāgatakālikattā taṃ vaj‍jaitvā ayaṃ vinicchayau katau, sau ca khau niruttinayaṃyaiva nissāya| ayaṃ kac‍cāyanai vattamānānantaraṃ vuttānaṃ pañ‍camīsattamīnaṃ anvatthasañ‍ñaṃ icchantānaṃ amhākaṃ ruci, aisā ca saddhammavidūhi garūhi appaṭik‍kausitā anumatā sampaṭicchitā  ‘‘aivamaivaṃ āvusau, aivamaivaṃ āvusau’’ti| vaiyyākaraṇaihipi appaṭik‍kausitā anumatā sampaṭicchitā ‘‘aivamaivaṃ bhantai, aivamaivaṃ bhantai’’ti| aivaṃ sabbaihipi taihi pubbācariyaihi abbhanumauditā appaṭik‍kausitā|

yasmā hi kātantakac‍cāyanāni añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ visadisavibhattik‍kamānipi antaraina kiñ‍ci visaisaṃ niruttiyaṃ vuttātītādikālavibhāgavasainaikajjhaṃ saṃsandanti samainti, tasmā niruttinayañ‍ñaiva sāratau gahaitvā pañ‍camīsattamīvibhattīnaṃ anvatthasañ‍ñāparikappanai amhākaṃ ruci pubbācariyaihi abbhanumauditā appaṭik‍kausitā, tasmā aiva yau kauci imaṃ vādaṃ madditvā añ‍ñaṃ vādaṃ patiṭṭhāpaituṃ sakkhissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ayañhi nayau atīva sukhumau duddasau ca paramāṇuriva, dukkhaugāḷhau ca mahāgahanamiva, atigambhīrau ca mahāsamuddau viya, tasmā imissaṃ saddanītiyaṃ saddhāsampan‍naihi kulaputtaihi sāsanaupakāratthaṃ yaugau suṭṭhu karaṇīyau| tathā hi idha katayaugaihi nāmākhyātādīsu catūsu padaisu uppan‍navādā paravādinau jitāva haunti|

muninā munināgaina, duṭṭhā pabbajitā jitā|

yathā yathā asaddhamma-pūraṇā pūraṇādayau||

tathā tathāgatādāyā-nugāyaṃ saddanītiyaṃ|

katayaugaihipi jitā, savanti paravādinauti||

ayaṃ pañ‍camīsattamīnaṃ paṭipāṭiṭṭhapanai pakaraṇasaṃsandanā|

atha vattamānādīnaṃ vacanatthaṃ kathayāma – tattha vattamānāti kainaṭṭhaina vattamānā? vattamānakālavacanaṭṭhaina| pac‍cuppan‍nabhāvaina hi vattatīti vattamānau, pac‍cuppan‍nakriyāsaṅkhātau kālau| tabbācakavasaina vattamānau kālau aitissā atthīti ayaṃ ti antiādivibhatti vattamānā| tathā  hi ‘‘gacchati daivadattau’’ti aittha daivadattassa pac‍cuppan‍naṃ gamanakriyaṃ vibhattibhūtau tisaddauyaiva vadati, tasmā tabbācakavasaina vattamānau kālau aitissā atthīti vattamānāti vuc‍cati| pañ‍camīti kainaṭṭhaina pañ‍camī? pañ‍camaṃ vattamānaṭṭhānaṃ gamanaṭṭhaina, pañ‍can‍nañ‍ca saṅkhyānaṃ pūraṇaṭṭhaina| tathā hi niyaugā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nakālikānaṃ paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanībhavissantīvattamānāsaṅkhātānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ vibhattīnamantarai pañ‍camībhūtāya vattamānāya sayampi pac‍cuppan‍nakālikabhāvaina samānaṭṭhānattā pañ‍camaṃ vattamānaṭṭhānaṃ gacchatīti pañ‍camī| yathā nadantī gacchatīti nadī| tathā niyaugā atītānāgatakālikā paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanībhavissantīsaṅkhātā catassau vibhattiyau upādāya sayampi vattamānāvibhatti viya pañ‍can‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ pūraṇīti pañ‍camī| sattamīti kainaṭṭhaina sattamī? sattan‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ pūraṇaṭṭhaina| tathā hi atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nakālikā paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanībhavissantīvattamānāpañ‍camīsaṅkhātā cha vibhattiyau upādāya sayampi pac‍cuppan‍nakālikā hutvā sattan‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ pūraṇīti sattamī|

paraukkhāti kainaṭṭhaina paraukkhā? paraukkhai bhavāti atthaina| tathā hi cakkhādindriyasaṅkhātassa akkhassa parau tiraubhāvau paraukkhaṃ, tabbācakabhāvaina paraukkhai bhavāti paraukkhā| hiyyattanīti kainaṭṭhaina hiyyattanī? hiyyau pabhuti atītai kālai bhavā tabbācakabhāvaināti atthaina| aj‍jatanīti kainaṭṭhaina aj‍jatanī? aj‍ja pabhuti atītai kālai bhavā tabbācakabhāvaināti atthaina| bhavissantīti kainaṭṭhaina bhavissantī? ‘‘aivaṃ anāgatai bhavissatī’’ti atthaṃ pakāsaintī aiti gacchatīti atthaina| kālātipattīti kainaṭṭhaina kālātipatti? kālassātipatanavacanaṭṭhaina| tathā hi kālassa atipatanaṃ ac‍cayau atik‍kamitvā pavatti kālātipatti, labhitabbassa atthassa nipphattirahitaṃ kriyātik‍kamanaṃ| kālauti caittha kriyā adhippaitā| karaṇaṃ kārau, kārau aiva kālau rakārassa lakāraṃ katvā uc‍cāraṇavasaina| ayaṃ pana vibhatti tabbācakattā kālātipattīti| ayaṃ pana vattamānādīnaṃ vacanatthavibhāvanā|

vippakiṇṇavividhanayai, saṃkiṇṇalakkhaṇadharavarasāsanai|

sumatimativaḍḍhanatthaṃ, kathitau pakiṇṇakavinicchayau||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

pakiṇṇakavinicchayau nāma

tatiyau paricchaidau|

4. bhūdhātumayanāmikarūpavibhāga

‘‘bhū sattāya’’nti dhātussa, rūpamākhyātasañ‍ñitaṃ|

tyādyantaṃ lapitaṃ nāna-ppakāraihi anākulaṃ||

syādyantaṃ, dāni tassaiva, rūpaṃ nāmikasavhayaṃ|

bhāsissaṃ bhāsitatthaisu, paṭubhāvāya sautunaṃ||

yadatthai’ttani nāmaiti, para’matthaisu vā sayaṃ|

namatīti tadāhaṃsu, nāmaṃ iti vibhāvinau||

nāmaṃ nāmikamic‍catra, aikamaivaitthatau bhavai|

tadaivaṃ nāmikaṃ ñai yyaṃ, saliṅgaṃ savibhattikaṃ||

satvābhidhānaṃ liṅganti, itthipumanapuṃsakaṃ|

vibhattītidha sattaiva, tattha caṭṭha pavuc‍carai||

paṭhamā dutiyā tatiyā, catutthī pañ‍camī tathā|

chaṭṭhī ca sattamī cāti, haunti satta vibhattiyau||

liṅgatthai paṭhamā sāyaṃ, bhin‍nā dvaidhā siyau iti|

kammatthai dutiyā sāpi, bhin‍nā aṃ yau iti dvidhā||

karaṇai  tatiyā sāpi, bhin‍nā nā hi iti dvidhā|

sampadānai catutthī sā, bhin‍nā dvaidhā sa naṃ iti||

apādānai pañ‍camī sā, bhin‍nā dvaidhā smā hi iti|

chaṭṭhī sāmimhi sā cāpi, bhin‍nā dvaidhā sa naṃ iti||

aukāsai sattamī sāpi, bhin‍nā dvaidhā smiṃsu iti|

āmantanaṭṭhamī sāyaṃ, siyauyaivāti cuddasa||

vacanadvayasaṃyuttā, aikaikā tā vibhattiyau|

satvamitiha viñ‍ñaiyyau, atthau sau dabbasañ‍ñitau||

yau karautisa kattātu, taṃ kammaṃ yaṃ karauti vā|

kubbatai yaina vā tantu, karaṇaṃ iti sañ‍ñitaṃ||

daiti raucati vā yassa, sampadānanti taṃ mataṃ|

yataupaiti bhayaṃ vā taṃ, apādānanti kittitaṃ||

yassāyattau samūhauvā, taṃ vai sāmīti daisitaṃ|

yasmiṃ karauti kiriyaṃ, tadaukāsanti sadditaṃ||

yadālapati taṃ vatthu, āmantanamudīritaṃ|

saddainābhimukhīkārau, vij‍jamānassa vā pana||

vinā ālapanatthaṃ liṅgatthādīsu paṭhamādivibhattuppatti upalakkhaṇavasaina vuttāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

idamaittha niruttilakkhaṇaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ – pac‍cattavacanai paṭhamā vibhatti bhavati, upayaugavacanai dutiyā vibhatti bhavati, karaṇavacanai tatiyā vibhatti bhavati, sampadānavacanai catutthī vibhatti bhavati, nissak‍kavacanai pañ‍camī vibhatti bhavati, sāmivacanai chaṭṭhī vibhatti bhavati, bhummavacanai sattamī vibhatti bhavati, āmantanavacanai aṭṭhamī vibhatti bhavati| tatruddānaṃ –

pac‍cattamupayaugañ‍ca, karaṇaṃ sampadāniyaṃ|

nissak‍kaṃ sāmivacanaṃ, bhummamālapanaṭṭhamaṃ||

tatra  pac‍cattavacanaṃ nāma tividhaliṅgavavatthānagatānaṃ itthipumanapuṃsakānaṃ pac‍cattasabhāvaniddaisatthau| upayaugavacanaṃ nāma yau yaṃ karauti, taina tadupayuttaparidīpanatthau| karaṇavacanaṃ nāma taj‍jāpakatanibbattakaparidīpanatthau| sampadānavacanaṃ nāma tappadānaparidīpanatthau| nissak‍kavacanaṃ nāma tan‍nissaṭatadapagamaparidīpanatthau| sāmivacanaṃ nāma tadissaraparidīpanatthau| bhummavacanaṃ nāma tappatiṭṭhāparidīpanatthau| āmantanavacanaṃ nāma tadāmantanaparidīpanatthau| aivaṃ ñatvā payaugāni asammuyhantaina yaujaitabbāni|

bhūtau, bhāvakau, bhavau, abhavau, bhāvau, abhāvau, sabhāvau, sabbhāvau, sambhavau, pabhavau, pabhāvau, anubhavau, ānubhāvau, parābhavau, vibhavau, pātubhāvau, āvibhāvau, tiraubhāvau, vinābhāvau, sautthibhāvau, atthibhāvau, natthibhāvauti aukārantapul‍liṅgaṃ|

abhibhavitā, paribhavitā, anubhavitā, samanubhavitā, bhāvitā, pac‍canubhavitāti ākārantapul‍liṅgaṃ|

bhavaṃ, parābhavaṃ, paribhavaṃ, abhibhavaṃ, anubhavaṃ, samanubhavaṃ, pac‍canubhavaṃ, pabhavaṃ, appabhavanti niggahītantapul‍liṅgaṃ|

dhanabhūti, siribhūti, sautthibhūti, suvatthibhūtīti ikārantapul‍liṅgaṃ|

bhāvī, vibhāvī, sambhāvī, paribhāvīti īkārantapul‍liṅgaṃ|

sayambhū, pabhū, abhibhū, vibhū, adhibhū, patibhū, gautrabhū, vatrabhū, parābhibhū, rūpābhibhū, saddābhibhū, gandhābhibhū, rasābhibhū, phauṭṭhabbābhibhū, dhammābhibhū, sabbābhibhūti ūkārantapul‍liṅgaṃ|

imānaittha chabbidhāni pul‍liṅgāni bhūdhātumayāni uddiṭṭhāni|

ukārantaṃ  pul‍liṅgaṃtu bhūdhātumayamappasiddhaṃ, añ‍ñadhātumayaṃ panukārantapul‍liṅgaṃ pasiddhaṃ ‘‘bhikkhu, haitu’’iti| taina saddhiṃ sattavidhāni pul‍liṅgāni haunti, sabbānaitāni sabhāvatauyaiva pul‍liṅgānīti daṭṭhabbāni| aittha sattauti atthavācakau bhūtasaddauyaiva niyaugā pul‍liṅgantipi daṭṭhabbau|

yai pana ‘‘yau dhammau bhūtau, yā dhammajāti bhūtā, yaṃ dhammajātaṃ bhūta’’nti aivaṃ liṅgattayai yaujanārahattā aniyataliṅgā añ‍ñaipi bhūtaparābhūtasambhūtasaddādayau sandissanti pāvacanavarai, taipi nānaupasagganipātapadaihi yaujanavasaina saddaracanāyaṃ sukhumatthaggahaṇai ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ kausal‍lajananatthaṃ niyatapul‍liṅgaisu pakkhipitvā dassaissāma|

saiyyathidaṃ? bhūtau, parābhūtau, sambhūtau, vibhūtau, pātubhūtau, āvibhūtau, tiraubhūtau, vinābhūtau, bhabbau, paribhūtau, abhibhūtau, adhibhūtau, addhabhūtau, anubhūtau, samanubhūtau, pac‍canubhūtau, bhāvitau, sambhāvitau, vibhāvitau, paribhāvitau, anuparibhūtau, paribhavitabbau, paribhautabbau, paribhavanīyau, abhibhavitabbau, abhibhautabbau, abhibhavanīyau, adhibhavitabbau, adhibhautabbau, adhibhavanīyau, anubhavitabbau, anubhautabbau, anubhavanīyau, samanubhavitabbau, samanubhautabbau, samanubhavanīyau, pac‍canubhavitabbau, pac‍canubhautabbau, pac‍canubhavanīyau, bhāvaitabbau, bhāvanīyau, sambhāvaitabbau, sambhāvanīyau, vibhāvaitabbau, vibhāvanīyau, paribhāvaitabbau, paribhāvanīyau, bhavamānau, vibhavamānau, paribhavamānau, abhibhavamānau, anubhavamānau, samanubhavamānau, pac‍canubhavamānau, anubhauntau, samanubhauntau, pac‍canubhauntau, sambhauntau, abhisambhauntau, bhāvaintau, sambhāvaintau, vibhāvaintau, paribhāvaintau, paribhaviyamānau, paribhuyyamānau, abhibhaviyamānau, abhibhūyamānau, anubhaviyamānau, anubhuyyamānau, samanubhaviyamānau, samanubhuyyamānau, pac‍canubhaviyamānau, pac‍canubhuyyamānauti imāni niyatapul‍liṅgaisu pakkhittaliṅgāni| aivamaukārantādivasaina chabbidhāni pul‍liṅgāni bhūdhātumayāni pakāsitāni| ayaṃ tāva pul‍liṅgavasaina udāharaṇuddaisau|

bhāvikā, bhāvanā, vibhāvanā, sambhāvanā, paribhāvanāti ākārantaitthiliṅgaṃ|

bhūmi, bhūti, vibhūti| ikārantaitthiliṅgaṃ|

bhūrī, bhūtī, bhautī, vibhāvinī, parivibhāvinī, sambhāvinī, pātubhavantī, pātubhauntī, paribhavantī, paribhauntī, abhibhavantī, abhibhauntī, adhibhavantī, adhibhauntī, anubhavantī, anubhauntī, samanubhavantī, samanubhauntī, pac‍canubhavantī, pac‍canubhauntī, abhisambhavantī, abhisambhauntīti īkārantaitthiliṅgaṃ|

bhū, abhū| ūkārantaitthiliṅgaṃ|

imānaittha catubbidhāni itthiliṅgāni bhūdhātumayāni uddiṭṭhāni|

ukārantitthiliṅgaṃ bhūdhātumayamappasiddhaṃ, añ‍ñadhātumayaṃ pana ukārantitthiliṅgaṃ pasiddhaṃ ‘‘dhātu, dhainu’’iti| taina saddhiṃ pañ‍cavidhāni itthiliṅgāni haunti, aukārantassa vā gausaddassa itthiliṅgabhāvai taina saddhiṃ chabbidhānipi haunti, sabbānaitāni sabhāvatauyaivitthiliṅgānīti daṭṭhabbāni| aitthāpi aniyataliṅgā bhūtaparābhūtasambhūtasaddādayau itthiliṅgavasaina yuj‍jantai|

kathaṃ? bhūtā, parābhūtā, sambhūtāti sabbaṃ vitthāratau gahaitabbaṃ ‘‘anubhauntau samanubhauntau’’tiādīni nava padāni  vaj‍jaitvā| tāni hi īkārantavasaina yaujitāni| imāni niyataliṅgaisu pakkhittaliṅgāni| aivaṃ ākārantādivasaina catubbidhāni itthiliṅgāni bhūdhātumayāni pakāsitāni| ayaṃ itthiliṅgavasaina udāharaṇuddaisau|

bhūtaṃ, mahābhūtaṃ, bhavittaṃ, bhūnaṃ, bhavanaṃ, parābhavanaṃ, sambhavanaṃ, vibhavanaṃ, pātubhavanaṃ, āvibhavanaṃ, tiraubhavanaṃ, vinābhavanaṃ, sautthibhavanaṃ, paribhavanaṃ, abhibhavanaṃ, adhibhavanaṃ, anubhavanaṃ, samanubhavanaṃ, pac‍canubhavananti niggahītantanapuṃsakaliṅgaṃ|

atthavibhāvi, dhammavibhāvi| ikārantanapuṃsakaliṅgaṃ|

gautrabhu, cittasahabhu, nacittasahabhu| ukārantanapuṃsakaliṅgaṃ|

sabbānaitāni sabhāvatauyaiva napuṃsakaliṅgānīti daṭṭhabbāni| aittha sattabhūtarūpavācakau bhūtasaddauyaiva niyaugā napuṃsakaliṅgautipi daṭṭhabbaṃ| aitthāpi aniyataliṅgā bhūta parābhūta sambhūtasaddādayau napuṃsakaliṅgavasaina yuj‍jantai|

kathaṃ? bhūtaṃ, parābhūtaṃ, sambhūtaṃ, vibhūtaṃ| paiyyālau| samanubhavamānaṃ, pac‍canubhavamānaṃ, anubhauntaṃ, anubhavantaṃ, samanubhauntaṃ, samanubhavantaṃ, pac‍canubhauntaṃ, pac‍canubhavantaṃ, sambhauntaṃ, sambhavantaṃ, abhisambhauntaṃ, abhisambhavantaṃ, pātubhauntaṃ, pātubhavantaṃ, paribhauntaṃ, paribhavantaṃ, abhibhauntaṃ, abhibhavantaṃ, adhibhauntaṃ, adhibhavantaṃ, bhāvaintaṃ, sambhāvaintaṃ, vibhāvaintaṃ, paribhāvaintaṃ, paribhāviyamānaṃ, paribhuyyamānaṃ, paiyyālau| pac‍canubhaviyamānaṃ, pac‍canubhuyyamānanti imāni niyatanapuṃsakaliṅgaisu pakkhittaliṅgāni| aivaṃ niggahītantādivasaina tividhāni napuṃsakaliṅgāni bhūdhātumayāni pakāsitāni| ayaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgavasaina udāharaṇuddaisau, aivaṃ pul‍liṅgādivasaina liṅgattayaṃ bhūdhātumayamuddiṭṭhaṃ|

aittha mai appasiddhāti, yai yai saddā pakāsitā|

tai tai pāḷippadaisaisu, maggitabbā vibhāvinā||

au, ā, bindu, i, ī, u, ū-antimai sattadhā ṭhitā|

ñai yyā pul‍liṅgabhaidāti, niruttañ‍ñūhi bhāsitā||

ā ivaṇṇau cuvaṇṇau ca, pañ‍ca antā sarūpatau|

itthibhaidāti viñ‍ñaiyyā, aukārantaina chāpi vā||

bindu, i, u-imai antā, tayau ñaiyyā vibhāvinā|

napuṃsakappabhaidāti, niruttañ‍ñūhi bhāsitā||

antā sattaiva pul‍liṅgai, itthiyaṃ pañ‍ca vā cha vā|

napuṃsakai tayau aivaṃ, dasa pañ‍cahi chabbidhā||

yasmā panaittha ‘‘bhūtau’’tiādayau saddā nibbacanābhidhaiyyakathanatthasādhakavacanapariyāyavacanatthuddhāravasaina vuc‍camānā pākaṭā haunti suviñ‍ñaiyyā ca, tasmā imaisaṃ nibbacanādīni yathāsambhavaṃ vakkhāma viñ‍ñūnaṃ tuṭṭhijananatthañ‍caiva sautārānamatthaisu paṭutarabuddhipaṭilābhāya ca| tatra bhūtauti khandhapātubhāvaina bhavatīti bhūtau, idaṃ tāva nibbacanaṃ| ‘‘bhūtau’’ti sabbasaṅgāhakavasaina sattau vuc‍cati, idamabhidhaiyyakathanaṃ| ‘‘yau ca kālaghasau bhūtau| sabbaiva nikkhipissanti, bhūtā laukai samussaya’’nti ca idamaitassa atthassa sādhakavacanaṃ| atha vā bhūtauti aivaṃnāmakau amanussajātiyau sattavisaisau, idamabhidhaiyyakathanaṃ| ‘‘bhūtavij‍jā, bhūtavaij‍jau, bhūtaviggahitau’’ti ca idamaitassa atthassa sādhakavacanaṃ| yañ‍ca pana ‘‘sattau mac‍cau pajā’’tiādikaṃ tattha tattha āgataṃ vacanaṃ, idaṃ sattauti  atthavācakassa bhūtasaddassa pariyāyavacanaṃ| yañ‍ca niddaisapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘mac‍cauti sattau narau mānavau pausau puggalau jīvau jagu jantu hindagu manujau’’ti āgataṃ, idampi pariyāyavacanamaiva| tāni sabbāni piṇḍaitvā vuc‍cantai –

sattau mac‍cau janau bhūtau, pāṇau hindagu puggalau|

jantu jīvau jagu yakkhau, pāṇī daihī tathāgatau||

sattavau mātiyau laukau, manujau mānavau narau|

pausau sarīrīti pumai, bhūtamiti napuṃsakai||

pajāti itthiyaṃ vuttau, liṅgatau, na ca atthatau|

aivaṃ tiliṅgikā haunti, saddā sattābhidhānakā||

yau sau jaṅghāya ulati, sau sattau jaṅghalau idha|

pāṇadaihābhidhānaihi, sattanāmaṃ papañ‍citaṃ||

imasmiṃ pakaraṇai ‘‘pariyāyavacana’’nti ca ‘‘abhidhāna’’nti ca ‘‘saṅkhā’’tiādīni ca aikatthāni adhippaitāni, atthuddhāravasaina pana bhūtasaddau pañ‍cakkhandhāmanussadhātusassatavij‍jamānakhīṇāsavasattarukkhādīsu dissati, tappayaugau upari atthattikavibhāgai āvibhavissati| bhāvakauti bhāvaitīti bhāvakau, idaṃ nibbacanaṃ| yau bhāvanaṃ karauti, sau bhāvakau| idamabhidhaiyyakathanaṃ| ‘‘bhāvakau nipakau dhīrau’’ti idamaitassa atthassa sādhakavacanaṃ| ‘‘bhāvakau bhāvanāpasutau bhāvanāpayuttau bhāvanāsampan‍nau’’ti idaṃ pariyāyavacanaṃ| imāni ‘‘bhūtau bhāvakau’’ti dvai padāni suddhakattuhaitukattuvasaina vuttānīti| itau paraṃ nayānusāraina suviñ‍ñaiyyattā ‘‘idaṃ nibbacana’’nti ca ādīni  avatvā katthaci atthasādhakavacanaṃ pariyāyavacanaṃ atthuddhārañ‍ca yathārahaṃ dassaissāma| taisu hi sabbattha dassitaisu ganthavitthārau siyā, tasmā yaisamatthau uttānau, taisampi padānamabhidhaiyyaṃ na kathaissāma, nibbacanamattamaiva naisaṃ kathaissāma| yaisaṃ pana gambhīrau atthau, taisamabhidhaiyyaṃ kathaissāma|

bhavanaṃ bhavau, bhavau vuc‍cati vuddhi| bhūsaddassa atthātisayayaugatau vaḍḍhanaipi dissamānattā bhavanaṃ vaḍḍhananti katvā| ‘‘bhavau ca rañ‍ñau abhavau ca rañ‍ñau’’ti idaṃ vuddhiatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| atha vā bhavauti vuc‍cati sassataṃ| ‘‘sassatau attā ca laukau cā’’ti hi sassatavasaina pavattā diṭṭhi sassatadiṭṭhi, tasmā bhavadiṭṭhī’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| tathā bhavauti bhavadiṭṭhi, bhavati sassataṃ tiṭṭhatīti pavattanatau sassatadiṭṭhi bhavadiṭṭhi nāma| bhavadiṭṭhi hi uttarapadalaupaina bhavauti vuc‍cati| ‘‘bhavaina bhavassa vippamaukkhamāhaṃsū’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| aitthāyaṃ pāḷivacanatthau – aikac‍cai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavadiṭṭhiyā vā kāmabhavādinā vā sabbabhavatau vimuttiṃ saṃsāravisuddhiṃ kathayiṃsūti| atha vā bhavanti vaḍḍhanti sattā aitaināti bhavauti atthaina sampattipuñ‍ñāni bhavauti ca vuc‍canti| ‘‘itibhavābhavatañ‍ca vītivattau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| aittha panāyaṃ pāḷivacanatthau – bhavauti sampatti, abhavauti vipatti| tathā bhavauti vuddhi, abhavauti hāni| bhavauti sassataṃ, abhavauti ucchaidau| bhavauti puñ‍ñaṃ, abhavauti pāpaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ vītivattauti|

sahaukāsā  khandhāpi bhavau| ‘‘kāmabhavau rūpabhavau’’ ic‍caivamādi aitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| aittha pana khandhā ‘‘yau pañ‍ñāyati, sau sarūpaṃ labhatī’’ti katvā ‘‘bhavati avij‍jātaṇhādisamudayā nirantaraṃ samudaitī’’ti atthaina vā ‘‘bhavā’’ti vuc‍canti| aukāsau pana ‘‘bhavanti jāyanti aittha sattā nāmarūpadhammā cā’’ti atthaina ‘‘bhavau’’ti| apica kammabhavaupi bhavau, upapattibhavaupi bhavau| ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau duvidhaina atthi kammabhavau, atthi upapattibhavau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| tattha kammamaiva bhavau kammabhavau| tathā upapatti aiva bhavau upapattibhavau| aitthūpapatti bhavatīti bhavau, kammaṃ pana yathā sukhakāraṇattā ‘‘sukhau buddhānamuppādau’’ti vuttau| aivaṃ bhavakāraṇattā phalavauhāraina bhavauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā bhāvanalakkhaṇattā bhāvaitīti bhavau| kiṃ bhāvaiti? upapattiṃ| iti upapattiṃ bhāvaitīti bhavauti vuc‍cati| bhāvaitītimassa ca nibbattaitīti haitukattuvasainatthau| atha vā ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jātī’’ti vacanatau bhavati aitaināti bhavauti kammabhavau vuc‍cati|

‘‘khandhānañ‍ca paṭipāṭi, dhātuāyatanāna ca|

abbaucchin‍naṃ vattamānā, saṃsārauti pavuc‍catī’’ti

vuttalakkhaṇau saṃsāraupi bhavau| ‘‘bhavai dukkhaṃ bhavadukkhaṃ, bhavai saṃsarantau’’ti imānaitassatthassa sādhakāni vacanāni| tatra kainaṭṭhaina saṃsārau bhavauti kathīyati? bhavati aittha sattasammuti khandhādipaṭipāṭisaṅkhātai dhammapuñ‍jasminti atthaina| idaṃ bhavasaddassa bhāvakattukaraṇādhikaraṇasādhanavasainatthakathanaṃ|

aittha bhavasaddassa atthuddhāraṃ vadāma –

vuddhisampattipuñ‍ñāni, khandhā saukāsasañ‍ñitā|

saṃsārau sassatañ‍caitaṃ, bhavasaddaina sadditaṃ||

bhavataṇhā bhavadiṭṭhi, upapattibhavau tathā|

kammabhavau ca sabbantaṃ, bhavasaddaina sadditaṃ||

bhavataṇhābhavadiṭṭhi-dvayaṃ katthaci pāḷiyaṃ|

uttarapadalaupaina, bhavasaddaina sadditaṃ||

abhavauti na bhavau abhavau|

vipatti hāni ucchaidau, pāpañ‍caiva catubbidhā|

imai abhavasaddaina, atthā vuc‍canti sāsanai||

bhāvauti ajjhāsayau, yau ‘‘adhippāyau’’tipi vuc‍cati| ‘‘thīnaṃbhāvau durājānau| nāmac‍cau rājabhariyāsu, bhāvaṃ kubbaitha paṇḍitau| hadayaṅgatabhāvaṃ pakāsaitī’’ti aivamādi aitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| apica vatthudhammaupi bhāvau| ‘‘bhāvasaṅkaitasiddhīna’’nti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ, cittampi bhāvau| ‘‘ac‍cāhitaṃ kammaṃ karausi luddaṃ, bhāvai ca tai kusalaṃ natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| kriyāpibhāvau| ‘‘bhāvalakkhaṇaṃ bhāvasattamī’’ti ca idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| apica bhāvauti sattavaivacananti bhaṇanti, dhātu vā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| tattha ajjhāsayau ca vatthudhammau ca cittañ‍ca sattau cāti imai bhavatīti bhāvau| tathā pana bhāvaitīti bhāvau, kriyā  tu bhavananti bhāvau| sā ca bhavanagamanapacanādivasainānaikavidhā| apica bhāvarūpampi bhāvau, yaṃ itthibhāvau pumbhāvau itthindriyanti ca vuc‍cati| tatrāyaṃ vacanatthau – ‘‘itthī’’ti vā ‘‘purisau’’ti vā bhavati aitaina cittaṃ abhidhānañ‍cāti bhāvau|

nattanaumatiyā aitaṃ, nibbacanamudāhaṭaṃ|

pubbācariyasīhānaṃ, mataṃ nissāya māhaṭaṃ||

vuttañhaitaṃ paurāṇaihi ‘‘itthiyā bhāvau itthibhāvau, itthīti vā bhavati aitaina cittaṃ abhidhānañ‍cāti itthibhāvau’’ti, tasmā pumbhāvauti aitthāpi pumassa bhāvau pumbhāvau, pumāti vā bhavati aitaina cittaṃ abhidhānañ‍cāti pumbhāvauti nibbacanaṃ samadhigantabbaṃ| idaṃ bhāvasaddassa kattubhāvakaraṇasādhanavasainatthakathanaṃ|

abhāvauti na bhāvauti abhāvau, kau sau? suñ‍ñatā natthitā| sabhāvauti attanau bhāvau sabhāvau, attanau pakati ic‍caivatthau| atha vā sabhāvauti dhammānaṃ sati atthasambhavai yau kauci sarūpaṃ labhati, tassa bhāvau lakkhaṇamiti sañ‍ñitau namanaruppanakakkhaḷaphusanādiākārau ic‍caivatthau| ‘‘sāmañ‍ñaṃ vā sabhāvau vā, dhammānaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ mata’’nti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| apica sabhāvauti salakkhaṇau paramatthadhammau| kainaṭṭhaina? saha bhāvaināti atthaina| sabbhāvauti sataṃ bhāvau sabbhāvau, sappurisadhammau ic‍caivatthau| atha vā attanau bhāvau sabbhāvau| ‘‘gāhāpayanti sabbhāva’’nti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| saṃvij‍jamānau vā bhāvau sabbhāvau| ‘‘aivaṃ gahaṇasabbhāvau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ  vacanaṃ| idaṃ sabhāva sabbhāvasaddānaṃ bhāvasādhanavasainatthakathanaṃ|

sambhavauti sambhavanaṃ sambhavau, sambhavanakriyā, yutti vā| yutti hi sambhavauti vuc‍cati ‘‘sambhavau gahaṇassa kāraṇa’’ntiādīsu| atha vā sambhavati aitasmāti sambhavau| yatau hi yaṃ kiñ‍ci sambhavati, sau sambhavau| pabhavauti pabhavanaṃ pabhavau, acchin‍natā, pabhavati aitasmāti vā pabhavau| yatau hi yaṃ kiñ‍ci pabhavati, sau pabhavau| imai pana sambhavapabhavasaddā katthaci samānatthā katthaci bhin‍natthāti vaiditabbā| kathaṃ? sambhavasaddau hi bhavanakriyampi vadati yuttimpi pañ‍ñattimpi sambhavarūpampi pac‍cayatthampi, pabhavasaddau pana bhavanakriyampi vadati nadippabhavampi pac‍cayatthampi, tasmā pac‍cayatthaṃ vaj‍jaitvā bhin‍natthāti gahaitabbā, pac‍cayatthaina pana samānatthāti gahaitabbā| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘pac‍cayau haitu nidānaṃ kāraṇaṃ sambhavau pabhavautiādi atthatau aikaṃ, byañ‍janatau nāna’’nti|

‘‘mūlaṃ haitu nidānañ‍ca, sambhavau pabhavau tathā|

samuṭṭhānāhārārammaṇaṃ, pac‍cayau samudayaina cā’’ti

ayampi gāthā aitassatthassa sādhikā| idaṃ sambhavapabhavasaddānaṃ bhāvāpādānasādhanavasainatthakathanaṃ|

aivamaittha bhāvakattukammakaraṇāpādānādhikaraṇavasaina cha sādhanāni pakāsitāni| tāni sampadānasādhanaina sattavidhāni bhavanti, taṃ pana uttari āvibhavissati ‘‘dhanamassa bhavatūti dhanabhūtī’’tiādinā| ic‍caivaṃ kitakavasaina sabbathāpi sattavidhāni sādhanāni haunti, yāni ‘‘kārakānī’’tipi vuc‍cati, itau añ‍ñaṃ sādhanaṃ natthi| idha payaugaisvatthaisu ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ  pāṭavatthaṃ sādhananāmaṃ pakāsitaṃ| tathā hi dun‍nikkhittasādhanaihi padaihi yaujitā saddappayaugā dubbaudhatthā haunti, sunikkhittasādhanaihi pana padaihi yaujitā subaudhatthā haunti, tasmā payaugāsādhanamūlakā, atthau ca payaugamūlakau| payaugānurūpañhi aviparītaṃ katvā atthaṃ kathanasīlā ‘‘yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau’’ti aivamādīsu sādhanavasaina gahaitabbaisu atthaisu, añ‍ñaisu catthaisu paṭutarabuddhinau paṇḍitāyaiva aikantaina bhagavatau pariyattisāsanadharā nāma hauntīti vaiditabbaṃ| itau paraṃ nayānusāraina suviñ‍ñaiyyattā ‘‘idaṃ nāma sādhana’’nti na vakkhāma, kaivalamidha dassitaisu payaugaisu viñ‍ñūnaṃ bahumānuppādanatthañ‍caiva vividhavicittapāḷigatikai vividhatthasārai jinavaravacanai sautūnaṃ buddhivijambhanatthañ‍ca atthasādhakavacanāniyaiva yathārahaṃ suttagaiyyavaiyyākaraṇagāthādīsu tatau tatau āharitvā dassaissāma|

pabhāvauti pakāratau bhavatīti pabhāvau, sauyamānubhāvauyaiva| ‘‘pabhāvaṃ tai na passāmi, yaina tvaṃ mithilaṃ vajai’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| anubhavauti anubhavanaṃ anubhavau, kiṃ taṃ? paribhuñ‍janaṃ| ānubhāvauti taijussāhamantapabhūsattiyau| ‘‘taijasaṅkhatau ussāhamantapabhūsattisaṅkhātau vā mahantau ānubhāvau aitassāti mahānubhāvau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ|

taijau ussāhamantā ca, pabhūsattīti pañ‍cimai|

ānubhāvāti vuc‍canti, pabhāvāti ca tai vadai||

taijādivācakattamhi, ānubhāvapadassa tu|

atthanibbacanaṃ dhīrau, yathāsambhavamuddisai||

atha  vā ānubhāvauti anubhavitabbaphalaṃ| ‘‘anubhavitabbassa phalassa mahantatāya mahānubhāvau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| parābhavauti parābhavanaṃ parābhavau, atha vā parābhavatīti parābhavau| ‘‘suvijānau parābhavau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| apica ‘‘dhammadaissī parābhavau’’ti pāṭhānurūpatau parābhavissatīti parābhavauti anāgatakālavasainapi nibbacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā parābhavanti aitaināti parābhavau| kiṃ taṃ? dhammadaissitādi| ‘‘paṭhamau sau parābhavau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| vibhavauti nibbānaṃ| tañhi bhavatau vigatattā bhavatau vigatauti vibhavau, bhavassa ca taṃhaitu vigatattā vigatau bhavau aitasmāti vibhavau| vibhavanti ucchij‍janti vinassanti itau ariyadhanavilaumakā kilaisamahācaurātipi vibhavau| vibhavasaddassa nibbānābhidhānattai ‘‘aivaṃ bhavai vij‍jamānai, vibhavau icchitabbakau’’ti idamaittha sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| imāni pana nibbānassa pariyāyavacanāni –

nibbānaṃ vibhavau maukkhau, niraudhau amataṃ samaṃ|

saṅkhārūpasamau dukkha-niraudhau ac‍cuta’kkhayau||

vivaṭṭa’makataṃ atthaṃ, santipada’masaṅkhataṃ|

pāraṃ taṇhākkhayau dukkha-kkhayau sañ‍ñaujanakkhayau||

yaugakkhaimau virāgau ca,

laukantau ca bhavakkhayau|

apavaggau visaṅkhārau,

sabbhi suddhi visuddhi ca||

vimutyā’pacayau mutti, nibbuti upadhikkhayau|

santi asaṅkhatā dhātu, disā ca sabbataupabhaṃ||

vināpaitāni  nāmāni, visaisakapadaṃ idha|

nibbānavācakānīti, sal‍lakkhaiyya sumaidhasau||

‘‘tāṇaṃ laiṇa’’ntiādīni-paikkhikāni bhavanti hi|

visaisakapadānanti, aitthaitāni pakāsayai||

tāṇaṃ laiṇa’marūpañ‍ca, santaṃ sac‍ca’manālayaṃ|

sududdasaṃ saraṇañ‍ca, parāyaṇa’manītikaṃ||

anāsavaṃ dhuvaṃ nic‍caṃ, viñ‍ñāṇa’manidassanaṃ|

abyāpaj‍jaṃ sivaṃ khaimaṃ, nipuṇaṃ apalaukikaṃ||

ananta’makkharaṃ dīpau, ac‍cantaṃ kaivalaṃ padaṃ|

paṇītaṃ ac‍cutañ‍cāti, bahudhāpi vibhāvayai||

gautrabhūti padassatthaṃ, vadantaihi garūhi tu|

gauttaṃ vuc‍cati nibbāna-miti gauttanti bhāsitaṃ||

vibhavauti vā vināsasampattidhanucchaidadiṭṭhiyaupi vuc‍canti| tattha vināsau vibhavanaṃ ucchij‍janaṃ nassananti atthaina vibhavau| ‘‘vibhavau sabbadhammānaṃ, itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapaintī’’ti ca idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| sampatti pana visaisatau bhavatīti vibhavau| ‘‘rañ‍ñau sirivibhavaṃ daṭṭhukāmā’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| dhanaṃ pana bhavanti vaḍḍhanti vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti sattā aitaināti vibhavau| ‘‘asītikauṭivibhavassa brāhmaṇassa puttau hutvā nibbattī’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| idaṃ pana pariyāyavacanaṃ –

dhanaṃ saṃ vibhavau dabbaṃ, sāpataiyyaṃ pariggahau|

auḍḍaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ sakaṃ atthau, ic‍caitai dhanavācakā||

ucchaidadiṭṭhi pana vibhavati ucchij‍jati ‘‘attā ca laukau ca puna cutitau uddhaṃ na jāyatī’’ti gahaṇatau vibhavauti| ‘‘vibhavataṇhā’’ti  idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| vibhavataṇhāti hi ucchaidadiṭṭhisahagatāya taṇhāya nāmaṃ| aittha atthuddhārau vuc‍cati –

dhananibbānasampatti-vināsucchaidadiṭṭhiyau|

vuttā vibhavasaddaina, iti viñ‍ñū vibhāvayai||

pātubhāvauti pātubhavanaṃ pātubhāvau| āvibhāvauti āvibhavanaṃ āvibhāvau, ubhin‍namaitaisaṃ pākaṭatā ic‍caivatthau| tiraubhāvauti tiraubhavanaṃ tiraubhāvau, paṭicchan‍nabhāvau| vinābhāvauti vinābhavanaṃ vinābhāvau, viyaugau| sautthibhāvauti sautthibhavanaṃ sautthibhāvau, suvatthibhāvau sukhassa atthitā, atthatau pana nibbhayatā nirupaddavatā aiva| atthibhāvauti atthitā vij‍jamānatā avivittatā| natthibhāvauti natthitā avij‍jamānatā vivittatā rittatā tucchatā suñ‍ñatā| aukārantapul‍liṅganiddaisau|

abhibhavatīti abhibhavitā, paraṃ abhibhavantau yau kauci| aivaṃ paribhavitā, anubhavatīti anubhavitā, sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā anubhavantau yau kauci| aivaṃ samanubhavitā| pac‍canubhavitā, aittha pana yathā ‘‘amatassa dātā| anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā’’tiādīsu ‘‘dātā’’ti padānaṃ kattuvācakānaṃ ‘‘amatassā’’tiādīhi padaihi kammavācakaihi chaṭṭhiyantaihi saddhiṃ yaujanā dissati, tathā imaisampi padānaṃ ‘‘pac‍cāmittassa abhibhavitā’’tiādinā yaujanā kātabbā| aivaṃ añ‍ñaisampi aivarūpānaṃ padānaṃ| ākārantapul‍liṅganiddaisau|

bhavatīti bhavaṃ| bhavissatīti vā bhavaṃ, vaḍḍhamānau puggalau| ‘‘suvijānau bhavaṃ hauti, suvijānau parābhavau| dhammakāmau  bhavaṃ hauti, dhammadaissī parābhavau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| atha vā yaina saddhiṃ kathaiti, sau ‘‘bhava’’nti vattabbau, ‘‘bhavaṃ kac‍cāyanau| bhavaṃ ānandau| mañ‍ñai bhavaṃ patthayati, rañ‍ñau bhariyaṃ patibbata’’ntiādīsu| aittha pana dhātuatthai ādarau na kātabbau, sammutiatthaiyaivādarau kātabbau ‘‘saṅkaitavacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, laukasammutikāraṇa’’nti vacanatau| vauhāravisayasmiñhi laukasammuti aiva padhānā avilaṅghanīyā| parābhavatīti parābhavaṃ| aivaṃ paribhavaṃ| abhibhavaṃ| anubhavaṃ| pabhavati pahauti sak‍kautīti pabhavaṃ, pahauntau yau kauci| na pabhavaṃ appabhavaṃ, ‘‘appabhava’’nti ca idaṃ jātakai diṭṭhaṃ –

‘‘chin‍nabbhamiva vātaina, ruṇṇau rukkhamupāgamiṃ|

sauhaṃ appabhavaṃ tattha, sākhaṃ hatthaihi aggahi’’nti

tattha sādhakavacanamidaṃ| niggahītantapul‍liṅganiddaisau|

dhanabhūtīti dhanamassa bhavatūti dhanabhūti| siribhūtīti saubhāya caiva pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñānañ‍ca adhivacanaṃ| sā assa bhavatūti siribhūti| aivaṃ sautthibhūti, suvatthibhūti| ikārantapul‍liṅganiddaisau|

bhāvīti bhavanasīlau bhāvī, bhavanadhammau bhāvī, bhavanai sādhukārī bhāvī| aivaṃ vibhāvī| sambhāvī| paribhāvīti| tatra vibhāvīti atthavibhāvanai samatthau paṇḍitau vuc‍cati| aittha vidvā, vij‍jāgatau, ñāṇītiādi pariyāyavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| bhavanti catra –

vidvā vij‍jāgatau ñāṇī, vibhāvī paṇḍitau sudhī|

budhau visāradau viñ‍ñū, dausañ‍ñū viddasu vidū||

vipassī  paṭibhāṇī ca, maidhāvī nipakau kavi|

kusalau vidurau dhīmā, gatimā mutimā cayaṃ||

cakkhumā kaṇṇavā dabbau, dhīrau bhūri vicakkhaṇau|

sappañ‍ñau buddhimā pañ‍ñau, aivaṃnāmā vibhāvinauti||

īkārantapul‍liṅganiddaisau|

sayambhūti sayamaiva bhavatīti sayambhū| kau sau? antaraina paraupadaisaṃ sāmaṃyaiva sabbaṃ ñaiyyadhammaṃ paṭivijjhitvā sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau sakyamuni bhagavā| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘na mai ācariyau atthi, sadisau mai na vij‍jati|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, natthi mai paṭipuggalau||

ahañhi arahā laukai, ahaṃ satthā anuttarau|

aikaumhi sammāsambuddhau, sītībhūtausmi nibbutau’’ti||

atthatau pana pāramitāparibhāvitau sayambhūñāṇaina saha vāsanāya vigataviddhastaniravasaisakilaisau mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiaparimaiyyaguṇagaṇādhārau khandhasantānau sayambhū| sau aivaṃbhūtau khandhasantānau laukai aggapuggalauti vuc‍cati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘aikapuggalau bhikkhavai laukai uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati acchariyamanussau, katamau aikapuggalau? tathāgatau bhikkhavai arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti| sau aikapuggalau aitarahi ‘‘sabbañ‍ñū, sugatau’’tiādīhi yathābhuc‍caguṇādhigatanāmaihi ca pasiddhau, ‘‘gautamau ādic‍cabandhū’’ti gauttatau ca pasiddhau, sakyaputtau sak‍kau sakyamuni sakyasīhau sakyapuṅgavauti kulatau ca pasiddhau, suddhaudanimāyādaivīsutauti  mātāpititau ca pasiddhau, siddhatthauti gahitanāmaina ca pasiddhau| bhavanti catra –

yau aikapuggalau āsi, buddhau sau vadataṃ varau|

gauttatau gautamau nāma, tathaivādic‍cabandhu ca||

sakyakulai pasūtattā, sakyaputtauti vissutau|

sak‍kau iti ca avhitau, tathā sakyamunīti ca||

sabbattha saiṭṭhabhāvaina, sakyai ca saiṭṭhabhāvatau|

sakyasīhauti sau sakya-puṅgavauti ca sammatau||

suddhaudanīti pititau, nabhai candauva vissutau|

mātitaupi ca sañ‍ñātau, māyādaivīsutau iti||

sabbañ‍ñū sugatau buddhau, dhammarājā tathāgatau|

samantabhaddau bhagavā, jinau dasabalau muni||

satthā vināyakau nāthau,

munindau laukanāyakau|

narāsabhau laukajinau,

sambuddhau dvipaduttamau||

daivadaivau laukagaru, dhammassāmī mahāmuni|

samantacakkhu purisa-dammasārathi māraji||

dhammissarau ca advaijjha-vacanau satthavāhakau|

visuddhidaivau daivāti-daivau ca samaṇissarau||

bhūripañ‍ñau’nadhivarau, narasīhau ca cakkhumā|

munimuni naravarau, chaḷabhiñ‍ñau janai sutau||

aṅgīrasau yatirājā, laukabandhu’matandadau|

vattā pavattā saddhamma-cak‍kavattī yatissarau||

laukadīpau  sirīghanau, samaṇindau naruttamau|

laukattayavidū lauka-paj‍jautau purisuttamau||

sac‍cadasau satapuñ‍ña-lakkhaṇau sac‍casavhayau|

ravibandhā’samasamau, pañ‍canaitta’ggapuggalau||

sabbābhibhū sabbavidū, sac‍canāmau ca pāragū|

purisātisayau sabba-dassāvī narasārathi||

sammāsambuddhau iti sau, ñātau sattuttamauti ca|

tādī vibhaj‍javādīti, mahākāruṇikauti ca||

cakkhubhūtau dhammabhūtau, ñāṇabhūtauti vaṇṇitau|

brahmabhūtauti purisā-jañ‍ñau iti ca thaumitau||

laukajaiṭṭhau sayambhū ca, mahaisi mārabhañ‍janau|

amaughavacanau dhamma-kāyau mārābhibhū iti||

asaṅkhyaiyyāni nāmāni, saguṇaina mahaisinau|

nāmaṃ guṇaihi nissitaṃ, kau kavindau kathaissati||

tatra sabbañ‍ñu ic‍cādi-nāmaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ bhavai|

sabbaisānampi buddhānaṃ, gautamau itiādi na||

buddhau pac‍caikabuddhau ca, ‘‘sayambhū’’iti sāsanai|

kaici ‘‘brahmā sayambhū’’ti, sāsanāvacaraṃ na taṃ||

‘‘buddhau tathāgatau satthā, bhagavā’’ti padāni tu|

ṭhānainaikasahassamhi, sañ‍caranti abhiṇhasau||

tatra cādipadaṃ anta-padañ‍caiva imāni tu|

aikataupi carantīti, vibhāvaiyya visāradau||

visaisakapadānaṃ tu, apaikkhakapadāni ca|

anapaikkhapadānīti, padāni duvidhā siyuṃ||

tathā  hi satthavāhau naravarau chaḷabhiñ‍ñauti aivaṃpakārāni abhidhānapadāni visaisakapadāpaikkhakāni| kathaṃ?

‘‘aivaṃ vijitasaṅgāmaṃ, satthavāhaṃ anuttaraṃ|

sāvakā payirupāsanti, taivij‍jā mac‍cuhāyinau||

yaṃ laukau pūjayatai,

salaukapālau sadā namassati ca|

tassaita sāsanavaraṃ,

vidūhi ñaiyyaṃ naravarassā’’ti,

‘‘chaḷabhiñ‍ñassa sāsana’’nti ca aivaṃ visaisakapadāpaikkhakāni bhavanti| buddhau jinau bhagavāti aivaṃpakārāni pana nau visaisakāpaikkhānīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kaici panaittha aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘munindau samaṇindau samaṇissarau yatissarau ādic‍cabandhu ravibandhūti aivaṃpakārānaṃ idha vuttānamabhidhānānaṃ visaisatthābhāvatau punaruttidausau atthī’’ti| tan‍na, abhidhānānaṃ abhisaṅkharaṇīyānabhisaṅkharaṇīyavasaina abhisaṅkhatābhidhānāni anabhisaṅkhatābhidhānānīti dvaidhā dissanatau| tathā hi katthaci kaici ‘‘sakyasīhau’’ti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘sakyakaisarī sakyamigādhipau’’tiādinā nānāvividhamabhidhānamabhisaṅkharaunti, pāvacanaipi hi ‘‘dviduggamavarahanutta’malatthā’’ti pāṭhau dissati| tathā kaici ‘‘dhammarājā’’ti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘dhammadisampatī’’tiādīni abhisaṅkharaunti| ‘‘sabbañ‍ñū’’ti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘sabbadassāvī sabbadassī’’tiādīni abhisaṅkharaunti, ‘‘sahassakkhau’’ti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘dasasatalaucanau’’tiādīni abhisaṅkharaunti| ‘‘ādic‍cabandhū’’ti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘aravindasahāyabandhū’’tiādīni abhisaṅkharaunti| ‘‘ambuja’’nti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘nīrajaṃ kuñ‍ja’’ntiādīni abhisaṅkharaunti| pāvacanaipi hi  yaṃ padumaṃ, taṃ jalajaṃ nāmāti mantvā paṭisambhidāppattaihi ariyaihi daisanāvilāsavasaina vuttau ‘‘padumuttaranāminau’’ti vattabbaṭṭhānai ‘‘jalajuttaranāminau’’ti pāṭhau dissati| aivaṃ abhisaṅkhatābhidhānāni dissanti|

‘‘buddhau bhagavā’’ti abhidhānāni pana anabhisaṅkhatābhidhānāni| vuttañhaitaṃ dhammasaināpatinā āyasmatā sāriputtaina ‘‘buddhauti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ, na daivatāhi kataṃ, vimaukkhantikamaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ baudhiyā mūlai saha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇappaṭilābhā sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ buddhau’’ti, tathā ‘‘bhagavāti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ…pai॰… sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ bhagavā’’ti| aivaṃ ‘‘buddhau bhagavā’’ti abhidhānāni anabhisaṅkhatābhidhānāni| na hi tāni abhidhānāni caiva ‘‘satthā sugatau jinau’’tiādīni ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca abhisaṅkhatāni, nāpi añ‍ñāni abhidhānāni aitāni paṭic‍ca abhisaṅkhatāni dissanti| tathā hi ‘‘buddhau’’ti abhidhānaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘bujjhitā baudhaitā baudhakau’’tiādīni nāmābhidhānāni na abhisaṅkharaunti| tathā ‘‘bhagavā satthā sugatau’’tiādīni nāmābhidhānāni paṭic‍ca ‘‘sampan‍nabhagau anusāsakau sundaravacanau’’tiādīni nāmābhidhānāni nābhisaṅkharaunti| aivaṃ imaṃ vibhāgaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘munindau samaṇindau samaṇissarau yatissarau ādic‍cabandhu ravibandhū’’tiādinā nayaina punarutti amhaihi katāti daṭṭhabbā| aivamañ‍ñatrāpi nayau naitabbau| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘abhisaṅkhatanāmañ‍ca, nāmañ‍cānabhisaṅkhataṃ|

dviduggamavarau buddhau, iti nāmaṃ dvidhā bhavai’’ti||

pabhūti paraṃ pasayha bhavatīti pabhū, issarau| ‘‘arañ‍ñassa pabhū ayaṃ luddakau’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| abhibhūti  abhibhavatīti abhibhū, asañ‍ñasattau| kiṃ sau abhibhavi? cattārau khandhai arūpinau| iti cattārau khandhai arūpinau abhibhavīti abhibhū| sau ca khau nic‍caitanattā abhibhavanakriyāyāsati pubbaivā’sañ‍ñuppattitau jhānalābhikālai attanā adhigatapañ‍camajjhānaṃ sañ‍ñāvirāgavasaina bhāvaitvā cattārau arūpakkhandhai asañ‍ñibhavai appavattikaraṇaina abhibhavitumārabhi, tadabhibhavanakic‍caṃ idāni siddhanti abhibhavīti abhibhūti vuc‍cati| apica nic‍caitanabhāvaina abhibhavanabyāpārai asatipi pubbai sacaitanakālai sabyāpārattā sacaitanassa viya nic‍caitanassāpi satau tassa upacāraina sabyāpāratāvacanaṃ yuj‍jataiva| dissati hi laukai sāsanai ca sacaitanassa viya acaitanassapi upacāraina sabyāpāratāvacanaṃ| taṃ yathā? kūlaṃ patitukāmaṃ, aivaṃ laukai| sāsanai pana –

‘‘raudantai dārakai disvā, ubbiggā vipulā dumā|

sayamaivaunamitvāna, upagacchanti dārakai’’ti ca

‘‘aṅgārinau dāni dumā bhadantai, phalaisinau chadanaṃ vippahāyā’’ti ca ‘‘phalaṃ tausaiti kassaka’’nti ca ādi| abhibhūsaddassa asañ‍ñasattābhidhānattai ‘‘abhibhuṃ abhibhutau mañ‍ñatī’’ti idamaittha sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| atha vā abhibhavatīti abhibhū, paraisamabhibhavitā yau kauci| visaisatau pana tathāgatauyaiva abhibhū| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘tathāgatau bhikkhavai abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī’’ti| kaici pana ‘‘abhibhū nāma sahassau brahmā’’ti vadanti|

vibhūti visaisabhūtauti vibhū, ‘‘bhavasautaṃ sacai buddhau, tiṇṇau laukantagū vibhū’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ  vacanaṃ| vibhūti haittha rūpakāyadhammakāyasampattiyā visaisabhūtauti atthau| āha ca –

‘‘dissamānaupi tāvassa, rūpakāyau acintiyau|

asādhāraṇañāṇaṭṭhai, dhammakāyai kathāva kā’’ti||

adhibhūti adhibhavatīti adhibhū, issarau|

‘‘tadā maṃ tapataijaina, santattau tidivādhibhū|

dhāraintau brāhmaṇaṃ vaṇṇaṃ, bhikkhāya maṃ upāgamī’’ti –

idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| patibhūti patibhūtauti patibhū, ‘‘gauṇassa patibhū’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| gautrabhūti gauttasaṅkhātaṃ amatamahānibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā bhūtauti gautrabhū, sautāpattimaggassa anantarapac‍cayaina sikhāppattabalavavipassanācittaina saman‍nāgatau puggalau| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘katamau ca puggalau gautrabhū? yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarā ariyadhammassa avak‍kanti hauti, taihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau gautrabhū’’ti, idamaivaittha atthasādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| apica samaṇauti gauttamattamanubhavamānau kāsāvakaṇṭhasamaṇaupi gautrabhū| sau hi ‘‘samaṇau’’ti gauttamattaṃ anubhavati vindati, na samaṇadhammai attani avij‍jamānattāti ‘‘gautrabhū’’ti vuc‍cati, ‘‘bhavissanti khau panānanda anāgatamaddhānaṃ gautrabhunau kāsāvakaṇṭhā dussīlā pāpadhammā’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| vatrabhūti sak‍kau| sau hi mātāpitibharaṇādīhi sattahi vattaihi sak‍kattaṃ labhitvā añ‍ñai daivai vattaina abhibhavatīti vatrabhū| āgamaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana bhūdhātumhi labbhamānaṃ pattiatthampi gahaitvā ‘‘vattaina añ‍ñai abhibhavitvā daivissariyaṃ pattauti vatrabhū’’ti  vuttaṃ, ‘‘vatranāmakaṃ vā asuraṃ abhibhavatīti vatrabhū’’ti ca, ‘‘vatrabhū jayataṃpitā’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| aittha hi vatrabhūti vatranāmakassa asurassa abhibhavitā| jayataṃ pitāti jayantānaṃ pitā| ‘‘sak‍kau indau purindadau’’ ic‍cādi pariyāyavacanaṃ| idaṃ tu dhātādhikārai pakāsaissāma| parābhibhūti paramabhibhavatīti parābhibhū| aivaṃ rūpābhibhūtiādīsupi| sabbābhibhūti sabbamabhibhavitabbaṃ abhibhavatīti sabbābhibhū| sabbābhibhūti ca idaṃ nāmaṃ tathāgatassaiva yuj‍jati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘sabbābhibhū sabbavidūhamasmi,

sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittau|

sabbañ‍jahau taṇhakkhayai vimuttau,

sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kamuddisaiyya’’nti||

ūkārantapul‍liṅganiddaisau| niyatapul‍liṅganiddaisauyaṃ|

idāni aniyataliṅgānaṃ niyataliṅgaisu pakkhittānaṃ bhūtaparābhūta sambhūtasaddādīnaṃ niddaisau vuc‍cati| tatra bhūtauti attanau pac‍cayaihi abhavīti bhūtau, bhūtauti jātau sañ‍jātau nibbattau abhinibbattau pātubhūtau, bhūtauti vā laddhasarūpau yau kauci saviñ‍ñāṇakau vā aviñ‍ñāṇakau vā| atha vā tathākāraina bhavatīti bhūtau, bhūtauti sac‍cau tathau avitathau aviparītau yau kauci, aittha yau bhūtasaddau sac‍catthau, tassa ‘‘bhūtaṭṭhau’’ti idamaittha sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| parābhūtauti parābhavīti parābhūtau| suṭṭhu bhūtauti sambhūtau| visaisaina bhūtauti vibhūtau| vissutau bhūtauti vā vibhūtau, ‘‘vibhūtārammaṇa’’nti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| vibhavīti vā vibhūtau, vinaṭṭhauti atthau, ‘‘rūpai vibhūtai na phusanti phassā’’ti  idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| pākaṭau bhūtauti pātubhūtau| āvi bhavatīti āvibhūtau| aivaṃ tiraubhūtau| vinābhūtau| bhavitumanucchavikauti bhabbau| paribhaviyatai sauti paribhūtau| yaina kainaci yau pīḷitau hīḷitau vā, sau paribhūtau| gamyamānatthau yathākāmacārī| abhibhaviyyatai sauti abhibhūtau| adhibhaviyatai sauti adhibhūtau| aivaṃ addhabhūtau| aittha adhisaddaina samānatthau addhasaddau, ‘‘cakkhu bhikkhavai addhabhūtaṃ, rūpā addhabhūtā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ addhabhūta’’nti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ, tathā ‘‘idha bhikkhavai bhikkhu na haiva anaddhabhūtaṃ attānaṃ na addhabhāvaitī’’ti padampi| tattha anaddhabhūtanti dukkhaina anadhibhūtaṃ| dukkhaina anadhibhūtau nāma manussattabhāvau vuc‍cati, taṃ na addhabhāvaiti nābhibhavatīti suttapadatthau|

anubhaviyatai sauti anubhūtau| aivaṃ samanubhūtau| pac‍canubhūtau| bhāvitau| aittha bhāvitauti iminā samānādhikaraṇaṃ ‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau khau kassapa mayā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau’’tiādīsu guṇīvācakaṃ padhānapadaṃ sāsanai daṭṭhabbaṃ| titthiyasamayai pana bhāvitauti kāmaguṇau vuc‍cati| vuttañhaitaṃ pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘na bhāvitamāsīsatī’’ti| tatra bhāvitā nāma pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā, tai na āsīsati na saivatīti suttapadatthau| sambhāviyatai sauti sambhāvitau| aivaṃ vibhāvitau| paribhāvitau| anuparibhūtau| manaṃparibhūtauti manaṃ paribhaviyittha sauti manaṃparibhūtau| aittha manaṃparibhūtauti īsakaṃ appattaparibhavanau vuc‍cati| mananti hi nipātapadaṃ| ‘‘atipaṇḍitaina puttaina, manamhi upakūlitau, daivadattaina attanau abuddhabhāvaina caiva khantimaittādīnañ‍ca  abhāvaina kumārakassapatthairau ca thairī ca manaṃ nāsitau, manaṃ vuḷhau ahausī’’tiādīsu cassa payaugau vaiditabbau| atra manaṃsaddassa kiñ‍ci yuttiṃ vadāma|

manaṃsaddau dvidhā bhin‍nau, nāmaṃ naipātikañ‍citi|

santaṃ tassa manaṃ hauti, manamhi upakūlitauti||

paribhavitabbauti añ‍ñaina paribhavituṃ sak‍kuṇaiyyauti paribhavitabbau| aivaṃ paribhauttabbau paribhavanīyau| tabbapac‍cayaṭṭhānai hi sak‍kuṇaiyyapadayaujanā dissati ‘‘aladdhaṃ ārammaṇaṃ laddhabbaṃ labhanīyaṃ laddhuṃ vā sak‍kuṇaiyya’’nti| atha vā paribhavanamarahatīti paribhavitabbau| aivaṃ paribhauttabbau paribhavanīyau| tathā hi tabbapac‍cayaṭṭhānai arahatipadayaujanā dissati ‘‘parisak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ lābhamarahatīti laddhabba’’nti| aittha pana paribhauttabbauti padassa atthibhāvai ‘‘khattiyau khau mahārāja daharauti na uñ‍ñātabbau na paribhauttabbau’’ti pāḷi nidassanaṃ| abhiadhipubbā bhūdhātuyau samānatthā| saisāni dukāni nayānusāraina ñaiyyāni| bhamānauti bhavatīti bhamānau, majjhai vakāralaupau daṭṭhabbau| atridaṃ vattabbaṃ –

‘‘kiṃ sau bhamānau sac‍cakau’’, ic‍catra pāḷiyaṃ pana|

rūpaṃ bhavatidhātussa, valaupainaiva dissati||

atrāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘kiṃ sau bhamānau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau, yau bhagavatau vādaṃ āraupaissatī’’ti| vibhavamānauti vibhavatīti vibhavamānau| aivaṃ paribhavamānautiādīsu| tattha ‘‘abhisambhauntau’’timassa karauntau nipphādaintau ic‍caivatthau| ‘‘sabbāni abhisambhauntau, sa rājavasatiṃ vasai’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| yasmā panimāni  ‘‘bhavamānau’’tiādīni vippakatapac‍cattavacanāni, tasmā saramānau raudati, gacchantau gaṇhāti, ‘‘gacchantau sau bhāradvājau, addasa ac‍cutaṃ isi’’ntiādīni viya paripuṇṇuttarakriyāpadāni katvā rājā bhavamānau sampattimanubhavatītiādinā yaujaitabbāni| ‘‘saramānau gacchantau’’tiādīni hi ‘‘yātau gatau pattau’’tiādīhi sadisāni na haunti, uttarakriyāpadāpaikkhakāni haunti tvāpac‍cayantapadāni viyāti|

paribhaviyamānauti paribhaviyatai sauti paribhaviyamānau| aivaṃ paribhuyyamānautiādīsupi| imānipi vippakatapac‍cattavacanāni, tasmā ‘‘rājapurisaihi nīyamānau caurau aivaṃ cintaisī’’tiādīni viya paripuṇṇuttarakriyāpadāni katvā añ‍ñaihi paribhaviyamānau tāṇaṃ gavaisati| bhaugau puggalainānubhaviyamānau parikkhayaṃ gacchatītiādinā yaujaitabbāni| aivaṃ sabbatra īdisaisu vippakatavacanaisu yaujaitabbāni| ayaṃ aniyataliṅgānaṃ niyataliṅgaisu pakkhittānaṃ bhūta parābhūta sambhūtasaddānaṃ niddaisau| ic‍caivaṃ pul‍liṅgānaṃ bhūdhātumayānaṃ yathārahaṃ nibbacanādivasaina niddaisau vibhāvitau|

idāni itthiliṅganiddaisau vuc‍cati – tatra bhāvikāti bhāvaitīti bhāvikā| yā bhāvanaṃ karauti, sā bhāvikā| bhāvanāti vaḍḍhanā brūhanā phātikaraṇaṃ āsaivanā bahulīkārau| vibhāvanāti pakāsanā sandassanā| atha vā vibhāvanāti abhāvanā antaradhāpanā| sambhāvanāti uk‍kaṃsanā thaumanā| paribhāvanāti vāsanā, samantatau vā vaḍḍhanā| ākārantitthiliṅganiddaisau|

bhūmīti sattāyamānā bhavatīti bhūmi, atha vā bhavanti jāyanti vaḍḍhanti caittha thāvarā ca jaṅgamā cāti bhūmi| bhūmi vuc‍cati  pathavī| ‘‘paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā’’tiādīsu pana laukuttaramaggau bhūmīti vuc‍cati| yā panandhabālamahājanaina viñ‍ñātā pathavī, tassimāni abhidhānāni –

‘‘pathavī maidanī bhūmi, bhūrī bhū puthuvī mahī|

chamā vasumatī ubbī, avanī ku vasundharā|

jagatī khiti vasudhā, dharaṇī gau dharā’’iti||

atra bhū ku gausaddā pathavīpadatthai vattantīti kutra diṭṭhapubbāti cai?

vidvā bhūpāla kumuda-gaurakkhādipadaisu vai|

bhū ku gauiti pathavī, vuc‍catīti vibhāvayai||

bhūtīti bhavanaṃ bhūti| vibhūtīti vināsau, visaisatau bhavanaṃ vā, atha vā visaisatau bhavanti sattā aitāyāti vibhūti, sampattiyaiva, ‘‘rañ‍ñau vibhūti| pihanīyā vibhūtiyau’’ti ca idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| ikārantitthiliṅganiddaisau|

bhūrīti pathavī| sā hi bhavanti aitthāti bhūrīti vuc‍cati, bhavati vā pañ‍ñāyati vaḍḍhati cāti bhūrī, atha vā bhūtābhūtā tan‍nissitā sattā ramanti aitthāti bhūrī| pathavīnissitā hi sattā pathaviyaṃyaiva ramanti, tasmā sā imināpi atthaina bhūrīti vuc‍cati| bhūrīsaddassa pathavīvacanai ‘‘bhūripañ‍ñau’’ti atthasādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| apica bhūrī viyāti bhūrī, pañ‍ñā, bhūrīti pathavīsamāya vitthatāya pañ‍ñāya nāmaṃ, ‘‘yaugā vai jāyatī bhūrī, ayaugā bhūrisaṅkhayau’’ti aittha aṭṭhakathāvacanaṃ imassatthassa sādhakaṃ| atha vā bhūtai atthai ramatīti bhūrī, pañ‍ñāyaitaṃ nāmaṃ, ‘‘bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā’’ti aittha  aṭṭhakathāvacanaṃ imassatthassa sādhakaṃ| atha vā pañ‍ñāyaiva rāgādayau dhammai abhibhavatīti bhūrī, rāgādiarayau abhibhavatītipi bhūrī| tathā hi paṭisambhidāmaggai āyasmatā sāriputtaina vuttaṃ ‘‘rāgaṃ abhibhūyatīti bhūrī, pañ‍ñā| dausaṃ mauhaṃ…pai॰… rāgau ari, taṃ ariṃ maddatīti bhūrī, pañ‍ñā| dausau| mauhau…pai॰… sabbai bhavagāminau kammā ari, taṃ ariṃ maddatīti bhūrī, pañ‍ñā’’| aittha pana ‘‘gautrabhū’’ti padamiva ‘‘aribhū’’ti vattabbaipi bhūsaddaṃ pubbanipātaṃ katvā sandhivasaina bhūrīti padamuc‍cāritanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| apica īdisaisu nāmikapadaisu vināpi upasaggaina abhibhavanādiatthā labbhantiyaiva, nākhyātikapadaisūti daṭṭhabbaṃ| idaṃ pana pañ‍ñāya pariyāyavacanaṃ –

pañ‍ñā pajānanā cintā, vicayau upalakkhaṇā|

pavicayau ca paṇḍic‍caṃ, dhammavicayamaiva ca||

sal‍lakkhaṇā ca kausal‍laṃ, bhūrī pac‍cupalakkhaṇā|

naipuñ‍ñañ‍caiva vaibhabyā, maidhā cupaparikkhakā||

sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca pariṇā-yikā caiva vipassanā|

pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ, amauhau sammādiṭṭhi ca|

pataudau cābhidhammasmā, imāni gahitāni mai||

ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāṇamummaṅgau, satthau sautau ca diṭṭhi ca|

mantā baudhau buddhi buddhaṃ, paṭibhānañ‍ca baudhiti||

dhammau vij‍jā gati maunaṃ, naipak‍kaṃ gau matī muti|

vīmaṃsā yauni dhaunā ca, paṇḍā paṇḍic‍cayampi ca|

vaidau paṇḍitiyañ‍caiva, cikicchā miriyāpi ca||

‘‘sautau baudhī’’ti yaṃ vuttaṃ, ñāṇanāmadvayaṃ idaṃ|

buddhapac‍caikasambuddha-sāvakānampi rūhati||

‘‘abhisambaudhi  sambaudhi’’, iti nāmadvayaṃ pana|

pac‍caikabuddhasabbañ‍ñu-buddhānaṃyaiva rūhati||

abhisambaudhisaṅkhātā, paramaupapadā pana|

ñāṇapaṇṇatti sabbañ‍ñu-sambuddhassaiva rūhati||

sammāsambaudhisaṅkhātā, anuttarapadādikā|

buddhā vā ñāṇapaṇṇatti, sabbañ‍ñussaiva rūhati||

‘‘sabbañ‍ñutā’’ti yaṃ vuttaṃ, ñāṇaṃ sabbañ‍ñunauva taṃ|

yuj‍jatai avasaisā tu, ñāṇapañ‍ñatti sabbagā||

ñāṇabhāvamhi santaipi, dhammacakkhādikaṃ pana|

payaujanantarābhāvā, nātra sandassitaṃ mayāti||

bhūtīti bhūtassa bhariyā| yathā hi paitassa bhariyā ‘‘paitī’’ti vuc‍cati, aivamaiva bhūtassa bhariyā ‘‘bhūtī’’ti vuc‍cati| bhautīti yāya saddhiṃ kathaintaina sā itthī ‘‘bhautī’’ iti vattabbā, tasmā iminā padaina itthī vauhariyatīti ca daṭṭhabbaṃ| yathā hi purisaina saddhiṃ kathaintaina purisau ‘‘bhavaṃ’’ iti vauhariyati, aivamaiva itthiyā saddhiṃ kathaintaina itthī ‘‘bhautī’’iti vauhariyati| ‘‘kutau nu bhavaṃ bhāradvājau, imai ānaisi dārakai’’ti, ‘‘ahaṃ bhautiṃ upaṭṭhissaṃ, mā bhautī kupitā ahū’’ti caittha nidassanaṃ| atha vā idhaikac‍cau sattau itthiliṅgavasaina laddhanāmau, sau ‘‘bhautī’’iti vattabbau, tasmā iminā padaina itthīpi itthiliṅgaina laddhanāmā anitthīpi vauhariyatīti ca daṭṭhabbā| tathā hi daivaputtaupi ‘‘daivatā’’ti itthiliṅgavasaina vauharitabbattā daivatāsaddamapaikkhitvā ‘‘bhautī’’iti vauharitau, pagaiva daivadhītā| tathā hi ‘‘bhautī carahi jānāti, taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitā’’ti aittha pana daivatāsaddamapaikkhitvā ‘‘bhautī’’iti itthi liṅgavauhārau katau| atrāyaṃ  suttapadatthau ‘‘yadi sau kuhakau dhanatthikau tāpasau na jānāti, bhautī daivatā pana jānāti ki’’nti| apica –

‘‘atthakāmausi mai yakkha, hitakāmāsi daivatai|

karaumi tai taṃ vacanaṃ, tvaṃsi ācariyau mamā’’ti –

maṭṭhakuṇḍalīvatthusmiṃ pul‍liṅgayakkhasaddamapaikkhitvā ‘‘atthakāmau’’ti pul‍liṅgavasaina itthiliṅgañ‍ca daivatāsaddamapaikkhitvā ‘‘hitakāmā’’ti itthiliṅgavasaina purisabhūtau maṭṭhakuṇḍalī vauharitau| añ‍ñatrāpi daivatāsaddamapaikkhitvā daivaputtau itthiliṅgavasaina vauharitau –

‘‘na tvaṃ bālai vijānāsi, yathā arahataṃ vacau’’ti|

‘‘atthakāmāsi mai amma, hitakāmāsi daivatai’’ti||

aittha pana ‘‘aihi bālai khamāpaihi, kusarājaṃ mahabbala’’nti aittha ca itthīyaiva itthiliṅgavasaina vauharitā, tasmā katthaci itthipurisapadatthasaṅkhātaṃ atthaṃ anapaikkhitvā liṅgamattamaivāpaikkhitvā bhautī daivatā, bhautī silā, bhautī jambū, bhautiṃ daivatantiādīhi saddhiṃ pac‍cattavacanādīni yaujaitabbāni| katthaci pana liṅgañ‍ca atthañ‍ca apaikkhitvā ‘‘bhautī itthī, bhautiṃ daiva’’ntiādinā yaujaitabbāni| vibhāvinīti vibhāvaitīti vibhāvinī| aivaṃ paribhāvinītiādīsupi| īkārantitthiliṅganiddaisau|

bhūti sattāyamānā bhavatīti bhū| atha vā bhavanti jāyanti vaḍḍhanti caittha sattasaṅkhārāti bhū| bhū vuc‍cati pathavī| abhūti vaḍḍhivirahitā kathā, na bhūtapubbāti vā abhū, abhūtapubbā kathā| na bhūtāti vā abhū, abhūtā kathā| ‘‘abhuṃ mai kathaṃ nu bhaṇasi, pāpakaṃ vata bhāsasī’’ti idamaitaisamatthānaṃ  sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| ūkārantitthiliṅganiddaisau| niyatitthiliṅganiddaisauyaṃ|

aniyataliṅgānaṃ pana niyatitthiliṅgaisu pakkhittānaṃ bhūtaparābhūtasambhūtasaddādīnaṃ niddaisau nayānusāraina suviñ‍ñaiyyauva| ic‍caivaṃ itthiliṅgānaṃ bhūdhātumayānaṃ yathārahaṃ nibbacanādivasaina niddaisau vibhāvitau|

idāni napuṃsakaliṅganiddaisau vuc‍cati – tatra bhūtanti catubbidhaṃ pathavīdhātuādikaṃ mahābhūtarūpaṃ| tañhi añ‍ñaisaṃ nissayabhāvaina bhavatīti bhūtaṃ, bhavati vā tasmiṃ tadadhīnavuttitāya upādārūpanti bhūtaṃ| atha vā bhūtanti sattau bhūtanāmakau vā| bhūtanti hi napuṃsakavasaina sakalau sattau aivaṃnāmakau ca yakkhādikau vuc‍cati| ‘‘kālau ghasati bhūtāni, sabbānaiva sahattanā| yānīdha bhūtāni samāgatāni, ujjhāpaitvāna bhūtāni, tamhā ṭhānā apak‍kamī’’ti aivamādīsu napuṃsakappayaugau vaiditabbau| gātābandhasukhatthaṃ liṅgavipal‍lāsauti cai? tan‍na, ‘‘yakkhādīni mahābhūtāni yaṃ gaṇhanti, naiva taisaṃ tassa antau, na bahi ṭhānaṃ upalabbhatī’’ti cuṇṇiyapadaracanāyampi bhūtasaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgattadassanatauti avagantabbaṃ| mahābhūtanti vuttappakāraṃ catubbidhaṃ mahābhūtarūpaṃ| tassa mahantapātubhāvādīhi kāraṇaihi mahābhūtatā vaiditabbā| kathaṃ? mahantaṃ bhūtanti mahābhūtaṃ, māyākārasaṅkhātaina mahābhūtaina samantipi mahābhūtaṃ, yakkhādīhi mahābhūtaihi samantipi mahābhūtaṃ, mahantaihi ghāsacchādanādipac‍cayaihi bhūtaṃ pavattantipi mahābhūtaṃ, mahāvikārabhūtantipi mahābhūtaṃ| aivaṃ mahantapātubhāvādīhi kāraṇaihi mahābhūtatā vaiditabbā| atridaṃ suṭṭhupalakkhitabbaṃ –

pun‍napuṃsakaliṅgau  ca, bhūtasaddau pavattati|

paṇṇattiyaṃ guṇai caiva, guṇaiyaivitthiliṅgakau||

bhūta sambhūtasaddādi-nayai paṇṇattivācakā|

yaujaitabbā tiliṅgai tai, iti ñai yyaṃ visaisatau||

‘‘bhūtau tiṭṭhati, bhūtāni, tiṭṭhanti, samaṇau ayaṃ|

idāni bhūtau, cittāni, bhūtāni vimalāni tu||

vañjhā bhūtā vadhū aisā’’, ic‍cudāharaṇānimai|

vuttāni suṭṭhu lakkhaiyya, sāsanatthagavaisakau||

bhavittanti vaḍḍhitaṭṭhānaṃ| tañhi bhavanti vaḍḍhanti aitthāti bhavittanti vuc‍cati, ‘‘janittaṃ mai bhavittaṃ mai, iti paṅkai avassayi’’nti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ|

‘‘bhavittaṃ’’ iti ‘‘bhāvitta’’-nti ca pāṭhau dvidhā mayā|

rassattadīghabhāvaina, diṭṭhau bhaggavajātakai||

bhūnanti bhavanaṃ bhūnaṃ vaddhi| ‘‘ahamaiva dūsiyā bhūnahatā, rañ‍ñau mahāpatāpassā’’ti, ‘‘bhūnahac‍caṃ kataṃ mayā’’ti ca idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ|

bhavananti bhavanakriyā| atha vā bhavanti vaḍḍhanti aittha sattā puttadhītāhi nānāsampattīhi cāti bhavanaṃ vuc‍cati gaihau, ‘‘paittikaṃ bhavanaṃ mamā’’ti idamaitassatthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ| ‘‘gaihau gharañ‍ca āvāsau, bhavanañ‍ca nikaitana’’nti idaṃ pariyāyavacanaṃ| parābhavananti avaddhimāpaj‍janaṃ| sambhavananti suṭṭhu bhavanaṃ| vibhavananti ucchaidau vināsau vā| pātubhavananti pākaṭatā sarūpalābhau ic‍caivatthau| āvibhavananti pac‍cakkhabhāvau| tiraubhavananti paṭicchan‍nabhāvau| vinābhavananti vinābhāvau| sautthibhavananti suvatthitā| paribhavananti pīḷanā hīḷanā vā| abhibhavananti vidhamanaṃ| adhibhavananti ajjhauttharaṇaṃ| anubhavananti paribhuñ‍janaṃ| samanubhavananti suṭṭhu paribhuñ‍janaṃ| pac‍canubhavananti adhipatibhāvainapi suṭṭhu paribhuñ‍janaṃ| niggahītantanapuṃsakaliṅganiddaisau|

atthavibhāvīti atthassa vibhāvanasīlaṃ cittaṃ vā ñāṇaṃ vā kulaṃ vā atthavibhāvi| aivaṃ dhammavibhāvi| ikārantanapuṃsakaliṅganiddaisau|

gautrabhūti pañ‍ñattārammaṇaṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ vā gautrabhucittaṃ| tañhi kāmāvacaragauttamabhibhavati, mahaggatagauttañ‍ca bhāvaiti nibbattaitīti ‘‘gautrabhū’’ti vuc‍cati| apica gautrabhūti nibbānārammaṇaṃ maggavīthiyaṃ pavattaṃ gautrabhuñāṇaṃ vā saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ vā phalasamāpattivīthiyaṃ pavattaṃ gautrabhuñāṇaṃ| taisu hi paṭhamaṃ puthuj‍janagauttamabhibhavati, ariyagauttañ‍ca bhāvaiti, gauttābhidhānā ca nibbānatau ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina bhavatīti ‘‘gautrabhū’’ti vuc‍cati, dutiyaṃ pana saṅkhārārammaṇampi samānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayabhāvaina sasampayuttāni phalacittāni gauttābhidhānai nibbānamhi bhāvaitīti ‘‘gautrabhū’’ti vuc‍cati| idaṃ pāḷivavatthānaṃ –

‘‘gautrabhu’’iti rassatta-vasaina kathitaṃ padaṃ|

napuṃsakanti viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, ñāṇacittādipaikkhakaṃ||

‘‘gautrabhū’’iti dīghatta-vasaina kathitaṃ pana|

pul‍liṅgamiti viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, puggalādikapaikkhakaṃ||

dīghabhāvaina vuttaṃ tu, napuṃsakanti nau vadai|

binduvantī’tarai bhaidā, tayau iti hi bhāsitā||

īkārantā ca ūdantā, rassattaṃ yanti sāsanai|

napuṃsakattaṃ patvāna, sahabhu sīghayāyiti||

cittaina saha bhavatīti cittasahabhu, cittaina saha na bhavatīti nacittasahabhu, rūpaṃ| ukārantanapuṃsakaliṅganiddaisau| niyatanapuṃsakaliṅganiddaisauyaṃ|
