aniyataliṅgānaṃ  niyatanapuṃsakaliṅgaisu pakkhittānaṃ bhūtaparābhūtasaddādīnaṃ niddaisau nayānusāraina suviñ‍ñaiyyauva| ic‍caivaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgānaṃ bhūdhātumayānaṃ yathārahaṃ nibbacanādivasaina niddaisau vibhāvitau| ic‍caivaṃ sabbathāpi liṅgattayaniddaisau samattau|

ul‍liṅganaina vividhaina nayaina vuttaṃ,

bhūdhātusaddamayaliṅgatikaṃ yadaitaṃ|

āliṅgiyaṃ piyatarañ‍ca sutaṃ suliṅgaṃ,

pausau karai manasi liṅgaviduttamicchaṃ||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

bhūdhātumayānaṃ tividhaliṅgikānaṃ nāmikarūpānaṃ vibhāgau

catutthau paricchaidau|

5. aukārantapul‍liṅganāmikapadamālā

bhū dhātutau pavattānaṃ, nāmikānamitau paraṃ|

nāmamālaṃ pakāsissaṃ, nāmamālantarampi ca||

vippakiṇṇakathā aittha, aivaṃ vuttai na haissati|

pabhaidau nāmamālānaṃ, paripuṇṇauva haihiti||

pubbācariyasīhānaṃ, tasmā idha mataṃ sutaṃ|

puraicaraṃ karitvāna, vakkhāmi savinicchayaṃ||

purisau, purisā| purisaṃ, purisai| purisaina, purisaihi, purisaibhi| purisassa, purisānaṃ| purisā, purisasmā, purisamhā, purisaihi, purisaibhi| purisassa, purisānaṃ| purisai, purisasmiṃ, purisamhi, purisaisu| bhau purisa, bhavantau purisā|

ayamāyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina pabhin‍napaṭisambhidaina katasmā niruttipiṭakatau uddharitau purisaic‍caitassa pakatirūpassa  nāmikapadamālānayau| tatra purisavacanaaikavacanaputhuvacanaisu pac‍cattavacanādīni bhavanti| taṃ yathā? purisau tiṭṭhati, purisā tiṭṭhanti| tatra purisauti purisavacanai aikavacanai pac‍cattavacanaṃ bhavati, purisāti purisavacanai puthuvacanai pac‍cattavacanaṃ bhavati|

purisaṃ passati, purisai passati| tatra purisanti purisavacanai aikavacanai upayaugavacanaṃ bhavati, purisaiti purisavacanai puthuvacanai upayaugavacanaṃ bhavati|

purisaina kataṃ, purisaihi kataṃ, purisaibhi kataṃ| tatra purisaināti purisavacanai aikavacanai karaṇavacanaṃ bhavati, purisaihi, purisaibhīti purisavacanai puthuvacanai karaṇavacanaṃ bhavati|

purisassa dīyatai, purisānaṃ dīyatai| tatra purisassāti purisavacanai aikavacanai sampadānavacanaṃ bhavati, purisānanti purisavacanai puthuvacanai sampadānavacanaṃ bhavati|

purisā nissaṭaṃ, purisasmā nissaṭaṃ, purisamhā nissaṭaṃ, purisaihi nissaṭaṃ, purisaibhi nissaṭaṃ| tatra purisāti purisavacanai aikavacanai nissak‍kavacanaṃ bhavati| purisasmāti…pai॰… purisamhāti purisavacanai aikavacanai nissak‍kavacanaṃ bhavati, purisaihi, purisaibhīti purisavacanai puthuvacanai nissak‍kavacanaṃ bhavati|

purisassa pariggahau, purisānaṃ pariggahau| tatra purisassāti purisavacanai aikavacanai sāmivacanaṃ bhavati, purisānanti purisavacanai puthuvacanai sāmivacanaṃ bhavati|

purisai patiṭṭhitaṃ, purisasmiṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ, purisamhi patiṭṭhitaṃ, purisaisu patiṭṭhitaṃ| tatra purisaiti purisavacanai aikavacanai bhummavacanaṃ bhavati, purisasminti…pai॰… purisamhīti…pai॰… purisaisūti purisavacanai puthuvacanai bhummavacanaṃ bhavati|

bhau  purisa tiṭṭha, bhavantau purisā tiṭṭhatha| tatra bhau purisaiti purisavacanai aikavacanai ālapanaṃ bhavati, bhavantau purisāiti purisavacanai puthuvacanai ālapanaṃ bhavatīti iminā nayaina sabbattha nayau vitthāraitabbau|

yamakamahāthairaina katāya pana cūḷaniruttiyaṃ ‘‘bhau purisa’’iti rassavasaina ālapanaikavacanaṃ vatvā ‘‘bhau purisā’’iti dīghavasaina ālapanabahuvacanaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍cāpi tādisau nayau niruttipiṭakai natthi, tathāpi bahūnamālapanavisayai ‘‘bhau yakkhā’’itiādīnaṃ ālapanabahuvacanānaṃ jātakaṭṭhakathādīsu dissanatau pasatthatarauva hauti viñ‍ñūnaṃ pamāṇañ‍ca, tasmā iminā yamakamahāthairamatainapi ‘‘purisau purisā purisa’’ntiādīni vatvā āmantanai ‘‘bhau purisa, bhau purisā, bhavantau purisā’’ti nāmikapadamālā yaujaitabbā|

tattha purisauti paṭhamāya aikavacanaṃ| purisāti bahuvacanaṃ| purisanti dutiyāya aikavacanaṃ| purisaiti bahuvacanaṃ| purisaināti tatiyāya aikavacanaṃ| purisaihi, purisaibhīti dvai bahuvacanāni| purisassāti catutthiyā aikavacanaṃ| purisānanti bahuvacanaṃ| purisā, purisasmā, purisamhāti tīṇi pañ‍camiyā aikavacanāni| purisaihi, purisaibhīti dvai bahuvacanāni| purisassāti chaṭṭhiyā aikavacanaṃ| purisānanti bahuvacanaṃ| purisai, purisasmiṃ, purisamhīti tīṇi sattamiyā aikavacanāni| purisaisūti bahuvacanaṃ| bhau purisāti aṭṭhamiyā aikavacanaṃ| bhau purisā, bhavantau purisāti dvai bahuvacanāni|

kiñ‍cāpaitaisu ‘‘purisā’’ti idaṃ paṭhamāpañ‍camīaṭṭhamīnaṃ, ‘‘purisai’’ti idaṃ dutiyāsattamīnaṃ, ‘‘purisaihi, purisaibhī’’ti tatiyāpañ‍camīnaṃ, ‘‘purisāna’’nti catutthīchaṭṭhīnaṃ aikasadisaṃ, tathāpi atthavasaina asaṅkarabhāvau vaiditabbau| kathaṃ? ‘‘purisau  tiṭṭhati, purisā tiṭṭhanti| purisaṃ passati, purisai passatī’’tiādinā|

tattha ca bhauti āmantanatthai nipātau| sau na kaivalaṃ aikavacanaṃyaiva hauti, atha khau bahuvacanampi hautīti ‘‘bhau purisā’’iti bahuvacanappayaugaupi gahitau| ‘‘bhavantau’’tidaṃ pana bahuvacanamaiva hautīti ‘‘purisā’’ti puna vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| iti yamakamahāthairaina ‘‘bhau purisa’’iti rassavasaina ālapanaikavacanaṃ vatvā ‘‘bhau purisā’’iti dīghavasaina ālapanabahuvacanaṃ vuttaṃ| tathā hi pāḷiyaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu ca nipātabhūtau bhausaddau aikavacanabahuvacanavasaina dvidhā bhij‍jati| atrimāni nidassanapadāni – ‘‘api nu khau sapariggahānaṃ taivij‍jānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ apariggahaina brahmunā saddhiṃ saṃsandati samaitīti, nau hidaṃ bhau gautama| acchariyaṃ bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ bhau ānanda, aihi bhau samaṇa, bhau pabbajita’’ic‍cādipāḷitau aṭṭhakathātau ca bhausaddassa aikavacanappayaugai pavattinidassanaṃ, ‘‘taina hi bhau mamapi suṇātha| yathā mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ, nāhaṃ bhau samaṇassa gautamassa subhāsitaṃ subhāsitatau nābbhanumaudāmi, passatha bhau imaṃ kulaputtaṃ| bhau yakkhā ahaṃ imaṃ tumhākaṃ bhājaitvā dadaiyyaṃ| aparisuddhau panamhi, bhau dhuttā tumhākaṃ kriyā mayhaṃ na ruc‍cati| sau tai purisai āha bhau tumhai maṃ māraintā rañ‍ñau dassaitvāva māraithā’’ti ic‍cādi pana pāḷitau aṭṭhakathātau ca bhausaddassa bahuvacanappayaugai pavattinidassanaṃ| kac‍cāyanappakaraṇai pana ‘‘bhau purisa, bhau purisā’’ti padadvayaṃ ālapanaikavacanavasaina vuttaṃ| taṃ yathā āgamaihi na virujjhati, tathā gahaitabbaṃ|

kaici  pana adūraṭṭhassālapanai ‘‘bhau purisa’’iti rassavasaina ālapanaikavacanaṃ icchanti, dūraṭṭhassālapanai pana ‘‘bhau purisā’’iti dīghavasaina ālapanaikavacanaṃ icchanti| adūraṭṭhānaṃ dūraṭṭhānañ‍ca purisānaṃ itthīnañ‍ca ālapanai na kiñ‍ci vadanti| tathā adūraṭṭhāya dūraṭṭhāya ca itthiyā ālapanai tai pucchitabbā ‘‘adūraṭṭhānaṃ dūraṭṭhānañ‍ca purisānamālapanai kathaṃ vattabba’’nti| addhā tai aivaṃ puṭṭhā uttari kiñ‍ci vattuṃ na sakkhissanti| aivampi tai cai vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘bhavantau purisāti imināva adūraṭṭhānaṃ dūraṭṭhānañ‍ca purisānamālapanaṃ bhavatī’’ti| tadā tai vattabbā ‘‘yadi bhavantau purisā’’ti iminā advaijjhaina vacanaina adūraṭṭhānaṃ dūraṭṭhānañ‍ca purisānamālapanaṃ bhavati, aivaṃ santai ‘‘bhau purisa’’iti rassapadainapi dūraṭṭhassa ca purisassālapanaṃ vattabbaṃ, aivaṃ avatvā kimatthaṃ adūraṭṭhassālapanai ‘‘bhau purisa’’iti rassavasaina ālapanaikavacanaṃ icchatha, kimatthañ‍ca dūraṭṭhassālapanai ‘‘bhau purisā’’iti dīghavasaina ālapanaikavacanaṃ icchatha|

nanu ‘‘taggha bhagavā baujjhaṅgā, taggha sugata baujjhaṅgā’’tiādīsu ālapanapadabhūtaṃ ‘‘bhagavā’’iti dīghapadaṃ samīpai ṭhitakālaipi dūrai ṭhitakālaipi buddhassālapanapadaṃ bhavitumarahataiva, tathā ālapanapadabhūtaṃ ‘‘sugata’’iti rassapadampi| yasmā panaitaisu ‘‘bhagavā’’ti ālapanapadassa na katthacipi rassattaṃ dissati, ‘‘sugatā’’ti ālapanapadassa ca na katthacipi dīghattaṃ dissati, tasmā dīgharassamattābhaidaṃ acintaitvā ‘‘purisa’’iti rassavasaina vuttapadaṃ pakatissaravasaina samīpai ṭhitassa purisassa āmantanakālai adūraṭṭhassālapanapadaṃ bhavati, āyatassaravasaina dūrai ṭhitapurisassa āmantanakālai dūraṭṭhassālapanapadaṃ bhavatīti gahaitabbaṃ| tathā ‘‘bhavantau purisā, bhau yakkhā, bhau dhuttā’’tiādīni  dīghavasaina vuttāni ālapanabahuvacanapadānipi pakatissaravasaina samīpai ṭhitapurisānaṃ āmantanakālai adūraṭṭhānamālapanapadāni bhavanti, āyatassaravasaina dūrai ṭhitapurisādīnaṃ āmantanakālai dūraṭṭhānamālapanapadāni bhavantīti gahaitabbāni| tathā hi brāhmaṇā katthaci katthaci rassaṭṭhānaipi dīghaṭṭhānaipi āyataina saraina majjhimāyataina saraina ac‍cāyataina ca saraina vaidaṃ paṭhanti likhitumasak‍kuṇaiyyaina gītassaraina viya| iti sabbakkharaisupi āyataina sarainuc‍cāraṇaṃ labbhataiva likhitumasak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ, tasmā asampathamanautaritvā ‘‘bhau purisa’’iti vacanaina dūraṭṭhassa ca adūraṭṭhassa ca purisassālapanaṃ bhavati, ‘‘bhau purisā, bhavantau purisā’’ti imaihi vacanaihipi dūraṭṭhānañ‍ca adūraṭṭhānañ‍ca purisānamālapanaṃ bhavatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| iti dūraṭṭhassa adūraṭṭhānañ‍ca āyataina saraina āmantanamaiva pamāṇaṃ, na dīgharassamattāvisaisau, tasmā ‘‘bhau sattha, bhau rāja, bhau gaccha, bhau muni, bhau daṇḍi, bhau bhikkhu, bhau sayambhu, bhauti kañ‍ñai, bhauti patti, bhauti itthi, bhauti yāgu, bhauti vadhu, bhau kula, bhau aṭṭhi, bhau cakkhu’’ic‍caivamādīhi padaihi adūraṭṭhassālapanañ‍ca dūraṭṭhassālapanañ‍ca bhavati| ‘‘bhavantau satthā, satthārau, bhautiyau kañ‍ñā, kañ‍ñāyau’’ti aivamādīhipi padaihi adūraṭṭhānañ‍cālapanaṃ bhavatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

idaṃ panaittha san‍niṭṭhānaṃ –

tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, rañ‍ñau puttaṃ adassayuṃ|

puttau ca pitaraṃ disvā, dūratauvajjhabhāsatha||

āgacchu dauvārikā khaggabandhā,

kāsāviyā hantu mamaṃ janinda|

akkhāhi mai pucchitau aitamatthaṃ,

aparādhau kau nvidha mamaj‍ja atthi||

aivaṃ  saddhammarājaina, vauhārakusalaina vai|

sudaisitai saumanassa-jātakai sabbadassinā||

dūraṭṭhānaipi rassattaṃ, ‘‘janinda’’iti dissati|

na katthacipi dīghattaṃ, iti nīti mayā matā||

idampaittha vattabbaṃ ‘‘kutau nu bhau idamāyātaṃ ‘dūraṭṭhassālapanaṃ adūraṭṭhassālapanami’ti’’? saddasatthatau| saddasatthaṃ nāma na sabbasau buddhavacanassaupakārakaṃ, aikadaisaina pana hauti|

imasmiṃ pakaraṇai ‘‘bahuvacana’’nti vā ‘‘puthuvacana’’nti vā ‘‘anaikavacana’’nti vā atthatau aikaṃ, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ, tasmā sabbattha ‘‘bahuvacana’’nti vā ‘‘puthuvacana’’nti vā ‘‘anaikavacana’’nti vā vauhārau kātabbau, puthuvacanaṃ anaikavacananti ca idaṃ sāsanai niruttañ‍ñūnaṃ vauhārau, itaraṃ saddasatthavidūnaṃ|

kasmā pana imasmiṃ pakaraṇai dvivacanaṃ na vuttanti? yasmā buddhavacanai dvivacanaṃ nāma natthi, tasmā na vuttanti| nanu buddhavacanai vacanattayaṃ atthi, tathā hi ‘‘āyasmā’’ti idaṃ aikavacanaṃ, ‘‘āyasmantā’’ti idaṃ dvivacanaṃ, ‘‘āyasmantau’’ti idaṃ bahuvacananti? tan‍na, yadi ‘‘āyasmantā’’ti idaṃ vacanaṃ dvivacanaṃ bhavaiyya, ‘‘purisau purisā’’tiādīsu kataraṃ dvivacananti vadaiyyātha, tasmā buddhavacanai dvivacanaṃ nāma natthi| tainaiva hi si yau aṃ yau nā hītiādinā aikavacanabahuvacanānaiva dassitānīti|

nanu ca bhau ‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā, aj‍ja upausathau pan‍narasau| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, mayaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ karaiyyāmā’’ti pāḷiyaṃ dvai sandhāya ‘‘āyasmantā’’ti vuttaṃ, ‘‘uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau cattārau pārājikā dhammā’’tiādīsu pana pāḷīsu bahavau sandhāya ‘‘āyasmantau’’ti vuttaṃ, na ca sak‍kā vattuṃ ‘‘yathā tathā vutta’’nti, parivāsādiāraucanaipi aṭṭhakathācariyaihi viñ‍ñātasugatādhippāyaihi ‘‘dvin‍naṃ āraucaintaina ‘āyasmantā dhāraintū’ti, tiṇṇaṃ āraucaintaina ‘āyasmantau dhāraintū’ti vattabba’’nti vuttattāti? sac‍caṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ pana vinayavauhāravasaina vuttanti| nanu vinayau buddhavacanaṃ, kasmā ‘‘buddhavacanai dvivacanaṃ nāma natthī’’ti vadathāti? sac‍caṃ vinayau buddhavacanaṃ, tathāpi vinayakammavasaina vuttattā upalakkhaṇamattaṃ, na sabbasādhāraṇabahuvacanapariyāpan‍naṃ| yadi hi ‘‘āyasmantā’’ti idaṃ dvivacanaṃ siyā, tappayaugānipi kriyāpadāni dvivacanānaiva siyuṃ, tathārūpānipi kriyāpadāni na santi| na hi akkharasamayakauvidau jhānalābhīpi dibbacakkhunā vassasatampi vassasahassampi samavaikkhantau buddhavacanai aikampi kriyāpadaṃ dvivacananti passaiyya, aivaṃ kriyāpadaisu dvivacanassābhāvā nāmikapadaisu dvivacanaṃ natthi| nāmikapadaisu tadabhāvāpi kriyāpadaisu tadabhāvau vaiditabbau| sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ dvīsupi dvivacanāni santi, māgadhabhāsāyaṃ pana natthi|

apica ‘‘puthuvacana’’nti niruttivauhāraupi ‘‘buddhavacanai dvivacanaṃ natthī’’ti aitamatthaṃ dīpaiti| tañhi sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ vuttā dvivacanatau bahuvacanatau ca visuṃbhūtaṃ vacanaṃ, tattha vā vuttaihi atthaihi visuṃbhūtassa atthassa vacanaṃ ‘‘puthuvacana’’nti vuc‍cati| kathamidaṃ sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ vuttā dvivacanatau bahuvacanatau ca visuṃbhūtaṃ vacananti cai? yasmā sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ ‘‘puthuvacana’’nti vauhārau natthi, tasmā idaṃ taihi sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ vuttaihi dvivacanabahuvacanaihi visuṃbhūtaatthassa vacananti vuc‍cati| kathañ‍ca pana sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ vuttaihi visuṃbhūtassa atthassa vacananti puthuvacananti cai? yasmā sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ dvai upādāya dvivacanaṃ vuttaṃ, na ticatupañ‍cādikai  bahavau upādāya, bahavau pana upādāya bahuvacanaṃ vuttaṃ, na dvai upādāya, ayaṃ sak‍kaṭabhāsāya visaisau| māgadhabhāsāyaṃ pana dviticatupañ‍cādikai bahavau upādāya puthuvacanaṃ vuttaṃ, tasmā sak‍kaṭabhāsāyaṃ vuttaihi atthaihi visuṃbhūtassa atthassa vacananti puthuvacananti vuc‍cati| ayaṃ māgadhabhāsāya visaisau| tasmātra puthubhūtassa, puthunau vā atthassa vacanaṃ ‘‘puthuvacana’’nti atthau samadhigantabbau|

idāni ‘‘purisau, purisā, purisa’’nti niruttipiṭakatau uddharitanayaṃ nissāya pakatirūpabhūtassa bhūtasaddassa nāmikapadamālā vuc‍catai –

bhūtau, bhūtā| bhūtaṃ, bhūtai| bhūtaina, bhūtaihi, bhūtaibhi| bhūtassa, bhūtānaṃ| bhūtā, bhūtasmā, bhūtamhā, bhūtaihi, bhūtaibhi| bhūtassa, bhūtānaṃ| bhūtai, bhūtasmiṃ, bhūtamhi, bhūtaisu| bhau bhūta, bhavantau bhūtā|

atha vā ‘‘bhau bhūtā’’iti bahuvacanaṃ viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| yathā panaittha bhūtaic‍caitassa pakatirūpassa nāmikapadamālā purisanayaina yaujitā, aivaṃ bhāvakādīnañ‍ca añ‍ñaisañ‍ca taṃsadisānaṃ nāmikapadamālā purisanayaina yaujaitabbā| aitthañ‍ñāni taṃsadisāni nāma ‘‘buddhau’’tiādīnaṃ padānaṃ buddhaic‍cādīni pakatirūpāni|

buddhau dhammau saṅghau maggau,

khandhau kāyau kāmau kappau|

māsau pakkhau yakkhau bhakkhau,

nāgau maighau bhaugau yāgau||

rāgau dausau mauhau mānau,

makkhau thambhau kaudhau laubhau|

hāsau vairau dāhau taijau,

chandau kāsau sāsau raugau||

assau  sassau issau sissau,

sīhau byagghau rukkhau sailau|

indau sak‍kau daivau gāmau,

candau sūrau aughau dīpau||

passau yañ‍ñau cāgau vādau,

hatthau pattau sausau gaidhau|

saumau yaudhau gacchau acchau,

gaihau māḷau aṭṭau sālau||

narau nagau migau sasau,

suṇau bakau ajau dijau|

hayau gajau kharau sarau,

dumau talau paṭau dhajau||

uragau paṭagau vihagau bhujagau,

kharabhau sarabhau pasadau gavajau|

mahisau vasabhau asurau garuḷau,

taruṇau varuṇau balisau palighau||

sālau dhavau ca khadirau,

gaudhumau saṭṭhikau yavau|

kaḷāyau ca kulatthau ca,

tilau muggau ca taṇḍulau||

khattiyau brāhmaṇau vaissau,

suddau dhuttau ca puk‍kusau|

caṇḍālau patikau paṭṭhau,

manussau rathikau rathau||

pabbajitau  gahaṭṭhau ca,

gauṇau auṭṭhau ca gadrabhau|

mātugāmau ca auraudhau,

ic‍cādīni vibhāvayai||

kaicaittha vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘nanu bhau ‘auraudhā ca kumārā cā’ti pāṭhassa dassanatau auraudhasaddau itthiliṅgau’’ti? tan‍na, tattha hi ‘‘auraudhā’’ti idaṃ aukārantapul‍liṅgamaiva, nā’kārantitthiliṅgaṃ, tumhai pana ‘‘ākārantitthiliṅga’’nti mañ‍ñamānā aivaṃ vadatha, na panidaṃ ākārantitthiliṅgaṃ, atha khau ‘‘mātugāmā’’tipadaṃ viya bahuvacanavasaina vuttamākārantapadanti| nanu ca bhau sammauhavinaudaniyādīsu auraudhasaddassa itthiliṅgatā pākaṭā, kathanti cai? ‘‘rukkhai adhivatthā daivatā thairassa kuddhā paṭhamamaiva manaṃ palaubhaitvā ‘itau tai sattadivasamatthakai upaṭṭhākau rājā marissatī’ti supinai āraucaisi| thairau taṃ kathaṃ sutvā rājauraudhānaṃ ācikkhi| tā aikappahārainaiva mahāviravaṃ viraviṃsū’’ti| aittha hi ‘‘rājauraudhāna’’nti vatvā ‘‘tā’’ti vuttattāva auraudhasaddassa itthiliṅgatā pākaṭāti? tan‍na, atthassa duggahaṇatau| duggahitau hi aittha tumhaihi atthau, aittha pana auraudhasaddaina itthipadatthassa kathanatau itthipadatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘tā’’ti vuttattā ‘‘tā itthiyau’’ti ayamaivatthau| tumhai pana amātāpitarasaṃvaddhattā ācariyakulai ca anivuṭṭhattā aitaṃ sukhumatthamajānantā yaṃ vā taṃ vā mukhārūḷhaṃ vadatha|

bhuñ‍janatthaṃ kathanatthaṃ, mukhaṃ hautīti nau vadai|

yaṃ vā taṃ vā mukhārūḷhaṃ, vacanaṃ paṇḍitau narauti||

na mayaṃbhau yaṃ vā taṃ vā mukhārūḷhaṃ vadāma, aṭṭhakathācariyānañ‍ñaiva vacanaṃ gahaitvā vadāma, aṭṭhakathāyaiva amhākaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ, na mayaṃ tumhākaṃ saddahāmāti| amhākaṃ saddahatha vā mā vā, mā tumhai ‘‘aṭṭhakathācariyānañ‍ñaiva vacanaṃ gahaitvā vadāmā’’ti aṭṭhakathācariyai abbhācikkhatha| na hi aṭṭhakathācariyaihi ‘‘auraudhasaddau itthiliṅgau’’ti vuttaṭṭhānamatthi, tasmāpi aṭṭhakathācariyai abbhācikkhatha, na yuttaṃ buddhādīnaṃ garūnamabbhācikkhanaṃ mahatau anatthassa lābhāya saṃvattanatau| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhati, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, tatau attānañ‍ca khaṇatī’’ti|

aivaṃ abbhācikkhanassa ayuttataṃ sāvaj‍jatañ‍ca dassaitvā punapi tai idaṃ vattabbā – jātakaṭṭhakathāyampi tumhaihi āhaṭaudāharaṇasadisaṃ udāharaṇamatthi, taṃ suṇātha| kausiyajātakaṭṭhakathāyañhi ‘‘satthā jaitavanai viharantau aikaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ mātugāmaṃ ārabbha kathaisi| sā kiraikassa saddhassa pasan‍nassa upāsakabrāhmaṇassa brāhmaṇī dussīlā pāpadhammā’’ti pāṭhau dissati| aittha hi ‘‘mātugāmaṃ ārabbha kathaisī’’ti vatvā ‘‘sā’’ti vuttattā tumhākaṃ mataina mātugāmasaddau itthiliṅgauyaiva siyā, na pul‍liṅgau, kimidaṃ aṭṭhakathāvacanampi na passatha, tadaiva pana aṭṭhakathāvacanaṃ passatha, kiṃ sā aiva aṭṭhakathā tumhākaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ, na tadañ‍ñāti|

yadi tāsaddaṃ apaikkhitvā auraudhasaddassa itthiliṅgattamicchatha, aitthāpi sāsaddamapaikkhitvā mātugāmasaddassa itthiliṅgattamicchathāti| aivaṃ vuttā tai niruttarā appaṭibhānā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyaiyyuṃ| aitthāpi mātugāmasaddaina itthipadatthassa kathanatau itthipadatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘sā’’ti vuttattā ‘‘sā itthī’’ti ayamaivatthau| katthaci  hi padhānavācakaina pul‍liṅgaina napuṃsakaliṅgaina vā samānādhikaraṇassa guṇasaddassa abhidhaiyyaliṅgānuvattittāpul‍liṅgavasaina vā napuṃsakaliṅgavasaina vā niddisitabbattaipi liṅgamanapaikkhitvā itthipadatthamaivāpaikkhitvā itthiliṅganiddaisau dissati| taṃ yathā? ‘‘idha visākhai mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantā hauti saṅgahitaparijanā bhattumanāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhatī’’ti ca, ‘‘kau nu khau bhantai haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā dassanāya, daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca| idha mal‍likai aikac‍cau mātugāmau kaudhanā hauti upāyāsabahulā, appampi vuttā samānā abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthiyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarautī’’ti ca, ‘‘taṃ khau pana bhikkhavai itthiratanaṃ rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa pubbuṭṭhāyinī pacchānipātinī kiṃkārapaṭissāvinī’’ti ca imai payaugā|

katthaci pana padhānavācakaina napuṃsakaliṅgaina samānādhikaraṇassa guṇasaddassa abhidhaiyyaliṅgānuvattittā napuṃsakaliṅgavasaina niddisitabbattaipi liṅgamanapaikkhitvā purisapadatthamaivāpaikkhitvā pul‍liṅganiddaisau dissati| taṃ yathā? ‘‘pañ‍ca pac‍caikabuddhasatāni imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciranivāsinau ahaisuṃ| taṃ khau pana rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa pariṇāyakaratanaṃ ñātānaṃ pavaisaitā añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā’’ti| katthaci padhānavācakaina liṅgattayaina samānādhikaraṇassa guṇasaddassa abhidhaiyyaliṅgānurūpaṃ niddaisau dissati| taṃ yathā? sā itthī ‘‘sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā| aṭṭhahi khau nakulamātai dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā parammaraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ  sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| saddhau purisapuggalau, saddhaṃ kulaṃ, cittaṃ dantaṃ sukhāvaha’’nti|

saiyyaiti saddau pana yaibhuyyaina aukārantabhāvai ṭhatvā liṅgattayānukūlau bhavati aikākārainaiva tiṭṭhanatau| kathaṃ? saiyyau amittau matiyā upaitau| aisāva pūjanā saiyyau, aikāhaṃ jīvitaṃ saiyyau|

‘‘dhammaina ca alābhau yau,

yau ca lābhau adhammikau|

alābhau dhammikau saiyyau,

yañ‍cai lābhau adhammikau||

yasau ca appabuddhīnaṃ, viñ‍ñūnaṃ ayasau ca yau|

ayasauva saiyyau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, na yasau appabuddhinaṃ||

dummaidhaihi pasaṃsā ca, viñ‍ñūhi garahā ca yā|

garahāva saiyyau viñ‍ñūhi, yañ‍cai bālappasaṃsanā||

sukhañ‍ca kāmamayikaṃ, dukkhañ‍ca pavivaikikaṃ|

pavivaikaṃ dukkhaṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai kāmamayaṃ sukhaṃ||

jīvitañ‍ca adhammaina, dhammaina maraṇañ‍ca yaṃ|

maraṇaṃ dhammikaṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai jīvai adhammika’’nti||

aivamayaṃ saiyya iti saddau aukārantabhāvai ṭhatvā liṅgattayānukūlau bhavati| katthaci pana ākārantabhāvai ṭhatvā itthiliṅgānukūlau dissati ‘‘itthīpi hi aikac‍ciyā, saiyyā pausa janādhipā’’ti| niggahītantau pana hutvā napuṃsakaliṅgānukūlau apasiddhau| aivaṃpakārai payaugai kiṃ tumhai na passathāti| aivaṃ vuttā ca tai niruttarāva bhavissanti|

sacaipi  tai aittha aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘tattha tattha suttappadaisai aṭṭhakathādīsu ca ‘mātugāmau’ti vā ‘mātugāmainā’ti vā aukārantapul‍liṅgabhāvaina mātugāmasaddassa dassanatau pul‍liṅgabhūtaṃ mātugāmasaddaṃ anapaikkhitvā itthipadatthamaiva apaikkhitvā ‘‘sā itthī’’ti itthīsaddaina sāsaddassa sambandhaggahaṇaṃ mayaṃ sampaṭicchāma, ‘auraudhau’ti vā ‘auraudhainā’ti vā aukārantapul‍liṅgabhāvaina ṭhitassa auraudhasaddassa adassanatau pana tumhaihi vuttaṃ purimatthaṃ na sampaṭicchāmā’’ti| tadā taisaṃ imāni vinayapāḷiyaṃ āgatapadāni dassaitabbāni ‘‘taina khau pana samayaina rājā udainau uyyānai paricāraisi saddhiṃ auraudhaina, atha khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhau rājānaṃ udainaṃ aitadavaucā’’ti| aivaṃ imāni suttapadāni dassaitvā suttanipātaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘rāmau nāma rājā kuṭṭharaugī auraudhaihi ca nāṭakaihi ca jigucchamānau’’ti vacanañ‍ca dassaitvā ‘‘gacchatha tumhai garukulamupagantvā bhagavatau saddhammassa ciraṭṭhitatthaṃ sādhukaṃ padabyañ‍janāni uggaṇhathā’’ti uyyaujaitabbā|

idāni mātugāmasaddādīsu kiñ‍ci vinicchayaṃ vadāma – mātugāmasaddau ca auraudhasaddau ca dārasaddau cāti imai itthipadatthavācakāpi samānā aikantaina pul‍liṅgā bhavanti| taisu dārasaddassa aikasmiṃ atthai vattamānassāpi bahuvacanakattamaiva saddasatthavidū icchanti, na aikavacanakattaṃ| mayaṃ pana dārasaddassa aikasmiṃ atthai aikavacanakattaṃ, yaibhuyyaina pana bahuvacanakattaṃ anujānāma, bavhatthai vattabbamaiva natthi| pāḷiyañhi dārasaddau yaibhuyyaina bahuvacanakau bhavati, aikavacanakau appau| tatrimai payaugā –

‘‘dāsā  ca dāsyau anujīvinau ca,

puttā ca dārā ca mayañ‍ca sabbai|

dhammañ‍carāmapparalaukahaitu,

tasmā hi amhaṃ daharā na miyyarai’’ti ca,

‘‘yau ñātīnaṃ sakhīnaṃ vā, dāraisu paṭidissati|

sahasā sampiyāyaina, taṃ jañ‍ñāvasalau itī’’ti ca,

‘‘saihi dāraihi’santuṭṭhau, vaisiyāsu padissati|

dissati paradāraisu, taṃ parābhavatau mukha’’nti ca,

‘‘puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā’’ti ca byāsai, samāsai pana ‘‘puttadārā disā pacchā, puttadāraihi mattanau’’ti ca aivamādayau bahuvacanappayaugā bahavau bhavanti|

aikavacanappayaugā pana appā| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘garūnaṃ dārai, dhammaṃ carai yaupi samuñ‍jakaṃ carai, dārañ‍ca pausaṃ dadamappakasmi’’nti ca,

‘‘yai gahaṭṭhā puñ‍ñakarā, sīlavantau upāsakā|

dhammaina dāraṃ pausainti, tai namassāmi mātalī’’ti ca,

‘‘paradāraṃ na gacchaiyyaṃ, sadārapasutau siya’’nti ca,

‘‘yau icchai purisau hautuṃ, jātiṃ jātiṃ punappunaṃ|

paradāraṃ vivaj‍jaiyya, dhautapādauva kaddama’’nti ca

aivamādayau aikavacanappayaugā appā|

samāhāralakkhaṇavasaina panaisa dārasaddau napuṃsakaliṅgaikavacanaupi katthaci bhavati| ‘‘ādāya puttadāraṃ| puttadārassa saṅgahau’’iti aivaṃ idha vuttappakāraina liṅgañ‍ca atthañ‍ca sal‍lakkhaitvā ‘‘purisau purisā’’ti pavattaṃ purisasaddanayaṃ nissāya  sabbaisaṃ ‘‘bhūtau bhāvakau bhavau’’tiādīnaṃ bhūdhātumayānaṃ añ‍ñaisañ‍caukārantapadānaṃ nāmikapadamālāsu saddhāsampan‍naihi kulaputtaihi saddhammaṭṭhitiyā kausal‍lamuppādaitabbaṃ|

kiṃ pana sabbāni aukārantapadāni purisanayai sabbappakāraina aikasadisānaiva hutvā paviṭṭhānīti? na paviṭṭhāni| kānici hi aukārantapadāni purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhāni ca haunti, aikadaisaina paviṭṭhāni ca, kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhāni ca haunti, aikadaisaina na paviṭṭhāni ca, kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhānaiva| tatra katamāni kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhāni ca haunti, aikadaisaina paviṭṭhāni ca? ‘‘sarau vayau caitau’’tiādīni| sarauiti hi ayaṃsaddau ususaddasaravanaakārādisaravācakau cai, purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhau| rahadavācakau cai, manaugaṇapakkhikattā purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhau| vayauiti saddau parihānivācakau cai, purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhau| āyukauṭṭhāsavācakau cai, manaugaṇapakkhikattā purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhau| caitau iti saddau yadi paṇṇattivācakau, purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhau| yadi pana cittavācakau, manaugaṇapakkhikattā purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhau| manaugaṇau ca nāma –

manau vacau vayau taijau,

tapau caitau tamau yasau|

ayau payau sirau chandau,

sarau urau rahau ahau –

imai sauḷasa|

idāni yathāvuttassa pākaṭīkaraṇatthaṃ manasaddādīnaṃ nāmikapadamālaṃ kathayāma –

manau, manā| manaṃ, manau, manai| manasā, manaina, manaihi, manaibhi| manasau, manassa, manānaṃ| manā, manasmā, manamhā, manaihi, manaibhi| manasau, manassa, manānaṃ| manasi, manai, manasmiṃ, manamhi, manaisu| bhau mana, bhavantau manā|

atha vā ‘‘bhau manā’’iti bahuvacanampi ñaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ vacau, vacā| vacaṃ, vacau, vacai| vacasātiādinā nāmikapadamālā yaujaitabbā| ahasaddassa pana bhummaikavacanaṭṭhānai ahasi, ahai, ahasmiṃ, ahamhi, ahu, ahanīti yaujaitabbā|

idāni rūpantaravisaisadassanatthaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgassa manasaddassapi nāmikapadamālaṃ vadāma, aṭṭhānai ayaṃ kathitāti na caudaitabbaṃ|

manaṃ, manāni, manā| manaṃ, manāni, manai| manaina, manaihi, manaibhi| manassa, manasau, manānaṃ| manā, manasmā, manamhā, manaihi, manaibhi| manassa, manasau, manānaṃ| manai, manasmiṃ, manamhi, manaisu| bhau mana, bhavantau manā| atha vā ‘‘bhau manāni, bhau manā’’aivampi bahuvacanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| aivamuttaratrāpi nayau|

aittha ca pul‍liṅgassa manasaddassa pac‍cattakaraṇasampadānasāmibhummavacanāni manau manasā manasau manasīti rūpāni ṭhapaitvā yāni saisāni, napuṃsakaliṅgassa ca manasaddassa pac‍cattavacanāni ‘‘manaṃ manānī’’ti rūpāni ca, aṭṭhamyaupayaugavacanānaṃ ‘‘manaṃ manānī’’ti rūpadvayañ‍ca ṭhapaitvā yāni saisāni, tāni sabbāni kamatau samasamāni|

kaici aukārantau manauiti saddau napuṃsakaliṅgauti vadanti, tai vattabbā – yadi sau napuṃsakaliṅgau siyā, tassadisaihi vacau vayautiādisaddaihipi napuṃsakaliṅgaihaiva bhavitabbaṃ, na ‘‘tai napuṃsakaliṅgā’’ti garū vadanti, ‘‘pul‍liṅgā’’ic‍caiva vadanti| yasmā ca pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘kāyau anic‍cau; manau  anic‍cau’’ti ca ‘‘kāyau dukkhau, manau dukkhau’’ti ca ‘‘nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vāti anic‍cau bhantai’’ti ca aivamādayau pul‍liṅgappayaugā bahavau diṭṭhā| taina ñāyati manausaddau aikantaina pul‍liṅgauti| yadi pana napuṃsakaliṅgausiyā, ‘‘anic‍cau dukkhau’’ti aivamādīni taṃsamānādhikaraṇāni anaikapadasatānipi napuṃsakaliṅgānaiva siyuṃ| na hi tāni napuṃsakaliṅgāni, atha khau abhidhaiyyaliṅgānuvattakāni vāc‍caliṅgāni| aivaṃ manausaddassa pul‍liṅgatā pac‍caitabbāti, sacai manausaddau napuṃsakaliṅgau na hauti, kathaṃ ‘‘manānī’’ti napuṃsakarūpaṃ dissatīti? sac‍caṃ ‘‘manānī’’ti napuṃsakaliṅgamaiva, tathāpi manaugaṇai pamukhabhāvaina gahitassaukārantassa manasaddassa rūpaṃ na hauti| atha kiñ‍carahīti cai? cittasaddaina samānaliṅgassa samānasutittaipi manaugaṇai apariyāpan‍nassa niggahītantassaiva manasaddassa rūpaṃ| manasaddau hi pun‍napuṃsakavasaina dvidhā bhij‍jati ‘‘manau manaṃ’’iti yathā ‘‘aj‍javauaj‍java’’nti| ‘‘manau cai nappadussati| santaṃ tassa manaṃ hautī’’ti hi pāḷi| yadi ca sau manausaddau napuṃsakaliṅgau na hauti|

‘‘garu caitiyapabbatavattaniyā,

pamadā pamadā pamadā vimadaṃ|

samaṇaṃ sunisamma akā hasitaṃ,

patitaṃ asubhaisu munissa manau’’ti

aittha manausaddaina samānādhikaraṇau ‘‘patita’’nti saddau napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvaina kasmā san‍nihitau| yasmā ca samānādhikaraṇapadaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvaina san‍nihitaṃ, tasmā saddantarasan‍nidhānavasaina manausaddau napuṃsakaliṅgauti ñāyatīti? tan‍na, samānādhikaraṇapadassa sabbattha liṅgavisaisājautanatau| yadi hi samānādhikaraṇapadaṃ sabbattha liṅgavisaisaṃ jautaiyya, ‘‘cattārau indriyānī’’ti aitthāpi ‘‘cattārau’’ti  padaṃ indriyasaddassa pul‍liṅgattaṃ karaiyya, na ca kātuṃ sak‍kauti| indriyasaddau hi aikantaina napuṃsakaliṅgau| yadi tumhai ‘‘patita’’nti samānādhikaraṇapadaṃ nissāya manausaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgattamicchatha, ‘‘cattārau indriyānī’’ti aitthapi ‘‘cattārau’’ti samānādhikaraṇapadaṃ nissāya indriyasaddassa pul‍liṅgattaṃ icchathāti| na mayaṃ bhau indriyasaddassa pul‍liṅgattaṃ icchāma, atha khau napuṃsakaliṅgattaṃyaiva icchāma, ‘‘cattārau’’ti padaṃ liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina ṭhitattā ‘‘cattārī’’ti gaṇhāma, tasmā ‘‘cattāri indriyānī’’ti atthaṃ dhāraimāti| yadi aivaṃ ‘‘patitaṃ asubhaisu munissa manau’’ti aitthāpi ‘‘patita’’nti padaṃ liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina ṭhitanti mantvā ‘‘patitau’’ti atthaṃ dhāraithāti| na dhāraima aittha liṅgavipal‍lāsassa anicchitabbatau| yadi hi manausaddau pul‍liṅgau siyā, taṃsamānādhikaraṇapadaṃ ‘‘patitau’’ti vattabbaṃ siyā| kimācariyau aivaṃ vattuṃ na jāni, jānamānau aiva sau ‘‘patitau’’ti nāvauca, ‘‘patita’’nti panāvauca, taina ñāyati ‘‘manausaddau napuṃsakaliṅgau’’ti| mā tumhai aivaṃ vadaitha, samānādhikaraṇapadaṃ nāma katthaci padhānaliṅgamanuvattati, katthaci nānuvattati, tasmā na taṃ liṅgavisaisajautanai aikantatau pamāṇaṃ| ‘‘mātugāmau, auraudhau, āvusau visākha, aihi visākhai, cittāni aṭṭhīnī’’ti aivamādirūpavisaisauyaiva pamāṇaṃ| yadi samānādhikaraṇapadaiyaiva liṅgavisaisau adhigantabbau siyā, ‘‘cattārau ca mahābhūtā’’tiādīsu liṅgavavatthānaṃ na siyā| yasmā aivamādīsupi ṭhānaisu liṅgavavatthānaṃ hautiyaiva| kathaṃ? ‘‘cattārau’’ti pul‍liṅgaṃ ‘‘mahābhūtā’’ti napuṃsakanti, tasmā ‘‘patitaṃ asubhaisu munissa manau’’ti aitthāpi ‘‘patita’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ ‘‘manau’’ti pul‍liṅganti vavatthānaṃ bhavatīti| idaṃ sutvā tai tuṇhī bhavissanti| tatau  taisaṃ tuṇhībhūtānaṃ idaṃ vattabbaṃ – yasmā manaugaṇai pavattānaṃ padānaṃ samānādhikaraṇapadāni katthaci napuṃsakavasaina yaujaitabbāni, tasmā manaugaṇai pamukhassa manausaddassapi samānādhikaraṇapadāni katthaci napuṃsakavasaina yaujitāni| tathā hi pubbācariyā ‘‘saddhammataijavihataṃ vilayaṃ khaṇaina, vainaiyyasattahadayaisu tamau’payāti| dukkhaṃ vacau aitasminti dubbacau| avanataṃ sirau yassa sauyaṃ avaṃsirau, appakaṃ rāgādi rajau yaisaṃ pañ‍ñāmayai akkhimhi tai apparajakkhā’’tiādinā saddaracanaṃ kubbiṃsu, na pana taihi vacau sirau rajausaddādīnaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgattaṃ vibhāvaituṃ īdisī saddaracanā katā, atha khau siraumanausaddānaṃ manaugaṇai pavattānaṃ pul‍liṅgasaddānaṃ katthacipi īdisānipi liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina ṭhitāni samānādhikaraṇapadāni hauntīti paraisaṃ jānāpanādhippāyavatiyā anukampāya viracitā| aitthāpi tumhākaṃ mataina manausaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgattai sati vacau sirau ic‍cādayaupi napuṃsakaliṅgattamāpaj‍janti napuṃsakaliṅgavasaina samānādhikaraṇapadānaṃ niddiṭṭhattā| kiṃ panaitaisampi napuṃsakaliṅgattaṃ icchathāti| addhā tai idampi sutvā nibbaiṭhaitumasak‍kauntā tuṇhī bhavissanti| kiñ‍cāpi tai añ‍ñaṃ gahaitabbakāraṇaṃ apassantā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘yadi bhau manau saddau napuṃsakaliṅgauna hauti, kasmā vaiyyākaraṇā ‘manausaddau napuṃsakaliṅgau’ti vadantī’’ti? tai vattabbā – yadi tumhai vaiyyākaraṇamataṃ gahaitvā manausaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgattaṃ raucaitha, nanu bhagavāyaiva laukai asadisau mahāvaiyyākaraṇau mahāpurisau visāradau parappavādamaddanau| bhagavantañhi padakā vaiyyākaraṇā ambaṭṭhamāṇavapaukkharasātisauṇadaṇḍādayau ca brāhmaṇā sac‍cakanigaṇṭhādayau ca paribbājakā  vādaina na sampāpuṇiṃsu, añ‍ñadatthu bhagavāyaiva mattavāraṇagaṇamajjhai kaisarasīhau viya asambhītau naisaṃ naisaṃ vādaṃ maddaisi, mahantai ca nai atthai patiṭṭhāpaisi, aivaṃvidhaina bhagavatā vauhārakusalaina yasmā ‘‘kāyau anic‍cau’’ti ca ‘‘kāyau dukkhau, manau anic‍cau, manau dukkhau’’ti ca aivamādinā vuttā manausaddassa pul‍liṅgabhāvasūcanikā bahū pāḷiyau dissanti, tasmā manausaddau pul‍liṅgauyaivāti sāratau pac‍caitabbauti| aivaṃ vuttā tai niruttarā appaṭibhānā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyissanti|

idāni sarasaddādīnaṃ nāmikapadamālā visaisatau vuc‍catai –

sarau, sarā| saraṃ, sarai| saraina, saraihi, saraibhi| sarassa, sarānaṃ| sarā, sarasmā, saramhā, saraihi, saraibhi| sarassa, sarānaṃ| sarai, sarasmiṃ, saramhi, saraisu| bhau sara, bhavantau sarā|

ayaṃ purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhassa ususaddasaravanaakārādisaravācakassa sarasaddassa nāmikapadamālā|

ayaṃ pana purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhassa manaugaṇapakkhikassa rahadavācakassa sarasaddassa nāmikapadamālā –

sarau, sarā| saraṃ, sarau, sarai| sarasā, saraina, saraihi, saraibhi| sarasau, sarassa, sarānaṃ| sarā, sarasmā, saramhā, saraihi, saraibhi| sarasau, sarassa, sarānaṃ| sarasi, sarai, sarasmiṃ, saramhi, saraisu| bhau sara, bhavantau sarā, bhau sarā iti vā|

vayau, vayā| vayaṃ, vayai| vayaina, vayaihi, vayaibhīti purisanayaina ñai yyau| ayaṃ purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhassa parihānivācakassa vayasaddassa nāmikapadamālā|

ayaṃ  pana purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhassa manaugaṇapakkhikassa āyukauṭṭhāsavācakassa vayasaddassa nāmikapadamālā – vayau, vayā| vayaṃ, vayau, vayai| vayasā, vayaina, vayaihi, vayaibhīti mananayaina ñaiyyau| tassa caitau paṭissausi, arañ‍ñai luddagaucarau| caitā haniṃsu vaidabbaṃ|

caitau, caitā| caitaṃ, caitai| caitaina, caitaihi, caitaibhīti purisanayaina ñaiyyau| ayaṃ purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhassa paṇṇattivācakassa caitasaddassa nāmikapadamālā|

ayaṃ pana purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhassa cittavācakassa caitasaddassa nāmikapadamālā – caitau, caitā| caitaṃ, caitau, caitai| caitasā, caitaina, caitaihi, caitaibhīti mananayaina ñaiyyau| yasau kulaputtau, yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ, yasaina kulaputtaināti aikavacanavasaina purisanayaina yaujaitabbā, aikavacanaputhuvacanavasaina vā| aivaṃ kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai sabbathā paviṭṭhāni ca haunti, aikadaisaina paviṭṭhāni cāti iminā nayaina sabbapadāni pañ‍ñācakkhunā upaparikkhitvā visaisau vaiditabbau| avisaisañ‍ñunau hi aivamādivibhāgaṃ ajānantā yaṃ vā taṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ raupaintā yathādhippaitaṃ atthaṃ virādhainti, tasmā yau aittha amhaihi pakāsitau vibhāgau, sau saddhāsampan‍naihi kulaputtaihi sak‍kac‍camuggahaitabbau| katamāni kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhāni ca aikadaisaina na paviṭṭhāni ca? manau vacau taijausaddādayau caiva ayyasaddau ca, tatra manasaddādīnaṃ nāmikapadamālā haiṭṭhā vibhāvitā|

ayyasaddassa  pana nāmikapadamālāyaṃ ‘‘ayyau, ayyā| ayyaṃ, ayyai’’ti purisanayaina vatvā ālapanaṭṭhānai ‘‘bhau ayya, bhau ayyau’’ti dvai aikavacanāni, ‘‘bhavantau ayyā, bhavantau ayyau’’ti dvai bahuvacanāni ca vattabbāni| aittha ayyau iti saddau pac‍cattavacanabhāvai aikavacanaṃ, ālapanavacanabhāvai aikavacanañ‍caiva bahuvacanañ‍ca| tatrimai payaugā ‘‘ayyau kira sāgatau ambatitthikaina nāgaina saṅgāmaisi, pivatu bhantai ayyau sāgatau kāpautikaṃ pasan‍na’’nti aivamādīni ayyausaddassa pac‍cattaikavacanappayaugāni, ‘‘atha khau sā itthī taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca ‘nāyyau sau bhikkhu maṃ nippāṭaisi, apica ahamaiva taina bhikkhunā gacchāmi, akārakau sau bhikkhu, gaccha khamāpaihī’ti’’ aivamādīni ayyausaddassa ālapanaikavacanappayaugāni, ‘‘aitha’yyau rājavasatiṃ, nisīditvā suṇātha mai| aitha mayaṃ ayyau samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajissāmā’’ti aivamādīni ayyausaddassa ālapanabahuvacanappayaugāni| bhavati catra –

ayyau iti ayaṃ saddau, pac‍cattaikavacau bhavai|

ālapanai bahuvacau, bhavai aikavacaupi ca –

aivaṃ kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhāni ca haunti aikadaisaina na paviṭṭhāni ca|

katamāni kānici aukārantapadāni purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhāni? gausaddauyaiva| gausaddassa hi ayaṃ nāmikapadamālā –

gau, gāvau, gavau| gāvuṃ, gāvaṃ, gavaṃ, gāvau, gavau| gāvaina, gavaina, gauhi, gaubhi| gāvassa, gavassa, gavaṃ, gun‍naṃ, gaunaṃ| gāvā, gāvasmā, gāvamhā, gavā, gavasmā, gavamhā, gauhi, gaubhi| gāvassa, gavassa, gavaṃ, gun‍naṃ, gaunaṃ| gāvai, gāvasmiṃ, gāvamhi, gavai, gavasmiṃ, gavamhi, gāvaisu, gavaisu, gausu| bhau gau, bhavantau gāvau, gavau| ayaṃ purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhassa gausaddassa nāmikapadamālā|

nanu ca bhau gausaddau attanā sambhūtagauṇasaddamālāvasaina purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhau caiva aikadaisaina na paviṭṭhau cāti? sac‍caṃ| gauṇasaddau gausaddavasaina sambhūtaupi ‘‘vatticchānupubbikā saddapaṭipattī’’ti vacanatau gausaddatau visuṃ amhaihi gahaitvā purisanayai pakkhittau| tassa hi visuṃ gahaṇai yutti dissati syādīsu aikākārainaiva tiṭṭhanatau, tasmā gausaddatau sambhūtampi gauṇasaddaṃ anapaikkhitvā suddhaṃ gausaddamaiva gahaitvā purisanayai sabbathā gausaddassa appaviṭṭhatā vuttā|

nanu ca bhau pac‍cattavacanabhūtau gauiti saddau purisauti saddaina sadisattā purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhauti? tan‍na, gausaddau hi nic‍camaukārantau, na purisasaddādayau viya paṭhamaṃ akārantabhāvai ṭhatvā pacchā paṭiladdhaukārantaṭṭhau| tainaiva hi pac‍cattavacanaṭṭhānaipi ālapanavacanaṭṭhānaipi gauic‍caiva tiṭṭhati| yadi pac‍cattavacanattaṃ paṭic‍ca gausaddassa purisanayai aikadaisaina paviṭṭhatā icchitabbā, ‘‘kānici aukārantapadānī’’ti aivaṃ vuttā aukārantakathā kamatthaṃ dīpaiyya, nipphalāva sā kathā siyā, tasmā amhaihi yathāvuttau nayauyaiva āyasmantaihi manasi kātabbau| aivaṃ gausaddassa purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhatā daṭṭhabbā|

kaicaittha aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ ‘‘gausaddassa tāva ‘gau, gāvau, gavau| gāvuṃ, gāvaṃ, gavaṃ’ ic‍cādinā nayaina purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhatā amhaihi ñātā, jaraggava puṅgavādisaddā pana kutra nayai  paviṭṭhā’’ti? taisaṃ aivaṃ byākātabbaṃ ‘‘jaraggava puṅgavādisaddā sabbathāpi purisanayai paviṭṭhā’’ti| tathā hi taisaṃ gausaddatau ayaṃ visaisau, jarantau ca sau gau cāti jaraggavau| aittha nakāralaupau takārassa ca gakārattaṃ bhavati samāsapadattā, samāsai ca simhi parai gausaddassaukārassa avādaisau labbhati, tasmā pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘visāṇaina jaraggavau’’ti aikavacanarūpaṃ dissati| tathā hi añ‍ñattha anupapadattā gavauiti bahuvacanapadaṃyaiva dissati| idha pana saupapadattā samāsapadabhāvamāgamma ‘‘jaraggavau’’ti aikavacanapadaṃyaiva dissati| tathā hi jaraggavauti aittha jarantā ca tai gavau cāti aivaṃ bahuvacanavasaina nibbacanīyatā na labbhati laukasaṅkaitavasaina aikasmiṃ atthai nirūḷhattāti| ‘‘jaraggavau, jaraggavā| jaraggavaṃ, jaraggavai| jaraggavainā’’ti purisanayaina nāmikapadamālā yaujaitabbā| aisa nayau puṅgavau sakyapuṅgavautiādīsupi|

tatra puṅgavauti gun‍naṃ yūthapati nisabhasaṅkhātau usabhau| yau pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘muhuttajātauva yathā gavaṃpati, samaihi pādaihi phusī vasundhara’’nti ca ‘‘gavañ‍cai taramānānaṃ, ujuṃ gacchati puṅgavau’’ti ca āgatau| īdisaisu pana ṭhānaisu kaici ‘‘pumā ca sau gau cāti puṅgavau’’ti vacanatthaṃ bhaṇanti| mayaṃ pana padhānai nirūḷhau ayaṃ saddauti vacanatthaṃ na bhaṇāma| na hi puṅkaukilautiādisaddānaṃ kaukilādīnaṃ pumbhāvappakāsanamattai samatthatā viya imassa pumbhāvappakāsanamattai samatthatā sambhavati, atha khau padhānabhāvappakāsanai ca samatthatā sambhavati| taina ‘‘sakyapuṅgavau’’tiādīsu nisabhasaṅkhātau puṅgavau viyāti puṅgavau, sakyānaṃ, sakyaisu  vā puṅgavau sakyapuṅgavautiādinā samāsapadatthau gahaitabbau| atha vā uttarapadattai ṭhitānaṃ sīhabyagghanāgādisaddānaṃ saiṭṭhavācakattā ‘‘sakyapuṅgavau’’tiādīnaṃ ‘‘sakyasaiṭṭhau’’tiādinā atthau gahaitabbau| iti sabbathāpi purisanayai pavattanatau jaraggava puṅgavādisaddānaṃ gausaddassa padamālatau visadisapadamālatā vavatthapaitabbā| gausaddassa pana purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhatā ca vavatthapaitabbā|

āpasaddai ācariyānaṃ liṅgavacanavasaina matibhaidau vij‍jati, tasmā taṃmataina tassa purisanayai sabbathā appaviṭṭhatā bhavati| ‘‘aṅguttarāpaisū’’ti pāḷiyā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘mahiyā pana nadiyā uttaraina āpau’’ti vuttaṃ, ṭīkāyaṃ pana taṃ ul‍liṅgitvā ‘‘mahiyā nadiyā āpau tassa janapadassa uttaraina haunti, tāsaṃ avidūrattā sau janapadau uttarāpau’’ti vuttaṃ| aivaṃ āpasaddassa aikantaina itthiliṅgatā bahuvacanatā ca ācariyaihi icchitā, taisaṃ matai āpauiti itthiliṅgai paṭhamābahuvacanarūpai hauntai dutiyātatiyāpañ‍camīsattamīnaṃ bahuvacanarūpāni kīdisāni siyuṃ| tathā hi ‘‘purisai, purisaihi purisaibhi purisaisū’’ti rūpavatau pul‍liṅgassa viya aukārantitthiliṅgassa aikāraaihi kārādiyuttāni rūpāni katthacipi na dissanti| atau taisaṃ matai padamālānayau atīva duk‍karau|

āpasaddassa garavau, saddasatthanayaṃ pati|

bahuvacanatañ‍citthi-liṅgabhāvañ‍ca abravuṃ||

ic‍cāpasaddassa  itthiliṅgabahuvacanantatā vaiyyākaraṇānaṃ mataṃ nissāya anumatāti vaiditabbā| aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ pana āpau iti saddassa napuṃsakaliṅgaikavacanavasaina vuttau payaugau diṭṭhau ‘‘aumattaṃ pana āpau adhimattapathavīgatikaṃ jāta’’nti| jātakapāḷiyaṃ tu tassaikavacanantatā diṭṭhā| tathā hi ‘‘suciṃ sugandhaṃ salilaṃ, āpau tatthābhisandatī’’ti| imasmiṃ padaisai āpau iti saddau aikavacanaṭṭhānai ṭhitau diṭṭhau| kaicaittha vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘āpauti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ salilaṃ suci sugandhaṃ hutvā tattha abhisandatīti salilaṃsaddavasaina aikavacanappayaugau katau, na nāmasaddavasaina| āpasaddau hi aikantainitthiliṅgau caiva bahuvacanantau ca| tathā hi ‘āpau tatthābhisandantī’ti bahuvacanavasaina tappayaugau vattabbaupi chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ vacanavipal‍lāsavasaina niddiṭṭhau’’ti| tan‍na, ‘‘āpau tatthābhisandarai’’ti vattuṃ sak‍kuṇaiyyattā ‘‘tāni aj‍ja padissarai’’ti bahuvacanappayaugā viya| yasmā aivaṃ na vuttaṃ, yasmā ca pana pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘āpau labbhati, taijau labbhati, vāyau labbhatī’’ti aikavacanappayaugau dissati, tasmā ‘‘āpau’’ti saddassa aikavacanantatā pac‍cakkhatau diṭṭhāti|

athāpi cai vadaiyyuṃ – nanu pāḷiyaṃyaiva tassa bahuvacanantatā pac‍cakkhatau diṭṭhā ‘‘āpau ca daivā pathavī ca, taijau vāyau tadāgamu’’nti? tampi na| aittha hi ‘‘daivā’’ti saddaṃ apaikkhitvā ‘‘āgamu’’nti bahuvacanappayaugau katau, na ‘‘āpau’’ti saddaṃ| yadi ‘‘āpau’’ti saddaṃ sandhāya bahuvacanappayaugau katau siyā, ‘‘pathavī’’ti ‘‘taijau’’ti ‘‘vāyau’’ti ca saddampi sandhāya bahuvacanappayaugau katau siyā| aivaṃ santai pathavī taijau vāyausaddāpi bahuvacanakabhāvamāpaj‍jaiyyuṃ, na pana āpaj‍janti| na haitai bahuvacanakā, atha khau aikavacanakā aiva| rūḷhīvasaina tai pavattā pakatiāpādīsu atthaisu appavattanatau| tathā hi āpaukasiṇādīsu parikammaṃ katvā nibbattā daivā ārammaṇavasaina ‘‘āpau’’tiādināmaṃ labhantīti| aivaṃ vuttāpi tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘nanu ca bhau ‘aṅguttarāpaisū’ti bahuvacanapāḷi dissatī’’ti? tai vattabbā – asampathamavatiṇṇā tumhai, na hi tumhai saddappavattiṃ jānātha, ‘‘aṅguttarāpaisū’’ti bahuvacanaṃ pana ‘‘kurūsu aṅgaisu aṅgānaṃ magadhāna’’ntiādīni bahuvacanāni viya rūḷhīvasaina aikassāpi janapadassa vuttaṃ, na āpasaṅkhātaṃ atthaṃ sandhāya| ‘‘aṅguttarāpaisū’’ti aittha hi āpasaṅkhātau atthau upasaj‍janībhūtau, pul‍liṅgabahuvacanaina pana vuttau janapadasaṅkhātau atthauyaiva padhānau ‘‘āgatasamaṇau saṅghārāmau’’ti aittha samaṇasaṅkhātaṃ atthaṃ upasaj‍janakaṃ katvā pavattassa āgatasamaṇasaddassa saṅghārāmasaṅkhātau atthau viya, tasmā āpasaṅkhātaṃ atthaṃ gahaitvā yau aṅguttarāpau nāma janapadau, tasmiṃ aṅguttarāpaisu janapadaiti atthau vaiditabbau| tathā hi ‘‘aṅguttarāpaisu viharati āpaṇaṃ nāma aṅgānaṃ nigamau’’ti pāḷi dissati| tattha uttaraina mahāmahiyā nadiyā āpau yaisaṃ tai uttarāpā, aṅgā ca tai uttarāpā cāti aṅguttarāpā, taisu aṅguttarāpaisu| aivaṃ aikasmiṃ janapadaiyaiva bahuvacanaṃ na āpasaṅkhātai atthai, taina aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘tasmiṃ aṅguttarāpaisu janapadai’’ti| aivaṃ vuttā tai niruttarā bhavissanti|

tathāpi yai aivaṃ vadanti ‘‘āpasaddau itthiliṅgau caiva bahuvacanakaucā’’ti| tai pucchitabbā ‘‘kiṃ paṭic‍ca tumhai āyasmantau ‘āpasaddau itthiliṅgau caiva bahuvacanakau cā’ti vadathā’’ti? tai aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘‘aṅgāyaiva sau janapadau, mahiyā pana nadiyā uttaraina āpau, tāsaṃ avidūrattā uttarāpauti vuc‍catī’’ti ca ‘‘mahiyā pana nadiyā āpau tassa janapadassa uttaraina haunti, tāsaṃ avidūrattā sau janapadau uttarāpauti vuc‍catī’’ti ca aivaṃ pubbācariyaihi abhisaṅkhatau saddaracanāvisaisau dissati, tasmā itthiliṅgau caiva bahuvacanakau cā’’ti vadāmāti| sac‍caṃ dissati, sau pana saddasatthai vaiyyākaraṇānaṃ mataṃ gahaitvā abhisaṅkhatau, saddasatthañ‍ca nāma na sabbathā buddhavacanassaupakārakaṃ, aikadaisaina pana hauti, tasmā kac‍cāyanappakaraṇai icchitānicchitasaṅgahavivaj‍janaṃ kātuṃ ‘‘jinavacanayuttañhi, liṅgañ‍ca nippaj‍jatai’’ti lakkhaṇāni vuttāni|

yadi ca āpasaddau itthiliṅgabahuvacanakau, kathaṃ āpauti padaṃ sijjhatīti? āpasaddatau paṭhamāyauvacanaṃ katvā tassaukārādaisañ‍ca katvā āpauti padaṃ sijjhati ‘‘gāvau’’ti padamivāti| visamamidaṃ nidassanaṃ, ‘‘gāvau’’ti padañhi nic‍caukārantaina gausaddaina sambhūtaṃ| tathā hi yaumhi parai gausaddantassāvādaisaṃ katvā tatau yaunamaukārādaisaṃ katvā ‘‘gāvau’’ti nipphaj‍jati, āpasaddai pana dvai ādaisā na santi| buddhavacanañhi patvā āpasaddau akārantatāpakatikau jātau, na añ‍ñathāpakatikauti|

aivaṃ vuttāpi tai ‘‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ, nāñ‍ña’’nti caitasi san‍nidhāya ādhānaggāhiduppaṭinissaggibhāvai, ‘‘na vacanapac‍canīkasātaina suvijānaṃ subhāsita’’nti aivaṃ vuttapac‍canīkasātabhāvai ca ṭhatvā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘yathaiva gāvausaddau, tathaiva āpausaddau kiṃ itthiliṅgau na bhavissati bahuvacanakau cā’’ti? tatau taisaṃ imāni suttapadāni dassaitabbāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘āpaṃ āpatau sañ‍jānāti, āpaṃ āpatau  sañ‍ñatvā āpaṃ mañ‍ñati, āpasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, āpaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, āpaṃ abhinandatī’’ti aivaṃ suttapadāni dassaitvā ‘‘āpanti idaṃ kataravacana’’nti pucchitabbā| addhā tai āpasaddassa bahuvacanantabhāvamaiva icchamānā vakkhanti ‘‘dutiyābahuvacana’’nti| tai vattabbā ‘‘nanu yauvacanaṃ na suyyatī’’ti? tai vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘yauvacanaṃ kataamādaisattā na suyyatī’’ti| yaṃ yaṃ bhauntau icchanti, taṃ taṃ mukhārūḷhaṃ vadanti|

‘‘āpatau’’ti idaṃ pana kiṃ bhauntau vadantīti? ‘‘āpatau’’ti idampi ‘‘bahuvacanakaṃ taupac‍cayanta’’nti vadāma taupac‍cayassa aikatthai ca bavhatthai ca pavattanatau| iti tumhai bahuvacanakattaṃyaiva icchamānā ‘‘āpausaddau ca yauvacanantau’’ti bhaṇatha, ‘‘āpatau’’ti idampi ‘‘bahuvacanakaṃ taupac‍cayanta’’nti bhaṇatha, ‘‘āpasmiṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti aittha pana ‘‘āpasmi’’ntidaṃ kataravacanantaṃ katarādaisaina sambhūtanti? addhā tai aivaṃ puṭṭhā niruttarā bhavissanti| tathā yaisaṃ aivaṃ hauti ‘‘āpasaddau itthiliṅgau caiva bahuvacanakau cā’’ti, tai pucchitabbā ‘‘yaṃ ācariyaihi vaiyyākaraṇamataṃ gahaitvā ‘yā āpau’ti ca ‘tāsa’nti ca vuttaṃ, tattha ‘kiṃ tāsa’nti vacanai ‘āpāna’nti padaṃ ānaitvā atthau vattabbau, udāhu āpassā’’ti? ‘‘āpāna’nti padamānaitvā atthau vattabbau’’ti cai, aivañ‍ca sati ‘‘yā āpā’’ti vattabbaṃ ‘‘yā kañ‍ñā tiṭṭhantī’’ti padamiva| atha ‘‘āpā’’ti padaṃ nāma natthi, ‘‘āpau’’ti padaṃyaiva bahuvacanakanti cai, aivaṃ sati ‘‘tāsa’’nti aitthāpi ‘‘āpassā’’ti padaṃ ānaitvā atthau vaiditabbau| kasmāti cai? yasmā ‘‘āpau’’ti pac‍cattaikavacanassa tumhākaṃ mataina bahuvacanattai sati ‘‘āpassā’’ti padampi bahuvacananti katvā tāsaṃsaddaina yaujaitvā vattuṃ yuttitauti| aivaṃ sati ‘‘āpāna’’nti  padassa abhāvainaiva bhavitabbaṃ| yathā pana ‘‘purisau, purisā| purisaṃ, purisai’’ti ca, ‘‘gau, gāvau, gavau| gāvu’’nti ca aikavacanabahuvacanāni bhavanti, aivaṃ ‘‘āpau, āpā| āpaṃ, āpai’’ti aikavacanabahuvacanaihi bhavitabbaṃ| aivañ‍ca sati ‘‘āpasaddau bahuvacanakauyaiva hautī’’ti na vattabbaṃ|

yai aivaṃ vadanti, taisaṃ vacanaṃ sadausaṃ duppariharaṇīyaṃ mūlapariyāyasuttai ‘‘āpaṃ mañ‍ñati āpasmi’’nti aikavacanapāḷīnaṃ dassanatau, visuddhimaggādīsu ca ‘‘vissandanabhāvaina taṃ taṃ ṭhānaṃ āpauti appautīti āpau’’tiādikassa aikavacanavasaina vuttanibbacanassa dassanatau| yathā pana pāḷiyaṃ itthiliṅgaipi pariyāpan‍nau gausaddau ‘‘tā gāvau tatau tatau daṇḍaina ākauṭaiyyā’’ti ca ‘‘an‍nadā baladā caitā’’ti ca ādinā bavhatthadīpakaihi itthiliṅgabhūtaihi sabbanāmikapadaihi ca asabbanāmikapadaihi ca samānādhikaraṇabhāvaina vuttau dissati, na tathā pāḷiyaṃ bavhatthadīpakaihi itthiliṅgabhūtaihi sabbanāmikapadaihi vā asabbanāmikapadaihi vā samānādhikaraṇabhāvaina vuttau āpasaddau dissati| yadi hi āpasaddau itthiliṅgau siyā, kañ‍ñasaddatau āpac‍cayau viya āpasaddatau āpac‍cayau vā siyā, nadasaddatau viya ca īpac‍cayau vā siyā, ubhayampi natthi, ubhayābhāvatau itthiliṅgai vuttaṃ sabbampi vidhānaṃ tattha na labbhati, taina ñāyati ‘‘āpasaddau anitthiliṅgau’’ti| nanu ca bhau gausaddataupi āpac‍cayau natthi, tadabhāvatau itthiliṅgai vuttavidhānaṃ na labbhati, aivaṃ santai kasmā sauyaiva itthiliṅgau hauti, na panāyaṃ āpasaddauti?

aittha vuc‍catai – gausaddau na niyaugā itthiliṅgau, atha khau pul‍liṅgauva| itthiliṅgabhāvai pana tamhā āpac‍cayai  ahauntaipi īpac‍cayau vikappaina hauti, añ‍ñampi itthiliṅgai vuttavidhānaṃ labbhati| sau hi nic‍camaukārantatāpakatiyaṃ ṭhatvā ‘‘gau, gāvī’’tiādinā attanau itthiliṅgarūpānaṃ nibbattikāraṇabhūtau, taina sau itthiliṅgau bhavati| āpasaddai pana īpac‍cayādi na labbhati| taina sau itthiliṅgauti na vattabbau| yathā vā gausaddassa avisadākāravauhārataṃ paṭic‍ca itthiliṅgabhāvau upapaj‍jati, na tathā āpasaddassa| āpasaddassa hi anākularūpak‍kamattā avisadākāravauhāratā na dissati, yāya aisau itthiliṅgau siyā| aivaṃ vuttā tai niruttarā bhavissanti|

tathā yaisaṃ aivaṃ hauti ‘‘āpasaddau sabbadā itthiliṅgau caiva bahuvacanakau cā’’ti, tai vattabbā – yathā itthiliṅgabhūtassa kañ‍ñāsaddassa paṭhamaṃ kañ‍ñaiti rassavasaina ṭhapitassa āpac‍cayatau paraṃ smiṃvacanaṃ sarūpatau na tiṭṭhati, yaṃbhāvaina ca yābhāvaina ca tiṭṭhati ‘‘kañ‍ñāyaṃ, kañ‍ñāyā’’ti, na tathā ‘‘itthiliṅga’’nti tumhaihi gahitassa āpausaddassa paṭhamaṃ āpaiti rassavasaina ṭhapitassa paraṃ smiṃvacanaṃ yaṃbhāvaina ca yābhāvaina ca tiṭṭhati, atha khau sarūpatauyaiva tiṭṭhati ‘‘āpasmiṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| yadi pana āpasaddau itthiliṅgau siyā, smiṃvacanaṃ sarūpatau na tiṭṭhaiyya| yasmā ca smiṃvacanaṃ sarūpatau tiṭṭhati, tasmā āpasaddau na itthiliṅgau| na hi caturāsītidhammakkhandhasahassasaṅgahaisu anaikakauṭisatasahassaisu pāḷippadaisaisu aikasmimpi pāḷippadaisai paṭhamaṃ akārantabhāvaina ṭhapaitabbānaṃ itthiliṅgasaddānaṃ paratau ṭhitaṃ smiṃvacanaṃ sarūpatau tiṭṭhatīti| aivaṃ vuttā tai niruttarā bhavissanti|

kaici panaittha aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘āpasaddau napuṃsakaliṅgau, tathā hi aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ ‘aumattaṃ pana āpau adhimattapathavīgatikaṃ  jāta’nti napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvaina taṃsamānādhikaraṇapadāni niddiṭṭhānī’’ti? tan‍na, manaugaṇai pavattaihi tama vaca sirasaddādīhi viya āpasaddainapi samānādhikaraṇapadānaṃ katthaci napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvaina niddisitabbattā| pubbācariyānañhi saddaracanāsu ‘‘saddhammataijavihataṃ vilayaṃ khaṇaina, vainaiyyasattahadayaisu tamau payātī’’ti aittha ‘‘tamau’’tipadaina samānādhikaraṇaṃ ‘‘vihata’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ dissati, tathā ‘‘dukkhaṃ vacau aitasmiṃ vipac‍canīkasātai puggalaiti dubbacau’’ti aittha ‘‘vacau’’ti padaina samānādhikaraṇaṃ ‘‘dukkha’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ, ‘‘avanataṃ sirau yassa sau avanatasirau’’ti aittha ‘‘sirau’’ti padaina samānādhikaraṇaṃ ‘‘avanata’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ, ‘‘appaṃ rāgādirajau yaisaṃ pañ‍ñāmayai akkhimhi tai apparajakkhā’’ti aittha ‘‘rajau’’ti padaina samānādhikaraṇaṃ ‘‘appa’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ dissati| na tai ācariyā taihi samānādhikaraṇapadaihi tamavacasirasaddādīnaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgattaviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ tathāvidhaṃ saddaracanaṃ kubbiṃsu, atha khau ‘‘saubhanaṃ manau tassāti sumanau’’ti aittha viya manaugaṇai pavattapul‍liṅgānaṃ payaugai napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvainapi samānādhikaraṇapadāni katthaci hauntīti dassanatthaṃ kubbiṃsu| yathā ca ‘‘vihata’’ntiādikā saddaracanā tamavacasirasaddādīnaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgattaviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ na katā, tathā ‘‘aumatta’’nti ca ‘‘adhimattapathavīgatikaṃ jāta’’nti ca saddaracanāpi āpasaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgattaviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ na katā| yasmā pana manaugaṇai pavattaihi manasaddādīhi aikadaisaina samānagatikattā āpasaddainapi napuṃsakaliṅgassa samānādhikaraṇatā yuj‍jati, tasmā aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ ‘‘aumattaṃ pana āpau adhimattapathavīgatikaṃ jāta’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgassa āpasaddaina samānādhikaraṇatā katā| tathāpi āpasaddau  manasaddādīhi aikadaisaina samānagatikau samāsapadattai majjhaukārassa ‘‘āpaukasiṇaṃ, āpaugata’’ntiādippayaugassa dassanatau, tasmā ‘‘aumatta’’ntiādivacanaṃ āpasaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgattaviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ vuttanti na gahaitabbaṃ, liṅgavipariyayavasaina pana katthaci aivampi saddagati hautīti ñāpanatthaṃ vuttanti gahaitabbaṃ| ‘‘aumattau’’ti ca ‘‘adhimattapathavīgatikau jātau’’ti ca liṅgaṃ parivattaitabbaṃ| yadi hi āpasaddau napuṃsakaliṅgau siyā, sanikārāni’ssa pac‍cattaupayaugarūpāni buddhavacanādīsu vij‍jaiyyuṃ, na tādisāni santi| kiñ‍ci bhiyyau – aukārantaṃ nāma napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ katthacipi natthi, niggahītantaikāranta ukārantavasaina hi tividhāniyaiva napuṃsakaliṅgāni| taina āpasaddassa napuṃsakaliṅgatā nupapaj‍jatīti| aivaṃ vuttā tai niruttarā bhavissanti| ic‍caukārantavasaina gahitassa āpasaddassa itthiliṅgatā ca napuṃsakaliṅgatā ca aikantatau natthi, niggahītantavasaina pana gahitassa katthaci napuṃsakaliṅgatā siyā ‘‘bhantai nāgasaina samuddau samuddauti vuc‍cati, kaina kāraṇaina āpaṃ udakaṃ samuddauti vuc‍catī’’ti payaugadassanatau| aittha panaikai vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘yadi bhau aukārantavasaina gahitassa āpasaddassa itthinapuṃsakaliṅgavasaina dviliṅgatā natthi, aukārantau āpasaddau kataraliṅgau’’ti? pul‍liṅgauti mayaṃ vadāmāti|

yadi ca bhau āpasaddau pul‍liṅgau| yathā āpasaddassa pul‍liṅgatā pañ‍ñāyaiyya, nijjhānakkhamatā ca bhavaiyya, tathā suttaṃ āharathāti| ‘‘āharissāmisuttaṃ, na nau suttāharaṇai bhārau atthī’’ti aivañ‍ca pana vatvā taisaṃ imāni suttapadāni dassaitabbāni| saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘āpau upalabbhatīti? āmantā| āpassa kattā kāraitā upalabbhatīti? na  haivaṃ vattabbai| atītau āpau atthīti? āmantā| taina āpaina āpakaraṇīyaṃ karautīti? na haivaṃ vattabbai| āpaṃ mañ‍ñati āpasmiṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti imāni suttapadāni|

aittha ca ‘‘upalabbhatī’’tiādinā āpasaddassa aikavacanatā siddhā, tāya siddhāya bahuvacanatāpi siddhāyaiva| aikavacanatāyaiva hi saddasatthai paṭisiddhā, na bahuvacanatā, taina ‘‘āpainā’’ti iminā pana āpasaddassa itthiliṅgabhāvavigamau siddhau itthiliṅgai ainādaisābhāvatau| ‘‘āpassa, āpasmi’’nti imināpi itthiliṅgabhāvavigamauyaiva itthiliṅgai sarūpatau nā smā smiṃ vacanānamabhāvā| ‘‘atītau’’ti iminā itthiliṅganapuṃsakaliṅgabhāvavigamau aukārantanapuṃsakaliṅgassa abhāvatau, aukārantassa guṇanāmabhūtassa itthiliṅgassa ca abhāvatau|

apica buddhavacanādīsu ‘‘cittāni, rūpānī’’tiādīni viya sanikārānaṃ rūpānaṃ adassanatau aukārantabhāvaina gahitassa napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvavigamau atīva pākaṭau| aparampaittha vattabbaṃ – ‘‘atītau āpau atthīti? āmantā’’ti aittha ‘‘atītau’’ti iminā āpasaddassa visadākāravauhāratāsūcakaina aukārantapadaina tassa avisadākāravauhāratāya ca ubhayamuttākāravauhāratāya ca abhāvau siddhau| tassa ca avisadākāravauhāratāya abhāvai siddhai itthiliṅgabhāvau dūratarau| ubhayamuttākāravauhāratāya ca abhāvai siddhai napuṃsakaliṅgabhāvaupi dūratarauyaiva| iti na katthacipi aukārantabhāvaina gahitau āpasaddau itthiliṅgau vā napuṃsakaliṅgau vā bhavati| milindapañhai pana niggahītantavasaina āgatau napuṃsakaliṅgauti vaiditabbau, na caittha vattabbaṃ ‘‘atītau’’ti ‘‘tainā’’ti  ca imāni liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina vuttānīti vāc‍caliṅgānamanuvattāpakassa abhidhaiyyaliṅgabhūtassa āpasaddassa ‘‘kañ‍ñāya cittānī’’tiādīnaṃ viya itthinapuṃsakaliṅgarūpānaṃ abhāvatau| apica vauhārakusalā tathāgatā tathāgatasāvakā ca, taihiyaiva uttamapurisaihi vauhārakusalaihi ‘‘atītau āpau’’tiādinā vuttattāpi ‘‘atītau’’ti ‘‘tainā’’ti ca imāni liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina vuttānīti na cintaitabbāni, tasmā taṃsamānādhikaraṇau aukārantabhāvaina gahitau āpasaddau aikavacanantau pul‍liṅgau caiva yathāpayaugaṃ aikavacanabahuvacanakau cāti vaiditabbau ‘‘āpau, āpā| āpaṃ, āpai’’tiādinā yaujaitabbattā| aivaṃ vuttāni suttapadāni savinicchayāni sutvā addhā tai āpasaddassa itthiliṅgabahuvacanatāvādinau niruttarā bhavissanti|

aittha kauci vadaiyya – pāḷiyaṃ pul‍liṅganayau aikavacananayau ca kiṃ aṭṭhakathāṭīkācariyaihi na diṭṭhau, yai āpasaddassa itthiliṅgabahuvacanattaṃ vaṇṇaisunti? nau na diṭṭhau, diṭṭhauyaiva sau nayau taihi| yasmā pana tai na kaivalaṃ sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai buddhavacanaiyaiva visāradā, atha khau sakalaipi saddasatthai visāradā, tasmā saddasatthai attanau paṇḍic‍caṃ pakāsaituṃ, ‘‘saddasatthai ca īdisau nayau vuttau’’ti viñ‍ñāpaituñ‍ca saddasatthanayaṃ gahaitvā āpasaddassa itthiliṅgabahuvacanakattaṃ vaṇṇaisunti natthi taisaṃ dausau| tathā hi mūlapariyāyasuttantaṭṭhakathāyaṃ taihiyaiva vuttaṃ āpasaddassa pul‍liṅgaikavacanakattasūcanakaṃ ‘‘lakkhaṇasasambhārārammaṇasammutivasaina catubbidhau āpau, taisū’’tiādi, tasmā natthi taisaṃ dausau| pūjārahā hi tai āyasmantau, namauyaiva taisaṃ  karauma, na taisaṃ vacanaṃ caudanābhājanaṃ| yai pana ujuvipac‍canīkavādā daḷhamaiva āpasaddassa itthiliṅgabahuvacanattaṃ mamāyanti, taisaṃyaiva vacanaṃ caudanābhājanaṃ| yasmā pana mayaṃ pāḷinayānusāraina antadvayavatau āpasaddassa pul‍liṅgattaṃ napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca vidadhāma, tasmā yau kauci idaṃ vādaṃ madditvā añ‍ñaṃ vādaṃ patiṭṭhāpaituṃ sakkhissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, idañ‍ca pana ṭhānaṃ mahāgahanaṃ duppaṭivijjhanaṭṭhaina, paramasukhumañ‍ca katañāṇasambhāraihi paramasukhumañāṇaihi paṇḍitaihi vaidanīyattā| sabbamidañhi vacanaṃ taisu taisu ṭhānaisu atthabyañ‍janapariggahaṇai sautūnaṃ paramakausal‍lajananatthañ‍caiva sāsanai ādaraṃ akatvā saddasatthamataina kālaṃ vītināmaintānaṃ sāthalikānaṃ pamādavihāranisaidhanatthañ‍ca sāsanassātimahantabhāvadīpanatthañ‍ca vuttaṃ, na attuk‍kaṃsanaparavambhanatthanti imissaṃ nītiyaṃ saddhāsampan‍naihi kulaputtaihi yaugau karaṇīyau bhagavatau sāsanassa ciraṭṭhitatthaṃ|

yasmā pana pāḷitau aṭṭhakathā balavatī nāma natthi, tasmā pāḷinayānurūpainaiva āpasaddassa nāmikapadamālaṃ yaujaissāma sautūnamasammauhatthaṃ, kimaittha saddasatthanayau karissati| atrāyaṃ udānapāḷi ‘‘kiṃ kayirā udapānaina, āpā cai sabbadā siyu’’nti|

āpau, āpā| āpaṃ, āpai| āpaina, āpaihi, āpaibhi| āpassa, āpānaṃ| āpā, āpasmā, āpamhā, āpaihi, āpaibhi| āpassa, āpānaṃ| āpai, āpasmiṃ, āpamhi, āpaisu| bhau āpa, bhavantau āpā|

sabbanāmādīhipi yaujaissāma – yau āpau, yai āpā| yaṃ āpaṃ, yai āpai| yaina āpaina, saisaṃ naiyyaṃ, sau āpau, tai āpā| atītau āpau, atītā āpā| saisaṃ naiyyaṃ| ic‍caivaṃ –

purisaina samā āpa-saddādī sabbathā matā|

na sabbathāva gausaddau, purisaina samau matau||

manādī aikadaisaina, purisaina samā matā|

sarādī aikadaisaina, sabbathā vā samā matā||

yai panaittha saddā ‘‘manaugaṇau’’ti vuttā, kathaṃ taisaṃ manaugaṇabhāvau sal‍lakkhaitabbauti? vuc‍catai taisaṃ manaugaṇabhāvasal‍lakkhaṇakāraṇaṃ –

manaugaṇau manaugaṇā-

dikā caivā’manaugaṇau|

iti saddā tidhā ñaiyyā,

manaugaṇavibhāvanai||

yai tai nā sa smiṃvisayai,

sā sau syantā bhavanti ca|

samāsataddhitantattai,

majjhaukārā ca haunti hi||

saukārantapayaugā ca, kriyāyaugamhi dissarai|

aivaṃvidhā ca tai saddā, ñaiyyā ‘‘manaugaṇau’’iti||

atra tassatthassa sādhakāni payaugāni sāsanatau ca laukatau ca yathārahamāharitvā dassaissāma – manasā cai pasan‍naina, bhāsati vā karauti vā| na mayhaṃ manasau piyau| sādhukaṃ manasi karautha| manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā| manauramaṃ, manaudhātu, manaumayaina kāyaina, iddhiyā upasaṅkami| yau vai ‘‘dassa’’nti vatvāna, adānai kurutai manau| vacasā paricitā| vacasau vacasi|

vacaurasmīhi  baudhaisi, vainaiyyakumudañ‍cidaṃ|

rāgau sārāgarahitau, visuddhau buddhacandimā||

kassapassa vacau sutvā, alātau aitadabravi|

aisa bhiyyau pasīdāmi, sutvāna muninau vacau||

sakhā ca mittau ca mamāsi sīvika, susikkhitau sādhu karauhi mai vacau| aikūnatiṃsau vayasā subhadda| vayasau, vayasi, vayauvuddhau, vayauguṇā anupubbaṃ jahanti| jalantamiva taijasā| taijasau, taijasi, taijaudhātukusalau| taijaukasiṇaṃ| tapasā uttamau, tapasau, tapasi, tapaudhanau, tapaujigucchā| kasmā bhavaṃ vijanamarañ‍ñanissitau, tapau idha krubbasi brahmapattiyā| caitasā añ‍ñāsi, aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi, aitamatthaṃ caitasi san‍nidhāya, caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya| caitaupariyañāṇaṃ, caitau paricchindati, sau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca jānāti| tamasā, tamasau, tamasi, tamaunudau, tamauharau| navāhamaitaṃ yasasā dadāmi| yasasau, yasasi, yasau bhaugasamappitau| yasauladdhākhau panasmākaṃ bhaugā| yasaudharā daivī, yasau laddhā na maj‍jaiyya| ayasāva malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ| ayasau, ayasi, ayaupākārapariyantaṃ, ayasā paṭikuj‍jitaṃ| saiyyau ayauguḷau bhuttau, ayaupattau, ayaumayaṃ, ayau kantatīti ayaukantau| ghataina vā bhuñ‍jassu payasā vā, sādhu khalu payasau pānaṃ yañ‍ñadattaina, payasi aujā, payaudharā, payaunidhi| sahassanaittau sirasā paṭiggahi| sirasau, sirasi añ‍jaliṃ katvā, vanditabbaṃ isiddhajaṃ| sirauruhā, sirau chindati| yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva padāsirau| sirau tai vajjhayitvāna| sarasā, sarasau, tīṇi uppalajātāni, tasmiṃ sarasi brāhmaṇa| sarauruhaṃ| yaṃ aitā upasaivanti, chandasāvā dhanainavā| sāvittī chandasau mukhaṃ| chandasi, chandauviciti, chandaubhaṅgau, urasā panudahissāmi, urasau, urasi jāyati, urasilaumau, uraumajjhai vijjhi| rahasā, rahasau, rahasi, rahaugatau nisīditvā, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā| ahasā, ahasi| jāyanti tattha pārauhā, ahaurattānamac‍cayaiti imāni payaugāni| aittha ca ‘‘manaina, manassa, manai, manasmiṃ, manamhī’’tiādīni ca ‘‘manaāyatanaṃ tamaparāyanau ayapattau chandahānī’’tiādīni ca ‘‘na manaṃ añ‍ñāsi| yasaṃ laddhāna dummaidhau| ‘‘siraṃ chindatī’’tiādīni ca rūpāni manaugaṇabhāvappakāsakāni na hauntīti na dassitāni, na alabbhamānavasaina, tasmātra imā āditau paṭṭhāya manaugaṇabhāvavibhāvinī gāthāyau bhavanti –

‘‘manasā  manasau manasi’’,

itiādivasā ṭhitā|

sā sau syantā saddarūpā,

vuttā ‘‘manaugaṇau’’iti||

manaudhātu vacaurasmi,

vayauvuddhau tapauguṇau|

taijaudhātu tamaunāsau,

yasaubhaugasamappitau||

caitauparivitak‍kau ca, ayaupattau payaudharā|

sirauruhā sarauruhaṃ, uraumajjhai rahaugatau||

chandaubhaṅgau ahaurattaṃ, manaumaya’mayaumayaṃ|

aivaṃvidhau visaisau yau, lakkhaṇantaṃ manaugaṇai||

‘‘vacau sutvā, sirau chindi, ayau kantati’’ ic‍capi|

upayaugassa saṃsiddhi, lakkhaṇantaṃ manaugaṇai||

manaugaṇai vuttanayau, itthiliṅgai na labbhati|

pun‍napuṃsakaliṅgaisu, labbhataiva yathārahaṃ||

ic‍caivaṃ sabbathāpi –

sā sau syantāni rūpāni, sandissanti manaugaṇai|

majjhaukārantarūpā ca, saukārantūpayaugatā||

idaṃ manaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| aivaṃ manaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ anākulaṃ niggumbaṃ nij‍jaṭaṃ samuddiṭṭhaṃ|

atha manaugaṇādilakkhaṇaṃ kathayāma –

yai tai nā sasmiṃvisayai,

sā sau syantā yathārahaṃ|

samāsataddhitantattai,

majjhaukārau na haunti tu||

saukārantūpayaugā  ca,

kriyāyaugai na haunti tai|

saddā aivaṃvidhā sabbai|

manaugaṇādikā matā||

saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘bilaṃ padaṃ mukha’’mic‍cādayau| taisaṃ rūpāni bhavanti – bilasā, bilasau, bilasi, bilagatau, bilaṃ pāvisi| padasāva agamāsi, tīṇi padavārāni, mākāsi mukhasā pāpaṃ, mukhagataṃ bhaujanaṃ chaḍḍāpaiti| sac‍caina dantau damasā upaitau| rasavaraṃ rasamayaṃ rasaṃ pivīti| idaṃ manaugaṇādikalakkhaṇaṃ|

aparampi bhavati –

yai samāsādibhāvamhi, majjhaukārāva haunti tu|

nā sa smiṃvisayai sāsau-syantā pana na hauntihi||

saukārantūpayaugā ca,

kriyāyaugai na haunti tai|

saddā aivaṃvidhā cāpi,

manaugaṇādikā matā||

saiyyathidaṃ? ‘‘āpau vāyau saradau’’ic‍caivamādayau| taisaṃ rūpāni bhavanti – āpaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, āpaukasiṇaṃ, vāyaukasiṇaṃ, āpaumayaṃ, vāyaumayaṃ, jīva tvaṃ saradausataṃ, saradakālau| āpaina, āpassa, āpai, āpasmiṃ, āpamhi| vāyaina, vāyassa, vāyai, vāyasmiṃ, vāyamhi| saradaina, saradassa, saradai, saradasmiṃ, saradamhi| āpaṃ āpatau sañ‍jānāti| vāyaṃ vāyatau sañ‍jānāti| saradaṃ patthaiti, saradaṃ ramaṇīyā nadī|

kaici  panaittha vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘nanu sāsanai vāyasaddau viya vāyusaddaupi manaugaṇādīsu icchitabbau’’ti? aittha vuc‍catai –

‘‘vāyu vāyau’’ti aitaisu, pacchimauyaiva icchitau|

manaugaṇādīsu nādi, ādiggahavasainidha||

‘‘manaudhātu vāyaudhātu’’, ic‍cādīni padāni hi|

akārantavasainaiva, majjhaukārāni sijjharai||

vāyusaddamhi gahitai, ādiggahavasainidha|

‘‘vāyaudhātū’’ti aumajjhaṃ, rūpamaiva na haissati||

yathā hi āyusaddassa, rūpaṃ dissati sāgamaṃ|

‘‘āyusā aikaputta’’nti, manasādipadaṃ viya||

na tathā vāyusaddassa, rūpaṃ dissati sāgamaṃ|

tasmā manaugaṇādimhi, tassau’kāsau na vij‍jati||

tathā hi ‘‘vāyati iti, vāyau’’ iti garū vaduṃ|

‘‘vāyaudhātū’’ti aitassa, padassatthaṃ tahiṃ tahiṃ||

yattha pathavī ca āpau ca, taijau vāyau na gādhati|

aittha āpādikaṃ sadda-ttikaṃ manaugaṇādikai||

idampi manaugaṇādikalakkhaṇaṃ| aittha manaugaṇādikā dvidhā bhij‍janti bila padāditau āpāditau ca| aivaṃ manaugaṇādikalakkhaṇaṃ anākulaṃ niggumbaṃ nij‍jaṭaṃ samuddiṭṭhaṃ|

atha amanaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ kathayāma –

yai ca nāvisayai sauntā, yai ca smāvisayai siyuṃ|

saddā aivaṃpakārā tai, amanaugaṇasañ‍ñitā||

kai  tai? atthabyañ‍janakkharasaddādayau caiva dīghaurasaddā ca| aitaisu hi atthasaddādīnaṃ nāvacanaṭṭhānai ‘‘atthasau byañ‍janasau akkharasau suttasau upāyasau sabbasau ṭhānasau’’tiādīni sauntāni rūpāni bhavanti| dīghaurasaddānaṃ pana smāvacanaṭṭhānai ‘‘dīghasau aurasau’’ti sauntāni rūpāni bhavanti| idaṃ amanaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ|

aparampi bhavati –

sabbathā vinimuttā yai, sā sau syantādibhāvatau|

aivaṃvidhāpi tai saddā, amanaugaṇasañ‍ñitā||

kai tai? ‘‘purisau kañ‍ñā citta’’mic‍cādayau| idampi amanaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ| aivaṃ amanaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ anākulaṃ niggumbaṃ nij‍jaṭaṃ samuddiṭṭhaṃ|

aivaṃ dassitaisu manaugaṇalakkhaṇādīsu kauci vadaiyya ‘‘yadidaṃ tumhaihi vuttaṃ ‘yai samāsādibhāvamhi, majjhaukārāva haunti tū’tiādinā manaugaṇādikalakkhaṇaṃ, taina ‘‘parausataṃ gaumayaṃ gaudhanau’’ic‍cādīsu gauparasaddādayaupi manaugaṇādikabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jantīti? nāpaj‍janti| kasmāti cai? yasmā –

aittha manaugaṇādīnaṃ, antassauttaṃ paṭic‍cidaṃ|

‘‘majjhaukārā’’ti vacanaṃ, vuttaṃ na tvāgamādikaṃ||

‘‘parausataṃ gaumaya’’nti-ādīsu amanaugaṇau|

pubbabhūtaṃ padaṃ aussā-gamattā’nic‍catāya ca||

tasmā nāpaj‍janti| iti sabbathāpi amanaugaṇalakkhaṇaṃ nissaisatau dassitaṃ| ic‍caivaṃ manaugaṇavibhāvanāyaṃ manaugaṇau manaugaṇādikau amanaugaṇau cāti tidhā bhaidau vaiditabbau|

tattha  manaugaṇai pariyāpan‍nasaddānaṃ samāsaṃ patvā ‘‘abyaggamanasau narau, thiracaitasaṃ kulaṃ, saddhaiyyavacasā upāsikātiādinā liṅgattayavasaina añ‍ñathāpi rūpāni bhavanti| aittha pana kaici aivaṃ vadanti ‘‘yadā manasaddau sakatthai avattitvā ‘abyaggau manau yassa sauyaṃ abyaggamanasau, alīnau manau yassa sauyaṃ alīnamanasau’ti aivaṃ añ‍ñatthai vattati, tadā purisanayainaiva nāmikapadamālā labbhati, na manaugaṇanayainā’’ti| taṃ na gahaitabbaṃ ubhin‍nampi yathārahaṃ labbhanatau| tathā hi visuddhimaggai puggalāpaikkhanavasaina ‘‘khantisaurac‍camaittādi-guṇabhūsitacaitasau| ajjhaisanaṃ gahaitvānā’’ti aittha manaugaṇanayau dissati| taṭṭīkāyampi ‘‘ajjhaisitau dāṭhānāga-tthairaina thiracaitasā’’ti manaugaṇanayau dissati, tasmā taisaṃ vacanaṃ na gahaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ vadantā ca tai abyaggamanasaddādīnaṃ abyaggamanasaic‍cādinā sakārantapakatibhāvaina ṭhapaitabbabhāvaṃ vibbhantamativasaina cintaitvā sabbāsu vibhattīsu, dvīsu ca vacanaisu purisanayaina yaujaitabbataṃ mañ‍ñanti| aivañ‍ca sati ‘‘guṇabhūsitacaitasau, thiracaitasā’’ti chaṭṭhīcatutthītatiyārūpāni nasiyuṃ, añ‍ñāniyaiva anabhimatāni rūpāni siyuṃ| yasmā siyuṃ, tasmā aivaṃ aggahaitvā ayaṃ visaisau gahaitabbau|

yattha hi samāsavasaina manasaddau caitasaddādayau ca sakatthai avattitvā añ‍ñatthai vattanti, tattha sakārāgamānaṃ padānaṃ nāmikapadamālā purisanayaina ca manaugaṇai mananayaina ca yathārahaṃ labbhati| nissakārāgamānaṃ pana purisanayainaiva labbhati| yattha pana samāsavisayaiyaiva manādisaddā sakatthai vattanti, tattha nissakārāgamānaṃ nāmikapadamālā purisanayaina ca manaugaṇai mananayaina ca labbhati|

idāni  imassatthassa āvibhāvatthaṃ, saddagatīsu ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ kausal‍luppādanatthaṃ yathāvuttānaṃ padānaṃ padamālā tidhā katvā dassayissāma – ‘‘byāsattau manau yassa sauyaṃ byāsattamanasau narau’’ti aivamac‍cantaṃ puggalāpaikkhakassa imassa padassa –

byāsattamanasau narau, byāsattamanasā narā| byāsattamanasaṃ naraṃ, byāsattamanasai narai| byāsattamanasā, byāsattamanaina naraina, byāsattamanaihi, byāsattamanaibhi naraihi| byāsattamanasau, byāsattamanassa narassa, byāsattamanānaṃ narānaṃ| byāsattamanā, byāsattamanasmā, byāsattamanamhā narā, byāsattamanaihi, byāsattamanaibhi naraihi| byāsattamanasau, byāsattamanassa narassa, byāsattamanānaṃ narānaṃ| byāsattamanasi, byāsattamanai, byāsattamanasmiṃ, byāsattamanamhi narai, byāsattamanaisu naraisu| bhau byāsattamanasa nara, bhavantau byāsattamanasā narāti nāmikapadamālā bhavati|

aivaṃ sakārāgamassa labbhamānālabbhamānatā vavatthapaitabbā| aittha hi paṭhamādutiyāvibhattīnaṃ aikavacanabahuvacanaṭṭhānai ca tatiyācatutthīchaṭṭhīsattamīnaṃ aikavacanaṭṭhānai ca yathārahaṃ sāgamau bhavati ādaisasaravibhattisaraparattā| ayañ‍ca nayau sukhumau sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

aparau nayau – ‘‘byāsattau manau yassa sauyaṃ byāsattamanau’’ti aivampi puggalāpaikkhakassa imassa padassa ‘‘byāsattamanau narau, byāsattamanā narā| byāsattamanaṃ nara’’ntiādinā purisanayainaiva nāmikapadamālā bhavati| aittha pana sabbathāpi sāgamau natthi| aparaupi nayau – ‘‘byāsattau ca sau manau cāti byāsattamanau’’ti aivaṃ cittāpaikkhakassapi imassa padassa ‘‘byāsattamanau, byāsattamanā| byāsattamanaṃ, byāsattamanai| byāsattamanasā, byāsattamanainā’’tiādinā manaugaṇai mananayaina nāmikapadamālā bhavati, aittha pana tatiyācatutthīchaṭṭhīsattamīnaṃ  aikavacanaṭṭhānaiyaiva sāgamau bhavati ādaisasaraparattā| yathā ca aittha, aivaṃ ‘‘alīnamanasau narau’’tiādīsupi ayaṃ tividhau nayau vaiditabbau|

napuṃsakaliṅgai pana vattabbai ‘‘byāsattamanasaṃ kulaṃ, byāsattamanāni kulāni| byāsattamanasaṃ kulaṃ, byāsattamanāni kulāni| byāsattamanasā kulainā’’tiādinā nāmikapadamālā yaujaitabbā| aittha pana paṭhamādutiyātatiyācatutthīchaṭṭhīsattamīnaṃ aikavacanaṭṭhānaiyaiva yathārahaṃ sāgamau bhavati ādaisasaravibhattisaraparattā, ayampi nayau sukhumau sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

itthiliṅgai pana vattabbai ‘‘byāsattamanasā itthī’’ti aivaṃ paṭhamaikavacanaṭṭhānaiyaiva sāgamaṃ vatvā tatau ‘‘byāsattamanā, byāsattamanāyau itthiyau| byāsattamanaṃ itthi’’nti kañ‍ñānayaina yaujaitabbā| ‘‘aivaṃ saddhaiyyavacasā upāsikā, saddhaiyyavacāyau upāsikāyau| saddhaiyyavacaṃ upāsika’’ntiādināpi| ‘‘byāsattamanaṃ kulaṃ, byāsattamanā itthī’’tiādinā pana cittakañ‍ñānayaina yaujaitabbā| aittha pana sabbathāpi sāgamau natthi|

sautūnaṃ ñāṇappabhaidajananatthaṃ aparāpi nāmikapadamālāyau dassayissāma sahanibbacanaina – manau aiva mānasaṃ, samussāhitaṃ mānasaṃ yassa sauyaṃ samussāhitamānasau| ‘‘samussāhitamānasau, samussāhitamānasā| samussāhitamānasaṃ, samussāhitamānasai| samussāhitamānasainā’’ti purisanayaina yaujaitabbā| sundarā maidhā assa atthīti sumaidhasau| ‘‘sumaidhasau, sumaidhasā| sumaidhasaṃ, sumaidhasai| sumaidhasainā’’ti purisanayaina, aivaṃ ‘‘bhūrimaidhasau’’tiādīnampi| tatrimai payaugā –

‘‘yaṃ  vadanti sumaidhauti, bhūripañ‍ñaṃ sumaidhasaṃ|

kiṃ nu tamhā vippavasi, muhuttamapi piṅgiya|

gautamā bhūripañ‍ñāṇā, gautamā bhūrimaidhasā||

nāhaṃ tamhā vippavasāmi, muhuttamapi brāhmaṇa|

gautamā bhūripañ‍ñāṇā, gautamā bhūrimaidhasā’’ti||

itthiliṅgai vattabbai ‘‘samussāhitamānasā sumaidhasā’’ti rūpāni, napuṃsakai vattabbai ‘‘samussāhitamānasaṃ sumaidhasa’’nti rūpāni, kañ‍ñā cittanayaina aitaisaṃ padamālā yaujaitabbā| aukārantapul‍liṅgaṭṭhānai itthiliṅgādivinicchayau nayappakāsanatthaṃ katau| visaisatau hi aukārantakathāyaiva idhādhippaitā| apica laukai nīti nāma nānappakāraihi kathitā aiva saubhati, ayañ‍ca sāsanai nīti, tasmā nānappakāraihi kathitāti|

sabbāni nayatau aivaṃ, aukārantapadānimai|

pul‍liṅgāni pavuttāni, sāsanatthaṃ mahaisinau||

visaisau taisu kaisañ‍ci, pāḷiyaṃ yau padissati|

pac‍cattavacanaṭṭhānai, pakāsaissāmi taṃ’dhunā||

‘‘vanappagumbai yatha phussitaggai’’, itiādinayaina hi|

katthacaudantapul‍liṅga-rūpāni añ‍ñathā siyuṃ||

pac‍cattavacanic‍caiva, tañ‍ca rūpaṃ pakāsayai|

‘‘pac‍cattai bhummaniddaisau’’, iti bhāsanti kaicana||

tatra kānici suttapadāni dassaissāma – natthi attakārai, natthi parakārai, natthi purisakārai, pariyantakatai saṃsārai, jīvai sattamai, na haivaṃ vattabbai, bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantīti| imāni  aikavacanabahuvacanavasaina dvidhā gahaitabbāni| pac‍cattaikavacanabahuvacanānañ‍ca aikārādaisau vaiditabbau|

yai pana ‘‘vanappagumbaiti pac‍cattavacanassa bhummavacananiddaisau’’ti vadanti, tai vattabbā ‘‘yadi vanappagumbaiti pac‍cattavacanassa bhummavacananiddaisau, aivañ‍ca sati ‘thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacatī’ti aittha viya ādhārasutisambhavatau ‘gimhāna māsai paṭhamasmiṃ gimhai’ti idaṃ kataratthaṃ jautaitī’’ti? tai vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘na mayaṃ bhau ‘vanappagumbaiti idaṃ bhummavacana’nti vadāma, atha khau ‘pac‍cattavacanassa bhummavacananiddaisau’ti vadāmā’’ti| aivampi dausauyaiva tumhākaṃ, nanu ‘‘saṅghai gautami daihī’’ti aitthāpi sampadānavacanassa bhummavacananiddaisauti vuttaipi saṅghassa dānakriyāya ādhārabhāvatau ‘‘saṅghai’’ti vacanaṃ suṇantānaṃ ādhārasuti ca ādhāraparikappau ca hautiyaiva| na hi sak‍kā aivaṃ pavattaṃ cittaṃ nivāraituṃ, tasmā aittha aivaṃ pana visaisau gahaitabbau ‘‘pac‍cattavacanassapi katthaci bhummavacanassa viya rūpaṃ hautī’’ti| aivañhi gahitai na kauci viraudhau| īdisaisu hi ṭhānaisu niruttippabhaidakusalau laukānukampakau bhagavā pac‍cattavacanavasaina niddisitabbai sati aivaṃ aniddisitvā laukassa sammauhamuppādayantau viya kathaṃ bhummavacananiddaisaṃ karissati, tasmā saddasāmañ‍ñalaisamattaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘bhummavacananiddaisau’’ti na vattabbaṃ| yadi saddasāmañ‍ñaṃ gahaitvā bhummavacananiddaisaṃ icchatha, ‘‘pac‍cattaikavacanassa upayaugabahuvacananiddaisau’’tipi icchitabbaṃ siyā|

apica tathaiva ‘‘attakārai’’ti pac‍cattavacanassa bhummavacananiddaisai sati ādhārasutisambhavatau ‘‘attakārasmiṃ kiñ‍ci vatthu natthī’’ti anadhippaitau atthau siyā, na pana ‘‘attakārau natthī’’ti adhippaitau atthau| ‘‘upayaugabahuvacananiddaisau’’ti gahaṇaipi upayaugatthassa natthisaddaina  avattabbattā dausauyaiva siyā, atthisaddādīnaṃ viya pana natthisaddassapi paṭhamāya yaugatau ‘‘attakārai’’ti idaṃ pac‍cattavacanamaivāti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantī’’ti aitthāpi pac‍cattavacanassa ‘‘bhummavacananiddaisau’’ti vā ‘‘upayaugavacananiddaisau’’ti vā gahaṇai sati ‘‘bālā ca paṇḍitā cā’’ti aittakampi vattuṃ ajānanadausau siyā, ‘‘karissantī’’ti padayaugatau pana ‘‘bālai cā’’tiādi pac‍cattavacanamaivāti viñ‍ñāyati| yathā pana niggahītāgamavasainuc‍cāritai ‘‘cakkhuṃ udapādī’’ti padai pac‍cattavacanassa ‘‘cakkhuṃ mai daihi yācitau’’ti aittha upayaugavacanaina sutivasaina samānattaipi pac‍cattavacanatthauyaiva sautārai paṭibhāti ‘‘udapādī’’tiākhyātaina kathitattā, na pana vibhattivipal‍lāsatthabhūtau upayaugavacanatthau ‘‘udapādī’’tiākhyātaina avacanīyattā, ‘‘cakkhuṃ udapādī’’ti hi bhagavatā vuttakālai kau ‘‘cakkhuṃ udapādī’’ti padaṃ parivattitvā atthamācikkhati, tathā ‘‘bālai paṇḍitai’’tiādīnampi pac‍cattavacanānaṃ aparaihi ‘‘bālai paṇḍitai’’tiādīhi bhummaupayaugavacanaihi sutivasaina samānattaipi pac‍cattavacanatthauyaiva sautārai paṭibhāti, na itaravacanatthau yathāpayaugaṃ atthassa gahaitabbattā| iti ‘‘vanappagumbai, bālai, paṇḍitai’’tiādīnaṃ suddhapac‍cattavacanattañ‍ñaiva sāratau pac‍caitabbaṃ, na sutisāmañ‍ñaina bhummaupayaugavacanattaṃ|

yaṃ panācariyaina jātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ –

‘‘tayau giriṃ tiantaraṃ kāmayāmi,

pañ‍cālā kuruyau kaikakai ca|

tatuttariṃ brāhmaṇa kāmayāmi,

tikiccha maṃ brāhmaṇa kāmanīta’’nti –

imassa  kāmanītajātakassa saṃvaṇṇanāyaṃ ‘‘kaikakai cāti pac‍cattai upayaugavacanaṃ, taina kaikakassa raṭṭhaṃ dassaitī’’ti vuttaṃ| aivaṃ vadantau ca sau ‘‘purisai passati, purisai patiṭṭhita’’nti, ‘‘passāmi laukai sadhanai manussai’’ti ca ādīsu yaibhuyyaina ‘‘purisai, laukai, sadhanai, manussai’’tiādīnaṃ upayaugabahuvacanabhummaikavacanabhāvaina āgatattā pac‍cattaikavacanabahuvacanabhāvassa pana apākaṭattā yaibhuyyappavattiṃ sandhāya ‘‘idampi tādisamaivā’’ti mañ‍ñamānau vadati mañ‍ñai| ācariyā hi katthaci attanau ruciyāpi visuṃ visuṃ kathainti| ayaṃ pana amhākaṃ ruci – ‘‘kaikakai’’ti idaṃ pac‍cattavacanamaiva ‘‘pañ‍cālā, kuruyau’’ti sahajātapadāni viya, raṭṭhavācakattā pana ‘‘kuruyau’’ti padamiva bahuvacanavasaina vuttaṃ| na hi bhagavā ‘‘khattiyau, brāhmaṇau, vaissau’’tiādīsu viya samānavibhattīhi niddisitabbaisu sahajātapadaisu pacchimaṃ upayaugavacanavasaina niddisaiyya, yutti ca na dissati ‘‘pañ‍cālā’’ti, ‘‘kuruyau’’ti pac‍cattavacanaṃ vatvā ‘‘kaikakai’’ti upayaugavacanassa vacanai, tasmā ‘‘kaikakai’’ti idaṃ pac‍cattavacanamaiva| tathā hi sandhivisaudhanavidhāyakau ācariyau tādisānaṃ padānaṃ pac‍cattavacanattañ‍ñaiva vibhāvaintau sāmaṃ katai pakaraṇai ‘‘vanappagumbau vanappagumbai, sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ jīvau, sukhai dukkhai jīvai’’ti āha, ṭīkāyampi ca taisaṃ pac‍cattavacanabhāvamaiva vibhāvaintau ‘‘vanappagumbau, sukhaṃ, dukkhaṃ, jīvau’’ti sādhanīyaṃ rūpaṃ patiṭṭhapaitvā niggahītalaupavasaina akāraukārānañ‍ca aikārādaisavasaina ‘‘vanappagumbai, sukhai, dukkhai, jīvai’’ti rūpanipphattimāha| sā pāḷinayānu kūlā| kac‍cāyanācariyainapi pāḷinayaṃ nissāya ‘‘dvipadai tulyādhikaraṇai’’ti pac‍cattabahuvacanapadaṃ vuttaṃ| taināha vuttiyaṃ ‘‘dvai padāni tulyādhikaraṇānī’’ti| ‘‘dvipadai tulyādhikaraṇai’’ti  ca idaṃ ‘‘aṭṭha nāgāvāsasatānī’’ti vattabbai ‘‘aṭṭha nāgāvāsasatai’’ti padamiva vuc‍catīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kaici pana taisaṃ bhummaikavacanattaṃ icchanti| tattha yadi ‘‘vanappagumbai’’ti pac‍cattai bhummavacanaṃ, ‘‘kaikakai’’ti ca pac‍cattai upayaugavacanaṃ, ‘‘aisaisai aikai aikaṭṭhai’’ti aittha ‘‘aisaisai’’ti imānipi pac‍cattai bhummavacanāni vā siyuṃ, upayaugavacanāni vā| yathaitāni aivaṃvidhāni na haunti, suddhapac‍cattavacanāniyaiva haunti, tathā ‘‘vanappagumbai, kaikakai’’tiādīnipi tathāvidhāni na haunti, suddhapac‍cattavacanāniyaiva haunti| ic‍caivaṃ sabbathāpi ‘‘vanappagumbai, bālai, paṇḍitai, kaikakai’’ti, ‘‘virattai kausiyāyanai, aṭṭha nāgāvāsasatai, kai purisai, aisaisai’’ti aivamādīnaṃ anaikaisaṃ purisaliṅgaitthiliṅganapuṃsakaliṅgasabbanāmaaikavacanaanaikavacanavasaina sāsanavarai ṭhitānaṃ padānaṃ nipphatti pac‍cattaikavacanaputhuvacanānamaikārādaisavasainaiva bhavatīti avassamidaṃ sampaṭicchitabbaṃ| aivaṃ ‘‘vanappagumbai, bālai, paṇḍitai’’tiādīnaṃ suddhapac‍cattavacanatā atīva sukhumā dubbiñ‍ñaiyyā, saddhaina kulaputtaina ācariyai payirupāsitvā tadupadaisaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ gahaitvā jānitabbā| buddhavacanasmiñhi saddatau ca atthatau ca adhippāyatau ca akkharacintakānaṃ ñāṇacakkhusammuyhanaṭṭhānabhūtā pāḷinayā vividhā dissanti|

tattha saddatau tāva idaṃ sammuyhanaṭṭhānaṃ – ‘‘virattā kausiyāyanī’’ti vattabbai ‘‘virattai kausiyāyanai’’ti itthiliṅgapac‍cattavacanaṃ dissati, ‘‘kau purisau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘kai purisai’’ti sabbanāmikapac‍cattavacanaṃ dissati, ‘‘kin‍nāmau tai upajjhāyau’’ti vattabbai ‘‘kaunāmau tai upajjhāyau’’ti samāsapadaṃ pul‍liṅgavisayaṃ dissati| kiṃ nāmaṃ aitassāti  kaunāmauti hi samāsau| taina ‘‘kaunāmā itthī, kaunāmaṃ kula’’nti ayampi nayau gahaitabbau| ‘‘k‍va tai balaṃ mahārājā’’ti vattabbai ‘‘kau tai balaṃ mahārājā’’ti aittha k‍vasaddaina īsakaṃ samānasutikau sattamiyantau kausaddau dissati, k‍va kausaddā hi añ‍ñamañ‍ñamīsakasamānasutikā| tathā ‘‘idha haimantagimhaisu, idha haimantagimhisu, na tainatthaṃ abandhi sau, na tainatthaṃ abandhisū’’ti añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni|

atthatau pana idaṃ sammuyhanaṭṭhānaṃ – ‘‘yaṃ na kañ‍canadvaipiñcha, andhaina tamasā gata’’nti aittha nakārau ‘‘kata’’nti iminā sambandhitabbau| na katanti kataṃ viyāti atthau| aittha hi nakārau upamānai vattati, na paṭisaidhai|

‘‘assaddhau akatañ‍ñū ca,

sandhicchaidau ca yau narau|

hatāvakāsau vantāsau,

sa vai uttamapaurisau’’ti

aivamādīnipi añ‍ñāni yaujaitabbāni|

adhippāyatau idaṃ sammuyhanaṭṭhānaṃ – ‘‘taṇhaṃ asmimānaṃ sassatucchaidadiṭṭhiyau dvādasāyatananissitaṃ nandirāgañ‍ca hantvā brāhmaṇau anīghau yātī’’ti vattabbaipi tathā avatvā tamaivatthaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñaina pariyāyaina

‘‘mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hantvā, rājānau dvai ca khattiyai|

raṭṭhaṃ sānucaraṃ hantvā, anīghau yāti brāhmaṇau’’ti

vuttaṃ|

‘‘vanaṃ  chindatha mā rukkhaṃ, vanatau jāyatai bhayaṃ|

chaitvā vanañ‍ca vanathaṃ, nibbanā hautha bhikkhavau’’ti

aivamādīnipi añ‍ñāni yaujaitabbāni| aivaṃ buddhavacanai saddatau ca atthatau ca adhippāyatau ca akkharacintakānaṃ ñāṇacakkhusammuyhanaṭṭhānabhūtā pāḷinayā vividhā dissanti| yathāha –

‘‘jānantā api saddasatthamakhilaṃ muyhanti pāṭhak‍kamai,

yaibhuyyaina hi laukanītividhurā pāṭhai nayā vij‍jarai|

paṇḍic‍campi pahāya bāhiragataṃ aitthaiva tasmā budhau,

sikkhaiyyāmaladhammasāgaratarai nibbānatitthūpagai’’ti||

aivaṃ pāḷinayānaṃ dubbiñ‍ñaiyyattā ‘‘vanappagumbai, bālai ca, paṇḍitai cā’’tiādīnaṃ suddhapac‍cattavacanattañ‍ñaiva sāratau pac‍caitabbaṃ, na sutisāmañ‍ñaina bhummaupayaugavacanattaṃ bhummaupayaugavacanaihi taisaṃ samānasutikattaipi pac‍cattajautakattā| samānasutikāpi hi saddā atthappakaraṇaliṅgasaddantarābhisambandhādivasaina atthavisaisajautakā bhavanti| taṃ yathā? ‘‘sīhau gāyatī’’ti vuttai ‘‘aivaṃnāmakau purisau’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘sīhau naṅguṭṭhaṃ cālaitī’’ti vuttai pana ‘‘migarājā’’ti viñ‍ñāyati| aivaṃ atthavasaina samānasutikānaṃ atthavisaisajautanaṃ bhavati| saṅgāmai ṭhatvā ‘‘sindhavamānaihī’’ti vuttai ‘‘assau’’ti viñ‍ñāyati| raugisālāyaṃ pana ‘‘sindhavamānaihī’’ti vuttai ‘‘lavaṇa’’nti viñ‍ñāyati| aivaṃ pakaraṇavasaina samānasutikānaṃ atthavisaisajautanaṃ bhavati| ‘‘issā’’ti vuttai ‘‘aivaṃnāmikā dhammajātī’’ti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘issau’’ti vuttai pana ‘‘acchamigau’’ti viñ‍ñāyati| aivaṃ liṅgavasaina aikadaisasamānasutikānaṃ atthavisaisajautanaṃ bhavati| aittha pana kiñ‍cāpi ‘‘daivadattaṃ pak‍kausa ghaṭadhārakaṃ  daṇḍadhāraka’’ntiādīsupi ghaṭadaṇḍādīni liṅgaṃ, tathāpi samānasutikādhikārattā na taṃ idhādhippaitaṃ|
