idāni dvisaddapariyāyassa sadā bahuvacanantassa sabbanāmikapadassa ubhasaddassa nāmikapadamālā vuc‍catai –

‘‘ubhau, ubhau, ubhauhi, ubhaubhi, ubhin‍naṃ, ubhauhi, ubhaubhi, ubhin‍naṃ, ubhausū’’ti ayaṃ pāḷinayānurūpaina vuttapadamālā| atrimai payaugā – ubhau kumārā nik‍kītā| ubhau itthiyau tiṭṭhanti, ubhau cittāni tiṭṭhanti, ubhau puttai adāsi| ubhau kañ‍ñāyau passati| ubhau pādāni bhinditvā, sañ‍ñamissāmi vau ahaṃ| ubhauhi hatthaihi| ubhauhi bāhāhi, ubhauhi cittaihi, ubhin‍naṃ janānaṃ, ubhin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ, ubhin‍naṃ cittānaṃ, ubhausu purisaisu, ubhausu itthīsu, ubhausu passaisūti, ayamasmākaṃ ruci| ācariyā pana ‘‘ubhaihi, ubhaibhi, ubhaisū’’tipi icchanti| kac‍cāyanaipi hi ‘‘ubhai tappurisā’’ti vuttaṃ| sabbānipi aitāni manasi kātabbāniyaiva| ubhasaddassa samāsau appasiddhau| liṅgattayasādhāraṇarūpāni|

idāni saṅkhāvacanānaṃ dviti catusaddānaṃ sadā bahuvacanantānaṃ sabbanāmānaṃ nāmikapadamālāyau vuc‍cantai –

dvai, dvai, dvīhi, dvībhi, dvin‍naṃ, duvin‍naṃ, dvīhi, dvībhi, dvin‍naṃ, duvin‍naṃ, dvīsu| cūḷaniruttiyaṃ pana ‘‘dvin‍nan‍na’’nti padamālā āgatā| imāni ahaṃsaddādīni viya itthi liṅgādibhāvavinimuttānipi tīsu liṅgaisu yuj‍jantai ‘‘dvai purisā, dvai itthiyau, dvai cittāni’’ic‍caivamādinā| imānipi liṅgattayasādhāraṇāni rūpāni|

‘‘dvai’’ti rūpaṃ dvisaddassa, yaṃ samāsamhi taṃ bhavai|

dvitippakatikaṃyaiva, nānādaisaihi sā siyā||

dvibhāvau caiva dvaibhāvau, dvirattañ‍ca duvassakau|

dauhaḷinī dupattañ‍ca, taddhitattai dvayaṃ dvayaṃ||

tayau, tayau, tīhi, tībhi, tiṇṇaṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ, tīhi, tībhi, tiṇṇaṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ, tīsu| imāni pul‍liṅgarūpāni|

tissau, tissau, tīhi, tībhi, tissan‍naṃ, tīhi, tībhi, tissan‍naṃ, tīsu| imāni itthiliṅgarūpāni| cūḷaniruttiyaṃ ‘‘tissan‍nan‍na’’nti catutthīchaṭṭhīnaṃ bahuvacanamāgataṃ, niruttipiṭakai pana ‘‘tiṇṇan‍na’’nti| tāni sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai punappunaṃ upaparikkhitvā dissanti cai, gahaitabbāni|

tīṇi, tīṇi, tīhi, tībhi, tiṇṇaṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ, tīhi, tībhi, tiṇṇaṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ, tīsu| imāni napuṃsakaliṅgarūpāni| katthaci pana pāḷippadaisai tīṇisaddassa ṇikāralaupaupi bhavati ‘‘dvai vāti vā udakaphusitānī’’ti| ‘‘tiṇṇan‍naṃ khau bhikkhavai indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina bhikkhunā añ‍ñā byākatā’’ti idaṃ ‘‘tiṇṇan‍na’’nti padassa atthibhāvai nidassanaṃ|

yāni rūpāni vuttāni, ‘‘tissau tīṇi tayau’’iti|

samāsavisayai tāni, titippakatikā siyuṃ||

yasmā tissa samāsamhi, saddhiṃ parapadaina vai|

‘‘tivaidanaṃ ticitta’’nti, ‘‘tilauka’’nti ca niddisai||

aittha napuṃsakattaṃva, pāsaṃsaṃ pāyavuttitau|

pumattampaittha icchanti, ‘‘tibhavau khāyatai’’iti||

cattārau, caturau, cattārau, caturau, catūhi, catūbhi, catubbhi, catun‍naṃ, catūhi, catūbhi, catubbhi, catun‍naṃ, catūsu| imāni pul‍liṅgarūpāni|

catassau, catassau, catūhi, catūbhi, catubbhi, catassan‍naṃ, catun‍naṃ, catūhi, catūbhi, catubbhi, catassan‍naṃ, catun‍naṃ, catūsu| imāni itthiliṅgarūpāni| itthiliṅgaṭṭhānai ‘‘catun‍na’’nti padaṃ cūḷaniruttiyaṃ niruttipiṭakai pāḷiyaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu ca dassanatau vuttaṃ| tathā hi cūḷaniruttiyaṃ itthiliṅgaṭṭhānai ‘‘catun‍na’’nti āgataṃ, niruttipiṭakai ‘‘catun‍naṃ kañ‍ñāna’’nti āgataṃ| pāḷiyaṃ pana sauṇadantasuttādīsu ‘‘samaṇau gautamau catun‍naṃ parisānaṃ piyau manāpau’’ti āgataṃ| aṭṭhakathāsu ca pana suttantaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘catūhi acchariyabbhutadhammaihi saman‍nāgatau catun‍naṃ parisānaṃ piyau manāpau’’ti āgataṃ| sattilaṅghajātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘ācariyau panassa catun‍naṃ sattīnaṃ laṅghanaṃ sippaṃ jānātī’’ti āgataṃ|

cattāri, cattāri, catūhi, catūbhi, catubbhi, catun‍naṃ, catūhi, catūbhi, catubbhi, catun‍naṃ, catūsu| imāni napuṃsakaliṅgarūpāni|

‘‘cattārau’’ti ‘‘catassau’’ti, ‘‘cattārī’’ti ca sadditaṃ|

rūpaṃ samāsabhāvamhi, catuppakatikaṃ bhavai||

nidassanapadānaittha, kamatau kamakauvidau|

‘‘catubbidhaṃ catussālaṃ, catusac‍ca’’nti niddisai||

imāni dvaiādikāni sabbanāmikāni bahuvacanāniyaiva bhavanti, na aikavacanāni| cūḷaniruttiyaṃ pana tīsu liṅgaisu ‘‘catassan‍na’’nti vuttaṃ, taṃ anijjhānakkhamaṃ viya dissati|

idāni tumhaamhasaddānaṃ nāmikapadamālā vuc‍cantai, taisu yaina kathaiti, tassālapanai tumhavacanāni bhavanti|

tvaṃ, tuvaṃ, tumhai| taṃ, tuvaṃ, tvaṃ, tavaṃ, tumhai| tayā, tvayā, tumhaihi, tumhaibhi| tuyhaṃ, tava, tumhaṃ, tumhākaṃ| tayā, tvayā, tumhaihi, tumhaibhi| tuyhaṃ, tava, tumhaṃ, tumhākaṃ| tayi, tvayi, tumhaisu| tatra ‘‘tvaṃ purisau, tvaṃ itthī, tvaṃ citta’’ntiādinā yaujaitabbāni|

attayaugai amhavacanāni bhavanti|

ahaṃ, ahakaṃ, mayaṃ, amhai| maṃ, mamaṃ, amhai| mayā, amhaihi, amhaibhi| mayhaṃ, mama, amhaṃ, amhākaṃ, asmākaṃ| mayā, amhaihi, amhaibhi| mayhaṃ, mama, amhaṃ, amhākaṃ, asmākaṃ| mayi, amhaisu, asmaisu|

aittha pana ‘‘kathaṃ amhai karaumasai’’ti pāḷidassanatau ‘‘tumhai’’ti pac‍cattavacanassa viya ‘‘amhai’’ti pac‍cattavacanassapi atthitā vaiditabbā, ‘‘ahaka’’nti rūpantarampi icchitabbaṃ| tassa atthibhāvai ‘‘ahakañ‍ca cittavasānugā bhāsissa’’nti aisā pāḷi nidassanaṃ| aittha hi ahakanti ahaṃ ic‍caivatthau| tatra ‘‘ahaṃ purisau, ahaṃ kañ‍ñā, ahaṃ citta’’ntiādinā yaujaitabbāni| imānipi liṅgattayasādhāraṇarūpāni|

kac‍cāyanacūḷaniruttiniruttipiṭakaisu pana ‘‘tumhākaṃ amhāka’’nti ca dutiyābahuvacanaṃ vuttaṃ| kac‍cāyanai ‘‘tumhānaṃ amhāna’’nti ca paṭhamādutiyābahuvacanaṃ, ‘‘tumhaṃ amha’’nti ca catutthīchaṭṭhaikavacanaṃ, paṭhamādutiyābahuvacanañ‍ca vuttaṃ, cūḷaniruttiniruttipiṭakai pana ‘‘tumhaṃ amha’’nti ca dutiyaikavacanaṃ vuttaṃ, ‘‘tumhai amhai’’ti ca catutthīchaṭṭhībahuvacanaṃ vuttaṃ| aitāni upaparikkhitvā sāṭṭhakathaisu suttantaisu dissanti cai, gahaitabbāni|

tumhaamhasaddānaṃ pana parapadaihi saddhiṃ samāsai ‘‘maṃdīpā’’tiādayau payaugā tathāgatādimukhatau sambhavanti| ‘‘aitai gāmaṇi maṃdīpā maṃlaiṇā maṃsaraṇā’’ti hi tathāgatamukhatau, ‘‘tayyaugau mayyaugau’’ti niruttañ‍ñumukhatau, kābyādāsai ca ‘‘tvaṃmukhaṃ kamalainaiva, tulyaṃ nāñ‍ñaina  kainacī’’ti ca ‘‘candaina tvaṃmukhaṃ tulya’’nti ca kavimukhatau|

tattha hi ahaṃ dīpau aitaisanti maṃdīpā, ahaṃ laiṇaṃ aitaisanti maṃlaiṇā, aivaṃ maṃsaraṇā| tumhaina yaugau tayyaugau, tumhasaddaina yaugau ic‍caivatthau| amhaina yaugau mayyaugau, amhasaddaina yaugau ic‍caivatthau| tava mukhaṃ tvaṃmukhaṃ| bahuvacanavasainapi nibbacanīyaṃ ‘‘tumhākaṃ mukhaṃ tvaṃmukha’’nti| aittha ca pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘maṃdīpā’’ic‍cādidassanatau ‘‘tvaṃdīpā’’tiādīni kābyādāsai ca ‘‘tvaṃmukha’’nti dassanatau tvaṃvaṇṇau, tvaṃsarau, maṃmukhaṃ, maṃvaṇṇau, maṃsarauādīni gahaitabbāni| tattha tvaṃ dīpau aitaisanti tvaṃdīpā, tumhai vā dīpā aitaisanti tvaṃdīpā, tava vaṇṇau tvaṃvaṇṇau| mama mukhaṃ maṃmukhaṃ, amhākaṃ vā mukhaṃ maṃmukhanti nibbacanāni| aisa nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu|

samāsai tumhaamhākaṃ, haunti parapadaihi vai|

‘‘tvaṃmukha’’nti ca ‘‘maṃdīpā, tayyaugau mayyaugau’’ti ca||

aitthāha ‘‘kiṃ aittakamaiva tumhaamhasaddānaṃ rūpaṃ, udāhu añ‍ñampi atthī’’ti? atthi ‘‘tai mai’’ ic‍cādīni| yadi aivaṃ kasmā padamālā visuṃ na vuttāti? avacanai kāraṇamatthi| atridaṃ kāraṇaṃ –

‘‘tai mai vau nau’’ti rūpāni, parāni padatau yatau|

tatau nāmikapantīsu, na tu vuttāni tāni mai||

aittha ca mayaṃ mai vau nausaddānamatthuddhārau vuc‍catai – taisaddassa pana vuttauva| yasmā aṭṭhakathācariyā mayaṃsaddaṭṭhānaipi mayāsaddau, mayāsaddaṭṭhānaipi ca mayaṃsaddau ic‍caiva vadanti, tasmā mayampi tathaiva vadāma| mayaṃsaddau ‘‘anuñ‍ñātapaṭiñ‍ñātā, taivij‍jāmayamasmubhau’’tiādīsu asmadatthai āgatau| ‘‘mayaṃ nissāya haimāya, jātā mandausisūpagā’’ti aittha pañ‍ñattiyaṃ| ‘‘manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā’’tiādīsu nibbattiatthai| bāhiraina pac‍cayaina vinā manasāva nibbattāti manaumayā| ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbamattikāmayaṃ kuṭikaṃ karaiyya’’ntiādīsu vikāratthai| ‘‘dānamayaṃ sīlamaya’’ntiādīsu padapūraṇamattai| ‘‘pīṭhaṃ tai sauvaṇṇamayaṃ uḷāra’’nti aittha vikāratthai padapūraṇamattai vā daṭṭhabbau| yadā hi suvaṇṇamaiva sauvaṇṇanti ayamatthau, tadā suvaṇṇassa vikārau sauvaṇṇamayanti vikāratthai mayasaddau daṭṭhabbau| nibbattiatthautipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati| yadā pana suvaṇṇaina nibbattaṃ sauvaṇṇanti ayamatthau, tadā sauvaṇṇamaiva sauvaṇṇamayanti padapūraṇamattai mayasaddau daṭṭhabbau|

maisaddau ‘‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsitu’’ntiādīsu karaṇai āgatau| mayāti atthau| ‘‘tassa mai bhantai bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitū’’tiādīsu sampadānai, mayhanti atthauti vadanti| ‘‘pubbaiva mai bhikkhavai sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau’’tiādīsu sāmiatthai, mamāti atthauti ca vadanti| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

karaṇai sampadānai ca, sāmiatthai ca āgatau|

maisaddau iti viñ‍ñaiyyau, viñ‍ñunā nayadassinā||

aittha  pana ṭhatvā aṭṭhakathācariyaihi katai tai maisaddānamatthavivaraṇai vinicchayaṃ brūma taisamadhippāyappakāsanavasaina sautūnaṃ saṃsayasamugghāṭanatthaṃ| tathā hi aṭṭhakathācariyā taimaisaddānaṃ sampadānatthavasaina ‘‘tuyhaṃ mayha’’nti atthaṃ saṃvaṇṇaisuṃ, sāmiatthavasaina pana ‘‘tava mamā’’ti| aivaṃ yvāyaṃ taihi asaṅkaratau niyamau dassitau, sau sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai kutau labbhā| tathā hi taimaisaddatthavācakā tuyhaṃ mayhaṃsaddā tava mamasaddā ca sampadānasāmiatthaisu aniyamatau pavattanti|

tatrimai payaugā – idaṃ tuyhaṃ dadāmi| tuyhaṃ vikappaimi| tuyhaṃ maṃsaina maidaina, matthakaina ca brāhmaṇa, āhutiṃ paggahaissāmi| aisa hi tuyhaṃ pitā narasīhau| tuyhaṃ pana mātā kahanti| mayhamaiva dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ, mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ| na mayhaṃ bhariyā aisā| assamau sukatau mayhaṃ| sabbañ‍ñutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ| tāta mayhaṃ mātu mukhaṃ añ‍ñādisaṃ, tumhākaṃ añ‍ñādisaṃ| mayhaṃ sāmikau idāni marissati| tava dīyatai, tava silāghatai, mama silāghatai, pabbaj‍jā mama ruc‍cati| tava puttau| ubhau mātāpitā mamāti aivaṃ aniyamatau pavattanti|

cūḷaniruttiyañhi yamakamahāthairaina catutthīchaṭṭhīnaṃ anañ‍ñarūpattaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘catutthīchaṭṭhīnaṃ sabbattha anañ‍ñaṃ, tatiyāpañ‍camīnaṃ bahuvacanañ‍cā’’ti| yadi aivaṃ aṭṭhakathācariyā ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña| namau tai buddha vīratthū’tiādīsu tuyhaṃsaddassa vasaina sampadānai, tuyhanti hi atthau| ‘kintai vataṃ kiṃ pana brahmacariya’ntiādīsu sāmiatthai, tavāti hi atthau’’tiādīni vadantā ‘‘ayuttaṃ saṃvaṇṇanaṃ saṃvaṇṇaisu’’ntipi, ‘‘passitabbaṃ na passiṃsū’’tipi āpaj‍jantīti? yuttaṃyaiva tai saṃvaṇṇayiṃsu, passitabbañ‍ca passiṃsu| tathā hi tai ‘‘saddasatthampi aikadaisatau sāsanānukūlaṃ hautī’’ti paraisamanukampāya saddasatthatau nayaṃ gahaitvā sampadānatthavasaina tai maisaddānaṃ ‘‘tuyhaṃ mayha’’nti atthaṃ saṃvaṇṇayiṃsu, sāmiatthavasaina pana ‘‘tava mamā’’ti| saddasatthai hi catutthīchaṭṭhīrūpāni sabbathā visadisāni, sāsanai pana sadisāni| tasmā sāsanai sāmañ‍ñaina pavattāni catutthīchaṭṭhīrūpāni saddasatthai visaisaina pavattaihi catutthīchaṭṭhīrūpaihi samānagatikāni katvā paraisamanukampāya sampadānatthai tuyhaṃ mayhaṃsaddānaṃ pavattiniyamau, sāmiatthai ca tava mamasaddānaṃ pavattiniyamau dassitau| yasmā pana paraisamanukampāya ayaṃ niyamau, tasmā karuṇāyaivāyaṃparādhau, na aṭṭhakathācariyānaṃ| tāya aiva hi taihi aivaṃ saṃvaṇṇanā katāti|

kaici panaittha aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – nanu ca bhau aṭṭhakathācariyaihi saddanayaṃ nissāya tai maisaddānaṃ sāmiatthai vattamānānaṃ ‘‘tava mamā’’ti atthavacanaina ‘‘tuyhaṃ maṃsaina maidaina, na mayhaṃ bhariyā aisā’’tiādīsu sāmivisayaisu vibhattivipal‍lāsanayau dassitauti sak‍kā vattuṃ, tathā saddanayañ‍ñaiva nissāya tai maisaddānaṃ sampadānatthai vattamānānaṃ  ‘‘tuyhaṃ mayha’’nti atthavacanaina ‘‘bhattaṃ tava na ruc‍cati| pabbaj‍jā mama ruc‍catī’’tiādīsupi sampadānavisayaisu vibhattivipal‍lāsanayau dassitauti sak‍kā vattunti? na sak‍kā, gāthāsu viya cuṇṇiyapadaṭṭhānaipi tuyhaṃ mayhaṃ tava mamasaddānaṃ aniyamaina dvīsu atthaisu pavattanatau| na hi īdisai ṭhānai gāthāyaṃ vā cuṇṇiyapadaṭṭhānai vā vibhattivipal‍lāsau icchitabbau| ‘‘tassa raj‍jassahaṃ bhītau| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmī’’tiādīsuyaiva pana ṭhānaisu icchitabbau|

yadi saddanayaṃ nissāya ‘‘tuyhaṃ maṃsaina maidainā’’tiādīsu vibhattivipal‍lāsau icchitabbau siyā, ‘‘brāhmaṇassa piyaputtadānaṃ adāsi| brāhmaṇassa pitā adāsī’’tiādīsupi saddanayaṃ nissāya ‘‘brāhmaṇāyā’’tiādinā vibhattivipal‍lāsatthau vacanīyau siyā catutthīchaṭṭhīrūpānaṃ satthai visuṃ vacanatau| aivañ‍ca sati kau dausauti cai? atthaiva dausau, yasmā dānayaugai vā namauyaugai vā āyādaisasahitāni catutthīchaṭṭhīrūpāni sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai nupalabbhanti, tasmā ‘‘brāhmaṇāyā’’tiādinā vibhattivipal‍lāsatthavacanai ayaṃ dausau yadidaṃ avij‍jamānaggahaṇaṃ| yasmā pana īdisaisu ṭhānaisu vibhattivipal‍lāsakaraṇaṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, tasmā ‘‘tuyhaṃ maṃsaina maidainā’’tiādīsupi vibhattivipal‍lāsau na icchitabbau|

catutthīchaṭṭhīrūpāni hi anañ‍ñāni dissanti ‘‘purisassa adāsi, purisassa dhanaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ adāsi, brāhmaṇānaṃ santaka’’nti| tathā hi pāvacanai sa naṃsaddā sampadānasāmiatthaisu sāmañ‍ñaina  pavattanti, tappavatti ‘‘aggassa dātā maidhāvī’’tiādīhi payaugaihi dīpaitabbā| ‘‘aggassa dātā maidhāvī’’ti aittha hi ‘‘aggassā’’ti ayaṃ saddau yadā kriyāpaṭiggahaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca sampadānatthai pavattati, tadā ‘‘aggassa ratanattayassa dātā’’ti atthavasaina pavattati| yadā pana kriyaṃ paṭic‍ca kammabhūtai sāmiatthai pavattati, tadā ‘‘aggassa daiyyadhammassa dātā’’ti atthavasaina pavattati| aivaṃ sabbathāpi vipal‍lāsau tumhākaṃ saraṇaṃ na hautīti| tathā saddanayaṃ nissāya ‘‘sampadānavacana’’nti tumhaihi daḷhaṃ gahitassa mayhaṃsaddassa sāmiatthavasaina paṇṇattiyaṃ dassanatau vibhattivipal‍lāsau tumhākaṃ saraṇaṃ na hautaiva| tathā hi –

‘‘sakuṇau mayhakau nāma, girisānudarīcarau|

pak‍kaṃ pipphalimāruyha, ‘mayhaṃ mayha’nti kandatī’’ti

aittha mayhakauti aikāya sakuṇajātiyā nāmaṃ| sau hi lauluppacāritāya ‘‘idampi mayhaṃ, idampi mayha’’nti kāyati ravatīti mayhakauti vuc‍cati mayhasaddūpapadassa kai rai gai saddaiti dhātussa vasaina|

atrāyaṃ padasaudhanā – yadi tuyhaṃ mayhaṃsaddā dhuvaṃ sampadānatthai, tava mamasaddā ca sāmiatthai bhavaiyyuṃ, aivaṃ santai laukavauhārakusalaina sabbañ‍ñunā tassa sakuṇassa ‘‘mayhakau’’ti paṇṇatti na vattabbā siyā anantaugadhasampadānatthattā, antaugadhasāmyatthattā pana ‘‘mamakau’’ ic‍caiva pañ‍ñatti vattabbā siyā| aitthapi ‘‘mayhakau’’ti idaṃ vibhattivipal‍lāsavasaina vuttanti cai? na, paṇṇattivisayai vibhattivipariṇāmassa aṭṭhānattā anavakāsattā|

apicaittha mayhaṃsaddau sarūpatau vibhatyantabhāvaina tiṭṭhati kasaddaina aikapadattūpagamanatau, aivaṃ santaipi ‘‘mayhakau’’ti  ayaṃ sakuṇavisaisavācakau saddau pac‍cattavacanabhāvai ṭhitauyaiva īsakaṃ sāmiatthampi jautayati sujampati rājapurisasaddā viya| imināpi kāraṇaina vibhattivipal‍lāsau tumhākaṃ saraṇaṃ na hauti| iti ‘‘mayhakau’’ti paṇṇattiyaṃ vattamānassa padāvayavabhūtassa mayhasaddassa avipal‍lāsavacanalaisaina tuyhaṃ tava mamasaddaisupi vibhattivipal‍lāsau na icchitabbauti siddhaṃ| tasmā aṭṭhakathācariyaihi sampadānasāmiatthaisu sāmañ‍ñaina pavattānampi samānānaṃ tuyhaṃ mayhaṃ tava mamasaddānaṃ saddanayañ‍ñaiva nissāya paraisamanukampāya vuttappakārau niyamau dassitauti avagantabbaṃ| ic‍caivaṃ –

‘‘tuyhaṃ mayha’’ntimai saddai, sampadānai garū vaduṃ|

‘‘tava mamā’’ti sāmimhi, nayamādāya satthatau||

aivaṃ santaipi aitaisaṃ, niyamau natthi pāḷiyaṃ|

kauci taisaṃ visaisau ca, diṭṭhau amhaihi taṃ suṇa||

sāmyatthasampadānatthā, sambhavanti yahiṃ duvai|

‘‘tuyhaṃ mayha’’ntimai saddā, tai payaugā na dul‍labhā||

‘‘tava mamā’’timai saddā, pāyā sāmimhi vattarai|

sampadānai yahiṃ haunti, tai payaugā panappakā||

tavatau mamatau tuyhaṃ-mayhaṃsaddāva sāsanai|

pāṭhai naikasahassamhi, sāmiatthai pavattaraiti||

sabbāpi imā nītiyau paramasukhumā sududdasā vīrajātinā sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbā|

vau nausaddaisu pana vausaddau pac‍cattaupayaugakaraṇasampadānasāmivacanapadapūraṇaisu dissati| ‘‘kac‍ci vau  anuruddhā samaggā sammaudamānā’’tiādīsu hi pac‍cattai dissati| ‘‘gacchatha bhikkhavai paṇāmaimi vau’’tiādīsu upayaugai| ‘‘na vau mama santikai vatthabba’’ntiādīsu karaṇai| ‘‘vanapatthapariyāyaṃ vau bhikkhavai daisaissāmī’’tiādīsu sampadānai| ‘‘sabbaisaṃ vau sāriputtā subhāsita’’ntiādīsu sāmivacanai| ‘‘yai hi vauariyā parisuddhā kāyakammantā’’tiādīsu padapūraṇamattai| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘pac‍cattai upayaugai ca, karaṇai sampadāniyai|

sāmissa vacanai caiva, tathaiva padapūraṇai|

imaisu chasu ṭhānaisu, vausaddau sampavattati’’||

nausaddau pac‍cattaupayaugakaraṇasampadānasāmivacanāvadhāraṇanusaddatthaisu paṭisaidhai nipātamattai ca vattati| ayañhi ‘‘gāmaṃ nau gacchaiyyāmā’’ti aittha pac‍cattai dissati| ‘‘mā nau aj‍ja vikantiṃsu, rañ‍ñau sūdā mahānasai’’tiādīsu upayaugai| ‘‘na nau vivāhau nāgaihi, katapubbau kudācana’’ntiādīsu karaṇai| ‘‘saṃvibhajaitha nau raj‍jainā’’tiādīsu sampadānai| ‘‘satthā nau bhagavā anuppattau’’tiādīsu sāmivacanai| ‘‘na nau samaṃ atthi tathāgatainā’’ti aittha avadhāraṇai| ‘‘abhijānāsi nau tvaṃ mahārājā’’tiādīsu nusaddatthai, pucchāyantipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati| ‘‘subhāsitañ‍ñaiva bhāsaiyya, nau ca dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇai’’tiādīsu paṭisaidhai| ‘‘na nau sabhāyaṃ na karaunti kiñ‍cī’’tiādīsu nipātamattai| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

pac‍cattai  cupayaugai ca, karaṇai sampadāniyai|

sāmyāvadhāraṇai caiva, nusaddatthai nivāraṇai|

tathā nipātamattamhi, nausaddau sampavattati||

idāni sabbanāmānaṃ yathārahaṃ saṃkhittaina missakapadamālā vuc‍catai –

yau sau, yai tai| yaṃ taṃ, yai tai| yaina taina| saisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| yā sā, yā tā| yaṃ taṃ, yā tā| yāya tāya| saisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| yaṃ taṃ, yāni tāni| saisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| iminā nayaina liṅgattayayaujanā kātabbā|

‘‘aisau sau, aitai tai| ayaṃ sau, imai tai| sau ayaṃ tai imai’’tiādinā yathāpayaugaṃ padamālā yaujaitabbā| tathā hi ‘‘yau sau bhagavā sayambhū anācariyakau| aitai tai bhikkhavai ubhau antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā| ayaṃ sau sārathi aitī’’ti aivamādayau vicittappayaugā dissanti| iti sabbanāmikapadānaṃ missakapadamālā yaujaitabbā|

mayā sabbatthasiddhassa, sāsanai sabbadassinau|

sabbattha sāsanai suṭṭhu, kausal‍latthāya sautunaṃ||

asabbanāmanāmaihi, sabbanāmapadaihi vai|

saha sabbāni vuttāni, sabbanāmāni pantitau||

aitaisu katayaugānaṃ, sukhumatthavijānanaṃ|

akicchapaṭivaidhaina, bhavissati na saṃsayau||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

sabbanāmataṃsadisanāmānaṃ nāmikapadamālāvibhāgau nāma

dvādasamau paricchaidau|

13. savinicchayasaṅkhyānāmanāmikapadamālā

itau  paraṃ pavakkhāmi, saṅkhyānāmikapantiyau|

bhūdhātujaihi rūpaihi, añ‍ñaihi cupayaujituṃ||

yā hi sā haiṭṭhā amhaihi aika dviti catuic‍caitaisaṃ saṅkhyāsabbanāmānaṃ nāmikapadamālā kathitā, taṃ ṭhapaitvā idha asabbanāmānaṃ pañ‍ca cha sattādīnaṃ saṅkhyānāmānaṃ nāmikapadamālā bhūdhātumayaihi añ‍ñaihi ca rūpaihi yaujanatthaṃ vuc‍catai –

pañ‍ca, pañ‍cahi, pañ‍cabhi, pañ‍can‍naṃ, pañ‍casu| sattan‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ vasaina ñaiyyaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍ca bhūtā, pañ‍ca abhibhavitārau, pañ‍ca purisā, pañ‍ca bhūmiyau, pañ‍ca kañ‍ñāyau, pañ‍ca bhūtāni, pañ‍ca cittānī’’tiādinā sabbattha yaujaitabbaṃ| cha, chahi, chabhi, chan‍naṃ, chasu, chassu itipi| ‘‘chassu laukau samuppan‍nau, chassu krubbati santhava’’nti hi pāḷi| satta, sattahi, sattabhi, sattan‍naṃ, sattasu| aṭṭha, aṭṭhahi, aṭṭhabhi, aṭṭhan‍naṃ, aṭṭhasu| nava, navahi, navabhi, navan‍naṃ, navasu| dasa, dasahi, dasabhi, dasan‍naṃ, dasasu| aivaṃ aikādasa| dvādasa, bārasa| tairasa, taidasa, taiḷasa| catuddasa, cuddasa| pañ‍cadasa, pan‍narasa| sauḷasa| sattarasa| aṭṭhārasa, aṭṭhārasahi, aṭṭhārasabhi, aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ, aṭṭhārasasu| sabbamaitaṃ bahuvacanavasaina gahaitabbaṃ|

aikūnavīsati, aikūnavīsaṃ ic‍cādipi| aikūnavīsāya, aikūnavīsāyaṃ, aikūnavīsa bhikkhū tiṭṭhanti, aikūnavīsaṃ bhikkhū passati, aivaṃ ‘‘kañ‍ñāyau cittānī’’ti ca ādinā yaujaitabbaṃ| aikūnavīsāya bhikkhūhi dhammau daisitau, aikūnavīsāya kañ‍ñāhi kataṃ, aikūnavīsāya cittaihi kataṃ, aikūnavīsāya bhikkhūnaṃ cīvaraṃ daiti, aikūnavīsāya kañ‍ñānaṃ dhanaṃ daiti, aikūnavīsāya cittānaṃ ruc‍cati, aikūnavīsāya bhikkhūhi apaiti| aivaṃ kañ‍ñāhi cittaihi| aikūnavīsāya bhikkhūnaṃ santakaṃ, aivaṃ kañ‍ñānaṃ cittānaṃ| aikūnavīsāyaṃ bhikkhūsu patiṭṭhitaṃ| aivaṃ ‘‘kañ‍ñāsu cittaisū’’ti yaujaitabbaṃ| aikūnavīsati, aikūnavīsatiṃ, aikūnavīsatiyā, aikūnavīsatiyaṃ|

vīsati, vīsatiṃ, vīsatiyā, vīsatiyaṃ| vīsa, vīsaṃ, vīsāya, vīsāyaṃ| tathā aikavīsa dvāvīsa bāvīsa taivīsa catuvīsa ic‍cādīsupi| tiṃsa, tiṃsaṃ, tiṃsāya, tiṃsāyaṃ| cattālīsa, cattālīsaṃ, cattālīsāya, cattālīsāyaṃ| cattārīsa ic‍cādipi| pañ‍ñāsa, pañ‍ñāsaṃ, pañ‍ñāsāya, pañ‍ñāsāyaṃ| paṇṇāsa, paṇṇāsaṃ, paṇṇāsāya, paṇṇāsāyaṃ| saṭṭhi, saṭṭhiṃ, saṭṭhiyā, saṭṭhiyaṃ| sattati, sattatiṃ, sattatiyā, sattatiyaṃ| sattari ic‍cādipi| asīti, asītiṃ, asītiyā, asītiyaṃ| navuti| navutiṃ, navutiyā, navutiyaṃ|

itthañ‍ca añ‍ñathāpi saṅkhyārūpāni gahaitabbāni| aikūnavīsaihi, aikūnavīsānaṃ, aikūnavīsaisu| ‘‘chan‍navutīna’’nti ca ādināpi saṅkhyārūpānaṃ katthaci dassanatau kaici saddasatthavidū ūnavīsatisaddaṃ sabbadāpi aikavacanantamitthiliṅgamaiva payuñ‍janti| kaici ‘‘vīsatiādayau ānavuti aikavacanantā itthiliṅgā’’ti vadanti| kaici panāhu –

‘‘saddā saṅkhyaiyyasaṅkhāsu, aikattai vīsatādayau|

saṅkhatthai dvibahuttamhi, tā tu cānavutitthiyau’’ti||

aittha dvivacanaṃ chaḍḍaitabbaṃ buddhavacanai tadabhāvatau| sabbaisampi ca taisaṃ yathāvuttavacanaṃ kiñ‍ci pāḷippadaisaṃ patvā yuj‍jati, kiñ‍ci pana patvā na yuj‍jati vīsativīsaṃtiṃsaṃic‍cādīnañhi saṅkhatthānaṃ saddānaṃ bahuvacanappayaugavasainapi pāḷiyaṃ dassanatau, kac‍cāyanai ca yauvacanasambhūtarūpavantatādassanatau| tasmā yathāsambhavaṃ yathāpāvacanañ‍ca itthiliṅgabhāvai taisamaikavacanantatā vaiditabbā atthi natthisaddānaṃ viya|

atthinatthisaddā hi nipātattā aikattaipi bahuttaipi pavattanti ‘‘puttā matthi dhanammatthi| natthi attasamaṃ paimaṃ| natthi samaṇabrāhmaṇā’’tiādīsu| aliṅgattaipi panaitaisaṃ katthaci itthiliṅgabhāvau diṭṭhau| abhidhammai hi dhammasaināpatinā anudhammacak‍kavattinā vauhārakusalaina vauhārakusalasādhakaina ‘‘atthiyā nava| natthiyā navā’’ti aikavacanantaṃ itthiliṅgarūpaṃ dassitaṃ, tasmā vīsativīsatimic‍cādīnampi yathāsambhavaṃ yathāpāvacanañ‍ca itthiliṅgabhāvai aikavacanantatā vaiditabbā|

tatthaikai ‘‘haituyā adhipatiyā’’ti ca idaṃ liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina gahaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| tammativasaina ‘‘haitumhi adhipatimhī’’ti pul‍liṅgabhāvau paṭipādaitabbau, ‘‘haitupac‍cayai adhipatipac‍cayai’’ ic‍caivatthau| atha vā ‘‘haituyā adhipatiyā’’ti dvayamidaṃ itthiliṅgarūpapaṭibhāgaṃ pul‍liṅgarūpanti gahaitabbaṃ ‘‘haituyau jantuyau’’tiādīnaṃ itthiliṅgarūpapaṭibhāgānaṃ pul‍liṅgarūpānampi vij‍jamānattā, ‘‘atthiyā natthiyā’’ti idaṃ pana liṅgavipal‍lāsavasaina vuttanti na gahaitabbaṃ atthi natthisaddānaṃ aliṅgabhaidattā| na hi atthi natthisaddā tīsu liṅgaisu aikasmimpi antaugadhā| aitaisu hi atthisaddau ākhyātanipātavasaina bhij‍jati ‘‘atthi santi saṃvij‍jati| atthikhīrā brāhmaṇī’’tiādīsu, natthisaddau pana nipātauyaiva| ic‍caivaṃ atthi natthisaddānaṃ nipātānañ‍ca liṅgavacanavasaina kathanaṃ yuj‍jati itthiliṅgādivasaina aikattādivasaina ca appavattanatau| vuttañ‍ca –

‘‘sadisaṃ tīsu liṅgaisu, sabbāsu ca vibhattisu|

vacanaisu ca sabbaisu, yaṃ na byaiti tadabyaya’’nti||

aittha siyā – nanu ca bhau ‘‘atthi sak‍kā labbhā ic‍caitai paṭhamāyā’’ti vacanatau atthisaddau paṭhamāya vibhattiyā yuttau, aivaṃ santai kasmā ‘‘sadisaṃ tīsu liṅgaisū’’tiādi vuttanti? sac‍caṃ atthisaddau paṭhamāya vibhattiyā yuttau, tathā natthisaddau atthisaddassa vacanalaisaina gahaitabbattā yugaḷapadattā ca| idaṃ pana ‘‘sadisaṃ tīsu liṅgaisū’’tiādivacanaṃ upasagganipātasaṅkhātai asaṅkhyāsaddai sandhāya vuttaṃ, na aikaikamasaṅkhyāsaddaṃ sandhāya| tathā hi ‘‘asaṅkhyā’’ti ca ‘‘abyayā’’ti ca laddhavauhāraisu upasagganipātaisu upasaggā sabbaipi sabbavibhattivacanakā| nipātānaṃ pana aikac‍cai paṭhamādīsu yathārahaṃ vibhattiyuttā, aikac‍cai avibhattiyuttā| tattha yai yadaggaina vibhattiyuttā, tai tadaggaina tabbacanakā| upasagganipātaisu hi pac‍caikaṃ ‘‘idaṃ nāma vacana’’nti laddhuṃ na sak‍kā, sabbasaṅgāhakavasaina pana ‘‘sadisaṃ tīsu liṅgaisū’’tiādi pubbācariyaihi vuttaṃ| kac‍cāyanācariyainapi imamaivatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘sabbāsamāvusaupasagganipātādīhi cā’’ti vuttaṃ| na hi āvusausaddatau sabbāpi vibhattiyau labbhanti, atha khau ālapanatthavācakattā aikavacanikaanaikavacanikā paṭhamāvibhattiyauyaiva labbhanti| ayamasmākaṃ khanti|

kaici  pana sabbaihipi nipātaihi sabbavibhattilaupaṃ vadanti, taṃ na gahaitabbaṃ| ‘‘atthi sak‍kā labbhā ic‍caitai paṭhamāya| divā bhiyyau namau ic‍caitai paṭhamāya ca dutiyāya cā’’tiādivacanatau, padapūraṇamattānañ‍ca avibhattiyuttānaṃ atha khalu vata vatha ic‍cādīnaṃ nipātānaṃ vacanatau| aitthāpi siyā ‘‘nanu ca bhau avibhattiyuttānampi nipātānaṃ sambhavatau atthi natthisaddānaṃ avibhattikau niddaisau kātabbau, atha kimatthaṃ ‘atthiyā nava, natthiyā navā’ti savibhattikau niddaisau katau’’ti? sabbathā vibhattīhi vinā atthassa niddisitumasak‍kuṇaiyyattāti|

yadi aivaṃ ‘‘atthi sak‍kā labbhā ic‍caitai paṭhamāyā’’ti vacanatau atthi natthisaddā luttāya paṭhamāya vibhattiyā vasaina paṭhamāvibhattikāyaiva niddisitabbā, aivamakatvā kasmā sattamyantavasaina ‘‘atthiyā natthiyā’’ti niddiṭṭhāti? sac‍caṃ, atthi natthisaddā paṭhamāvibhattiyuttāyaiva niddisitabbā, tathāpi ‘‘atthipac‍cayai nava, natthipac‍cayai navā’’ti aitassatthassa paridīpanai paṭhamāya aukāsau natthi, sattamiyāyaiva pana atthi, tasmā ‘‘atthiyā nava, natthiyā navā’’ti vuttaṃ| iti atthiyānatthiyāsaddānaṃ sattamyantabhāvai siddhaiyaiva tatiyācatutthīpañ‍camīchaṭṭhiyantabhāvaupi siddhauyaiva hauti| tasmā ‘‘atthibhāvau atthitā’’tiādīsupi atthiyā bhāvau atthibhāvau, natthiyā bhāvau natthibhāvau, atthiyā bhāvau atthitābhiādinā samāsataddhitaviggahau avassamicchitabbau| yadidamamhaihi vuttaṃ, taṃ ‘‘pāḷiyā virujjhatī’’ti na vattabbaṃ pāḷinayānusāraina vuttattāti|

aivaṃ hautu, kasmā bhau ‘‘atthiyā, natthiyā’’ti itthiliṅganiddaisau katau, nanu nipātaupasaggā aliṅgabhaidāti? sac‍caṃ, idaṃ pana ṭhānaṃ atīva sukhumaṃ, tathāpi pubbācariyānubhāvañ‍ñaiva  nissāya vinicchayaṃ brūma| yathā hi vīsatiic‍cādīnaṃ saṅkhyāsaddānaṃ sarūpatau adabbavācakattaipi dabbavācakānaṃ latāmatirattiitthī yāguvadhūsaddānaṃ viya itthiliṅgabhāvau saddasatthavidūhi anumatau, aivaṃ adabbavācakattaipi atthi natthisaddānaṃ katthaci itthiliṅgabhāvau saddhammavidūhi anumatau| taināha āyasmā dhammasaināpati ‘‘atthiyā nava, natthiyā navā’’ti| atha vā ‘‘atthiyā, natthiyā’’ti imāni liṅgabhāvavinimuttāni sattamiyantāni nipātapadānītipi gahaitabbāni, na aittha caudaitabbaṃ, aivarūpāni nipātapadāni pubbācariyaihi vuttāni na santi, tasmā chaḍḍaitabbamidaṃ vacananti|

pāvacanasmiñhi garūhi aniddiṭṭhānipi anaikavihitāni nipātapadāni sandissanti, nāpi ‘‘haituyā, adhipatiyā, atthiyā, natthiyā’’ti aivamādīsu ‘‘apasaddā imai’’ti viraudhau uppādaitabbau| na hi acintaiyyānubhāvaina pāramitāpuñ‍ñaina nipphan‍naina anāvaraṇañāṇaina sabbaṃ ñaiyyamaṇḍalaṃ hatthatalai āmalakaṃ viya pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā passatau buddhassa vacanai añ‍ñaisaṃ vācāvippalāpau avassaṃ labbhatīti| nanu ca bhau ‘‘haituyā, adhipatiyā, atthiyā, natthiyā’’ti ca idaṃ sāriputtatthairavacanaṃ taina nikkhittattā| tathāgataina hi tāvatiṃsabhapanai daisitakālai imāni padāni na santi, aivaṃ santai kasmā ‘‘buddhavacana’’nti vadathāti? buddhavacanaṃyaiva nāma| āyasmatau hi sāriputtassa tathāgataina nayau din‍nau, tainapi pabhin‍napaṭisambhidaina satthukappaina aggasāvakaina satthu santikā nayaṃ labhitvā byañ‍janaṃ suraupitaṃ kataṃ| sabbaipi hi paṭisambhidappattā ariyā dun‍niruttiṃ na vadanti niruttipabhaidasmiṃ sukusalattā, tasmā añ‍ñaisamavisayau aisa ariyānaṃ vauhārauti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

idāni  satādīnaṃ nāmikapadamālā vuc‍catai –

sataṃ, satāni, satā| sataṃ, satāni, satai| sataina, sataihi, sataibhi| satassa, satānaṃ| satā, satasmā, satamhā, sataihi, sataibhi| satassa, satānaṃ| satai, satasmiṃ, satamhi, sataisu| aivaṃ sahassaṃ, sahassānīti yaujaitabbaṃ| dasasahassaṃ satasahassaṃ dasasatasahassanti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| ayaṃ panaittha payaugau ‘‘sataṃ bhikkhū, sataṃ itthiyau, sataṃ cittāni| bhikkhūnaṃ sataṃ, itthīnaṃ sataṃ, cittānaṃ sataṃ| sahassādīsupi aisaiva nayau| itthañ‍ca añ‍ñathāpi saddarūpāni bhavanti| kauṭi, kauṭī, kauṭiyau| rattinayaina ñaiyyaṃ|

aikappabhutitau yāva, dasakā yā pavattati|

saṅkhyā tāva sā saṅkhyaiyya-ppadhānāti garū vaduṃ||

vīsatitau yāva satā, yā saṅkhyā tāva sā pana|

saṅkhyāppadhānā saṅkhyaiyya-ppadhānāti ca vaṇṇayuṃ||

apica –

vīsatau yāva kauṭiyā, saṅkhyā tāva hi sā khalu|

saṅkhyāppadhānā saṅkhyaiyya-ppadhānā cāti niddisai||

tathā hi ‘‘asīti kauṭiyau hitvā, hirañ‍ñassāpi pabbaji’’nti, ‘‘khīṇāsavā vītamalā, samiṃsu satakauṭiyau’’ti ca pāḷi dissati|

imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai sabbaisaṃ saṅkhyāsaddarūpānaṃ pākaṭīkaraṇaina viñ‍ñūnaṃ sukhumañāṇapaṭilābhatthaṃ sāṭṭhakathaṃ udānapāḷippadaisaṃ añ‍ñañ‍ca pāḷippadaisamaṭṭhakathāvacanañ‍ca āharitvā dassayissāmi –

‘‘yaisaṃ  khau visākhai sataṃ piyāni, sataṃ taisaṃ dukkhāni, yaisaṃ navuti piyāni, navuti taisaṃ dukkhāni| yaisaṃ asīti…pai॰… yaisaṃ sattati| yaisaṃ saṭṭhi| yaisaṃ pañ‍ñāsaṃ, yaisaṃ cattārīsaṃ, yaisaṃ tiṃsaṃ| yaisaṃ khau visākhai vīsaṃ piyāni, vīsati taisaṃ dukkhāni| yaisaṃ dasa| yaisaṃ nava| yaisaṃ aṭṭha| yaisaṃ satta| yaisaṃ cha| yaisaṃ pañ‍ca| yaisaṃ cattāri| yaisaṃ tīṇi| yaisaṃ dvai| yaisaṃ aikaṃ piyaṃ, taisaṃ aikaṃ dukkha’’nti|

tattha sataṃ piyānīti sataṃ piyāyitabbavatthūni| ‘‘sataṃ piya’’ntipi kaici paṭhanti| aittha ca yasmā aikatau paṭṭhāya yāva dasa, tāva saṅkhyāsaṅkhyaiyyappadhānā, tasmā ‘‘yaisaṃ dasa piyāni, dasa taisaṃ dukkhānī’’tiādinā pāḷi āgatā| kaici pana ‘‘yaisaṃ dasa piyānaṃ, dasa taisaṃ dukkhāna’’ntiādinā paṭhanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ| yasmā pana vīsatitau paṭṭhāya yāva sataṃ, tāva saṅkhyaiyyappadhānā saṅkhyāppadhānā ca, tasmā tatthāpi saṅkhyaiyyappadhānaṃyaiva gahaitvā ‘‘yaisaṃ khau visākhai sataṃ piyāni, sataṃ taisaṃ dukkhānī’’tiādinā pāḷi āgatā| sabbaisampi ca yaisaṃ aikaṃ piyaṃ, aikaṃ taisaṃ dukkhanti pāṭhau, na pana dukkhassāti| aikasmiñhi padak‍kamai aikarasāva bhagavatau daisanā hautīti| tasmā yathāvuttanayāva pāḷi vaiditabbā| ayaṃ tāva sāṭṭhakathau udānapāḷippadaisau|

idāni añ‍ñau pāḷippadaisau aṭṭhakathāpāṭhappadaisau ca nīyatai –

‘‘sataṃ  hatthī sataṃ assā, sataṃ assatarīrathā|

sataṃ kañ‍ñā sahassāni, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā|

aikassa padavītihārassa, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasi’’nti

pāḷi| aittha ‘‘sataṃ hatthī’’tiādīni visaisitāni, ‘‘sahassānī’’ti visaisanaṃ, tasmā sataṃsaddaṃ sahassasaddaina yaujaitvā ‘‘hatthī’’tiādīni pana upapadaṃ katvā atthau gahaitabbau| hatthī sataṃ sahassāni| assā sataṃ sahassāni| assatarīrathā sataṃ sahassāni| āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā kañ‍ñā sataṃ sahassāni| idaṃ saṅkhyaiyyappadhānavasainatthagahaṇaṃ| saṅkhyāppadhānavasaina pana ayampi atthau gahaitabbau ‘‘hatthīnaṃ satasahassaṃ, assānaṃ satasahassaṃ, assatarīrathānaṃ satasahassaṃ, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalānaṃ kañ‍ñānaṃ satasahassa’’nti| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau| ‘‘yaujanānaṃ satānuc‍cau, himavā pañ‍ca pabbatau’’ti ayamaṭṭhakathāpāṭhau| aittha ‘‘pañ‍cā’’ti saddaṃ satasaddaina saddhiṃ yaujaitvā ‘‘sippikānaṃ sataṃ natthī’’ti aittha viya himavā pabbatau yaujanānaṃ pañ‍ca satāni uc‍cauti saṅkhyāppadhānavasaina atthau gahaitabbau| ‘‘pañ‍ca satānī’’ti ca addhunau ac‍cantasaṃyaugavasaina upayaugavacanaṃ| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau| satamiti saddau ‘‘sataṃ haumi, sahassaṃ haumī’’tiādīsu aikavacanau| ‘‘athaitthaikasataṃ khatyā, anuyantā yasassinau’’tiādīsu bahuvacanau| aivaṃ sahassādīnampi aikavacanabahuvacanatā labbhati| tathā hi ‘‘bhiyyau naṃ satasahassaṃ, yakkhānaṃ payirupāsatī’’ti aittha ‘‘satasahassa’’nti aikavacanaṃ| ‘‘parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavissantī’’ti aittha sahassanti bahuvacananti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘kappai  ca satasahassai, caturau ca asaṅkhiyai|

amaraṃ nāma nagaraṃ, dassanaiyyaṃ manaurama’’nti

pāḷi| aittha ‘‘kappai ca satasahassai caturau ca asaṅkhiyaiti sāmiatthai upayaugabahuvacanaṃ, tasmā ‘‘mahākappānaṃ satasahassānaṃ catun‍naṃ asaṅkhiyānaṃ matthakai’’ti atthau gahaitabbau, ‘‘matthakai’’ti caittha vacanasaisau| ‘‘kappasatasahassādhikānaṃ catun‍naṃ asaṅkhiyānaṃ matthakai’’ic‍caivatthau| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘kappai ca satasahassai, caturau ca asaṅkhiyai|

aitthantarai yaṃ caritaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ baudhipācana’’nti

pāḷi| aittha ‘‘kappai’’ti ac‍cantasaṃyaugavasaina upayaugabahuvacanaṃ| ‘‘satasahassai kappai’’ti kappasaddasambandhaina cāyaṃ pul‍liṅganiddaisau upayauganiddaisau ca| samānādhikaraṇañhi idaṃ kappasaddaina| ‘‘caturau ca asaṅkhiyai’’ti acchantasaṃyaugavasaina upayaugabahuvacanāni| kassa pana asaṅkhiyaiti? añ‍ñassa avuttattā kappassa ca vuttattā pakaraṇatau ‘‘kappāna’’nti ayamatthau viñ‍ñāyataiva| na hi vuttaṃ vaj‍jaitvā avuttassa kassaci gahaṇaṃ yuttanti| casaddau sampiṇḍanatthau ‘‘mahākappānaṃ caturau asaṅkhyaiyyai satasahassai ca mahākappai’’ti| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘ghaṭānaikasahassāni, kumbhīnañ‍ca satā bahū’’ti pāḷi| aittha ghaṭāti ghaṭānaṃ| sāmiatthai hi idaṃ pac‍cattavacanaṃ| ‘‘ghaṭānaṃ anaikasahassāni’’ ic‍caivatthau| kumbhīnañ‍ca satā bahūti anaikāni ca kumbhīnaṃ satāni| aittha nikāralaupau daṭṭhabbau| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘dasavīsasahassānaṃ, dhammābhisamayau ahu|

aikadvin‍naṃ abhisamayau, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyau’’ti

pāḷi| aittha dasavīsasahassānanti dasasahassānaṃ vīsasahassānañ‍ca| dhammābhisamayauti catusac‍cappaṭivaidhau| aikadvin‍nanti sīsamattakathanaṃ, taina ‘‘aikassa caiva dvin‍nañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ catun‍naṃ…pai॰… dasan‍na’’ntiādinā nayaina asaṅkhyaiyyauti atthau| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘cattāri satasahassāni, chaḷabhiñ‍ñā mahiddhikā|

dīpaṅkaraṃ laukaviduṃ, parivārainti sabbadā’’ti

pāḷi| aittha cattāri satasahassānīti idaṃ liṅgabhaidavasaina ‘‘chaḷabhiñ‍ñā mahiddhikā’’ti imaihi dvīhi padaihi samānādhikaraṇaṃ| īdisaisu hi ṭhānaisu asaṅkhyaiyya vācakaupi saddau napuṃsakauva hauti, tasmā ‘‘cattāri satasahassānī’’ti ca ‘‘chaḷabhiñ‍ñā’’ti ca ‘‘mahiddhikā’’ti ca aitaṃ padattayaṃ samānādhikaraṇaṃ| atha vā chaḷabhiñ‍ñā mahiddhikāti chaḷabhiñ‍ñānaṃ mahaddhikānanti sāmiatthai pac‍cattavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| imasmiṃ panatthai ‘‘cattāri satasahassānī’’ti ayaṃ saṅkhyāvacanau bhavati| ‘‘tīṇi satasahassāni, nāriyau samalaṅkatā’’tiādīsupi ayaṃ nayau naitabbau| ‘‘tā ca satta satā bhariyā, dāsyau satta satāni cā’’ti pāḷi| aittha satāti ‘‘satānī’’ti napuṃsakavasaina gahaitabbaṃ, na itthiliṅgavasaina| ‘‘satā’’ti hi ‘‘pañ‍ca cittā vipākā’’tiādīni viya napuṃsakarūpaṃ| itthiliṅgabhūtā hi satasaddau natthi, tathā pul‍liṅgabhūtau| yadi ca dviliṅgau satasaddau siyā, aivañ‍ca sati ‘‘purisau, kañ‍ñā’’ti ca aukārantapul‍liṅgaākārantitthirūpaihipibhavitabbaṃ| rūpadvayampi satasaddassa natthi, taina ñāyati ‘‘satasaddau aikantanapuṃsakau’’ti|

nanu ca bhau ‘‘tā daivatā sattasatā uḷārā’’ti aittha satasaddau itthiliṅgau hutvā dissatīti? na, napuṃsakauyaivāti| nanu ca bhau daivatāsaddaina samānādhikaraṇauti? sac‍caṃ samānādhikaraṇau, tathāpi napuṃsakauyaiva| īdisaisu hi saṅkhyāvisayaisu samānādhikaraṇabhāvau appamāṇau| tathā hi ‘‘pañ‍ca pac‍caikabuddhasatāni imasmiṃ isigilismiṃ pabbatai ciravāsinau ahaisu’’nti napuṃsakaliṅgaina pul‍liṅgassa samānādhikaraṇatā dissati, tasmā ‘‘tā daivatā sattasatā uḷārā’’ti aitthāpi ‘‘sattasatānī’’ti napuṃsakabhāvauyaivāti avagantabbau| ‘‘satta hatthisatai datvā’’tiādīsupi satasaddau napuṃsakauyaiva| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘navutikauṭisahassaihi, parivāraisi mahāmunī’’ti pāḷi| aittha ‘‘navutikauṭisahassaihi bhikkhūhī’’ti vā ‘‘bhikkhūnaṃ navutikauṭisahassaihī’’ti vā saṅkhyaiyyasaṅkhyāpadhānavasaina atthau gahaitabbau| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘satasahassavassāni, āyu tassa mahaisinau’’ti pāḷi| aittha ‘‘satasahassavassānī’’ti kālassa ac‍cantasaṃyaugavasaina upayaugavacanaṃ| tathā ‘‘dasavassasahassāni, agāra’majjha sau vasī’’ti pāḷiyampi| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘itau  satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau’’ti pāḷi, ‘‘aikanavutai itau kappai’’ti pāḷi ca| aittha satasahassamhi kappaiti satasahassānaṃ kappānaṃ matthakai| aikanavutai kappaiti aikanavutiyā kappānaṃ matthakaiti bhummavacanassa sāmibhummavacanavasaina atthau gahaitabbau| tathā hi ‘‘bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti aittha bhummavacanassa ‘‘bhagavatau santikai’’ti sāmibhummavacanavasaina atthau gahitau| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘yadi tattha sahassāni, satāni nahutāni ca|

naivamhākaṃ bhayaṃ kauci, vanai vāḷaisu vij‍jatī’’ti

pāḷi| ayamaitassā atthau – tattha vanai vāḷānaṃ sahassāni ca satāni ca nahutāni ca yadi vij‍janti| atha vā sahassāni satānīti satasahassāni, vāḷānaṃ satasahassāni ca nahutāni ca yadi vij‍janti, aivaṃ vij‍jantaisupi vāḷaisu kaucīti k‍vaci| kaucisaddau hi ‘‘kau tai balaṃ mahārājā’’ti aittha kausaddau viya k‍vasaddatthai vattati, nimittatthai cāyaṃ niddaisau| taina ‘‘kauci k‍vaci kismiñ‍ci vāḷai aikassapi vāḷamigassa kāraṇā naivamhākaṃ bhayaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti atthau gahaitabbau| atha vā kaucīti kiñ‍ci appamattakampi| aittha pana ‘‘vāḷaisū’’ti nimittatthai bhummaṃ| vāḷānaṃ kāraṇā appamattakampi amhākaṃ bhayaṃ na vij‍jatīti| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘sabbaṃ satasahassāni, chattiṃsaparimaṇḍalaṃ|

dasañ‍caiva sahassāni, aḍḍhuḍḍhāni satāni cā’’ti

aṭṭhakathāpāṭhau| aittha yasmā saddatau samānavibhattiliṅgavacanānaṃ padānaṃ asamānavibhattiliṅgavacanānaṃ vā atthatau pana samānānaṃ dūrai ṭhitānampi aikasambandhau hauti, itaraisaṃ samīpai ṭhitānampi na hauti, tasmā ‘‘sabba’’ntidaṃ ‘‘parimaṇḍala’’ntiminā sambandhitabbaṃ| ‘‘chattiṃsā’’ti idaṃ pana ‘‘satasahassānī’’timinā sambandhitabbaṃ| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘duvai satasahassāni, cattāri nahutāni ca|

aittakaṃ bahalattaina, saṅkhātāyaṃ vasundharā’’ti

aṭṭhakathāpāṭhau| aittha ‘‘duvai’’ti visaisanaṃ, ‘‘satasahassānī’’ti visaisitabbaṃ| tathā ‘‘cattārī’’ti visaisanaṃ, ‘‘nahutānī’’ti visaisitabbaṃ| tathā hi ‘‘satasahassāni nahutāni cā’’ti imāni ‘‘duvai cattārī’’ti imaihi visaisitabbattā ‘‘dvisatasahassaṃ catunahuta’’nti atthappakāsanāni bhavanti| aivaṃ santaipi ‘‘duvai’’ic‍cādīnaṃ saṅkhyāsaddānaṃ ‘‘satasahassānī’’tiādīhi saṅkhyāsaddaihi samānādhikaraṇatā pubbācariyaihi na vuttā| yasmā pana yathā ‘‘duvai puthuj‍janā vuttā| satasahassaṃ bhikkhū’’tiādīsu samānādhikaraṇatā labbhati dabbavācakattā visaisitabbapadānaṃ, na tathā ‘‘duvai satasahassānī’’tiādīsu adabbavācakattā visaisitabbapadānaṃ, tasmā īdisaisu ṭhānaisu samānādhikaraṇatā na icchitabbā yuttiyā abhāvatau| yadi aivaṃ ‘‘kusalā, rūpaṃ, cakkhumā’’tiādīnaṃ viya imaisamañ‍ñamañ‍ñasambandharahitā siyāti? na, visaisanavisaisitabbabhāvaina gahitattā| yaj‍jaivaṃ samānādhikaraṇabhāvau laddhabbauti? na, niyamābhāvatau| aikantaina hi  guṇaguṇīnaṃyaiva visaisanavisaisitabbānaṃ samānādhikaraṇabhāvau, na itaraisaṃ visaisanavisaisitabbattaipi|

tattha ‘‘aittaka’’nti pamāṇavacanaṃ| ‘‘bahalattainā’’ti visaisanai tatiyā| ubhayaina imamatthaṃ dassaiti ‘‘ayaṃ vasundharā bahalattaina yaujanānaṃ duvai satasahassāni cattāri nahutāni ca aittakaṃ saṅkhātā’’ti| ‘‘aittaka’’nti padassa ca ‘‘duvai satasahassāni cattāri nahutāni cā’’ti imaihi vā ‘‘vasundharā’’ti iminā vā samānādhikaraṇatā na icchitabbā| aittakanti hi bhāvanapuṃsakaṃ, yaṃ saddasatthai kriyāvisaisananti vadanti| tassa ‘‘aittakaina pamāṇaina’’ic‍caivatthau| apica ‘‘duvai satasahassāni cattāri na hutāni cā’’ti imaisampi ‘‘vasundharā’’ti iminā samānādhikaraṇatā na icchitabbā ‘‘bhikkhūnaṃ sata’’nti aittha satasaddassa viya saṅkhyāvacanamattattā| tathā hi ‘‘aittaka’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘saṅkhātā’’ti pana ‘‘aya’’nti ca imaisaṃ ‘‘vasundharā’’ti iminā samānādhikaraṇatā labbhati| sabbaupāyaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘dasaittha rājiyau saitā, dassanīyā manauramā|

cha piṅgalā pan‍narasa, haliddā tā catuddasā’’ti

pāḷi| aittha cha piṅgalā pan‍narasāti cha ca pan‍narasa cāti aikavīsati piṅgalā rājiyauti atthau gahaitabbau|

tathā –

‘‘puttāpi tassa bahavau, ‘aikanāmā’ti mai sutaṃ|

asīti dasa aikau ca, indanāmā mahabbalā’’ti

pāḷi| aittha pana ‘‘aikanavutī’’ti vattabbai ‘‘asīti dasa aikau cā’’ti vuttaṃ| vicitrasaddaracanañhi pāvacanaṃ| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘tiṃsapurisanāvutyau, sabbaivaikaikanic‍citā|

yaisaṃ samaṃ na passāmi, kaivalaṃ mahi’maṃ cara’’nti

pāḷi| aittha ‘‘purisānaṃ tiṃsasahassāni navuti ca satāni tiṃsa nāvutyau’’ti vuc‍canti| imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai tiṃsasaddatau sahassasaddassa navutisaddatau ca satasaddassa laupaṃ katvā ‘‘tiṃsa nāvutyau’’ti vuttanti na gahaitabbaṃ| aivañhi gahaṇai sati yattha katthacipi aidisī saddaracanā kātabbā siyā, katāya ca aidisāya saddaracanāya atthāvagamau vinā upadaisaina suṇantānaṃ na siyā, tasmā naivaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ pana gahaitabbaṃ – ‘‘tiṃsa nāvutyau’’ti idaṃ laukasaṅkaitarūḷhaṃ vacanaṃ, saṅkaitarūḷhassa pana vacanassatthau yasmā gahitapubbasaṅkaitaihi sutvā ñāyatai, na upadaisatau, tasmā brahmadattaina rañ‍ñā vuttakālaipi satthārā taṃ kathaṃ āharitvā vuttakālaipi sabbai manussā vināpi upadaisaina vacanatthaṃ jānantīti gahaitabbaṃ|

tiṃsañ‍caiva sahassāni, navuti ca satāni tu|

tiṃsa nāvutiyau nāma, vuttā umaṅgajātakai||

yasmā pāvacanai santi, nayā caiva acintiyā|

vauhārā ca suguḷhatthā, dayāpan‍naina daisitā||

tasmā sāṭṭhakathai dhīrau, gambhīrai jinabhāsitai|

upadaisaṃ sadā gaṇhai, garuṃ sammā upaṭṭhahaṃ||

garūpadaisahīnau hi, atthasāraṃ na vindati|

atthasāravihīnau sau, saddhammā parihāyati||

garūpadaisalābhī  ca, atthasārasamāyutau|

saddhammaṃ paripālaintau, saddhammasmā na hāyati||

saddhammatthāya mai tasmā, saṅkhyāmālāpi bhāsitā|

sappayaugā yathāyaugaṃ, sahaivatthavinicchayā||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

savinicchayau saṅkhyānāmānaṃ nāmikapadamālāvibhāgau

nāma

tairasamau paricchaidau|

14. atthattikavibhāga

bhūdhātu tāya nipphan‍na-rūpañ‍cāti idaṃ dvayaṃ|

katvā padhānamamhaihi, sabbamaitaṃ papañ‍citaṃ||

bhavatissa vasā dāni, vakkhāmatthattikaṃ varaṃ|

atthuddhārau tumantañ‍ca, tvādiyantaṃ tikaṃ idha||

tasmā tāva bhūdhātutau pavattassa bhūtasaddassa atthuddhārau nīyatai –

khandhasattāmanussaisu, vij‍jamānai ca dhātuyaṃ|

khīṇāsavai rukkhādimhi, bhūtasaddau pavattati||

uppādai cāpi viñ‍ñaiyyau, bhūtasaddau vibhāvinā|

vipulai saupasaggauyaṃ, hīḷanai vidhamaipi ca|

parājayai vaidiyanai, nāmai pākaṭatāya ca||

vuttañhaitaṃ – bhūtasaddau pañ‍cakkhandhāmanussadhātuvij‍jamānakhīṇāsavasattarukkhādīsu dissati| ‘‘bhūtamidanti bhikkhavai samanupassathā’’tiādīsu hi ayaṃ pañ‍cakkhandhaisu dissati| ‘‘yānīdha  bhūtāni samāgatānī’’ti aittha amanussai| ‘‘cattārau khau bhikkhu mahābhūtā haitū’’ti aittha dhātūsu| ‘‘bhūtasmiṃ pācittiya’’ntiādīsu vij‍jamānai| ‘‘yau ca kālaghasau bhūtau’’ti aittha khīṇāsavai| ‘‘sabbaiva nikkhipissanti, bhūtā laukai samussaya’’nti aittha sattai| ‘‘bhūtagāmapātabyatāyā’’ti aittha rukkhādīsūti| mūlapariyāyasuttaṭṭhakathāya vacanaṃ idaṃ| ṭīkāyamādisaddaina uppādādīni gayharai| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhata’’ntiādīsu bhūtasaddau uppādai dissati| saupasaggau pana ‘‘pabhūtamariyau pakarauti puñ‍ña’’ntiādīsu vipulai| ‘‘yaibhuyyaina bhikkhūnaṃ paribhūtarūpau’’tiādīsu hīḷanai| ‘‘sambhūtau sāṇavāsī’’tiādīsu pañ‍ñattiyaṃ| ‘‘abhibhūtau mārau vijitau saṅgāmau’’tiādīsu vidhamanai| ‘‘parābhūtarūpaukhau ayaṃ acailau pāthikaputtau’’tiādīsu parājayai| ‘‘anubhūtaṃ sukhadukkha’’ntiādīsu vaidiyanai| ‘‘vibhūtaṃ pañ‍ñāyā’’tiādīsu pākaṭīkaraṇai dissati, tai sabbai ‘‘rukkhādīsū’’tiādisaddaina saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbāti|

idāni tumantapadāni vuc‍cantai –

bhavituṃ, ubbhavituṃ, samubbhavituṃ, pabhavituṃ, parābhavituṃ, atibhavituṃ, sambhavituṃ, vibhavituṃ, bhautuṃ, sambhautuṃ, vibhautuṃ, pātubhavituṃ, pātubbhavituṃ vā, pātubhautuṃ| imāni akammakāni tumantapadāni|

paribhautuṃ  paribhavituṃ, abhibhautuṃ abhibhavituṃ, adhibhautuṃ adhibhavituṃ, atibhautuṃ atibhavituṃ, anubhautuṃ anubhavituṃ, samanubhautuṃ samanubhavituṃ, abhisambhautuṃ abhisambhavituṃ| imāni sakammakāni tumantapadāni, sabbānaitāni suddhakattari bhavanti|

‘‘bhāvaituṃ, pabhāvaituṃ, sambhāvaituṃ, vibhāvaituṃ, paribhāvaituṃ’’ic‍caivamādīni haitukattari tumantapadāni, sabbānipi haitukattari tumantapadāni sakammakāniyaiva bhavanti| uddaisauyaṃ|

tatra samānatthapadaisu aikamaivādipadaṃ gahaitvā niddaisau kātabbau – bhavitunti hautuṃ vij‍jituṃ pañ‍ñāyituṃ sarūpaṃ labhituṃ| aittha vuttanayānusāraina saisānampi tumantānaṃ niddaisau vitthāraitabbau, sabbāni tumantapadāni catutthiyatthai vattanti ‘‘tvaṃ mama cittamañ‍ñāya, naittaṃ yācitumāgatau’’ti aittha viya| yācitunti hi yācanatthāyāti atthau| tasmā bhavituntiādīnampi ‘‘bhavanatthāyā’’ti vā ‘‘bhavanattha’’nti vā ‘‘bhavanāyā’’ti vā ādinā atthau gahaitabbau| apica ‘‘naikkhammaṃ daṭṭhu khaimatau’’ti aittha ‘‘daṭṭhu’’nti padassa ‘‘disvā’’ti atthadassanatau yathārahaṃ tumantāni tvāsaddantapadatthavasainapi gahaitabbāni| aitāni ca nipātapadaisu saṅgahaṃgacchanti| vuttañhi niruttipiṭakai nipātapadaparicchaidai ‘‘tuṃ iti catutthiyā’’ti| tatrāyamatthau ‘‘tuṃ iti aitadantau nipātau catutthiyā atthai vattatī’’ti|

tumantakathā samattā|

idāni  tvādiyantapadāni vuc‍cantai –

bhavitvā, bhavitvāna, bhavituna, bhaviya, bhaviyāna| ubbhavitvā, ubbhavitvāna, ubbhavituna, ubbhaviya, ubbhaviyāna| aisa nayau ‘‘samubbhavitvā, parābhavitvā, sambhavitvā, vibhavitvā, pātubbhavitvā’’ti aitthāpi| imāni akammakāni ussuk‍kanatthāni tvādiyantapadāni|

bhutvā, bhutvāna, paribhavitvā, paribhavitvāna, paribhavituna, paribhaviya, paribhaviyāna, paribhuyya| abhibhavitvā, abhibhavitvāna, abhibhavituna, abhibhaviya, abhibhaviyāna, abhibhuyya| aisa nayau ‘‘adhibhavitvā, atibhavitvā, anubhavitvā’’ti aitthāpi| idañ‍caittha nidassanaṃ| ‘‘tamavauca rājā anubhaviyāna tampi, aiyyāsi khippaṃ ahamapi pūjaṃ kassa’’nti| anubhutvā, anubhutvāna| adhibhautvā, adhibhautvāna|

‘‘saṭṭhi kappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

añ‍ñai daivai adhibhautvā, issaraṃ kārayissatī’’ti

idamaittha pāḷinidassanaṃ, imāni sakammakāni ussuk‍kanatthāni tvādiyantapadāni| imāni cattāri suddhakattariyaiva bhavanti|

‘‘bhāvaitvā, bhāvaitvāna| pabhāvaitvā, pabhāvaitvāna| sambhāvaitvā, sambhāvaitvāna| vibhāvaitvā, vibhāvaitvāna| paribhāvaitvā, paribhāvaitvāna’’ic‍caivamādīni sakammakāni ussuk‍kanatthāni tvādiyantapadāni haitukattariyaiva bhavanti| uddaisauyaṃ|

tatra samānatthapadaisu aikamaivādipadaṃ gahaitvā niddaisau kātabbau – bhavitvāti hutvā pañ‍ñāyitvā sarūpaṃ labhitvā| aivaṃ vuttanayānusāraina saisānampi tvādiyantapadānaṃ niddaisau vitthāraitabbau| ayaṃ pana visaisau  bhutvāti sampattiṃ anubhutvāti sakammakavasaina atthau gahaitabbau| bhutvā anubhutvāti imaisañhi samānatthataṃ saddhammavidū icchanti| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘bhutvā bhutvāna’’ic‍caitai, ‘‘anubhutvā’’timassa hi|

atthaṃ sūcainti ‘‘hutvā’’ti, padassa pana naiva tai||

kaici ‘‘bhūtvā’’ti dīghattaṃ, tassa icchanti sāsanai|

dīghatā rassatā caiva, dvayampaitaṃ padissati||

saddasatthai ca ‘‘bhūtvā’’ti, dīghattasañhitaṃ padaṃ|

‘‘bhavitvā’’ti padassatthaṃ, dīpaiti na tu sāsanai||

‘‘hutvā’’iti padaṃyaiva, dīpaiti jinasāsanai|

‘‘bhavitvā’’ti padassatthaṃ, natthi añ‍ñattha taṃ padaṃ||

ic‍caivaṃ savisaisantu, vacanaṃ sāradassinā|

sāsanai saddasatthai ca, viñ‍ñunā paikkhitabbakaṃ||

aivaṃ ussuk‍kanatthai pavattāni tvādiyantapadānipi niddiṭṭhāni, sabbānaitāni avibhattikānīti gahaitabbāni| niruttipiṭakai hi nipātaparicchaidai avibhattikāni katvā tvādiyantapadāni vuttāni| saddatthavidūnaṃ pana matai paṭhamādivibhattivasaina savibhattikāni bhavanti|

imasmiñ‍ca pana tvādiyantādhikārai idañ‍cupalakkhitabbaṃ – bhutvā gacchati, bhutvā gatau, bhutvā gamissasi, kasitvā vapati| umaṅgā nikkhamitvāna, vaidaihau nāvamāruhi| bhutvāna bhikkhu bhikkhassu ic‍cādī samānakattukānaṃ dhātūnaṃ pubbakālai tvādisaddappayaugā| ‘‘bhutvā gacchatī’’ti aittha hi ‘‘bhutvā’’ti idaṃ pubbakālakriyādīpakaṃ padaṃ| ‘‘gacchatī’’ti idaṃ pana uttarakālakriyādīpakaṃ, samānakattukāni caitāni padāni aikakattukānaṃ kriyānaṃ vācakattā| tathā haittha yau gamanakriyāya  kattā, sau aiva bhuñ‍janakriyāya kattubhūtau daṭṭhabbau| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñatrāpi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

‘‘andhakāraṃ nihantvāna, uditauyaṃ divākarau|

vaṇṇaṃ pañ‍ñāvabhāsaihi, aubhāsaitvā samuggatau’’

ic‍cādīnipi pana samānakattukānaṃ samānakālai tvādisaddappayaugā| aittha hi ‘‘nihantvānā’’ti padaṃ samānakālakriyādīpakaṃ padaṃ| ‘‘uditau’’ti idaṃ pana uttarakālakriyādīpakaṃ padanti na vattabbaṃ samānakālakriyāya idhādhippaitattā| tasmāyaiva samānakālakriyādīpakaṃ padanti gahaitabbaṃ| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñatrāpi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

kaici pana ‘‘mukhaṃ byādāya sayati, akkhiṃ parivattaitvā passatī’’ti udāharanti| aparai ‘‘nisaj‍ja adhītai, ṭhatvā kathaitī’’ti| tattha byādānaparivattanuttarakālau byādānūpasamalakkhaṇaṃ passanakriyāya lakkhiyati| ‘‘nisaj‍ja adhītai, ṭhatvā kathaitī’’ti ca samānakālatāyapi ajjhainakathanaihi pubbaipi nisaj‍jaṭṭhānāni hauntīti sak‍kā pubbuttarakālatā sambhāvaituṃ, tasmā purimāniyaiva udāharaṇāni yuttāni| udayasamakālamaiva hi tan‍nivattanīyanivattananti|

‘‘dvāramāvaritvā pavisati’’ic‍cādi samānakattukānaṃ aparakālai tvādisaddappayaugau| yasmā panaittha pavisanakriyā purimā, āvaraṇakriyā pana pacchimā, tasmā ‘‘āvaritvā’’ti idaṃ aparakālakriyādīpakaṃ padanti vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘pavisatī’’ti idaṃ pana pubbakālakriyādīpakaṃ padanti| ayaṃ nayau  añ‍ñatrāpi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau| aparai ‘‘dha’nti kac‍ca patitau daṇḍau’’ti udāharaṇanti| abhighātabhūtasamāyaugai pana abhighātajasaddassa samānakālatā aittha labbhatīti idhāpi purimāniyaiva udāharaṇāni yuttānīti|

‘‘pisācaṃ disvā cassa bhayaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā’’ic‍cādi asamānai kattari payaugau| aittha hi pisācaṃ disvā purisassa bhayaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñāya disvā assa puggalassa āsavā parikkhīṇā| aivaṃ samānakattukatā dhātūnaṃ na labbhati dassanakriyāya purisaisu pavattanatau, bhavanādikriyāya ca bhayādīsu pavattanatauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñatrāpi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu naitabbau|

idampi panaittha upalakkhitabbaṃ ‘‘appatvā nadiṃ pabbatau, atik‍kamma pabbataṃ nadī’’ic‍cādi parāparayaugau| ‘‘sīhaṃ disvā bhayaṃ hauti, ghataṃ pivitvā balaṃ jāyatai, ‘dha’nti katvā daṇḍau patitau’’ic‍cādi lakkhaṇahaituādippayaugau| ‘‘nhatvā gamanaṃ, bhutvā sayanaṃ| upādāya rūpa’’mic‍cāpi byattayaina saddasiddhippayaugauti|

ic‍caivaṃ sabbathāpi samānakattukānaṃ dhātūnaṃ pubbakālai tvādisaddappayaugau, samānakattukānaṃ samānakālai tvādisaddappayaugau, samānakattukānaṃ aparakālai tvādisaddappayaugau, asamānakattukānaṃ tvādisaddappayaugau, parāparayaugau, lakkhaṇahaituādippayaugau, byattayaina saddasiddhippayaugauti sattadhā tvādiyantānaṃ padānaṃ payaugau vaiditabbau|

yadi aivaṃ kasmā kac‍cāyanai ‘‘pubbakālaikakattukānaṃ tuna tvāna tvā vā’’ti pubbakālaiyaiva aikakattukaggahaṇaṃ katanti? yaituyyaina tvādiyantānaṃ padānaṃ purimakālakriyādīpanatau| kac‍cāyanai hi yaibhuyyaina pavattiṃ sandhāya ‘‘pubbakālaikakattukāna’’nti vuttaṃ| yasmā pana ‘‘iti katvā’’tiādīnaṃ padānaṃ haituatthavasainapi pubbācariyaihi atthau saṃvaṇṇitau, tasmā ‘‘bhavitvā’’tiādīnaṃ bhūdhātumayānaṃ tvādisaddantānaṃ padānaṃ añ‍ñaisañ‍ca ‘‘pacitvā’’tiādīnaṃ yathāpayaugaṃ ‘‘bhavanahaitu pacanahaitū’’tiādinā haituatthaupi gahaitabbau| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

haitutthaipi yatau haunti, saddā ussuk‍kanatthakā|

tasmā haituvasaināpi, vadaiyyatthaṃ vicakkhaṇau||

‘‘iti katvā’’ti saddassa, atthasaṃvaṇṇanāsu hi|

‘‘iti karaṇahaitū’’ti, atthau dhīraihi gayhati||

‘‘gacchāmi dāni nibbānaṃ, yattha gantvā na saucati’’|

iti pāṭhaipi haitutthau, gayhatai pubbaviñ‍ñubhi||

‘‘yasmiṃ nibbānai gamana-haitū’’ti hi kathīyatai|

haitutthaivaṃ yathāyauga-mañ‍ñatrāpi ayaṃ nayau||

aivaṃ bhūtasaddassa atthuddhārau ca tumantapadañ‍ca tvādiyantapadañ‍cāti atthattikaṃ vibhattaṃ|

yau  imamatthatikaṃ suvibhattaṃ,

kaṇṇarasāyanamāgamikānaṃ|

dhārayatai sa bhavai gatakaṅkhā,

pāvacanamhi gatai sukhumatthai||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

atthattikavibhāgau nāma

cuddasamau paricchaidau|

aivaṃ nānappakāratau bhūdhāturūpāni dassitāni|

padamālā niṭṭhitā|
